# The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time — 100% RetroAchievements Walkthrough

## Metadata

- **Platform:** Nintendo 64
- **Developer:** Unknown
- **Publisher:** Nintendo
- **Genre:** Action-Adventure
- **Released:** 1998-11-21
- **Achievements:** 97 (690 points)

> [!NOTE]
> Every instruction in this guide was extracted from the sources listed at the bottom and checked against them (1172 facts confirmed by 2+ sources, 4901 from a single source). Statements marked 🟡 come from only one source. Anything we could not confirm says **"Unable to verify from collected sources."**

## Game Overview

### What this game is

*The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time* is a Nintendo 64 action-adventure game released on 1998-11-21. The achievement set has 97 achievements worth 690 points. Link can travel between childhood and adulthood by visiting the Temple of Time with the Master Sword and the Ocarina of Time [[gf1-10-2]]. When young Link opens the Door of Time, the Sacred Realm is unsealed and the three Triforce pieces go to Ganondorf (Power), Link (Courage) and Zelda (Wisdom) [[gf1-525-2]]. Things you do as a child, such as planting a magic bean, uncovering an item or befriending someone, directly affect the future [[gf1-10-3]].

The achievement set is built for the Ocarina of Time (U) (V1.0) ROM, MD5 5bd1fe107bf8106b2ab6650abecd54d6. A commenter also tells players to use the 1.0 U ROM [[ra-comments-1-2, ra-comments-4-7, ra-comments-3-1]]. Do not use any other version.

### Hardcore Mode and how the walkthrough is organised

This guide assumes Hardcore Mode, which turns off the emulator's save states, rewinds and cheats, so every attempt must be won with the game's own save system. Difficulty does not affect the set. Set Z-targeting to **Hold** for the boss challenges. Save before every missable boss and reset on failure. Saving inside a dungeon restarts you at its entrance.

You need **two separate save files**:

- **File 1 (Chapters 1-24)** is the 100% run. It collects everything, including every Map, Compass and Boss Key for the Cartographer achievement, and earns every achievement except the 3-heart ones.
- **File 2 (Chapters 25-27)** is the 3-heart run. It earns the nine 3-heart boss achievements and Blind Explorer. Blind Explorer forbids Maps, Compasses and any heart upgrade.

Achievements marked with "3 hearts" in parentheses need a 3-heart run [[ra-comments-3-2]]. One commenter describes the 3-heart run as limiting you to a maximum of 3 hearts, so Heart Containers cannot be collected [[ra-comments-1-1]]. A boss achievement reads your health bar, and any change to it fails the check [[ra-comments-132-1, ra-comments-132-2]].

Cartographer and Blind Explorer are mutually exclusive on one file. One commenter says you must choose between them at the start of the game, and that getting a heart, map or compass by accident forces a restart from the beginning [[ra-comments-141-6, ra-comments-141-7]]. Plenty of Hearts needs all 20 hearts, so it cannot share a file with the 3-heart run.

### Controls and combat basics

- **Z-targeting:** Navi's most important job is Z-Targeting, which locks Link's view onto one foe and changes his controls for battle [[gf1-13-1]].
- **A while targeted:** A strafes left or right, does a backflip when you move backward, and does the Jump Attack when you tilt forward. The Jump Attack is the most powerful attack in Link's repertoire [[gf1-13-2]].
- **R while targeted:** Holding R raises your shield while you keep moving [[gf1-13-3]].
- **B while targeted:** Tapping B gives a downward plunge, and forward plus B gives a stab [[gf1-13-4]].
- **Shield stab without lock-on:** Holding R without lock-on and pressing B stabs repeatedly, faster than a normal slash. It is recommended for killing bosses quickly [[gf3-9-1]].
- **Crouch stab:** This is done by holding R (not locked on) and pressing the attack button [[ra-comments-7-3]]. Commenters report that it copies the damage of your previous attack, so a hard hit followed by crouch stabs is very strong [[ra-comments-6-2, ra-comments-8-11]]. That stored damage resets when you enter a different room [[ra-comments-8-12]].
- **Spin attack:** The Advanced Spin Slash is done by holding B until the sword glows yellow, then releasing [[gf3-39-1]].
- **Deku Sticks:** A burning Deku Stick can be saved by drawing your sword, which puts it away before it burns out [[checklist-sidequests-6-3]].
- **Adult item limits:** Adult Link can only use the Deku Nuts, Ocarina of Time, Bombchus and Bombs among the items he got as a child. Bottles and magic powers work for either Link [[gf3-40-1]].

### Core systems you must understand

**The Ocarina.** Songs are learned from NPCs and Sheik, and the warp songs matter for travel. The Prelude of Light warps to the Temple of Time [[gf1-397-4]]. The Minuet of Forest warps to the Sacred Forest Meadow [[gf1-395-2]]. The Bolero of Fire warps to Death Mountain Crater [[gf1-395-4]]. The Serenade of Water warps to Lake Hylia [[gf1-396-2]]. The Nocturne of Shadow warps to the high ledge in Kakariko Graveyard [[gf1-397-2]]. The Requiem of Spirit warps to the Desert Colossus [[gf1-396-4]]. Playing Zelda's Lullaby in front of a Gossip Stone grants a health-restoring fairy [[gf3-19-2]]. Epona's Song gives free Lon Lon Milk from any cow in the game once you have an empty bottle [[gf1-43-2]].

**The Scarecrow's Song.** As a child, play any eight-note song of your own to the scarecrow at Lake Hylia. It cannot be one of the game's songs and must contain at least two different notes. Write it down, because you replay it as an adult to unlock the Scarecrow's Song [[gf1-98-2, gf1-165-1]]. If you forget it, Sheik at the Temple of Time teaches you how to return to childhood so you can teach Pierre a new one [[gf1-165-2]].

**Magic beans.** Beans are planted in soft soil as young Link. They only grow fully once you are an adult, and then become Magic Leaves that carry you to out-of-reach places [[gf3-173-2, gf1-385-1]]. Plan your bean planting as a child.

**Bottles.** Bottles hold fairies and Lon Lon Milk, and can capture bugs and Blue Fire [[gf1-383-3]].

**Heart Pieces.** There are 36 Pieces of Heart, and four make a Heart Container. Together with boss Heart Containers they give a maximum of 20 hearts [[gf3-123-1, ra-guide-20-2, gf2-159-17]].

**Gold Skulltulas.** Defeating Gold Skulltulas and taking their tokens frees members of the cursed family in Kakariko Village, and the House of Skulltula pays out prizes [[gf1-14-2]]. For skulltulas in soft soil, catch bugs from under rocks and drop them on the soil [[gf4-3-1]]. The Boomerang and later the Hookshot can collect tokens from a distance [[gf1-386-4, gf1-387-4]]. Take the token after killing the Skulltula, because the kill alone is not enough [[checklist-skulltulas-2-9]]. Always keep one bug when you empty a bottle, since you will need bugs again.

**Rupees.** Green rupees are worth 1, blue 5, red 20, purple 50 and huge yellow ones 200 [[gf3-168-3]]. You will need money for beans, Blue Fire, minigames and shop items.

**Silver Rupees.** Silver Rupees are puzzle rupees. They are worth five rupees each and open locked doors [[gf2-160-9]].

### Dungeon order logic

The first three dungeons are always the same. When you first become an adult you must obtain the Hookshot before anything else [[gf1-17-1]]. After that the order is flexible within limits:

- The Fire Temple and Ice Cavern can be completed without the Fairy Bow from the Forest Temple [[gf1-18-1]].
- With the Ice Cavern and Forest Temple done, you can start the Water Temple before the Fire Temple is finished [[gf1-18-2]].
- Once the Forest Temple is complete you can return to childhood at any time, and the Bottom of the Well becomes available [[gf1-18-4]].
- The Bottom of the Well must be completed before the Shadow Temple can be finished [[gf1-19-1]].
- Both the Bottom of the Well and Gerudo's Fortress must be done before the Spirit Temple can be attempted [[gf1-19-2]].

The chapters in this guide already follow a safe order that respects all of this. Follow them in sequence.

### The 100% plan in plain language

File 1 is one long pass through the game, split into 24 chapters. The first ten chapters take you through childhood: Kokiri Forest, the Deku Tree, Hyrule Castle Town, Kakariko, Lon Lon Ranch, the Goron and Zora areas, and the three child dungeons. They end with the Ocarina of Time. Chapters 11 to 21 cover adulthood: the temples, the Biggoron's Sword trading quest and the Gerudo areas. The remaining chapters mop up Lon Lon Ranch, the fishing pond and the Gossip Stone tour, then finish with Ganon's Castle.

Doing the child cleanup chapters fully before drawing the Master Sword matters because several things cannot be done later. Do not skip ahead.

File 2 is short. It covers only the child dungeons, the adult temples and Ganon's Castle, because the nine 3-heart boss achievements and Blind Explorer are the only things it needs. On that file you collect nothing that raises health or reveals a dungeon.

### Missables to keep in mind early

Each chapter warns you right before a trap, but these are worth knowing from the start, and the sources below are the reason for each:

- **Deku Nut upgrades.** Wearing the Mask of Truth on the Lost Woods forest stage and speaking to the Scrub Chief raises your Deku Nut capacity [[gf1-125-3]]. Commenters say this is missable because a flag set by the Poacher's Saw makes the second upgrade unobtainable [[ra-comments-44-1, ra-comments-44-2, ra-comments-44-3]]. A separate upgrade is sold by the Deku Scrub in a hidden grotto under a rock before the Sacred Forest Meadow entrance, and that one is the Stocking Nuts target [[ra-guide-8-7, ra-comments-73-1]]. It is **unconfirmed** whether the Lost Woods upgrades can still be earned as an adult before the Poacher's Saw. Get A Pretty Face, A Horrible Face and Stocking Nuts as Young Link before drawing the Master Sword.
- **The Hylian Shield chest.** A commenter warns that buying the shield from the Bazaar replaces the chest that gives the achievement with a rupee chest [[ra-comments-64-1]]. Do not buy the shield.
- **The dying soldier.** The guard in the Castle Town back alley is missable. He disappears for good after the Temple of Time cutscenes, so talk to him after Zelda gives you the Ocarina and before you go there [[ra-guide-10-4]]. One commenter says he appears after Zelda and Impa escape [[ra-comments-78-2]].
- **Zelda's window.** Shooting the window on the left with the slingshot right before speaking to Zelda angers a guard and triggers a dialogue. This is missable because you cannot return after collecting all three Spiritual Stones [[ra-guide-5-4, gf1-39-1]].
- **The gate guard's bribe.** It is **unconfirmed** exactly what triggers the bribe offer for Money Solves Everything. Comments mention a hatched egg from Malon and talking to the guard twice [[ra-comments-56-1]]. Have Malon's egg, be caught once and return, doing all three before meeting Zelda and no other story step in between.
- **Goron City rocks.** The entrance rocks in Goron City carry permanent flags, so destroying them can ruin the related achievement [[ra-comments-68-1]]. A commenter says you can instead jump to the side of them [[ra-comments-68-2]].
- **Dampe's heart piece.** If you dig up the heart piece in the gravedigging minigame and leave without collecting it, it is lost forever [[ra-comments-101-1]].
- **King Zora and the tunic.** Buy the Zora Tunic before you thaw King Zora with Blue Fire. Then he offers a kiss instead of the free Zora Tunic [[ra-comments-37-3, ra-guide-11-6]]. It is **unconfirmed** whether the Zora Tunic must be worn while thawing King Zora for Kiss That Frog. Buy the tunic, equip it, save, then thaw King Zora and talk to him while still wearing it. A commenter also suggests killing 30 Gold Skulltulas and getting the Giant's Wallet first [[ra-comments-37-1]].
- **Twinrova.** Saving after dying on this boss fight makes the achievement fail, and a soft reset and reload is needed [[ra-comments-46-1, ra-comments-46-2]].
- **Fishing.** Catching a fish of 10 lbs or more and then doing the early Golden Scale glitch locks you out of the heart piece [[ra-comments-112-2]]. It is **unconfirmed** how heavy the adult fish must be for Expert Angler, since commenters disagree between 12 and 13 lb. Keep fishing until the owner hands over the Golden Scale. The Sinking Lure is not allowed for the heart piece [[checklist-heartpieces-6-1]].

### Cartographer and the Jabu-Jabu map

Cartographer needs every Map, Compass and Boss Key from every dungeon [[ra-comments-3-4, ra-comments-121-2]]. A Boss Key on the way up Ganon's Castle should bring the counter to 26/26 [[ra-comments-121-3]]. Commenters report that the Jabu-Jabu's Belly map room is troublesome without Ruto. The reported method is to target the monster just outside the map room and press C-Up as you land on the switch, so the door opens even though the switch stays unpressed [[ra-comments-3-5, ra-comments-121-6]]. Treat this as a missable until the Jabu-Jabu chapter walks through it.

### Three-heart and special fight notes

- **My Last Breath (Ganon).** It is **unconfirmed** whether Bombchus count as bombs for My Last Breath. Use neither bombs nor Bombchus against Ganon.
- **Dangerously Delicious (King Dodongo).** It is **unconfirmed** whether Deku Stick hits stop it from triggering, since commenters disagree. Use the Bomb Flower and the Kokiri Sword only for King Dodongo on the 3-heart file.
- **Pieces of Heart on file 2.** One source says up to three Pieces of Heart are fine, and another says no heart upgrades are allowed [[ra-comments-103-5, ra-comments-132-1]]. This is **unconfirmed**. Pick up no Pieces of Heart and no Heart Containers on the 3-heart file.

### Practical advice before you start

Save often, but follow the per-chapter warnings about when saving is dangerous. Keep a bottle free for bugs, a spare for Blue Fire, and enough rupees on hand before each shop or minigame. Use the Song of Time, Zelda's Lullaby and the Sun's Song as the game's own tools. Write your eight-note scarecrow song on paper.

When you are ready, begin Chapter 1 on a fresh save file for the 100% run.

## Playthrough Planning

**Playthroughs required:** 2

Two save files, as the RA guide recommends. File 1 (chapters 1-24) is the 100% run: every collectible, Cartographer (every Map, Compass and Boss Key) and every non-3-heart achievement, ending with Ganon. File 2 (chapters 25-27) is the 3-heart run for the nine 3-heart boss achievements and Blind Explorer, which forbids Maps, Compasses and any heart upgrade. Cartographer and Blind Explorer are mutually exclusive on one file, and Plenty of Hearts needs all 20 hearts, so they cannot share a file.

**Settings:** Hardcore Mode recommended; difficulty does not affect the set. Set Z-targeting to Hold for the boss challenges. Save before every missable boss and reset on failure; saving inside a dungeon restarts you at its entrance.

[[ra-comments-141-6,ra-comments-1-1,ra-guide-20-2,ra-comments-121-2]]

## True 100% In-Game Completion Criteria

This guide covers complete in-game mastery in addition to all RetroAchievements:

| Category | 100% Requirement | Tracking Scope |
|---|---|---|
| **Gold Skulltulas** | All 100 Gold Skulltula tokens and every House of Skulltula reward. | Per-chapter checkpoint |
| **Pieces of Heart and Heart Containers** | All 36 Pieces of Heart and 8 boss Heart Containers for 20 hearts. | Per-chapter checkpoint |
| **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys** | All 26 dungeon Maps, Compasses and Boss Keys (Cartographer). | Per-chapter checkpoint |
| **Songs, equipment and upgrades** | All 12 songs, the four Bottles, Biggoron's Sword, Great Fairy magic and the defense upgrade, wallet, quiver, bomb, seed, stick and nut upgrades. | Endgame/Global scope |
| **Side quests and minigames** | Happy Mask and adult trading quests, Big Poes, fishing, archery, races, frogs and the 32 Gossip Stones. | Endgame/Global scope |

| Invisible Stalker (3-heart Bongo Bongo, no Lens) [[gf4-29-2,gf3-73-1,ra-comments-136-1,ra-comments-136-2]] | Invisible Stalker | [26](#chapter-26-3-heart-run-the-adult-temples) | During Bongo Bongo on the 3-heart file |
| Double Trouble (3-heart Twinrova, sword and Mirror Shield) [[gf2-96-1,gf2-96-2,ra-comments-138-1,ra-comments-138-2]] | Double Trouble | [26](#chapter-26-3-heart-run-the-adult-temples) | During Twinrova on the 3-heart file |
| Dark Lord (3-heart Ganon without a hit) [[gf3-91-1,ra-comments-139-1,ra-comments-139-2]] | Dark Lord | [27](#chapter-27-3-heart-run-ganons-castle-and-blind-explorer) | During Ganon on the 3-heart file |
| Blind Explorer (no Map or Compass, 3 hearts) [[ra-comments-141-1,ra-comments-141-2]] | Blind Explorer | [27](#chapter-27-3-heart-run-ganons-castle-and-blind-explorer) | The whole second file: any Map, Compass or heart upgrade ruins it |
| Mischievous Hero (sink the pond owner's hat) [[ra-comments-113-7,ra-comments-113-8,ra-comments-3-9]] | Mischievous Hero | [22](#chapter-22-lon-lon-ranch-dampes-race-and-the-fishing-pond) | Between hooking the owner's hat and letting it sink: a save-and-quit or reset in between leaves him hatless without the achievement |
| Plenty of Hearts (Dampe's gravedigging Piece of Heart) [[ra-guide-20-1,ra-comments-101-1]] | Plenty of Hearts | [3](#chapter-3-kakariko-village-the-graveyard-and-lon-lon-ranch) | Pick up the Piece of Heart Dampe digs up before leaving the Graveyard; left behind, it becomes 50 rupees for good |

## Points of No Return

| ⛔ Point of No Return | Chapter | What you lose access to |
|---|---|---|
| Drawing the Master Sword [[ra-guide-10-4,ra-guide-5-4,ra-comments-58-3]] | [10](#chapter-10-young-link-cleanup-and-the-ocarina-of-time) | After the Temple of Time cutscene the dying guard (Last Breath) is gone, and with three Spiritual Stones Zelda's courtyard (Troublemaker, bribe) is closed. Finish both, the Happy Mask quest and the Mask of Truth Deku upgrade first. |
| Poacher's Saw [[ra-comments-44-3,ra-comments-73-2]] | [13](#chapter-13-the-biggorons-sword-trading-quest) | Taking the Poacher's Saw makes the Lost Woods Deku upgrades unobtainable (A Horrible Face, Stocking Nuts). |
| Ganon's Tower [[ra-comments-121-3,gf1-345-1]] | [24](#chapter-24-ganons-castle-and-the-final-battle) | Beating Ganon ends the first file. Cartographer is checked on completion, so every Map, Compass and Boss Key must be in hand; finish every other first-file achievement before climbing the tower. |
| Starting the 3-heart file [[ra-comments-141-6,ra-comments-141-7]] | [25](#chapter-25-3-heart-run-child-dungeons) | The 3-heart achievements and Blind Explorer need a new save file; a single Map, Compass or heart upgrade picked up there cannot be undone by resetting. |

```mermaid
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Dying_Guard_Lockout,Final_Battle ponr;
  class Queen_Gohma,King_Dodongo,Barinade,Phantom_Ganon,Morpha,Bongo_Bongo,Twinrova,Ganondorf,Ganon,Dark_Link,Dead_Hand boss;
```

This chapter covers the whole opening of the game. You gather the Kokiri Sword and Deku Shield so Mido lets you pass [[gf1-21-3, gf1-24-1]], buy the first Deku Stick upgrade in the Lost Woods [[gf1-417-1]], clear Inside the Deku Tree, and defeat Queen Gohma for the Heart Container and the Kokiri Emerald [[gf1-32-5, gf1-32-6]]. Three achievements belong here: Bunches of Twigs, Spider Net (missable) and Entrusted Mission.

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Spider Net asks you to defeat Queen Gohma before she can retreat to the ceiling [[gf1-32-3, gf3-14-3, ra-comments-5-1, ra-comments-5-2]]. Prepare before you open the boss door: have the Slingshot and several Deku Sticks ready. The recommended plan is in the Queen Gohma section below [[ra-guide-4-2]].

### Preparation

- Mido will not let you pass unless both the Kokiri Sword and the Deku Shield are **equipped** in the Equipment subscreen, not just owned [[gf1-524-1]].
- The sword is free, the shield costs 40 rupees, and the Deku Stick upgrade costs another 40 rupees, so plan on collecting 80 rupees [[gf1-21-4, gf1-21-5, gf1-417-1]].
- Deku Sticks matter for both the dungeon puzzles and the Spider Net fight [[ra-guide-4-2, ra-comments-5-4]].
- Switching Z-targeting from Toggle to Hold (option menu on the file select screen) makes the Gohma combo easier [[ra-comments-5-3, ra-comments-7-1]].

### Chapter Items to Collect (11 Items Total)

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Kokiri Sword | Chest at the end of the boulder path in the Kokiri Training Ground | **TAKE** and equip | [[gf1-21-4]] |
| Deku Shield | Kokiri Shop, 40 rupees | **BUY** and equip | [[gf1-23-1, gf1-401-1]] |
| Deku Stick upgrade (20) | Business Scrub in the Lost Woods, 40 rupees | **BUY** | [[gf1-417-1]] |
| Dungeon Map | Chest on the upper ledge of the Trunk | **TAKE** | [[gf1-26-3]] |
| Fairy Slingshot + Bullet Bag (30) | Chest in the Slingshot Room | **TAKE** | [[gf1-27-4]] |
| Compass | Chest in the Compass Room | **TAKE** | [[gf1-28-2]] |
| Gold Skulltula #8 | Compass Room alcove | **KILL / TAKE** | [[gf1-28-4, checklist-skulltulas-4-1]] |
| Gold Skulltula #9 | First basement wet room, grate | **KILL / TAKE** | [[gf1-29-3, checklist-skulltulas-4-2]] |
| Gold Skulltula #10 | First basement wet room, ivy wall | **KILL / TAKE** | [[gf1-29-4, checklist-skulltulas-4-3]] |
| Heart Container | Queen Gohma | **TAKE** | [[gf1-32-5]] |
| Kokiri Emerald | Great Deku Tree after the boss | **RECEIVE** | [[gf1-32-6]] |

### Kokiri Forest

🗺️ **Map:** [View Kokiri Forest Map by kingofthisgame](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/1099-kokiri-forest-map)

#### Leaving the house and meeting Mido

1. Leave Link's tree house by the door in the northwest corner [[gf1-21-1]]. Saria approaches you on the porch [[gf1-21-2]].
2. Jump across the small pool of water to the large arch and speak with Mido. He refuses to let you pass until you have a sword and shield [[gf1-21-3]].

#### Getting the Kokiri Sword

1. Head to the crawl tunnel in the southwest corner of the forest, enter it by standing in front of it and pressing A, and crawl to the other side [[gf1-21-4, gf4-4-1]].
2. Go left and follow the path to the treasure chest while avoiding the rolling boulders [[gf4-4-1]]. Open the chest on the tree trunk for the **Kokiri Sword** and equip it from the Equipment submenu [[gf1-21-4, gf1-524-1]].

> [!TIP]
> Hiding in the small alcoves along the boulder path is the way to stay clear of the boulder [[gf1-21-6]].

#### Collecting rupees

You need 80 rupees in total: 40 for the shield and 40 for the Deku Stick upgrade [[gf1-21-5, gf1-417-1]]. Blue rupees are worth five rupees each [[gf2-6-5]].

- Along the boulder path, run left into the small alcove, take the blue rupee and slash the bush. Around the next corners take the blue rupee in the far alcove and slash its bush [[gf1-21-6]].
- Outside the tunnel, Z-target one of the rocks on the left and backflip (hold back and press A) repeatedly to the edge of the training ground, then repeat until you are awarded a blue rupee and a green rupee [[gf1-21-7]].
- Mido's House: open all his chests for 11 rupees and a Recovery Heart [[gf1-22-1]].
- Jump up to the ledge behind Mido's House for a blue rupee [[gf1-22-2]].
- Run across the platforms in the water for a blue rupee on reaching the opposite shore, then run back for another [[gf1-22-3]].
- The house in the southeast corner holds six rupees in pots, which respawn if you leave and re-enter [[gf1-22-4]].

#### Buying the Deku Shield

1. Enter the item shop in the northeast corner of the forest [[gf4-4-2]].
2. Speak to the clerk and buy the **Deku Shield** for 40 rupees. It is on the lower shelf on the right [[gf1-23-1, gf2-7-1, gf1-401-1]]. Equip it on the Equipment submenu [[gf1-524-1]].

> [!TIP]
> The Deku Shield is wooden and burns in fire, so avoid flames while holding it [[gf3-11-3]].

### Lost Woods

🗺️ **Map:** [View Lost Woods Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/54-lost-woods-map)

#### Bunches of Twigs

1. Head back to Mido's House in the northwest, climb onto the ledge behind it, climb the vine-covered wall, work around the ledge and enter the large hollow log into the Lost Woods [[gf1-23-2]].
2. From the first Lost Woods area, take the log on the left, then the hollow log in the left corner of the next area (two lefts). Ignore the two elevated logs. Jump off the ledge into the valley below and go to the Business Scrub at the back, next to the Gossip Stone [[checklist-sidequests-7-7, gf1-417-1]].
3. Defeat him: reflect his Deku Nut back with the Deku Shield, or use a jump attack [[gf1-23-3, gf2-20-3]].
4. Speak to him and pay 40 rupees. This raises your Deku Stick capacity to 20 and fills your sticks [[gf1-23-4, gf1-417-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Bunches of Twigs (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158156.png)
> *Purchase more Deku Sticks from the Deku Merchant in Lost Woods*


5. Climb the ladder back to the top, go through the hollowed log, take another hollowed log to the right, then either log to return to Kokiri Forest [[gf1-23-5]].

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave the Lost Woods, note that the Skull Kid Heart Pieces and the Bullet Bag target are here too, but they need the Ocarina and songs and are not required for the Deku Tree [[checklist-heartpieces-3-1, gf1-46-1]].

### Passing Mido and entering the tree

1. Go to the east side of the forest, back across the pond, and speak with Mido again. With sword and shield equipped, he lets you pass [[gf1-24-1, gf1-524-1]].
2. Halfway along the passage, Deku Babas appear. They die in one sword hit and leave Deku Sticks [[gf1-24-2]].
3. Enter the mouth of the Great Deku Tree to begin the dungeon [[gf1-24-3]].

### Inside the Deku Tree

The dungeon centers on the Trunk, with four levels: Top Floor, Main Floor, Basement and Sub-Basement [[gf1-26-1]].

#### 1F: the Trunk (Dungeon Map)

1. Kill the Deku Babas in the corners. Hold R while Z-targeting to block their attacks, and take the Deku Sticks and Nuts they drop [[gf1-26-2]].
2. Reach the upper ledge by the ladder or the vines [[gf3-12-1]].
3. Open the chest partway along the ledge for the **Dungeon Map** [[gf1-26-3]].
4. Keep going right and enter the door [[gf1-26-4]].

#### Scrub Room #1

1. Z-target the Deku Scrub and reflect his Deku Nut back at him. When he runs, stand on his nest so he bumps into you [[gf1-27-1]].
2. He opens both doors and hints to press forward on the joystick when landing to avoid fall damage [[gf1-27-2]].
3. Go through the northeast door [[gf1-27-3]].

#### Slingshot Room

1. Jump onto the gray platform and quickly to the next ledge, since it falls. Open the chest for the **Fairy Slingshot** and Bullet Bag x30 [[gf1-27-4]].
2. Climb the ivy wall on the right and open the chest for a Recovery Heart [[gf1-27-5]].
3. Shoot the ladder hanging above the entrance door with the Slingshot, climb it, and go back through the door to Scrub Room #1 [[gf1-27-6]].

#### The Trunk: climbing to the Compass Room

1. Go back along the ledge. Shoot the three Skullwalltullas on the ivy wall with the Slingshot (one seed each) [[gf1-28-1, gf3-12-5]].
2. Climb the wall to the top, then left to the highest level, and keep going left to a door [[gf1-28-1]].

#### Compass Room (Gold Skulltula #8)

1. Step on the switch next to the lantern; three platforms rise. Jump along them to the back ledge and open the chest for the **Compass** [[gf1-28-2]].
2. Press the switch again and use the platforms to reach the small northern alcove. Jump past the Big Skulltula and open the chest for a Recovery Heart [[gf1-28-3]]. Big Skulltulas die when struck in the stomach [[gf3-12-6]].
3. Kill the Gold Skulltula in the back of the alcove for token **#8** [[gf1-28-4, checklist-skulltulas-4-1]].
4. Light a Deku Stick on the lantern and use it to light the burned-out lantern. The door unlocks [[gf1-28-5]]. Press A to put the stick away and save it once done [[gf3-13-2]].

#### The Trunk: breaking the web

1. Run right past the first Big Skulltula. Beat the second by Z-targeting it, waiting for it to spin, then using a jump attack [[gf1-29-1]].
2. Jump off the edge and land directly on the webbing on the first floor to break through to the sub-basement [[gf1-29-2]].

#### Basement wet room (Gold Skulltulas #9 and #10)

1. Shoot the Gold Skulltula on the eastern grating with the Slingshot, jump out to take the token, and climb back. This is token **#9** [[gf1-29-3, checklist-skulltulas-4-2]].
2. Face the long ivy wall on the south side, kill the second Gold Skulltula, and climb up to collect token **#10** [[gf1-29-4, checklist-skulltulas-4-3]].
3. Climb back to the southeast ledge and open the chest behind the lantern for a Recovery Heart [[gf1-29-5]].
4. Light a Deku Stick on the lantern, jump onto the shallow part of the pond, then to the southwest ledge. Run past the Deku Baba and burn the webs on the wall to reveal a door [[gf1-29-6]].

> [!NOTE]
> A fourth Gold Skulltula (#11) sits behind a bombable wall and needs Bombs and the Boomerang, so it is left for later [[checklist-skulltulas-4-4, gf1-484-2]].

#### Scrub Room #2

1. Deflect the Scrub's nuts back with the Deku Shield (the Slingshot does not work). Stand on his nest when he runs. He says "23 is number 1", a hint for the last puzzle [[gf1-30-1]].
2. Shoot the Silver Eye above the west door with the Slingshot to unlock it [[gf1-30-2]].

#### Trap Room

1. Swim to the left wall directly beneath the rotating spikes and hold A to dive onto the button. The water lowers [[gf1-30-3]].
2. Swim back, jump onto the floating platform and hold R to crouch under the spikes, then hop to the far ledge [[gf1-30-4]].
3. Kill the Big Skulltula, push the large stone block all the way right, and climb it to the door [[gf1-30-5]].

#### Torch Room and Small Nest

1. Kill the Deku Baba in the center, light a Deku Stick on the lit torch, and light the two unlit sconces. The north door opens [[gf1-31-1]].
2. Run circles in the center so the Gohma Larvae drop. Jump-attack each once, avoiding their charge when their eye turns red [[gf1-31-2]].
3. Light a stick at the north torches, burn the webbing in the south, and crawl through the tunnel back to the Trunk [[gf1-31-3]].

#### The Trunk: down to the boss

1. Run left and push the stone block along its path off the cliff. Jump down, relight a Deku Stick at the southeast torch, and climb back up via the block [[gf1-32-1]].
2. Roll over the floor webbing with the lit stick (run at it and press A). It burns, opening a hole to the lowest level [[gf1-32-2]].
3. Hit the three Deku Scrubs middle first, far right second, left last [[gf1-32-7, gf2-11-1]].
4. The door to the boss unlocks once all three vanish [[gf1-32-7]].

### Queen Gohma

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Spider Net is missable. The boss cannot be refought, so you must defeat Queen Gohma before she retreats to the ceiling [[gf1-32-3, gf3-14-3, ra-comments-5-1]]. Practice the combo on regular enemies earlier in the dungeon [[ra-comments-7-4]]. Carry spare Deku Sticks (commenters used between 3 and 5) [[ra-comments-5-4, ra-comments-7-7, ra-comments-8-2]].

**Setup.** One commenter suggests simply entering and looking up so she notices you [[ra-comments-7-5]]. Stored crouch-stab damage resets when you enter a different room, so a jump attack beforehand will not carry into the boss [[ra-comments-8-12]].

**One-rotation plan** (for **Spider Net**):

1. Z-target Gohma and shoot her in the eye with the Slingshot while her eye glows red. This stuns her [[gf1-32-3, gf2-118-3]]. The Slingshot stun lasts longer than a Deku Nut stun, so do not re-stun with nuts [[ra-comments-5-2, ra-comments-6-8]].
2. Switch to a Deku Stick and do a jump slash while still targeted. A Deku Stick jump attack deals 4 damage [[ra-comments-6-3, ra-comments-7-2]].
3. Release the target, hold R without locking on and press the attack button repeatedly to crouch-stab. The stab copies the previous attack's damage [[ra-comments-7-3, ra-comments-6-2]].
4. Alternatives reported by commenters: Slingshot then five Deku Sticks attacked quickly with no jump slash [[ra-comments-8-6, ra-comments-8-8]]; or Slingshot, stick jump slash, then mash the stick button three times [[ra-guide-4-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Spider Net (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158117.png)
> *Defeat Queen Gohma before she can retreat to the ceiling*


**If she escapes.** If Gohma reaches the ceiling and lays eggs, break each egg with one hit before it hatches [[gf2-11-3, gf3-14-2]]. Shooting her red eye on the ceiling drops her to the floor, stunned for a long time [[gf1-32-4, gf3-14-4]]. Spider Net asks for the kill before she retreats, so this fallback only finishes the boss [[gf1-32-3, ra-guide-4-2]].

### After the boss

1. Take the **Heart Container** and step into the blue light [[gf1-32-5, gf3-15-1]].
2. Outside, listen to the Great Deku Tree's story. He gives you the Kokiri Emerald and asks you to seek the Princess of Destiny at Hyrule Castle [[gf1-32-6]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Entrusted Mission (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158118.png)
> *Receive the Spiritual Stone of the Forest*


3. Back in the forest, Mido blames you for the Deku Tree's death and leaves [[gf1-34-1]]. Cross the lake and head to the western edge; exit through the hollow log [[gf1-34-2]].
4. On the bridge to Hyrule Field, Saria gives you the Fairy Ocarina [[gf1-34-3]].

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave the forest: Gold Skulltulas #8, #9 and #10 are in your pocket [[checklist-skulltulas-4-1, checklist-skulltulas-4-2, checklist-skulltulas-4-3]]. Others in Kokiri Forest need night or a bottle, which come later [[checklist-skulltulas-3-1, checklist-skulltulas-3-2]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 1 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 3 / 100 (#8, #9, #10) [[checklist-skulltulas-4-1, checklist-skulltulas-4-2, checklist-skulltulas-4-3]]
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 0 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 1 / 8 [[gf1-32-5]]
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 2 / 26 (Dungeon Map, Compass) [[gf1-26-3, gf1-28-2]]
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 3 / 97 (20 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Kokiri Sword obtained and equipped
- [ ] Deku Shield bought and equipped
- [ ] Deku Stick upgrade bought (Bunches of Twigs)
- [ ] Dungeon Map and Compass
- [ ] Fairy Slingshot
- [ ] Gold Skulltulas #8, #9, #10
- [ ] Queen Gohma defeated in one rotation (Spider Net)
- [ ] Heart Container
- [ ] Kokiri Emerald (Entrusted Mission)
- [ ] Fairy Ocarina from Saria

## Chapter 2: Hyrule Castle Town and Princess Zelda

### Overview and preparation

This chapter takes you from the Deku Tree's final wish to your first meeting with Zelda: Hyrule Field, the Market, then the castle grounds and courtyard. The Great Deku Tree sent Link to Hyrule Castle to find the Princess of Destiny [[gf1-32-6]]. Six achievements are earned here: Sniper Fury, Rise and Shine!!, Money Solves Everything, Troublemaker, A Royal Autograph and Who Let the Dogs Out. Two of them are missable and both have deadlines before you speak to Zelda.

The order that keeps every achievement available:

1. Play the Shooting Gallery in the Market as Young Link (Sniper Fury).
2. Talk to Malon in the Market, then get the Weird Egg from her at the castle grounds (Rise and Shine!!).
3. Talk to the gate guard, get caught in the courtyard, leave, come back and pay the bribe (Money Solves Everything).
4. Shoot the left courtyard window with the Slingshot, then talk to Zelda (Troublemaker, then A Royal Autograph).
5. Learn Zelda's Lullaby from Impa, then return to the Market at night for the white dog (Who Let the Dogs Out).

Keep your Slingshot and Deku Seeds ready for the Shooting Gallery and the courtyard windows, and carry at least 20 Rupees for the gallery. The Shooting Gallery costs 20 Rupees per game [[gf2-13-3]]. The 10-Rupee bribe also needs cash [[gf2-32-3]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Money Solves Everything (bribe the castle guard) must be done before you meet Zelda. A player reports it cannot be completed after Zelda gets taken [[gf2-32-3, ra-comments-56-1, ra-comments-56-2]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Troublemaker (shoot the left courtyard window) must be done before Zelda leaves the courtyard, which happens after the third Spiritual Stone [[gf1-173-4, ra-guide-5-4, ra-comments-58-1, ra-comments-58-2]].

> [!TIP]
> **Shield warning:** Do NOT buy the Hylian Shield at the Bazaar (80 Rupees). You get the same shield soon enough for free, and a later chapter's achievement depends on it [[gf2-13-1, gf2-140-15, gf4-6-3]].

### Hyrule Field

🗺️ **Map:** [View Hyrule Field Map by thegamemaster8](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/5394-hyrule-field-map)

When you step into Hyrule Field, Kaepora Gaebora stops you to explain how to reach Hyrule Castle and how the map works [[gf1-34-4]]. Answer "Yes" if you do not want him to repeat the speech.

Once he flies off, run between the trees where a road begins and follow it to its end at Hyrule Castle. Roll all the way by pressing A continually as you run. Otherwise night will fall before you arrive and the gates will be closed [[gf1-34-5]].

> [!TIP]
> Do not stray far from the road on this first trip. Everything you need in this chapter is in the Market and the castle grounds.

### Hyrule Market

🗺️ **Map:** [View Hyrule Palace Map by ThE_bLImP](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/52-hyrule-palace-map)

#### The Gatehouse (guardhouse by the drawbridge)

Enter the gatehouse to your left as you come in [[gf1-35-1]]. Smash all the pots with your sword. They are an easy source of Rupees, and you can leave and re-enter to refill them as often as you like [[gf2-13-2, gf4-3-2]]. Collect about 100 Rupees so you can afford repeat attempts at the Shooting Gallery [[gf4-3-2]].

Roll into the crates in the rear of the room, next to the guard. One holds a Gold Skulltula, which is Gold Skulltula #18 [[gf1-35-1, checklist-skulltulas-5-7, gf1-458-1]]. Kill it and take the token [[gf1-35-1]].

#### The Market square

Go southwest from the drawbridge to the Market. Walk around to the opposite side of the fountain and speak with the little girl in the white dress. This is Malon [[gf1-35-2]]. Talk to her now. If you skip this, she will not appear at the castle later, and you would have to wait through another day/night cycle [[gf1-38-2]].

The Bazaar is the building with the scary face above it, and the Potion Shop is next door. You need nothing from either now [[gf2-13-1]].

#### Shooting Gallery

Enter the building behind Malon with the large target over the door [[gf1-35-2]]. Pay 20 Rupees for fifteen shots to hit ten Rupees. Hitting eight of ten earns a free game. Hitting all ten earns a Bullet Bag that raises your Slingshot capacity from 30 to 40 [[gf2-13-3, gf2-102-2]]. You must hit every target without a miss [[gf1-36-1]]. If you miss two or fewer, the clerk lets you try again free. If you miss more than two, you pay again [[gf1-510-1]].

The targets appear in the same order each time [[gf2-13-2]]:

| Target | Behavior | Citation |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Green Rupee, stationary in the middle | [[gf2-13-2]] |
| 2 | Blue Rupee from the left, stopping a bit left of center | [[gf2-13-2]] |
| 3 | Blue Rupee from the right, stopping a bit right of center | [[gf2-13-2]] |
| 4 | Green Rupee that pops up from the middle; hit it at the top of its jump | [[gf2-13-2]] |
| 5 and 6 | Blue Rupees from left and right at the same time; hit both before they leave | [[gf2-13-2]] |
| 7 and 8 | Red Rupees moving right to left at moderate speed | [[gf2-13-2]] |
| 9 and 10 | Red Rupees moving left to right, quickly | [[gf2-13-2]] |

Tips from other players:

- Lower your control sensitivity to about 0.6 or 0.7 for this challenge [[ra-comments-54-1]].
- Keep the Slingshot aimed at the middle except for the two side shots [[ra-comments-54-2]].
- On the round where the blue Rupees move toward the right, fire before you think you need to [[ra-comments-54-3]].

If you run out of Rupees, go back to the gatehouse and smash pots to refill [[gf1-36-4]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Sniper Fury (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158148.png)
> *Obtain a reward from the Shooting Gallery as Young Link*


Keep playing until the clerk gives you the Bullet Bag [[gf1-36-3, gf1-405-2]].

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave the Market: the Bombchu Bowling Alley and Treasure Chest Game here are not open to you yet. Do not spend money on them now [[gf1-410-1, gf1-428-1]]. A Bazaar purchase of the Hylian Shield is still off the list [[gf2-13-1]].

### Hyrule Castle

🗺️ **Map:** [View Path to Zelda Map by TLS64](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/55-path-to-zelda-map)

#### Castle grounds: Gold Skulltula #19 and Malon's egg

Leave the Market through the passage directly behind Malon [[gf1-37-1]]. Kaepora Gaebora appears again and rants about guards. When he flies away, roll into his tree to make a Gold Skulltula appear [[gf1-37-1, gf2-15-1]]. Kill it and take the token. This is Gold Skulltula #19 [[checklist-skulltulas-5-8, gf1-458-2]].

Malon now stands beside the long vine on the wall to your right [[gf1-38-2]]. She explains her father Talon went to the castle with milk and has not come back, and she asks you to find him [[gf1-38-2]]. Speak to her, twice if need be, and she gives you the Weird Egg [[gf2-15-2, gf1-38-2]]. A source says she hands it over at night [[gf3-165-1]]. If she is not there, you skipped talking to her in the Market, so return there first [[gf1-38-2]].

Equip the egg to a C button. Stand at the top of the vines and let the game pass through the night. In the morning, when you hear the rooster, the egg hatches into a Chicken [[gf2-15-3, gf2-156-5]].

#### Sneaking to Talon

Climb the ivy wall to the top. It has rough spots, so if you start in the wrong place you will fall off [[gf1-38-3]]. At the top, run forward past the gossip stone to the top of the large marble arch. Drop down on the far side, which puts you behind the gate that was blocking you and past the first two guards [[gf1-38-3, gf1-173-1]].

The next stretch is the hardest. Run up the hill on the left at an exact spot: it is the third graphical break in the hill, near its center. Go straight up toward the cluster of flowers at the top, then run forward and slightly left until you reach a wall with tiled bricks, and climb the bricks [[gf1-38-4, gf1-173-2]].

At the top, run forward past another gossip stone and jump off the cliff into the moat. Swim to the end of the moat where it breaks left, climb onto the small patch of dirt, then up to the stone platform. Run left from the platform to find Talon asleep [[gf1-38-5]].

If you are spotted by a guard, you are thrown back outside the gate [[gf1-90-3]]. A player's note says you have to sneak through the castle to reach the sleeping Talon, so be patient [[ra-comments-11-2]].

#### Wake Talon

Talking to Talon does not wake him. Stand next to him, where Navi flutters near him, and equip the Chicken. Press its C button [[gf2-15-4, gf1-38-6]]. If the egg has not hatched, wait until dawn [[gf1-38-6]]. Speak with Talon. He remembers he left Malon behind and runs off, clearing the path [[gf1-38-6, gf4-6-6]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Rise and Shine!! (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158119.png)
> *Help Malon locate her father*


#### The bribe: Money Solves Everything

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Do this before you meet Zelda, and do not complete any other story step in between. Reports say you only need the egg from Malon, not necessarily a hatched one [[ra-comments-57-3, ra-comments-56-1]]. You must get caught once, leave, and come back [[ra-comments-56-3, ra-guide-5-2]]. One player did all of this after going to Kakariko Village and back, as long as no other main quest was done [[ra-comments-56-6, ra-comments-57-1]].

What exactly triggers the bribe dialogue is unconfirmed by any collected source. Comments mention having Malon's egg, being caught once and returning. Do all three before meeting Zelda and do no other story step in between.

The sequence that players report:

1. Have Malon's egg and talk to the gate guard on the left upon entering the courtyard area [[ra-guide-5-2, ra-comments-56-9]]. One player talked to him twice at the gate [[ra-comments-56-1, ra-comments-56-9]].
2. While you are still on the castle grounds (climb back over the gate if you left), deliberately get spotted by a guard on the castle grounds (the hill below the moat, not Zelda's courtyard, which you have not entered yet). The guard throws you outside the gate [[gf1-90-3, gf1-173-4]].
3. Talk to the guard you see immediately after being thrown out. He offers to open the gate for 10 Rupees [[ra-comments-57-2]].
4. Pay the 10 Rupees. He lets you through [[gf2-32-3]].

Players report that having been caught once and then returning is what makes the offer appear [[ra-comments-56-7, ra-comments-56-8, ra-guide-5-2]]. If you are caught and the bribe option does not show up, leave the area and come back [[ra-comments-56-3, ra-guide-5-2]]. One player reports the achievement did not pop after waking Malon's father, which supports doing the full sequence above rather than just waking Talon [[ra-comments-56-4]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Money Solves Everything (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158150.png)
> *Bribe the guard in front of the gate to gain entry to the courtyard*


#### Into the courtyard

Daytime is required to enter the next area, because the guard is increased at night [[gf1-38-7]]. Push both milk crates through the gap Talon left, climb onto them, and jump to the drainage pipe that feeds the moat. If it is night, loiter until day, then climb through the pipe [[gf1-38-7]]. Another source describes the same step as pushing the two bull-head blocks off the ledge so the second stacks on the first, then climbing up and jumping to the crawlspace beside the waterfall, again only in daytime [[gf2-15-5, gf3-17-6]].

Inside the courtyard, the guards patrol the hedged areas. If you are spotted you are thrown back out [[gf1-90-3]]. Work your way to the northern edge of the topiary and run through the arch to reach the center of the courtyard, where Zelda waits [[gf1-39-3]]. Exact patrol timings: Unable to verify from collected sources.

#### Troublemaker: shoot the left window

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Do this before speaking to Zelda. You cannot come back to this area after collecting all three Spiritual Stones [[ra-guide-5-4, ra-comments-58-3]].

Before you talk to Zelda, equip the Slingshot. Shoot the window on the LEFT side of the courtyard. A guard throws a bomb at you, so be ready to move [[gf1-39-1, ra-comments-58-6]]. One shot should hit the window [[ra-comments-58-2]]. Shooting the left window is the reported trigger, and the goal is not simply to be caught [[ra-comments-58-7, ra-comments-58-8]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Troublemaker (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158151.png)
> *Anger a guard inside of the castle*


If you miss, you can try again as long as Zelda is still in the courtyard, which also means you do not need to shoot on the first visit [[ra-comments-58-5]]. One player reports a second shot is possible after the volcano dungeon [[ra-comments-58-4]].

Now shoot the window on the RIGHT, which shows Mario characters, for a Red Rupee [[gf1-39-2, gf2-16-1]]. A Deku Seed into this window gives a Red Rupee worth 20 Rupees [[gf3-17-3]]. Do not confuse the two windows: the right one only pays a Rupee and does not count for the achievement [[gf1-39-1, gf1-39-2]].

#### Meet Zelda

Speak with Zelda. She asks if you have the Spiritual Stone of the Forest, and it is fine to say yes. Promise not to tell, listen to her dream, and say you understand when asked. Agree to look through the window when Zelda offers, then agree to help her [[gf1-39-3]]. Pick the top option when given one [[gf3-17-4]]. She gives you Zelda's Letter with her signature [[gf1-39-4, gf3-17-4, gf2-161-1]]. The letter opens the gate to Death Mountain in Kakariko Village later [[gf1-39-4]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **A Royal Autograph (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158120.png)
> *Meet Princess Zelda for the first time*


#### Impa and Zelda's Lullaby

Run toward the courtyard exit. Impa blocks it and teaches you Zelda's Lullaby, then leads you out to Hyrule Field and points you toward Kakariko Village [[gf1-40-1, gf4-6-8]]. The song is C-Left, C-Up, C-Right, C-Left, C-Up, C-Right [[gf1-392-1]]. You are back in Hyrule Field with the letter and the song [[gf2-133-7]].

### Back to the Market at night: Who Let the Dogs Out

Return to the Market and wait for night. If you lack the Sun's Song, wait around the castle garden until nightfall and then re-enter the Market [[gf1-427-1]]. One comment says to play the Sun's Song to make the dog follow you [[ra-comments-55-1]]. You do not have that song yet, so use the waiting method [[gf1-427-1]].

At night the Market is mostly empty and many dogs run around. Find the small white dog behind the stall to the left of the Market entrance from the drawbridge [[gf1-37-2, ra-comments-55-2, ra-guide-8-5]]. Walk up to it and press A so it follows you [[ra-comments-55-2, gf2-14-1]].

Lead it to the back alley in the corner and into the door on the left. Sources disagree on which one: the second door on the left [[gf1-38-1, gf1-427-1]], or the first door [[gf2-14-1, ra-guide-8-5]]. The correct house holds a woman who recognizes the dog as hers. Talk to her [[ra-comments-55-3, ra-comments-55-4]]. She gives a Piece of Heart [[checklist-sidequests-2-1, gf1-38-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Who Let the Dogs Out (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158149.png)
> *Help a lady locate her lost puppy*


> [!TIP]
> Before you leave Hyrule Castle for Kakariko Village, confirm the following: the two Gold Skulltulas (#18 and #19), the Bullet Bag, Zelda's Letter and Zelda's Lullaby. The Bombchu Bowling Alley and Din's Fire need Bombs, so they come later [[gf2-32-1, gf2-32-4]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 2 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 5 / 100 (#18 Market guardhouse, #19 tree outside the castle gate) [[checklist-skulltulas-5-7, checklist-skulltulas-5-8]]
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 1 / 36 (lost dog) [[checklist-sidequests-2-1]]
> - **Heart Containers:** 1 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 2 / 26
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 9 / 97 (50 / 690 points)

### Chapter checklist

- [ ] Gold Skulltula #18 (Market guardhouse crate)
- [ ] Sniper Fury: Bullet Bag from the Shooting Gallery
- [ ] Talked to Malon in the Market, then got the Weird Egg at the castle
- [ ] Gold Skulltula #19 (tree outside the castle gate)
- [ ] Rise and Shine!!: woke Talon with the Chicken
- [ ] Money Solves Everything: bribed the gate guard before meeting Zelda
- [ ] Troublemaker: shot the left courtyard window before talking to Zelda
- [ ] A Royal Autograph: met Zelda, got Zelda's Letter
- [ ] Learned Zelda's Lullaby from Impa
- [ ] Who Let the Dogs Out: returned the white dog, got the Piece of Heart
- [ ] Did not buy the Hylian Shield

---

## Chapter 3: Kakariko Village, the Graveyard and Lon Lon Ranch

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Lon_Lon_Ranch["Lon Lon Ranch"]
  Kakariko_Village["Kakariko Village"]
  Kakariko_Graveyard["Kakariko Graveyard"]
  House_of_Skulltula["House of Skulltula"]
  King_Dodongo{{"⚔ King Dodongo"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Dead_Hand{{"⚔ Dead Hand"}}
  Kakariko_Village --> Kakariko_Graveyard
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class King_Dodongo,Bongo_Bongo,Dead_Hand boss;
```

This chapter clears out the first batch of side content you can reach as Young Link. Kakariko Village is in the northeast corner of Hyrule, at the foot of Death Mountain, and Lon Lon Ranch sits in the middle of Hyrule Field [[gf1-8-7, gf1-8-10]]. You will earn six achievements here: Catch Me If You Can, Cuccos' Revenge, Shield Raider, Super Cucco Catching, You Make Me Feel So Good and A Man's Best Friend. You will also pick up two Bottles, the Sun's Song, Epona's Song, the free Hylian Shield, three Pieces of Heart, the Adult's Wallet and eight Gold Skulltula tokens. There is no boss in this chapter.

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning (Shield Raider):** Do NOT buy a Hylian Shield at the Bazaar before you pull the flowered gravestone in the Graveyard. Buying it replaces the shield chest's contents with rupees, and the achievement can be permanently missed [[ra-comments-64-1, checklist-sidequests-9-3, gf1-401-2]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Warning (Dampe's Piece of Heart):** If Dampe digs up the Piece of Heart and you leave the Graveyard without picking it up, it is lost forever and you get 50 rupees instead [[ra-guide-20-1, ra-comments-101-1]]. Pick it up before you leave.

### Chapter Items to Collect (Rewards and Pickups)

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Empty Bottle | Kakariko Village, Cucco Lady | **GIVE** her all seven Cuccos in her pen | [[gf2-22-4, gf4-7-8]] |
| Hylian Shield | Graveyard, flowered gravestone (night) | **PULL** the gravestone, **OPEN** the chest | [[gf1-54-3, ra-guide-6-1]] |
| Sun's Song | Royal Family's Tomb | Zelda's Lullaby on the Triforce mark | [[gf1-53-2, gf1-52-2]] |
| Piece of Heart (Redead grave) | Graveyard, rear row | Defeat the Redead, play the Sun's Song | [[gf1-55-3]] |
| Piece of Heart (Dampe's dig) | Graveyard, random dirt patch | Pay Dampe 10 rupees per dig at nightfall | [[gf1-56-2, gf1-432-2]] |
| Empty Bottle (with Lon Lon Milk) | Lon Lon Ranch, Talon's house | Find the three Super Cuccos in 30 seconds | [[gf1-42-2, gf2-103-1]] |
| Epona's Song | Lon Lon Ranch pasture, Malon | Talk to her, then play the Ocarina | [[gf1-43-1]] |
| Piece of Heart (crawlspace) | Lon Lon Ranch, small building at the back | Rearrange the crates | [[gf1-44-1, gf1-429-1]] |
| Lon Lon Milk | Lon Lon Ranch cow | Play Epona's Song with an empty Bottle | [[ra-guide-6-8, gf1-43-2]] |
| Adult's Wallet | House of Skulltula | Hand in 10 tokens | [[gf1-58-1, gf1-419-2]] |

### Kakariko Village

Walk to the village in the northeast corner of Hyrule Field [[gf1-8-7]]. Make sure it is daytime before you start the Cucco hunt below [[gf2-156-16]].

#### The Cucco Hunt (Catch Me If You Can)

The Cucco Lady (Anju) wants her seven lost Cuccos back [[ra-guide-6-2]]. Pick each Cucco up and put it into her fenced pen, which is in the south of town near the arch leading to the Graveyard [[gf1-50-2, gf1-50-4, gf4-7-8]]. The seven are:

1. **Cucco 1:** Run through the arch at the front of town. A Cucco is running around right there [[gf1-50-3]].
2. **Cucco 2:** Another Cucco is very near the Cucco Lady's pasture, close to the arch to the Graveyard in the southeast corner [[gf1-50-4]].
3. **Cucco 3:** This one is inside a crate near the center of town, across from the stairs to the upper level [[gf1-50-5]].
4. **Cucco 4:** This one is on the north side of town, on the upper level, near the gate to Death Mountain [[gf1-50-6]].
5. **Cucco 5:** This Cucco is trapped on a fenced ledge near the large tree. Climb the stairs of the partially built house and jump off the edge toward it while holding another Cucco. The held Cucco floats you out to the ledge [[gf1-50-7]].
6. **Cucco 6:** Climb the long stairset east of the pasture, run to the edge and aim at the fenced-off elevated platform. A Cucco is running around in the small grotto behind the building [[gf1-50-8]].
7. **Cucco 7:** Drop back into the grotto and climb the tall ladder all the way up. Work around behind the Windmill and grab the last Cucco [[gf1-50-9]].

Three of the Cuccos can only be reached by gliding off cliffs and over fences with other Cuccos [[gf1-383-5]]. Once all seven are in the pen, talk to the Cucco Lady. She rewards you with an Empty Bottle [[gf2-22-4, gf3-20-3, gf3-117-5]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Catch Me If You Can (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158158.png)
> *Obtain a reward from Anju for catching her cuccos in Kakariko Village*


> [!TIP]
> Keep an empty Bottle on hand. The Graveyard bugs and the ranch cow's milk both need one [[gf1-52-1, ra-guide-6-8]]. You get another Bottle from Talon at Lon Lon Ranch [[gf1-383-4]].

#### Cuccos' Revenge

While you are among the Cuccos, hit one with your sword over and over until the whole flock turns on you [[ra-guide-6-3, ra-comments-66-1]]. This works only as Young Link, so do it now [[ra-comments-66-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Cuccos' Revenge (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158159.png)
> *Feel the wrath of the cuccos in Kakariko Village*


#### The Death Mountain Gate and the Keaton Mask

Go to the northern edge of the village and talk to the guard by the gate. He refuses to let you through until you show him Zelda's Letter, which opens the way to Death Mountain Trail [[gf1-58-2, gf2-23-3]]. The guard asks you to bring him a Keaton Mask from the Hyrule Castle Market, which starts the Happy Mask trading sequence and unlocks the Happy Mask side quest [[gf1-58-3, ra-guide-6-9]].

You can do the mask trade whenever it suits you. Borrow the Keaton Mask for 10 rupees, wear it and talk to the guard, who pays 15 rupees. Then pay the shop owner the 10 rupees [[gf1-59-3, gf1-350-1, gf3-28-6]]. Do not go up Death Mountain Trail yet. The rest of this chapter stays in the village area.

#### Impa's House Piece of Heart (Optional Now)

Piece of Heart: glide with a Cucco from the narrow wooden beam of the unfinished house. When the beam bends to the right, leap off and steer right for the balcony of Impa's House. Throw the Cucco aside and enter the crack in the building [[gf1-51-1]].

If you skip it, you can come back later. Kaepora Gaebora's ride can drop you on Impa's roof, and as an adult the Hookshot reaches it [[gf1-430-1, gf1-88-2]].

### Kakariko Graveyard

Leave through the arch in the southeast corner of town [[gf1-50-4]].

#### Gold Skulltula #29: The Bug Patch

Catch a bug from under a rock with a Bottle [[gf4-8-1]]. Pass under the arch and follow the stone path to the left side of the graveyard, where there is a patch of soft earth. Stand near the patch and release the bug [[gf1-52-1]]. Quickly recapture one bug in the Bottle [[checklist-sidequests-9-10]]. After a few seconds, a Gold Skulltula appears. Kill it and take the token (**Graveyard #29**) [[gf1-52-1, checklist-skulltulas-6-8, gf1-464-1]].

#### The Royal Family's Tomb and the Sun's Song

Head to the largest tombstone. Stand on the large Triforce symbol in front of it, take out your Ocarina and play Zelda's Lullaby. The tombstone explodes and reveals a pit. Drop down [[gf1-52-2, checklist-sidequests-9-2]]. Zelda's Lullaby is Left, Up, Right, Left, Up, Right [[gf2-21-5]].

Run down the ramp and circle the large bone pit to draw all the Keese. Shoot them with the Slingshot or cut them down, and the door at the top of the stairs unlocks [[gf1-53-1]]. Go through the door, pass the Redeads, go through the next opening and read the inscription on the wall [[gf3-234-4]]. Reading it teaches the Sun's Song [[gf1-53-2]].

The Sun's Song is Right, Down, Up, Right, Down, Up. It flips the time between noon and midnight [[gf1-393-3]].

> [!TIP]
> You can only reach the next two sections at night. If it is daytime, play the Sun's Song now. Midnight is fine for the shield and the Redead grave, but not for Dampe's tour [[gf1-393-3, checklist-sidequests-9-3, gf1-56-3]].

#### The Free Hylian Shield (Shield Raider)

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Reminder: do not buy a Hylian Shield from the Bazaar before this step [[ra-comments-64-1]].

At night, go to the front row of gravestones and find the grave with flowers in front of it. Stand behind it, grab it and pull back to reveal a pit. Drop down [[gf1-54-1, ra-guide-6-1, gf3-21-3]]. Do not pull other gravestones in this row, because they hold Poes that turn invisible when Z-targeted [[gf1-54-2]].

Inside, run forward and open the chest to find the **Hylian Shield**, whose metal is impervious to flame attacks [[gf1-54-3, gf2-22-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Shield Raider (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158157.png)
> *Obtain the Hylian Shield for free instead of buying it*


If you already bought the shield, players report the achievement can still unlock if a Like-Like steals the shield and you recover it. This works only as child Link and was reported to fail in the Fire Temple but succeed in the Bottom of the Well [[ra-comments-64-2, ra-comments-64-3, ra-comments-64-5, ra-comments-64-6]]. One player could not make it work and used a new save file [[ra-comments-64-4]]. Treat that route as unconfirmed.

Later, with bombs, the wall inside this chamber opens to a Fairy Fountain [[gf3-27-4]].

#### The Redead Grave (Piece of Heart)

Still at night, go to the rear row of gravestones and the far right. Counting from the right, pull aside the fourth gravestone to reveal another pit. Drop in [[gf1-55-1]]. Another guide describes this grave as the second gravestone on the left when facing away from the Royal Family's Tomb [[gf3-21-4]]. The sources word the location differently, so if you uncover a Poe instead of a pit, step back and try the next candidate [[gf1-54-2]]. You can only pull graves at night [[gf3-21-4]].

Inside, attack the Redead repeatedly. The faster you hit it, the less it can attack. If it grabs you, tap buttons and rotate the stick to break free [[gf1-55-2]]. Once it is permanently defeated, stand on the steps and play the Sun's Song. A chest appears with a **Piece of Heart** [[gf1-55-3, gf2-22-1, checklist-heartpieces-3-7]]. Open it and return to the Graveyard [[gf1-55-3]].

#### Dampe's Gravedigging Tour (Piece of Heart)

Dampe's game runs only in the first part of the night, not at midnight, and the Sun's Song will not start it. Leave to Hyrule Field and wait for nightfall (the wolf's howl), then return [[gf1-56-3, gf1-432-3, checklist-sidequests-14-5]]. Bring about 100 rupees [[checklist-heartpieces-3-6]]. Your starting wallet holds only 99, so that is the most you can carry before the Adult's Wallet [[gf1-450-2]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Warning:** When Dampe digs up the Piece of Heart, pick it up before you leave the Graveyard. Otherwise it is lost forever [[ra-comments-101-1, ra-guide-20-1]].

Dampe walks a circular route along the path. If you stand directly on a dirt patch in his path, he stops in front of it. Talk to him and pay 10 rupees to dig [[gf1-56-1]]. The Piece of Heart is in one of twelve patches, and most other patches give at least some rupees [[gf1-56-2, gf1-432-2]]. Only ask him to dig on the soil patches, not the bare path [[checklist-heartpieces-6-1]].

The Piece of Heart changes patches every time you leave the zone. If you leave to restock rupees, start your search over [[gf1-56-2]]. Dampe appears at random after nightfall, so wait for him [[ra-comments-101-5]]. One player needed about 20 digs [[ra-comments-101-4]].

When it comes up, grab the Piece of Heart before you leave.

### Kakariko Village at Night (Gold Skulltulas #22-#26)

Return to the village at night. These five Skulltulas need night, and you need your Slingshot [[checklist-skulltulas-6-1, gf1-57-1]]. If it is day, play the Sun's Song [[gf1-393-3]].

1. **Skulltula #22 (tree):** Roll into the tree near the village entrance and kill the Skulltula that falls out [[checklist-skulltulas-6-1, gf1-462-1, gf3-20-4]].
2. **Skulltula #23 (House of Skulltula window):** It sits on the window of the House of Skulltula, beneath the window on its left side [[checklist-skulltulas-6-2, gf1-57-2, gf3-20-5]]. Another guide places it on the backside window [[gf1-462-2]], so check both sides.
3. **Skulltula #24 (unfinished building):** At the Unfinished House, on the pile of bricks. Kill it with the Slingshot before jumping up for the token, because the sword hurts you at close range [[checklist-skulltulas-6-3, gf1-57-1, gf1-462-3]].
4. **Skulltula #25 (north house):** At the north end of town, the house on the left of the Death Mountain gate has a Skulltula on it. Smash the pots to get at it if it is hidden [[checklist-skulltulas-6-4, gf1-57-5, gf1-463-2]].
5. **Skulltula #26 (lookout tower ladder):** On the ladder of the tall lookout tower. Shoot it with two Slingshot shots, then climb up for the token [[checklist-skulltulas-6-5, gf3-20-8, gf2-21-3]].

Together with the Graveyard token and the five you already had, that makes 11 tokens. The Skulltula on Impa's roof needs the Hookshot, so skip it for now [[checklist-skulltulas-6-6]].

### Lon Lon Ranch

Lon Lon Ranch is in the middle of Hyrule Field [[gf1-8-10]]. Enter at night first, for the Skulltulas [[checklist-sidequests-9-12]]. If it is daytime, play the Sun's Song [[gf1-393-3]].

#### Gold Skulltulas #14 and #15 (Night)

1. **Skulltula #14 (tree):** Roll into the lone tree beside the ranch house, just left of the arch leading to the pasture. Kill the Skulltula that falls and take the token [[checklist-skulltulas-5-3, gf1-44-2, gf3-18-2]].
2. **Skulltula #15 (feeding shelter):** Circle around to the back of the horse corral and kill the Skulltula on the back of the feeding shelter. Watch out for Guay birds [[checklist-skulltulas-5-4, gf1-44-3, gf3-18-5]]. If the token is out of reach, you may need to backflip into it [[gf1-461-1]].

That brings you to 13 tokens. The window Skulltula and the rock-wall Skulltula need the Boomerang, so leave them for later [[checklist-skulltulas-5-5, checklist-skulltulas-5-6]].

#### Daytime: Talon's Super Cucco Game

Play the Sun's Song to make it day [[checklist-sidequests-9-12, gf1-393-3]]. Enter the house to the left of the entrance, where Talon runs a game [[gf4-6-11]].

For 10 rupees, you must find three Super Cuccos within 30 seconds [[gf2-103-1]]. The Super Cuccos look the same as the others [[gf3-18-1]].

The easiest method is to throw all the Cuccos into the corner behind the long counter Talon sits on, then "select" Cuccos by picking them up [[gf1-42-3, ra-comments-59-1]]. A green arrow shows over a Cucco you have already picked up. You can shield-drop a Cucco to release it faster [[ra-guide-6-7]].

Find all three and Talon gives you a Bottle filled with Lon Lon Milk [[gf1-42-2, gf2-103-2]]. Once the milk is gone, you have an Empty Bottle [[gf2-17-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Super Cucco Catching (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158152.png)
> *Obtain a reward from the Lon Lon Ranch minigame*


Players report this achievement sometimes unlocks along with the milk achievement. The cause is unconfirmed [[ra-comments-59-2, ra-comments-59-3, ra-comments-60-1]].

#### Epona's Song

Talk to Malon in the middle of the horse pen [[checklist-sidequests-9-15]]. Talk to her repeatedly. When she mentions her mother, take out the Ocarina and she teaches you Epona's Song [[gf1-43-1, gf3-234-2, gf2-17-4]]. Either Ocarina works [[gf1-353-2]].

The song is Up, Left, Right, Up, Left, Right [[gf1-392-3]]. It will later let you ride Epona and gets free milk from any cow [[gf1-43-2]].

#### The Crawlspace (Piece of Heart)

Go to the small building at the back of the ranch (the southwest corner) and enter it [[checklist-heartpieces-3-3, gf4-6-10]]. Pull the crates aside to open the crawlspace in the corner [[gf4-6-10, gf3-18-3]]. The details in one guide: pull the upper-right box, the one beside the cow, down toward the door once, then go to the other side and push it down. Then pull the box in the upper-left corner to the right [[gf2-17-3]]. Crawl through the tunnel to an empty nest with a **Piece of Heart** [[gf1-44-1, gf1-429-1]].

#### Milk from a Cow (You Make Me Feel So Good)

Make sure you have an empty Bottle. Go to a cow in the barn on the ranch [[ra-guide-6-8, gf3-18-3]]. Stand next to the cow and play Epona's Song with the empty Bottle [[gf2-17-5, ra-guide-6-8]]. It fills the Bottle with free Lon Lon Milk [[gf3-18-4, gf3-172-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **You Make Me Feel So Good (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158153.png)
> *Obtain some nutritious milk from a healthy cow*


The same cow trick works with any cow, including one in a hidden pit on Death Mountain Trail [[ra-comments-60-4, ra-comments-60-5, ra-comments-60-6, ra-comments-60-7, gf1-86-1]]. One player's report said that on the OoT 1.0 ROM this also unlocked Super Cucco Catching. That is unconfirmed [[ra-comments-60-1]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave Lon Lon Ranch:** As an adult you will return here for Ingo's races and Epona, which requires Epona's Song [[gf1-413-3, gf1-133-3]]. Malon's obstacle course comes much later and depends on waking Talon in the Biggoron Sword sequence [[gf1-166-1, checklist-sidequests-24-8]].

### House of Skulltula

Return to Kakariko Village. The cursed family's house is the first house on the right as you enter from the main gate [[checklist-skulltulas-2-1, gf4-8-3]]. You now hold 13 tokens, more than the 10 needed. Enter, turn right and talk to the man there. He gives you the **Adult's Wallet**, which raises your capacity from 99 to 200 rupees [[gf1-58-1, gf2-23-1, gf3-20-1, gf1-450-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **A Man's Best Friend (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158194.png)
> *Collect 10 Gold Skulltulas and obtain your reward*


Further rewards, for later:

| Tokens | Reward | Citation |
|---|---|---|
| 20 | Stone of Agony | [[gf1-420-3, gf1-89-1]] |
| 30 | Giant's Wallet | [[gf1-120-1, gf1-419-3]] |
| 40 | Bombchus | [[gf1-168-1, gf2-47-1]] |
| 50 | Piece of Heart | [[gf1-431-3, gf2-60-6]] |
| 100 | Huge Rupee (repeatable) | [[gf1-451-4, gf2-97-7]] |

### Left for Later

These Kakariko and Graveyard items need gear or conditions you do not have yet, according to the sources cited in each row.

| Item | What it needs | Citation |
|---|---|---|
| Windmill Piece of Heart | Boomerang | [[gf1-121-2, checklist-heartpieces-3-5]] |
| Secret Cave with Redeads (Huge Rupee) | Bombs, and the Sun's Song to deal with the Redeads | [[gf3-27-3, gf3-238-3]] |
| Red Rupee in a hole near the Potion Shop | Drop into the hole | [[gf3-238-4, gf2-104-1]] |
| Graveyard southeast-wall Skulltula | Boomerang | [[checklist-skulltulas-6-7, gf1-464-2]] |
| Hookshot from Dampe's race | Adult Link | [[gf1-135-3, gf1-387-3]] |
| Bean-platform Piece of Heart | A Magic Bean planted at the soft soil, ridden as an adult | [[gf1-120-3, gf1-433-1]] |

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave Kakariko:** Confirm you have the Bottles, the Hylian Shield, the Sun's Song, Epona's Song, the three Pieces of Heart and the Adult's Wallet. Sources also list the Windmill and the Dampe race as later tasks, so you are not leaving anything permanent behind [[gf1-136-1, gf1-245-1]].
