## Chapter 4: Lost Woods, Sacred Forest Meadow and Goron City

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Kokiri_Forest["Kokiri Forest"]
  Lost_Woods["Lost Woods"]
  Sacred_Forest_Meadow["Sacred Forest Meadow"]
  Death_Mountain_Trail["Death Mountain Trail"]
  Goron_City["Goron City"]
  Queen_Gohma{{"⚔ Queen Gohma"}}
  King_Dodongo{{"⚔ King Dodongo"}}
  Barinade{{"⚔ Barinade"}}
  Phantom_Ganon{{"⚔ Phantom Ganon"}}
  Morpha{{"⚔ Morpha"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Twinrova{{"⚔ Twinrova"}}
  Ganondorf{{"⚔ Ganondorf"}}
  Ganon{{"⚔ Ganon"}}
  Dark_Link{{"⚔ Shadow Link (Dark Link)"}}
  Dead_Hand{{"⚔ Dead Hand"}}
  Dying_Guard_Lockout>"⛔ Dying guard disappears after the Temple of Time"]
  Final_Battle>"⛔ Ganon's Tower final battle"]
  Lost_Woods --> Sacred_Forest_Meadow
  Death_Mountain_Trail --> Goron_City
  Ganondorf --> Final_Battle
  Final_Battle --> Ganon
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Dying_Guard_Lockout,Final_Battle ponr;
  class Queen_Gohma,King_Dodongo,Barinade,Phantom_Ganon,Morpha,Bongo_Bongo,Twinrova,Ganondorf,Ganon,Dark_Link,Dead_Hand boss;
```

This chapter takes you through the Lost Woods, where you earn the Bullet Bag upgrade and the Skull Kid Piece of Hearts, then to the Sacred Forest Meadow for Saria's Song, and finally up Death Mountain Trail to Goron City, where Darunia gives you the Goron's Bracelet. The Lost Woods is entered from Kokiri Forest [[gf1-23-2]]. The Sacred Forest Meadow lies in the midst of the Lost Woods [[gf1-8-2]]. Death Mountain Trail leads from Kakariko Village to Goron City [[gf1-62-5]].

Three achievements are placed here: Bullseye!! (Bullet Bag), You Can Be a Star (Ocarina minigame) and Dance Into the Fire (Darunia's gift) [[gf1-46-1, gf1-46-2, gf1-63-3]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Heads-Up for Later Deku Upgrades (A Horrible Face, Stocking Nuts):** The Deku Nut upgrade from the Mask of Truth on the Forest Stage cannot be obtained once Link has become an adult [[gf1-525-1, gf1-418-1]]. One source also says receiving the Poacher's Saw sets a flag that makes it unobtainable [[ra-comments-44-3]]. The Deku Nut upgrade under the boulder near the Sacred Forest Meadow entrance is also flagged as missable if you take the Poacher's Saw before any Lost Woods upgrades [[ra-comments-73-3]]. This chapter's route does not need the Mask of Truth yet. Do not trade ahead in the Biggoron's Sword chain before collecting these upgrades [[ra-guide-10-2]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Do Not Break These Boulders (Walking on a Tightrope, Chapter 6):** In Goron City, do NOT destroy the three boulders to the left of the entrance on the top floor. Their destruction is permanent and voids the achievement in chapter 6 [[ra-comments-68-1, ra-comments-68-3, ra-guide-8-2]]. Open the other, non-entrance rocks only where this guide tells you to.

### Chapter Items to Collect

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Bullet Bag (40 to 50 seeds) | Lost Woods target | **SHOOT** center 3 times in a row | [[gf1-46-1]] |
| Piece of Heart (memory game) | Lost Woods, twin Skull Kids | **PLAY** the Ocarina memory game | [[gf1-46-2]] |
| Saria's Song | Sacred Forest Meadow | **LEARN** from Saria | [[gf1-47-3]] |
| Piece of Heart (Saria's Song) | Lost Woods, lone Skull Kid | **PLAY** Saria's Song | [[gf1-49-1]] |
| Goron's Bracelet | Goron City, Darunia | **PLAY** Saria's Song | [[gf1-63-3]] |
| Piece of Heart (above Dodongo's Cavern) | Death Mountain Trail | **BACKFLIP** over fence | [[gf1-66-3]] |
| Gold Skulltula #1 | Kokiri Forest, back of the Know-It-All Brothers' House (night) | **SHOOT**, then **TAKE** | [[checklist-skulltulas-3-1]] |
| Gold Skulltula #2 | Kokiri Forest, soft soil by the shop | **DROP** bugs, **KILL** | [[checklist-skulltulas-3-2]] |
| Gold Skulltula #4 | Lost Woods, soft soil two lefts from the entrance | **DROP** bugs, **KILL** | [[checklist-skulltulas-3-4]] |
| Gold Skulltula #5 | Lost Woods, soft soil in the Forest Stage clearing | **DROP** bugs, **KILL** | [[checklist-skulltulas-3-5]] |
| Gold Skulltula #30 | Soft soil at Dodongo's Cavern entrance | **DROP** bugs, **KILL** | [[gf1-465-2]] |

### Kokiri Forest

🗺️ **Map:** [View Kokiri Forest Map by kingofthisgame](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/1099-kokiri-forest-map)

Return to Kokiri Forest as a child. The way into the Lost Woods is behind Mido's House: climb up on the ledge behind it, climb the vine wall, work around the ledge and enter the large hollow log [[gf1-23-2]].

Collect both Kokiri Forest Gold Skulltulas now (bring a Bottle with bugs, found under rocks). At night, one sits behind the Know-It-All Brothers' House (the house to the right as you enter from Hyrule Field). Kill it with the Slingshot, then backflip into the token while facing away from it [[gf1-452-1, gf1-45-2, checklist-skulltulas-3-1]]. The second is under the patch of soft earth by the Kokiri Shop; drop a Bottled Bug on it and kill the Skulltula [[gf1-452-2, checklist-skulltulas-3-2]]. A Magic Bean can be planted there too [[gf3-37-4]].

You need the Fairy Ocarina and Slingshot for this chapter. Saria already gave you the Ocarina when you left the forest [[gf1-34-3]].

### Lost Woods

🗺️ **Map:** [View Lost Woods Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/54-lost-woods-map)

#### Bullet Bag target

Enter via the log behind Mido's house and go right through the hollowed log. Run forward to the edge of the cliff and take out the Slingshot. Shoot the drum hanging from the tree limb at its exact center. A hit gives 100 points and a small victory sound. Three successive center shots make a Deku Scrub appear and give you the Bullet Bag x50 [[gf1-46-1]]. Another source describes it as upgrading seed capacity from 40 to 50 [[gf2-18-1]]. A "100" appears when you hit the right place [[gf1-406-1]]. Aim the center of the Slingshot's rubber band at the center of the drum before you fire [[gf1-46-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Bullseye!! (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158154.png)
> *Obtain the Bullet Bag upgrade in the Lost Woods*


#### Skull Kid memory game

Drop down the cliff to the small valley below and go all the way to the right. Stand on the low trunk and two Skull Kids appear. Take out the Ocarina and play the memory game: three rounds, each longer and harder [[gf1-46-2]]. They play each addition twice, so watch the first Skull Kid and play along with the second to learn the new part fastest [[gf1-46-2]]. Another source says you may want pen and paper ready [[checklist-heartpieces-3-2]]. The notes are five, six and eight in length, with a Green Rupee and a Blue Rupee as the earlier prizes and the Piece of Heart for the last [[gf2-20-1]]. Completing the game awards the Piece of Heart [[gf1-425-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **You Can Be a Star (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158155.png)
> *Obtain the final reward from the Ocarina Playing minigame in the Lost Woods*


#### Gold Skulltulas #4 and #5 (bugs)

You already own the Deku Stick upgrade from Chapter 1. Bring a Bottle with bugs for these two soft-soil Skulltulas, and recapture a bug each time.

1. **#4:** Take two lefts from the entrance, drop into the valley and find the patch of soft earth past the Business Scrub. Drop the bugs on it and kill the Gold Skulltula that appears [[checklist-skulltulas-3-4, gf1-454-1, gf1-125-1]].
2. **#5:** From the entrance go right, left, right, left and left to reach a large grassy clearing near the Forest Stage. Drop the bugs on its soft earth and kill the Skulltula [[checklist-skulltulas-3-5, gf1-454-2]].

#### Onward to the Sacred Forest Meadow

Climb the ladder back up, then take the hollowed log to the right of the ladder. Kaepora Gaebora appears; the stone arch here leads to Goron City but you can't use it yet [[gf1-46-3]]. Go through the log tunnel he stood on. On the other side is a pool that leads to Zora's Domain, also not usable yet [[gf1-46-4]]. Go left through a tunnel, straight through another, left through another, and right at the next crossroads to enter the Sacred Forest Meadow [[gf1-46-5]].

Optionally, bomb the rock near the entrance to the Sacred Forest Meadow and drop into the pit for a pair of Business Scrubs. One sells the Deku Nut upgrade for 40 rupees [[gf1-418-2, gf1-86-5]]. This is the upgrade the Stocking Nuts achievement refers to, not the Mask of Truth one [[ra-comments-73-1]]. The scrub on the left is the one that sells it [[ra-guide-8-7]]. You need Bombs, which you get in Dodongo's Cavern, so this is for the return trip.

### Sacred Forest Meadow

🗺️ **Map:** [View Sacred Forest Meadow Map by R351D3NT3V1L4](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/5354-sacred-forest-meadow-map)

Run forward a short way and a Gray Wolfos ambushes you. Stand still, wait for it to move close, backflip (Back + A while Z-targeted) as it attacks, then immediately Jump Attack (A while Z-targeted). That kills it in one shot [[gf1-47-1]]. Its death makes the gate blocking the meadow disappear [[gf1-47-2]].

Turn right and follow the path around the corner. Kill or run past the Deku Scrub and go around another corner. Kill or dodge another Scrub, round another corner, take a left down a grassy detour, then another left back onto the main road [[gf1-47-3]]. Jump into the pool at the end of the road, then jump back onto the road with the Deku Scrub in front of you. Kill or dodge it, go around the corner, take a left and go up the stairs. Run down the long corridor, killing or dodging two more Deku Scrubs, then run up the stairs. At the top Saria sits in a small overgrown courtyard and teaches you Saria's Song [[gf1-47-3]].

The song is C-Down, C-Right, C-Left, C-Down, C-Right, C-Left [[gf1-393-1]].

Head back down the stairs, down the corridor and down the second flight. Climb the ladder directly in front and jump along the cliffs to leave faster. The pit in the middle of the upper cliffs holds a Fairy Fountain [[gf1-47-3, gf1-47-4]]. Run back to the entrance and return to the Lost Woods.

> [!TIP]
> RetroAchievements commenters note that entering Goron City from the Lost Woods triggers Saria to appear in the Sacred Forest Meadow, so if Saria is missing, try that [[ra-comments-13-2]].

### Lost Woods (return)

Kaepora Gaebora ambushes you again. Take a right through the hollowed log to return to Kokiri Forest, then turn right back around and re-enter the Lost Woods [[gf1-49-1]].

Go left through the hollowed log and jump onto the small trunk by the Skull Kid. Play Saria's Song for him and he gives you a Piece of Heart [[gf1-49-1, checklist-heartpieces-3-1]]. This gives you another Heart Container [[gf1-49-1]].

### Death Mountain Trail

Get to Kakariko Village and show Zelda's Letter to the guard at the northern gate to be let through [[gf1-58-2]]. Equip the Hylian Shield before you go [[gf1-62-1]].

Go forward and kill the Tektite. If it tries to jump on you, hold R for the Turtle Defense. Around the corner defeat two more Tektites, then another that jumps down from above [[gf1-62-1, gf1-62-2]]. Watch for rolling boulders as you climb [[gf1-169-1]]. Note the Piece of Heart high above the giant boulder; you get it shortly [[gf1-62-4]].

Follow the path to the large wooden post with a red flag. Go right, past the large stone pedestal with the ring of rocks, and enter the archway into Goron City [[gf1-62-5]].

### Goron City

#### Darunia

Jump down to the lowest level. Find the sealed door with the carpet in front of it, stand on the carpet and play Zelda's Lullaby to open it [[gf1-63-1]]. Enter and find Darunia. Play Saria's Song standing directly in front of him to cheer him up [[gf1-63-2]]. He gives you the Goron's Bracelet, which lets you pick up and use Bomb Flowers [[gf1-63-3, gf3-21-5]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Dance Into the Fire (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158121.png)
> *Visit Darunia in Goron City and obtain a gift*


Before leaving the room, light a Deku Stick on the torch in his room. Run out and light the empty sconce outside. Put the stick away by swinging your sword, take out another and light it again. Light all the braziers on the bottom level; once all are lit the giant pot starts spinning [[gf1-63-4]]. You can also use the lit stick on the Bomb Flowers near the sealed room to blow the wall open and reach the Goron City Shop [[gf1-63-5]].

#### Second floor

Take the stairs between the yellow-striped posts to the second level. Go right to the two unlit torches and a Goron sitting in front of a tunnel. In the tunnel, pick up a Bomb Flower and drop it near the boulders to clear the path [[gf1-63-6]]. Lighting the two torches and the Bomb Flowers opens a shortcut tunnel to the Lost Woods [[gf2-24-3]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** Do NOT destroy the three boulders blocking the entrance to the boulder room on the top floor. Leave them intact so Walking on a Tightrope in chapter 6 remains available [[ra-comments-68-3, ra-guide-8-2]].

#### Deku Nut and Mask trips

The Skull Mask Forest Stage trip and the Mask of Truth upgrade belong to the Happy Mask sidequest, which you may do later. The Deku Stick upgrade (30) comes with the Skull Mask and the Deku Nut upgrade (40) with the Mask of Truth [[gf1-349-3]]. Unable to verify from collected sources whether this chapter's Slingshot and Ocarina progress is affected by either.

#### Dodongo's Cavern entrance and the Piece of Heart

From the second level, go to the far left and up the stairs between the red-striped posts. When the stairs split, go right. At the top, go right from the stairwell exit, then right again to return to Death Mountain Trail [[gf1-66-1]].

Go around the corner to the right to a small alcove with a Bomb Flower. Pick it up and throw it off the cliff so it lands on or near the boulder blocking Dodongo's Cavern [[gf1-66-2]]. Next, face away from the edge, hold Z without targeting anything, run backward toward the fence and backflip over it. If done right, you land on the cliff above Dodongo's Cavern and get the Piece of Heart [[gf1-66-3]]. This completes a Heart Container [[gf1-66-3]]. Drop down, and drop a bug into the soft earth at the cavern entrance to release a Gold Skulltula (recapture one bug) [[gf1-66-4, gf1-465-2]]. You can also plant a Magic Bean in this soil [[gf1-121-3]].

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave: you have Dodongo's Cavern next, which gives you Bombs. After you return you can finish the spinning pot (Piece of Heart), the rolling Goron Bomb Bag, the boulder maze Skulltula and other bomb-gated spots in Goron City [[gf1-85-3, gf1-85-4, gf1-468-3]].

### Bomb-gated content (Chapter 6)

The Death Mountain Trail wall Skulltula, the rolling Goron Bomb Bag, the spinning-pot Piece of Heart, the Goron City boulder maze, the Deku Nut upgrade grotto and the Great Fairy all need Bombs. Chapter 6 does them in order, once you have Bombs [[gf1-85-3, gf1-85-4, gf1-468-3, gf1-86-5]].

### Cumulative Progress

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 4 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 18 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 7 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 1 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 2 / 26
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 18 / 97 (95 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Bullet Bag x50 from the Lost Woods target
- [ ] Piece of Heart from the Skull Kid memory game
- [ ] Saria's Song learned in the Sacred Forest Meadow
- [ ] Piece of Heart from the lone Skull Kid (Saria's Song)
- [ ] Goron's Bracelet from Darunia
- [ ] Goron City top-floor entrance boulders left intact
- [ ] Bomb Flower thrown to open Dodongo's Cavern
- [ ] Piece of Heart above Dodongo's Cavern
- [ ] Gold Skulltulas #1, #2, #4, #5 and #30

## Chapter 5: Dodongo's Cavern

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Dodongos_Cavern["Dodongo's Cavern"]
  King_Dodongo{{"⚔ King Dodongo"}}
  Dodongos_Cavern --> King_Dodongo
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class King_Dodongo boss;
```

Dodongo's Cavern is the second dungeon. It opens when you throw a Bomb Flower at the sealed entrance, and it ends with King Dodongo and the Goron's Ruby. The dungeon's "Big Puzzle" is climbing to the catwalk above the giant Dodongo skull and forcing its mouth open to reach the boss [[gf1-9-7, gf1-68-1]]. Bombs, the Bomb Bag and the Bomb Flowers are the dungeon's main tools [[gf1-383-8]]. Two achievements belong here: **Bombalicious** (missable, a boss challenge) and **Soul Brothers** (story progression). You also collect the Map, the Compass, the Bomb Bag, a Heart Container and four Gold Skulltulas.

### Preparation

Darunia gives you the Goron's Bracelet in Goron City. You need it to pick up and throw Bomb Flowers, and nearly every puzzle here depends on that [[gf1-63-3, checklist-sidequests-9-25]].

Equip the Hylian Shield if you have it. Keese that fly through a torch flame catch fire, and the Hylian Shield blocks them and douses the flame. A Deku Shield is destroyed by them [[gf1-69-2]].

Bring a few Deku Sticks. You need a lit one to open a door later, and a Business Scrub inside the dungeon sells them for 15 rupees each [[gf1-71-2, gf1-71-3]].

> [!TIP]
> **Bomb Flowers vs. bag bombs:** Your Bomb Bag arrives mid-dungeon [[gf1-78-2]]. For the missable achievement, you must beat King Dodongo without using bombs from that bag. The fight takes place in a room with its own Bomb Flowers [[ra-guide-7-2, ra-comments-14-22]].

### Dungeon Items to Collect

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Dungeon Map | Chest behind a sealed wall to the left of the Bomb Flower near the unlocked door, west side of the Antechamber | **TAKE** after blasting the wall with a Bomb Flower | [[gf1-72-2]] |
| Compass | Chest in the Armos Trap Room | **TAKE** after defeating the Armos Knight with a Bomb Flower | [[gf1-73-3]] |
| Red Rupee | Small chest on a platform in the Blade Trap Room | **TAKE** | [[gf1-76-1]] |
| Bomb Bag (20 Bombs) | Chest on an upper ledge of the upper Blade Trap Room | **TAKE** | [[gf1-78-2]] |
| Deku Shield or Blue Rupee | Chest behind a sealed wall at the end of the catwalk | **TAKE** (Blue Rupee if you still own a Deku Shield) | [[gf1-79-3]] |
| Bombs | Chest in the room above the boss pit | **TAKE** | [[gf1-82-2]] |
| Heart Container | Left by King Dodongo | **TAKE** | [[gf1-82-7]] |
| Goron's Ruby | Given by Darunia outside | **RECEIVE** | [[gf1-82-8]] |

### Dodongo's Cavern

#### Entrance (Death Mountain Trail)

Stand at the sealed entrance and grab one of the Bomb Flowers to your right. Throw it near the cracked wall. The explosion destroys the wall and opens the cavern [[gf1-68-1]]. A second guide says to throw the bomb from a Bomb Flower at the sturdy door [[gf2-25-1]].

#### 1F: Antechamber and Main Chamber

Run into the large antechamber along the wooden flooring and jump out to the island in the middle. Dodge the Beamos and head to the right side of the room [[gf1-68-1]]. Grab the Bomb Flower in front of the large window. Z-target the Beamos beside it, run at it and throw the flower so it hits the Beamos on the head. The Beamos explodes [[gf1-68-2, gf4-10-1]]. Grab another Bomb Flower and blow open the sealed wall to the right of the Beamos, then go through the dark tunnel [[gf1-68-3]].

A second route description says to blast the door on the platform's right. The one on the left only reveals a Gossip Stone [[gf2-25-1]]. Unable to verify from collected sources whether the Gossip Stone matters beyond a free fairy when you play Zelda's Lullaby near it [[gf3-93-7]].

#### 1F: Lower Southeast Hall (Skulltula #38)

Run forward and kill the Baby Dodongos that burst from the ground. They explode when they die, so back away fast [[gf1-69-1, gf1-170-1]]. Further along, another Baby Dodongo appears next to a sealed wall. Stand next to the wall and kill the Baby Dodongo as it passes the wall, so its explosion destroys the barrier. If you botch it, leave the dungeon and re-enter to reset the Baby Dodongos [[gf1-69-1]]. A bombable wall on the left, before the ground becomes elevated, is the same wall. You can also blow it up with a Bomb Flower [[checklist-sidequests-10-1, checklist-skulltulas-8-1]].

Go through the door behind the wall into the Torch Room. Kill the Keese on the opposite wall. If they fly through the torch flame they catch fire, so use the Hylian Shield to absorb their attacks and douse them [[gf1-69-2, gf3-23-2]]. Kill the Gold Skulltula on the wall and take the token. You may need a backflip to reach it. This is **Skulltula #38** [[gf1-69-3, checklist-skulltulas-8-1, checklist-sidequests-3-17]]. Return to the Lower Southeast Hall.

Turn right and kill the respawned Baby Dodongos. Climb the ledge and run to the far end. Grab an Armos Statue and push it onto the gray switch to unlock the door. Climb the ledge and enter the door [[gf1-70-1]]. One guide notes the statues can be pushed onto a blue switch that does not stay pressed on its own, which is why you must hold it down with a statue [[gf2-25-3]].

#### 1F: Southeast Passage and Lizalfos Nest

In the Southeast Passage, Z-target the Keese on either side and kill them with the Slingshot. Break the pots and take their contents. Go through the door at the far end [[gf1-70-2]].

The lower Lizalfos Nest holds two Lizalfos. Don't chase them. Wait for them to come to you. Block each attack with the shield, then counter with the sword [[gf1-70-3]]. A jump attack while Z-targeting is also effective [[gf2-25-4]]. Holding the shield up until one attacks, then jump attacking twice with the Kokiri Sword, will make it flee [[checklist-sidequests-7-2]]. A Deku Nut stun followed by one Deku Stick jump attack also sends one running [[checklist-sidequests-7-1]]. When both are dead, the west door unlocks [[gf1-70-4]].

#### 1F: Dodongo Breeding Ground

Run to the end of the passage. A sealed wall on the right hides a room with a Business Scrub who sells Deku Sticks for 15 rupees each. Only visit him if you need sticks [[gf1-71-3, gf2-26-1]]. Another guide says to use a Bomb Flower on that wall [[gf3-23-3]].

In the long room, kill the pair of Dodongos by running behind them and hitting their tail. They explode when they die, so stay away afterward [[gf1-71-1]]. Return to the entrance of the room and light a Deku Stick on the torch. Use the flaming stick to light the three empty sconces along the room, each near where a Dodongo stood. When all three are lit, the door at the west end opens [[gf1-71-2, gf2-26-2]].

#### 1F: Back to the Antechamber (Dungeon Map)

You emerge inside the "window" in the Antechamber. Run forward and step on the switch to open the door on the far side. Then drop out of the window. If you miss the switch, you must go back around and hit it again [[gf1-72-1]].

Run across the platforms and cliffs to the west side of the room. Before using the unlocked door, grab the Bomb Flower near it and blow open the sealed wall to its left. Open the chest for the **Dungeon Map** (Map 1 of 2 for this dungeon) [[gf1-72-2, gf2-26-3]]. Another guide places this chest on the west side of the room [[gf3-23-1]]. Then take the door you unlocked in the northwest corner [[gf1-72-3]].

#### 1F: Bomb Flower Room (Compass, Skulltula #39)

The room is crowded with Bomb Flowers around an iron pillar [[gf1-73-1]]. First go to the south end and use a Bomb Flower against the oddly colored wall. Go through the opening and the door beyond [[gf1-73-1]].

In the Armos Trap Room, three Armos statues surround a chest. The one in front comes alive when touched. Grab a Bomb Flower and drop it near the Armos Knight. Don't throw it. Just before it explodes, strike the Armos with your sword to wake it. The explosion kills the Knight, which then spins and flashes and explodes after a few seconds, so stay clear [[gf1-73-2, gf2-26-4]]. Open the chest for the **Compass** (Compass 1 of 1) [[gf1-73-3]]. Return to the Bomb Flower Room.

Now lower the iron pillar. Grab the Bomb Flower to your right as you leave the door and throw it directly between the two long lines of Bomb Flowers. They all ignite at once and the pillar drops to form stairs. If your throw isn't in the exact center, it won't ignite properly [[gf1-74-1]]. Another guide describes the same puzzle: take the lone flower and set its bomb in the gap in the ring [[gf2-26-5, gf4-10-5]].

Climb the stairs and go left or right around the path to the top of the room. There is a Skulltula on the ivy plus a Gold Skulltula above it. Kill the normal Skulltula with a jump attack, then shoot the Gold Skulltula with the Slingshot. Climb the ivy and take the token. This is **Skulltula #39** [[gf1-74-2, gf2-26-6, checklist-skulltulas-8-2, checklist-sidequests-3-18]]. Go through the door by the ivy.

#### 2F: Pillar Room

A pair of Fire Keese fly around this room. Circle the room and take them out with the Slingshot, or block them with the shield [[gf1-75-1]]. Go to the south side of the pillar. An Armos Statue covers a ladder. Pull it away from the ladder, and don't touch any other statues. They are Armos Knights in disguise [[gf1-75-4]]. Another guide says to place a bomb first, as you did before, and kill the three disguised statues [[gf2-27-1]]. Climb the ladder, step on the pillar switch to unlock the east door, and go east [[gf1-75-2]].

You are now high on the rear catwalk of the Antechamber. Run along it, past or through the Fire Keese, leap the gap in the catwalk and run through the tunnel [[gf1-75-3]].

#### 2F: Blade Trap Room (Red Rupee)

This room has pillars and blade traps. Run between the pillars to the rear, where a stone block sits beneath a ladder. Climb the block, then the ladder. Stand on the narrow ledge, carefully turn to face the small platform and jump to it. You will just barely make it. Open the chest for a **Red Rupee** [[gf1-76-1]]. Another guide places the chest on a pillar with a Bomb Flower [[gf2-27-1]].

Next, grab the Bomb Flower and hold it until it is almost ready to explode. Throw it at the wall above the ladder to blast the seal open, jump to the opened tunnel and run through it [[gf1-76-2]]. Timing matters: wait until the flashing is extremely fast, then wait about one more second before throwing [[gf2-27-2]].

#### 2F: Solo Eye Room and Upper Lizalfos Nest

Shoot the golden eye above the circle of flames with the Slingshot. The ring of fire disappears. Jump across the platform and through the tunnel. Baby Dodongos unearth themselves along the way. Go through the door at the end of the hall [[gf1-77-1]].

This is the upper level of the Lizalfos Nest. Fight two more Lizalfos the same way as before: wait for them, block with the shield, and answer with jump attacks. When both are dead the west door opens [[gf1-77-2]].

#### 2F: Double Eye Room and the Bomb Bag

Shoot the eye on the high wall above the flames. The first ring disappears. Jump to the platform, look left, and shoot the second golden eye in the alcove. The second ring disappears and you can jump the rest of the way. Run through the dark tunnel [[gf1-78-1, gf4-10-7]].

You come out in the upper area of the Blade Trap Room. Jump the gap, turn right and see a chest on an upper ledge. Jump up and open it for the **Bomb Bag** with 20 Bombs. Turn around and take the tunnel to the right [[gf1-78-2, gf2-27-4, gf1-409-2, gf3-157-1]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** From now on, the Bomb Bag is yours. **Do not use bombs from the bag in the King Dodongo fight.** Bombalicious (48277) requires you to win before he breathes fire without using bombs from your bag, and it is missable [[gf1-82-4, ra-guide-7-2, ra-comments-14-22]]. Spend the bag bombs on the walls and skull eyes below, not on the boss.

#### 2F: Antechamber, Skull Eyes and the Mouth

You are back in the Antechamber on the upper level near the front. Step on the switch directly ahead. A floating pillar rises to give you a way back up should you fall [[gf1-79-1]].

Run right and across the catwalk. Jump the first gap so you stand between the two gaps. Using bombs from your new bag, drop bombs through each gap so they land in the skull's eye sockets. Press the C button assigned to Bombs twice to drop a bomb. Blasts in both sockets make the skull open its mouth [[gf1-79-2, gf2-27-6, gf3-24-3, gf4-10-10]].

Before dropping down, run to the end of the catwalk and blow open the sealed wall. Open the chest for a replacement **Deku Shield**, or a **Blue Rupee** if you still have yours [[gf1-79-3, gf2-27-5, gf3-24-4]]. Then drop to the central platform and enter the Dodongo's mouth. Go through the door inside [[gf1-79-4]].

#### 1F: Back Room, Goron Graveyard and Skulltula #40

Drop into the large pit and kill the Fire Keese. Climb the ledge to the east and run through the dark tunnel [[gf1-80-1, gf1-80-2]]. Follow the tunnel as it curves left, then jump into the pit. Two Fire Keese swoop in. Go left around the barrier, then right on its other side. Run to the far wall and jump up over the small ledge. Go down into the next pit. A sealed wall is here. Climb up next to it, drop a bomb beside it and go through the door [[gf1-80-3, gf1-80-4]].

This room has a Gold Skulltula behind an Armos Knight statue. Shoot the Gold Skulltula with the Slingshot, then carefully step forward to grab the token without touching the Knight. This is **Skulltula #40** [[gf1-81-1, checklist-skulltulas-8-3, checklist-sidequests-3-19]]. If you do brush the Knight, defeat it with bombs [[gf1-81-2]]. Another guide says to blow open the wall at the end of the passage after entering the skull and turning right, which agrees with this room [[checklist-skulltulas-8-3, gf1-486-1]].

Return to the previous room. Drop from the doorway and climb the ledge to the left. The Fire Keese have respawned. Climb the brown block, then the next ledge, run to the end and jump into the pit. Break the pots for recovery items and a Fairy. Put the Fairy in a bottle if you have a spare. Then enter the dark tunnel [[gf1-81-3]].

#### Back Room Puzzle and the Pit

Run to the end of the tunnel to a brown stone block on a track. Push it off the edge to the floor below. Kill the Fire Keese, pull the block away from the wall, move around to its other side and push it into the opening in the floor's center. The western door unlocks. Go through it [[gf1-82-1]].

Open the chest on the far end of the room for **Bombs**. Then drop a Bomb onto the iron plate in the middle of the room. After the explosion the plate disintegrates and you can drop through the pit into the boss room [[gf1-82-2, gf1-82-3]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before the boss door:** Make sure you are healthy and carry Deku Sticks and the Slingshot. Once you drop into the pit the fight begins. If you fail, reset the fight. Sources disagree on whether you must reset the game or can just die and walk back in [[ra-comments-14-4, ra-comments-14-14]]. Whatever you do, do not use a save state, as Hardcore Mode disables them.

### Boss: King Dodongo

Take your Slingshot, a Deku Stick and, if you have them, spare Deku Sticks. The fight has two phases. In phase one he lumbers around the room and breathes fire. In phase two he rolls into a ball and ricochets around the room. The cycle repeats [[gf1-82-4]].

**Standard method (all sources agree on the basics):** Wait for him to begin inhaling and throw a bomb into his open mouth. The bomb explodes in his stomach and stuns him for a few seconds. You get one sword swing, so make it a Jump Attack [[gf1-82-4, gf2-28-4]]. After the stun he rolls. Stand on the edge of the lava or in a corner to avoid the roll [[gf1-82-5]]. Three Jump Attacks, or six normal sword attacks, kill him [[gf1-82-6]]. One guide says four normal hits [[gf2-119-4]]. Another says faster results come from Deku Stick jump strikes and shield stabs once he is stunned [[gf3-25-3]].

**Safe spot:** A little 45-degree slope between the path and the central lava makes you immune to all his attacks while you stand on it [[gf2-28-3, gf2-119-1]]. The roll can also be dodged by standing on that slope [[gf2-119-3]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Bombalicious (48277) is missable. You must kill King Dodongo **before he breathes fire at all**, using only Bomb Flowers from the arena, not bombs from your Bomb Bag [[ra-guide-7-2, ra-comments-14-1, ra-comments-14-2, ra-comments-14-22]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Bombalicious (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158122.png)
> *Defeat King Dodongo before he breathes fire without using Bombs from your bag*


**Bombalicious plan.** The RetroAchievements guide says: as soon as the fight starts, turn around, grab a Bomb Flower and run toward the boss to time your throw. Jump slash with the Deku Stick, then repeat once more and he dies. Don't worry about taking hits. If he is about to breathe fire, shoot him with the Slingshot to cancel it [[ra-guide-7-2]].

Other players give this advice, and it does not all agree:

- Several say not to rush the Bomb Flower in the corner at the start [[ra-comments-14-1, ra-comments-14-11]]. Instead, run around him so he rolls, grab a Bomb Flower while he rolls, and get to him quickly to throw it into his mouth [[ra-comments-14-2, ra-comments-14-13]]. Running to Dodongo at the start triggers a roll instead of fire [[ra-comments-14-18]]. One player said running to the corner flower at the start did not work in time for them [[ra-comments-15-6]].
- Running behind him and chasing him also worked: throw the flower while he is charging, then jump slash with a Deku Stick [[ra-comments-15-5, ra-comments-15-7]].
- Throw the flower as the flame graphic appears. Dodongo must have fire in his mouth before the bomb goes in, or the achievement won't trigger [[ra-comments-14-5, ra-comments-14-20]]. Do not throw it as he is about to spew flames, because that does not work [[ra-comments-14-21]].
- Throw one flower, then jump slash with a Deku Stick for double damage. Repeat once and he is done [[ra-comments-14-19, ra-comments-14-23, ra-comments-14-3]].
- Shooting him with the Slingshot cancels the fire-breath animation and gives you time to get a Bomb Flower. Players called this the best method alongside patience [[ra-comments-14-17, ra-comments-15-2, ra-comments-15-3]].
- Staying out of his front makes fire breath less likely, and he stops at the second wall hit after rolling [[ra-comments-15-8]]. He rolls until he hits a corner [[ra-comments-14-7]].
- The challenge is tracked during the fight [[ra-comments-14-12]]. One commenter reports the achievement didn't trigger until their bomb bag was full [[ra-comments-14-9]]. Another says to have 20 bombs and just use Deku Sticks and jump strikes [[ra-comments-14-10]]. Others say a full bag and a C-button assignment are not needed under current logic [[ra-comments-14-15, ra-comments-14-16]], and one player did it without a full bag after dying to reset [[ra-comments-15-1]]. Whether a full bag matters is unconfirmed, so keep the bag topped off if you can. Picking up the flowers is the hardest part for some players [[ra-comments-15-4]].

When he dies, he rolls into the lava and the lava solidifies [[gf2-28-5, gf3-25-4]]. Grab the **Heart Container** from the middle of the room (Heart Container 2 of 8) [[gf1-82-7, gf2-145-2]]. Then enter the ring of light to warp outside, where Darunia gives you the Goron's Ruby and makes you a Blood Brother [[gf1-82-8, gf3-25-5]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Soul Brothers (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158123.png)
> *Receive the Spiritual Stone of Fire*


The Goron's Ruby is the second of the three Spiritual Stones [[gf1-421-2, ra-guide-7-3, gf3-160-2, gf2-158-10]].

### Optional: Skulltula #41 (after the boss)

Skulltula #41 is on the second floor near the staircase room. You don't need the stone to get it, but the guide says to take care of it after beating King Dodongo. Re-enter the dungeon and ride the fast-moving pillar up to the second floor. Make your way around to the staircase room, where the stairs are raised again. Climb up the left side of the staircase to reach the Skulltula [[checklist-skulltulas-8-4, gf3-37-12, gf4-14-8, checklist-sidequests-22-5]]. The Gold Skulltula sits in an alcove at the top of the vine on the left [[gf2-46-4, checklist-sidequests-23-1]]. This approach avoids setting off the Bomb Flower chain reaction a second time [[gf1-486-2]]. One guide reaches the alcove using the Boomerang, and the checklist says a vine climb is enough. Unable to verify from collected sources whether you will already own the Boomerang at this point.

On the way, a guide says to use the elevator in the main chamber: go to the middle platform in front of the giant skull, then use the moving platform on the right to reach the second floor and the tunnel opposite the plaque [[gf1-121-4]]. Dropping to the bottom of the Blade Trap Room, you can climb the southwest ladder and tunnel back to the Antechamber [[gf1-122-1, gf1-122-2, gf1-122-3]].

### Skulltula #42 (later)

Skulltula #42 needs adult Link with the Hookshot and the Scarecrow's Song. Do not attempt it now [[checklist-skulltulas-8-5]]. In the Baby Dodongo hall, walk in until Navi flutters up toward the ceiling, play the Scarecrow's Song, Hookshot to Pierre, and kill the Skulltula in the alcove [[gf1-487-1, gf3-42-4, gf1-170-2, checklist-sidequests-22-4, gf4-17-16]]. This is revisited in chapter 21.

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave Dodongo's Cavern:** Make sure you have the Map, the Compass, the Bomb Bag and four Gold Skulltulas (#38 to #41). A commenter notes that the Map and Compass are two achievement items here and that one player missed items by not revisiting as adult [[ra-comments-123-12, ra-comments-121-4]]. Skulltula #42 waits for later [[checklist-skulltulas-8-5]]. A commenter places the Delicious Stone at the back right of the main room [[ra-comments-81-13]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 5 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 22 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 7 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 2 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 4 / 26
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 20 / 97 (110 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Opened the entrance with a Bomb Flower
- [ ] Skulltula #38 (Torch Room behind the sealed wall)
- [ ] Dungeon Map
- [ ] Compass
- [ ] Skulltula #39 (vine wall by the staircase room exit)
- [ ] Red Rupee (Blade Trap Room)
- [ ] Bomb Bag
- [ ] Deku Shield or Blue Rupee (catwalk chest)
- [ ] Skulltula #40 (Armos Knight room)
- [ ] Bombs (chest above the pit)
- [ ] Heart Container
- [ ] Bombalicious
- [ ] Soul Brothers
- [ ] Skulltula #41 (optional return trip, left side of the raised staircase)

## Chapter 6: Death Mountain Summit, Goron City and Castle Town with Bombs

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Lost_Woods["Lost Woods"]
  Hyrule_Market["Hyrule Market"]
  Hyrule_Castle["Hyrule Castle"]
  Kakariko_Village["Kakariko Village"]
  Death_Mountain_Trail["Death Mountain Trail"]
  Goron_City["Goron City"]
  Death_Mountain_Crater["Death Mountain Crater"]
  King_Dodongo{{"⚔ King Dodongo"}}
  Phantom_Ganon{{"⚔ Phantom Ganon"}}
  Volvagia{{"⚔ Volvagia"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Dead_Hand{{"⚔ Dead Hand"}}
  Flare_Dancer{{"⚔ Flare Dancer"}}
  Hyrule_Market -->|"Castle path"| Hyrule_Castle
  Kakariko_Village -->|"Show Zelda's Letter to the gate guard"| Death_Mountain_Trail

  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
```

With Bombs in your bag, this chapter goes back through the places that were closed to you. You bomb the summit wall of Death Mountain Trail for the Great Fairy, ride the owl down to Kakariko, work through Goron City, play Bombchu Bowling in the Market, learn Din's Fire at Hyrule Castle, and finish the Happy Mask chain and the Lost Woods upgrades [[gf1-415-3, gf1-88-1, gf1-85-3, gf1-91-2, gf1-90-2, gf1-65-1, gf1-418-2]]. Two achievements here are tied to deadlines, so read the warnings before you act in Goron City and before you touch the Poacher's Saw sidequest [[ra-guide-8-2, ra-comments-73-3]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you start:** Bring plenty of rupees, because Bombchu Bowling costs 30 rupees per game and the Lost Woods upgrades cost 40 rupees each [[gf1-91-1, gf1-23-4, gf1-418-2]]. Smashing the pots in the room at the entrance of the Market is a source of rupees [[gf4-3-2]]. Bring Bottled Bugs for the soft-soil Gold Skulltulas, and a Deku Stick for the Goron City torches [[gf1-66-4, gf1-63-4]].

### Chapter Items to Collect (9 Entries)

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Spin Attack (Super Spin) and Magic Meter | Great Fairy Fountain at the summit of Death Mountain Trail | Bomb the wall, play Zelda's Lullaby on the Triforce emblem | [[gf1-87-1]] |
| Piece of Heart | Cow house in Kakariko, via the owl ride | Drop from the roof, enter the cracked wall | [[gf1-88-2, gf2-31-3]] |
| Bomb Bag x30 | Goron City, third floor tunnel | Bomb the rolling Goron inside the northern tunnel | [[gf1-85-3, ra-guide-8-3]] |
| Piece of Heart | Goron City spinning pot | Light the torches, bomb the pot, stop on the happy face | [[gf1-437-1]] |
| Piece of Heart, Bomb Bag x40, Bombchus | Bombchu Bowling Alley, Hyrule Market | Win the prizes the clerk shows you | [[gf1-91-2, gf1-91-3]] |
| Din's Fire | Great Fairy Fountain behind the boulder near Hyrule Castle | Bomb the boulder, play Zelda's Lullaby | [[gf1-90-1, gf1-90-2]] |
| Deku Stick upgrade to 30 | Forest Stage, Lost Woods | Wear the Skull Mask | [[gf1-65-1]] |
| Deku Nut upgrade to 30 | Grotto under the rock near Sacred Forest Meadow | Bomb the rock, pay 40 rupees | [[gf1-418-2]] |
| Stone of Agony | House of Skulltula, Kakariko | 20 Gold Skulltula tokens | [[gf1-420-3]] |

### Death Mountain Trail

Enter Death Mountain Trail by the northern gate of Kakariko Village. The guard lets you through when you show him Zelda's Letter [[gf1-58-2]]. Opening that gate also makes the Happy Mask Salesman sidequest available at the Happy Mask Shop in the Market [[gf1-349-1]].

#### Lower trail

On the right side after the first corner, an oddly colored wall hides a Gold Skulltula. Blast it with a Bomb, shoot the Skulltula inside with the Slingshot, then climb the wall to take the token (Gold Skulltula #31) [[gf1-84-1, gf1-465-1]].

Past the red flag on the way to Goron City, another odd wall holds a chest with a 50 rupee piece. Open it only if you need rupees [[gf1-84-2]].

### Goron City

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Do NOT destroy the three boulders at the entrance of the top-floor boulder room before you claim **Walking on a Tightrope**. Their flags are permanent and they do not respawn. You may jump to the side of the rocks instead [[ra-guide-8-2, ra-comments-68-1, ra-comments-68-2]].

#### Top floor boulder room

Walk onto the tightrope at the top of Goron City and make your way around to the room full of rocks. Keep the three boulders on the left of the entrance intact, cross the tightrope to the left, enter the boulder room and open a treasure chest there [[ra-comments-68-3, ra-guide-8-2]]. Rocks inside the chest room may be destroyed freely [[ra-comments-68-4]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Walking on a Tightrope (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158161.png)
> *Open any chest in Goron City's boulder room without destroying the entrance blockade*


Work through the maze with bombs. From the entrance go left to the barrier of boulders and blow a path through [[gf1-468-1]]. Follow the route: destroy the brown rock to your right on entering, then the one behind it, go through the gap, destroy the brown rock on the left, take the new gap, go right and destroy the rock directly ahead [[gf1-468-2]]. The guide's shorthand for the route is right, left, left, right [[checklist-sidequests-14-3]].

At the end, two chests each hold a Purple Rupee [[gf1-85-1, gf2-29-3]]. Roll into the crate in the corner to reveal a Gold Skulltula, then kill it and take the token (Gold Skulltula #34) [[gf1-468-3, checklist-skulltulas-7-5]].

#### Rolling Goron (Bomb Bag upgrade)

Drop to the third floor, the level directly below the top floor. A large Goron rolls around in a loop. Place a bomb so that it explodes as he passes over it inside the northern tunnel, then talk to him [[gf1-85-3]]. The bomb must go off while he is inside the tunnel. Bombing him outside the tunnel did not work for one player [[ra-guide-8-3, ra-comments-67-1, ra-comments-67-2]]. It can take a few tries, so carry spare bombs [[checklist-sidequests-14-4, ra-comments-67-3]]. This upgrades you to the 30-bomb bag [[gf2-29-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Roll Roll and Away (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158160.png)
> *Obtain the Bomb Bag upgrade in Goron City*


#### Spinning pot (Piece of Heart)

Go to Darunia's room on the bottom floor. Stand on the carpet in front of the sealed door and play Zelda's Lullaby to open it [[gf1-63-1]]. Light a Deku Stick on the torch in Darunia's room, then light every brazier on the bottom level of the city; when they are all lit the giant pot starts to spin [[checklist-heartpieces-3-10, gf1-63-4]].

From the second level, throw a bomb into the pot. If it stops with the happy face forward, a Piece of Heart appears [[gf1-437-1, gf1-85-4]]. This is Piece of Heart #10.

If you have not yet met Darunia, play Saria's Song in front of him for the Goron's Bracelet [[gf1-63-2, gf1-63-3]].

### Death Mountain Crater (child visit)

🗺️ **Map:** [View Path to Zelda Map by TLS64](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/55-path-to-zelda-map)

Head back to the trail and climb the steeper path beside the red flag. The last boulder at the top of the ledges hides a pit with a cow, which refills milk when you play Epona's Song [[gf1-86-1, ra-comments-60-4, ra-comments-60-7]]. When the volcano erupts, hold R under the Hylian Shield when you see a shadow below you [[gf1-86-2]].

Climb to the summit. Kaepora Gaebora waits there. Do not speak to him yet [[gf1-86-3]].

#### Great Fairy Fountain at the summit

Bomb the oddly colored wall to the left of the owl and enter the cave [[gf1-86-4, gf1-415-3]]. Stand on the Triforce emblem and play Zelda's Lullaby. The Great Fairy teaches the Super Spin Attack and grants a Magic Meter [[gf1-87-1, gf2-30-4]]. Hold B until the sword glows, then release [[gf2-154-10]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **You Spin Me Round (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158162.png)
> *Learn the Spin Attack technique*


#### Crater crate (Gold Skulltula)

Enter the cave to the right of the owl [[gf1-87-2]]. Immediately inside, roll into the crate on the left to reveal a Gold Skulltula (#36). Kill it, take the token and leave, because the heat kills child Link if time runs out [[gf1-87-3, checklist-skulltulas-7-7, gf1-435-2]].

### Kakariko Village

Now speak to Kaepora Gaebora. He carries you down to the roof of Impa's House [[gf1-88-1]].

#### Cow house Piece of Heart

Walk slowly off the edge of the roof and drop onto the small roof cover of the chicken coop. A secret entrance leads into the house, where the Piece of Heart is next to the cow [[gf2-31-3, checklist-heartpieces-3-8]]. If you miss the drop, play the Sun's Song to warp back to the roof [[gf1-88-3]]. This is Piece of Heart #8 in the roadmap order [[gf3-27-1]].

#### House of Skulltula

With 20 tokens, enter the House of Skulltula and talk to the freed man on the left for the Stone of Agony [[gf1-89-1, gf1-420-3]]. Your count at this point is the checkpoint total below.


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Secrets Detector (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158195.png)
> *Collect 20 Gold Skulltulas and obtain your reward*


### Hyrule Castle

🗺️ **Map:** [View Hyrule Palace Map by ThE_bLImP](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/52-hyrule-palace-map)

Din's Fire is found at the Hyrule Castle Garden, reached from the Market [[gf1-384-2]].

#### Din's Fire

Climb the ivy on the right to the cliff above the gate. Drop onto the other side of the marble gate and walk to the dead end marked with a sign. Bomb the boulder, crawl through the tunnel and stand on the Triforce emblem in the Great Fairy Fountain. Play Zelda's Lullaby to receive Din's Fire [[gf1-90-1, gf1-90-2]]. Din's Fire is later used to light many torches at once [[gf1-90-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **On Fire (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158165.png)
> *Learn Din's Fire*


Let a guard spot you and throw you out the gate, then return to the Market [[gf1-90-3]].

### Hyrule Market

#### Bombchu Bowling

Enter the Bombchu Bowling Alley in the Market; each game costs 30 rupees [[gf1-91-1]]. The clerk shows a prize first; completing all three goals wins that prize [[gf1-91-3]]. The prizes include Bombs, a Purple Rupee, Bombchus, a Piece of Heart, and the Bomb Bag upgrade [[gf2-105-2, gf1-410-1]].

Tips for the three goals:

- Stand in the exact middle of the field, slightly to the right of the center arrow, and time the shot to avoid the Blade Trap [[gf1-410-3]].
- The second target can appear at the top, left or right, and a small Cucco must be dodged. If it is on the right, adjust your position slightly [[gf1-410-4, gf1-91-5]].
- If the hole is on the left, angle the Bombchu along the floor and then the wall [[gf1-410-6]].
- The third target is in the center with a Fat Cucco, and you can sneak a Bombchu past it [[gf1-410-5]]. Another guide says to stand on the third black mark left of the middle arrow and aim straight ahead, so a ledge turns the Bombchu toward the hole [[gf3-28-3]].

Collecting any Bombchus completes the Bombchus achievement.


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Mouse Ka-Boom (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158164.png)
> *Obtain some Bombchus*


Keep playing until the clerk offers the Bomb Bag as the prize and win it [[gf1-410-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Bombchu Bowling Star (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158163.png)
> *Obtain the Bomb Bag upgrade at the Bombchu Bowling Alley*


Also win the Piece of Heart prize (#9) [[gf1-427-2, gf4-11-5]]. The Bomb Bag achievement has two requirements, the screen ID and the upgrade flag, so collect it inside the alley [[ra-comments-70-4]].

### Lost Woods

🗺️ **Map:** [View Lost Woods Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/54-lost-woods-map)

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Buy the Deku Nut upgrade for **Stocking Nuts** before you obtain the adult Poacher's Saw (chapter 13) [[ra-guide-8-7, ra-comments-73-3]]. Whether the Lost Woods Deku Nut upgrades can still be earned as an adult before the Poacher's Saw is unconfirmed, since some players report it worked after the Forest Temple and others report the trade flag locks it [[ra-comments-44-9, ra-comments-44-3]]. Get A Pretty Face, the Mask of Truth upgrade and Stocking Nuts as child Link before you draw the Master Sword [[gf1-525-1, ra-guide-10-2]].

#### Happy Mask chain

Show Zelda's Letter to the Kakariko gate guard and he asks for a Keaton Mask [[gf1-58-3]]. Borrow the Keaton Mask from the Happy Mask Shop near the Temple of Time, and sell it to the guard for 15 rupees [[gf1-59-1, gf1-59-2, gf1-59-3]]. Pay the 10-rupee fee at the shop, then borrow the Skull Mask [[gf1-60-1]].

#### Forest Stage

From the Lost Woods entrance, go right, left, right, left, left to reach a grassy screen with a large tree [[checklist-sidequests-13-23, gf3-237-4]]. Drop into the invisible pit marked by butterflies in the tall grass [[gf1-64-3]]. Put on the Skull Mask and step forward. Walk to the right side and speak to the Deku Scrub Chief to raise your Deku Stick capacity to 30 [[gf1-65-1, gf2-47-3]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **A Pretty Face (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158170.png)
> *Obtain the Deku Sticks upgrade from the Deku Scrub Brothers in the Lost Woods*


#### Selling the Skull Mask

Return to the lone Skull Kid. He will only buy the mask if you have already played Saria's Song for him and earned the Piece of Heart [[gf1-527-3]]. Wear the Skull Mask and speak to him to sell it [[gf1-65-3, gf2-47-4]].

#### Deku Nut upgrade

Go to the screen below Sacred Forest Meadow. Bomb the boulder near the entrance and drop into the hole. Two Business Scrubs are inside; defeat them with the Slingshot, and the one on the left sells the Deku Nut upgrade for 40 rupees [[gf1-86-5, gf2-33-3, ra-guide-8-7]]. This is the achievement item, not the Mask of Truth one [[ra-comments-73-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Stocking Nuts (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/511029.png)
> *Purchase a Deku Nuts upgrade in the Lost Woods*


Network issues while you get the Deku Nut and save can stop this achievement from triggering [[ra-comments-73-5]].

### Before you leave

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave:** Confirm the Spin Attack, Magic Meter and Din's Fire are learned, both Bomb Bag upgrades are in your bag, and the Lost Woods upgrades are bought before you draw the Master Sword [[gf1-87-1, gf1-90-2, gf1-85-3, gf1-91-3, gf1-525-1]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 6 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 25 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 10 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 2 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 4 / 26
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 29 / 97 (155 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Spin Attack learned (You Spin Me Round)
- [ ] Walking on a Tightrope chest opened without destroying the entrance rocks
- [ ] Bomb Bag x30 from the rolling Goron (Roll Roll and Away)
- [ ] Goron City spinning pot Piece of Heart
- [ ] Cow house Piece of Heart in Kakariko
- [ ] Bombchus, Bomb Bag x40 and Piece of Heart from Bombchu Bowling
- [ ] Din's Fire learned
- [ ] Deku Stick upgrade via the Skull Mask (A Pretty Face)
- [ ] Deku Nut upgrade bought under the boulder (Stocking Nuts)
- [ ] Gold Skulltulas #31, #34 and #36 collected
- [ ] Stone of Agony claimed at 20 tokens (Secrets Detector)

## Chapter 7: Zora's River and Zora's Domain

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Zoras_River["Zora's River"]
  Zoras_Domain["Zora's Domain"]
  Barinade{{"⚔ Barinade"}}
  Morpha{{"⚔ Morpha"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Dark_Link{{"⚔ Shadow Link (Dark Link)"}}
  Dying_Guard_Lockout>"⛔ Dying guard disappears after the Temple of Time"]
  Zoras_River -->|"Zelda's Lullaby on the waterfall"| Zoras_Domain
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Dying_Guard_Lockout ponr;
  class Barinade,Morpha,Bongo_Bongo,Dark_Link boss;
```

This chapter takes young Link up Zora's River and into Zora's Domain. You buy Magic Beans, collect two Pieces of Heart with the Cuccos, win two more from the Musical Frogs, light the Zora's Domain torches for a fifth, and win the Silver Scale in the Diving Game [[gf1-95-4, gf1-174-4, gf1-97-2, gf1-441-1]]. Two Gold Skulltulas fall to you here: the tree at the entrance and the ladder by the waterfall at night [[gf1-470-1, gf1-470-2]].

> [!TIP]
> **Preparation:** Bring Bombs, the Slingshot, Deku Sticks, Din's Fire if you have it, and the Ocarina with Zelda's Lullaby, Epona's Song, the Sun's Song, Saria's Song, the Song of Time and the Song of Storms [[gf1-95-3, gf1-470-2, gf1-97-1, gf1-174-2]]. Rupees matter too: the Diving Game costs rupees to enter, and every Magic Bean costs 10 more than the last [[gf2-36-1, gf1-385-2]].

### Zora's River

🗺️ **Map:** [View Zora's River/Domain Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/56-zoras-river-domain-map)

#### Entrance: the tree and the boulders

1. Before bombing the rocks, talk to the owl, then roll into the tree to the left of the stone wall near the entrance. A Gold Skulltula falls out and lands near you [[checklist-sidequests-10-9, gf1-470-1]].
2. Kill it and take its token [[gf1-95-2]].
3. Bomb the stones blocking the path upstream [[gf1-95-3]].

#### The Bean Man

Follow the path to the man sitting near the iron gate. He is the Magic Bean salesman [[gf1-95-4, gf2-159-8]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Magic Beans (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158167.png)
> *Obtain a Magic Bean*


Buy a Magic Bean from him [[ra-guide-8-8, gf2-34-2]]. He has ten beans in total. The first costs 10 rupees and each next one costs 10 more, up to 100 for the tenth. All ten cost 550 rupees [[gf1-385-2, gf2-34-3]]. Keep buying until you run out of money, and come back later for the rest [[gf1-95-4]].

Plant a bean in the patch of soft earth next to him [[gf1-95-5, gf3-173-3]]. Beans must be planted as young Link, and they only grow fully once you are an adult [[gf3-173-2, gf1-385-1]]. This patch holds no Gold Skulltula, so there is no need to drop a bug in it [[gf1-527-2]].

> [!TIP]
> You can still collect Gold Skulltulas from soft-soil holes after planting beans in them [[checklist-sidequests-11-1]].

#### Rock circle, Cucco and secret caves

Near the entrance, in the corner where a Cucco wanders, is a circle of rocks. Play the Song of Storms near it to reveal a Secret Cave with two Business Scrubs, one selling a Green Potion and one a Red Potion, both for 40 rupees [[gf3-41-8, gf3-238-11]]. You will use this Cucco for the Pieces of Heart below [[gf4-12-1]].

On the strip of land above, climbing the ladder leads to a Secret Cave with a small chest holding a Red Rupee. A large boulder surrounded by small rocks stands nearby. Bomb it to reveal a Fairy Fountain [[gf3-29-6, gf2-34-4, gf2-34-5, gf3-29-7]].

#### Musical Frogs: rupees

Walk out on the log upstream and stand on the white circle at its end. Take out the Ocarina [[gf1-95-6, gf4-21-9]]. Play Zelda's Lullaby, Epona's Song, the Sun's Song and Saria's Song. One guide says the frogs pay 50 rupees per song [[gf1-95-6]]. Another says each song earns a Purple Rupee [[gf2-35-3, gf3-30-4]]. Unable to verify from collected sources which payout is correct. The Song of Time also pays out [[gf1-124-3, gf2-78-5]].

#### Heart Piece: the pillar halfway up the river

1. Grab the Cucco near the circle of stones, hop across to the ledge on the right, go forward and to the left, and jump up around the ledges [[gf4-12-1]].
2. Use the Cucco to float to the ledge just before the Heart Piece. Drop the Cucco and jump to the platform with the Heart Piece [[gf4-12-1]].
3. Another route is to climb the ladder, grab another Cucco at the top, and glide to the tall pillar from the cliff with the Gossip Stone [[gf3-128-2, gf1-95-7]]. The Boomerang as Young Link or the Hover Boots as Adult Link also work [[gf1-438-1]].

#### Heart Piece: the lone platform and the high ledge

- Near the entrance to Zora's Domain, go to the high ledge, turn around and look back downstream. A platform holds a Piece of Heart. Glide out to it with a Cucco [[gf1-440-1, checklist-sidequests-11-2]].
- Another Piece of Heart sits on a lone ledge sticking out of the water, near the Gossip Stone platform. Glide to it with a Cucco [[checklist-sidequests-11-3]].
- Another way is to drop to the platform below and grab a Cucco, then make your way to the end of the river. Float down to the ledge below with the Heart Piece [[gf4-12-2]].
- You can also carry a Cucco to the waterfall at the end, turn around, climb the land bridge nearest the heart, and jump to it [[checklist-heartpieces-4-2]].

Behind the waterfall a ladder leads from the water to a hidden alcove with another Piece of Heart [[gf3-38-1]]. One guide says you can reach it with the Cucco [[gf3-38-1]].

#### Gold Skulltula on the waterfall ladder (night)

If it is daytime, play the Sun's Song. Time passes normally here, so you will not warp back to the entrance [[gf1-96-2]]. Drop down near the waterfall to the ladder rising from the river bed. Shoot the Gold Skulltula on it with the Slingshot, then climb the ladder for the token [[gf1-96-2, gf4-12-3, gf3-30-3]].

#### Musical Frogs: Song of Storms and the minigame

Return to the log as young Link. Play the Song of Storms to receive a Piece of Heart [[gf1-174-1, gf2-146-29, gf2-78-4]]. Once you have played all the basic songs for them, the frogs challenge you to a fly-eating minigame [[gf1-174-2]].

The lowest frog is matched with the A Button and the other four match the C-Pad directions [[gf1-174-3]]. From left to right, the frogs are A, Left, Down, Up, Right [[gf2-112-3]]. Enter this sequence quickly [[gf1-174-4, ra-comments-94-6]]:

A, C-Left, C-Right, C-Down, C-Left, C-Right, C-Down, A, C-Down, A, C-Down, C-Right, C-Left, A


Setting the C-Buttons to the D-pad is easier than using the analog stick. Controller drift can prevent you from hitting some notes [[ra-comments-94-5, ra-comments-94-4]]. You now hold both frog Pieces of Heart [[gf1-246-1]].

#### Entering Zora's Domain

Go around the zigzagging path and stand on the plaque before the waterfall. Play Zelda's Lullaby and the waterfall retracts. Jump to the doorway and go through [[gf1-96-3, gf3-30-5]].

Gold Skulltulas that need the Hookshot appear in this river in the future timeline. Unable to verify from collected sources whether any belong to your current route.

### Zora's Domain

🗺️ **Map:** [View Zora's River/Domain Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/56-zoras-river-domain-map)

#### Heart Piece: the torches

Light a Deku Stick on the lit torch near the shop. Use Deku Sticks and run quickly, because the torches do not stay lit for long [[checklist-heartpieces-4-3, gf3-30-7]]. One guide gives this order [[checklist-heartpieces-6-2]]:

1. Light the Deku Stick.
2. Light the torch near the circle of stones.
3. Run back and light the torch near the shop.
4. Light the torch near the circle of stones again.
5. Light the torches in the waterfall.

Another guide says the stick can be kept lit and rolled under the waterfall to light the last two torches [[checklist-sidequests-11-5]]. Din's Fire also lights the two in the alcove behind the waterfall [[gf1-97-1]]. When every torch is lit at once, a chest appears behind the waterfall with a Piece of Heart [[gf1-441-1, gf2-36-3]].

#### Diving Game and the Silver Scale

Go through the tunnel left of King Zora's throne room to reach the Zora running the Diving Game [[gf1-412-1, ra-guide-8-9]]. The game costs 20 rupees, though one guide says 30 [[gf2-36-1, checklist-sidequests-11-6]]. He throws rupees off the waterfall. Position yourself above them and hold A to dive [[gf1-514-1]]. Collect all five Blue Rupees, then return to the Zora [[gf2-36-1, gf1-514-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **The Way the World Learns to Dive (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158561.png)
> *Obtain a reward from the Zora's Diving minigame*


He gives you the Silver Scale, which doubles your dive depth [[gf1-97-2, gf2-36-2]].

#### Other things in the Domain

- Fish swimming in the shallow water near the waterfall can be caught in an empty Bottle [[gf1-102-3]].
- Playing the Song of Storms on the flat unsubmerged rock reveals a Fairy Fountain [[gf3-238-13, gf3-52-7]].
- The dirt hole here is the only one without a Skulltula [[checklist-skulltulas-9-1]].
- Diving into the tunnel south of the waterfall warps to Lake Hylia [[gf1-97-3]].
- Swimming to the bottom of the deep pool to the right of the waterfall at the entrance leads to Lost Woods [[gf1-124-1]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave Zora's Domain:** make sure you have the Magic Bean, both Cucco Hearts, both frog Hearts, the torch Heart and the Silver Scale [[gf1-246-1, gf1-97-2, gf1-441-1]]. You can leave by climbing to the path to the front entrance, returning to Zora's River and riding it downstream to Hyrule Field [[gf1-119-3]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 7 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 27 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 13 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 2 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 4 / 26
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 31 / 97 (165 / 690 points)

### Checklist

- [ ] Entrance tree Gold Skulltula
- [ ] Boulders bombed, Magic Bean bought and one planted
- [ ] Waterfall ladder Gold Skulltula (night)
- [ ] Both Cucco Pieces of Heart
- [ ] Song of Storms and minigame Pieces of Heart from the frogs
- [ ] Zora's Domain torch Piece of Heart
- [ ] Silver Scale from the Diving Game
