## Chapter 8: Lake Hylia, the Fishing Pond and Gerudo Valley

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Hyrule_Field["Hyrule Field"]
  Lake_Hylia["Lake Hylia"]
  Fishing_Pond["Fishing Pond"]
  Gerudo_Valley["Gerudo Valley"]
  King_Dodongo{{"⚔ King Dodongo"}}
  Barinade{{"⚔ Barinade"}}
  Morpha{{"⚔ Morpha"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Twinrova{{"⚔ Twinrova"}}
  Ganondorf{{"⚔ Ganondorf"}}
  Ganon{{"⚔ Ganon"}}
  Dark_Link{{"⚔ Shadow Link (Dark Link)"}}
  Dead_Hand{{"⚔ Dead Hand"}}
  Dying_Guard_Lockout>"⛔ Dying guard disappears after the Temple of Time"]
  Final_Battle>"⛔ Ganon's Tower final battle"]
  Lake_Hylia --> Fishing_Pond
  Hyrule_Field --> Gerudo_Valley
  Ganondorf --> Final_Battle
  Final_Battle --> Ganon
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Dying_Guard_Lockout,Final_Battle ponr;
  class King_Dodongo,Barinade,Morpha,Bongo_Bongo,Twinrova,Ganondorf,Ganon,Dark_Link,Dead_Hand boss;
```

This chapter starts at the bottom of the underwater tunnel from Zora's Domain. You pick up the third Bottle (it already holds Ruto's Letter) and then work through Lake Hylia, its Fishing Pond, Gerudo Valley and the fenced grotto in Hyrule Field as Young Link. The prize is four Pieces of Heart (#13, #11, #14 and #12), three Gold Skulltula tokens (#51, #52 and #60), the Scarecrow's song, a planted Magic Bean, and the Fishing Lessons achievement [[gf1-98-1, checklist-heartpieces-3-13, checklist-heartpieces-3-11, checklist-heartpieces-3-14, checklist-heartpieces-3-12, checklist-skulltulas-10-1, checklist-skulltulas-10-2, checklist-skulltulas-12-1]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you start:** Bring the following. You also need to be able to switch between day and night with the Sun's Song, and be a Young Link who can dive to the lake floor [[gf2-37-5, gf1-383-6]].
> - Bombs, for the Hyrule Field grotto [[gf1-426-1]].
> - At least 20 rupees for the fishing fee, and 10 more for the Business Scrub [[gf1-444-1, gf1-100-2]].
> - An empty Bottle you can fill with Bugs. The Ruto's Letter Bottle cannot be used until the letter is delivered [[gf3-152-2]].
> - One or more Magic Beans from the Bean Man in Zora's River [[gf1-385-1]].
> - The Silver Scale, to reach the lake floor [[gf1-383-6]].

Two mistakes cost time here, and both are recoverable. A fish that breaks free is gone, but leaving and re-entering the pond respawns the big fish [[gf1-413-2]]. A lost Cucco in Gerudo Valley is replaced by riding the river to Lake Hylia and walking back [[gf1-119-2]].

### Chapter Items to Collect (8 Items Total)

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Bottle (Ruto's Letter) | Lake Hylia floor, south of the tunnel exit | **TAKE** (dive with the Silver Scale) | [[gf1-98-1, gf1-383-6]] |
| Piece of Heart #13 | Fishing Pond | Catch a 10-pound or bigger fish as Young Link and show it to the owner | [[checklist-heartpieces-3-13, gf2-37-3]] |
| Gold Skulltula #51 | Island with two pillars, Lake Hylia | **KILL** at night | [[checklist-skulltulas-10-1, gf1-98-4]] |
| Gold Skulltula #52 | Soft-earth patch beside the Lakeside Laboratory | Release Bugs from a Bottle, then **KILL** | [[checklist-skulltulas-10-2, gf1-98-3]] |
| Piece of Heart #14 | Crate on the far-side ledge, Gerudo Valley | Glide over with a Cucco, roll into the crate | [[checklist-heartpieces-3-14, gf1-101-1]] |
| Piece of Heart #11 | Behind the waterfall, Gerudo Valley | Glide through the waterfall with a Cucco | [[checklist-heartpieces-3-11, gf4-9-7]] |
| Gold Skulltula #60 | Soft earth at the bottom of Gerudo Valley | Release Bugs, then **KILL** | [[checklist-skulltulas-12-1, gf1-101-2]] |
| Piece of Heart #12 | Fenced grotto in Hyrule Field | Bomb the ground, defeat the Business Scrub, **BUY** for 10 rupees | [[checklist-heartpieces-3-12, gf1-100-2]] |

### Lake Hylia

#### The Bottle with Ruto's Letter

Surface from the tunnel out of Zora's Domain and swim a short way south. On the floor, near a ruined pillar, lies a Bottle. Dive down and take it [[gf1-98-1]]. Another guide says it is right in front of you when you arrive, so look around the lake floor in front of the tunnel exit [[gf2-37-1]]. Reaching it needs the Silver Scale, which is why the Zora's Domain diving game comes first [[gf3-152-2]].

The Bottle already contains Ruto's Letter, so you cannot use it as a Bottle yet. This is your third Bottle [[gf3-152-2, gf3-30-8]]. Do not hurry back to King Zora, because there is still plenty to do here [[gf1-98-1]]. Carry the bottled Letter with you for now.

#### Teach the Scarecrow your song

Swim north to the shore and head to the east coast. There are two scarecrows [[gf1-98-2]]. The sources disagree on which scarecrow to use. One calls it the "lower" garden, one says farthest from the water, and one says closest to shore [[gf1-98-2, checklist-sidequests-11-8, gf3-30-9]]. The scarecrow you want is the dancing one named Bonooru, in the patch between the lab and the Fishing Pond [[gf4-9-3, ra-guide-14-1]]. Stand in front of him with your Ocarina out and he asks for a song [[gf1-98-2]].

Compose an eight-note song. It must have at least two different notes and must not be any song you already know or will learn later [[gf1-98-2, gf1-398-1]]. Play it for him and **write the notes down on paper**. You must play the exact same song as an adult, and Pierre the Wanderer in chapter 16 needs it too [[gf1-98-2, gf1-398-2, ra-guide-14-1]].

> [!TIP]
> If you forget the song, Sheik at the Temple of Time can send you back to childhood to teach Pierre a new one, but it is far less work to write it down now [[gf1-165-2]].

#### Gold Skulltula #52 and the Magic Bean

The tall building is the Lakeside Laboratory, which is to the right of the entrance from Hyrule Field [[gf4-9-2]]. A patch of soft earth sits beside it [[gf1-98-3, gf3-174-6]]. Release a Bug from your Bottle near the patch (recapture one afterwards) and kill the Gold Skulltula that comes out. Take its token for **Gold Skulltula #52** (✅ token 1/3 this chapter) [[gf1-98-3, checklist-skulltulas-10-2]].

Then plant one of your Magic Beans in the same patch. As an adult you will ride this plant to the roof of the laboratory, where a Piece of Heart waits [[gf1-98-3, checklist-sidequests-12-4, checklist-heartpieces-4-8]].

Where to catch Bugs for this chapter: Unable to verify from collected sources.

#### Gold Skulltula #51 (night)

From the laboratory, go around to its west side and cross the long wooden bridge over the lake. The bridge leads to the large island with the ancient dead tree [[gf1-98-3]]. Look east from the tree to see the small island with two pillars [[gf1-98-4]].

Swim to that island. If it is daytime, play the Sun's Song to bring on night [[gf2-37-5]]. A Gold Skulltula appears on one of the pillars. Kill it and take its token for **Gold Skulltula #51** (✅ token 2/3 this chapter) [[gf1-98-4, gf2-37-5, checklist-skulltulas-10-1]].

#### Optional extras at the lake

- A grave on one of the Lake Hylia islands can be pushed to reveal a Secret Cave with three Business Scrubs selling general pick-up items [[gf3-238-14]].
- Which island holds the grave: Unable to verify from collected sources.
- The Lake Hylia gossip stones are at the very far edge of the water, next to the wall, and one commenter counts three of them [[ra-comments-80-3, ra-comments-80-11]].

### Fishing Pond

🗺️ **Map:** [View Fishing Pond Map by TLS64](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/51-fishing-pond-map) [[map-fishing-1-1]]

Look to the north for a small island and swim to it. Enter the door built into the wall of the island to reach the Fishing Pond [[gf1-98-5]]. Speak to the clerk and pay 20 rupees to fish [[gf1-444-1, gf2-37-2]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** Fish as **Young Link**. The achievement needs a fish of at least 10 pounds [[ra-comments-111-1, ra-comments-111-2]]. One guide says nine pounds earns the Piece of Heart [[gf1-99-1]], but the achievement asks for ten, so do not stop at nine. A caught Hylian Loach reportedly does not trigger this achievement [[ra-comments-111-3, ra-comments-111-4]]. Do not use the sinking lure, because one source says doing so disqualifies the catch [[checklist-heartpieces-6-4]].

**The controls.** Z-target a fish, press B to cast, and use B, A and the stick to wiggle the lure and attract fish. When a fish is on the line, hold A and pull back on the control stick to reel it in [[gf1-515-1]]. Once the fish bites, keep the reel pulling against the fish's direction, which usually means holding left or right all the way [[ra-guide-10-1]]. Alternating between holding back-left and back-right every few seconds while reeling also helps [[gf1-413-2]].

**Where the big fish is.** The 10-pound fish is the one that stands out from the others and sits in the middle of the pond [[ra-comments-111-5, ra-comments-111-6]]. Several sources agree on the area around the log:
- The biggest fish hang near the submerged log on the left and the reeds on the right [[gf1-515-2]].
- One guide says the big fish lurks between the log and three sticks nearby [[gf4-9-1]].
- Another says to stand near the lilypads at the rear, where the fish stays underwater by the big log. You may have to catch the small fish that swims with it first [[checklist-heartpieces-6-4]].
- You can also stand on the log on the left side after entering and look for the big fish from there [[ra-guide-10-1]].

If a fish breaks free it is gone for good. If the pond runs low on big fish, leave and re-enter and they respawn [[gf1-413-2, gf1-217-2]].

When you land a fish of 10 pounds or more, the achievement unlocks:


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Fishing Lessons (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158200.png)
> *Catch a fish that weighs at least 10 pounds as Young Link*


Now carry the fish to the owner at the counter. He gives you the Piece of Heart (**Piece of Heart #13**, ✅ 1/4 this chapter) [[gf2-37-3, gf1-444-2, gf3-31-1, checklist-sidequests-12-1]]. Leave the pond when you have it.

> [!TIP]
> The 15-pound fish that wins the Gold Scale is a task for adult Link later. Do not chase it now [[gf1-445-2, gf2-107-2]].

### Gerudo Valley

From Lake Hylia, take the north exit to Hyrule Field. Head northwest until you hit the road, then go west into the rocky canyon area. Keep going west until you reach the zone break to Gerudo Valley [[gf1-100-3, gf1-118-4]]. The fenced corral you pass on the way is covered in the Hyrule Field section below.

Run forward a short way and cross the log bridge. Grab the Cucco on the other side [[gf1-101-1]]. In this valley a Cucco is a glider, and all three pickups here depend on it. Hold on to it carefully, and toss it against a wall whenever you set it down [[gf1-101-1, gf2-38-1]].

> [!TIP]
> **If you lose the Cucco:** Jump into the river and ride it to Lake Hylia, then walk back through Hyrule Field to the valley for a fresh Cucco [[gf1-101-1, gf1-119-2]].

#### Piece of Heart #14 (crate on the far ledge)

Run to the edge of the canyon and look across to the opposite side. A small platform with a wooden crate sits on the far wall [[gf1-101-1]]. A second guide says to jump off, force the stick forward and left, and aim for the small cliff on the other side of the valley [[checklist-heartpieces-3-14]]. Glide down to it, then roll into the crate [[gf1-101-1, gf1-446-1]]. Take the Piece of Heart that was in the crate (**Piece of Heart #14**, ✅ 2/4 this chapter), which makes you drop the Cucco, then grab the Cucco again as fast as you can [[gf1-101-1, checklist-sidequests-13-15]].

#### Gold Skulltula #60 (soft earth at the bottom)

From the crate ledge, glide down to the long strip of land at the bottom of the canyon. It holds a Gerudo, a cow and a small patch of dirt [[checklist-skulltulas-12-1, gf3-195-3]]. Head to the southern edge of the strip, where the patch of soft earth sits near the cow [[gf1-101-2]].

Release Bugs near the patch. Three bugs come out, so recapture one. Kill the Gold Skulltula that appears and take its token for **Gold Skulltula #60** (✅ token 3/3 this chapter) [[gf1-101-2, checklist-skulltulas-12-1, checklist-sidequests-13-17]]. Some guides also plant a Magic Bean in this soil [[gf3-37-21]]. This chapter only needs the Lakeside Laboratory bean; the Gerudo Valley bean is planted in Chapter 10.

#### Piece of Heart #11 (behind the waterfall)

Make sure you hold the Cucco again. Run to the northern edge of the strip and look behind the waterfall, where you will see a ladder [[gf1-101-3]]. Jump out toward the ladder and do not drop the Cucco until you are right beside it. There is no current immediately behind the waterfall, so you can climb out if you land in the water [[gf1-101-3, gf1-446-2]]. Climb to the top of the ladder to find **Piece of Heart #11** (✅ 3/4 this chapter) [[gf1-101-3, checklist-sidequests-13-16, checklist-sidequests-2-19]].

You can also reach this heart from the bridge. Take the Cucco from before the bridge, jump off to the right and float through the giant waterfall into the tiny nook in the wall [[gf4-9-7, checklist-heartpieces-3-11]].

> [!NOTE]
> The area across the broken bridge, with the Carpenter's tent and more tokens, is an adult-Link job. You cross it later with Epona or the Longshot, so leave it for now [[gf1-278-1, gf1-278-3, checklist-skulltulas-12-3]].

Jump into the river and ride it downstream to return to Lake Hylia [[gf1-101-3, gf1-119-2]].

### Hyrule Field

🗺️ **Map:** [View Hyrule Field Map by thegamemaster8](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/5394-hyrule-field-map) [[map-hyrule-field-1-1]]

Leave Lake Hylia through the north exit onto Hyrule Field. Jump into the middle of the small square of fences, which is the fenced corral outside the lake [[checklist-sidequests-13-19, gf1-100-1, gf1-426-1]].

Stand in the exact center of the corral and drop a Bomb to reveal a pit [[gf1-100-1, gf1-426-1]]. If you have the Stone of Agony, it makes the spot easier to find [[gf1-426-1]]. One guide places the bomb in the upper-right quadrant instead, so try there if the center does nothing [[checklist-heartpieces-3-12]]. Drop into the hole and defeat the Business Scrub with your Boomerang, then speak to it [[gf2-44-8]]. The Scrub sells a Piece of Heart for just 10 rupees, so buy it. That is **Piece of Heart #12** (✅ 4/4 this chapter) [[gf1-100-2, checklist-heartpieces-3-12, checklist-sidequests-2-20]].

**Optional rupees nearby:**
- A hole out in the open near the fence square holds a small chest with a Blue Rupee [[gf3-237-11]].
- On the way to the lake, a rock in the trees west of the corral hides a small chest with a Red Rupee [[gf2-44-7]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave:** Check that you have the four Pieces of Heart (#13, #11, #14, #12) and the three tokens (#51, #52, #60). Also check that you still carry the Bottle with Ruto's Letter [[gf3-152-2]]. The route back is the submerged ruins near the shore, east of the laboratory and west of the scarecrows. Dive in and swim into the tunnel to Zora's Domain [[gf1-102-1]]. Other tokens in these areas, such as the back of the Lakeside Laboratory, need the Boomerang and are not counted in this chapter's total [[checklist-skulltulas-10-3, checklist-skulltulas-12-2]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 8 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 30 / 100 (this chapter: #51, #52, #60 [[checklist-skulltulas-10-1, checklist-skulltulas-10-2, checklist-skulltulas-12-1]])
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 17 / 36 (this chapter: #13, #11, #14, #12 [[checklist-heartpieces-3-13, checklist-heartpieces-3-11, checklist-heartpieces-3-14, checklist-heartpieces-3-12]])
> - **Heart Containers:** 2 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 4 / 26
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 32 / 97 (170 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Picked up the third Bottle (Ruto's Letter) from the floor of Lake Hylia
- [ ] Taught Bonooru an eight-note song and wrote the notes down
- [ ] Gold Skulltula #52 (Bugs, Lakeside Laboratory soil) and Magic Bean planted there
- [ ] Gold Skulltula #51 (night, island with two pillars)
- [ ] Fishing Lessons (48348): 10-pound or larger fish caught as Young Link
- [ ] Piece of Heart #13 collected from the Fishing Pond owner
- [ ] Piece of Heart #14 (crate on the far ledge, Gerudo Valley)
- [ ] Gold Skulltula #60 (Bugs, bottom of Gerudo Valley)
- [ ] Piece of Heart #11 (behind the waterfall, Gerudo Valley)
- [ ] Piece of Heart #12 (Business Scrub, fenced grotto in Hyrule Field)

---

## Chapter 9: Zora's Fountain and Inside Jabu-Jabu's Belly

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Zoras_Domain["Zora's Domain"]
  Zoras_Fountain["Zora's Fountain"]
  Inside_Jabu["Inside Jabu-Jabu's Belly"]
  Barinade{{"⚔ Barinade"}}
  Morpha{{"⚔ Morpha"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Dark_Link{{"⚔ Shadow Link (Dark Link)"}}
  Dying_Guard_Lockout>"⛔ Dying guard disappears after the Temple of Time"]
  Zoras_Domain -->|"King Zora moves aside"| Zoras_Fountain
  Zoras_Fountain -->|"Feed Jabu-Jabu a fish"| Inside_Jabu
  Inside_Jabu --> Barinade
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Dying_Guard_Lockout ponr;
  class Barinade,Morpha,Bongo_Bongo,Dark_Link boss;
```

This chapter takes Young Link from King Zora's throne room into Zora's Fountain, then through Jabu-Jabu's Belly to the third Spiritual Stone. You learn Farore's Wind, get the Boomerang, Map and Compass, collect four dungeon Gold Skulltulas and two Fountain ones, and fight Barinade. Barinade carries the chapter's only missable achievement, Rolling Thunder, which asks for a clean kill with no hits [[gf1-102-2, gf1-103-3, gf1-109-2, gf1-110-3, gf1-111-3, gf1-115-3, ra-comments-17-1]].

### Preparation

> [!TIP]
> **Before you start:** You need an empty Bottle, Bombs, the Slingshot with seeds, Deku Nuts, Deku Sticks, the Deku Shield and the Ocarina with Zelda's Lullaby. The Slingshot, Deku Nuts and Bombs are used throughout the dungeon, and the Deku Shield is used for crouch-stab and deflect tricks [[gf1-105-2, gf1-107-2, gf2-40-1, gf2-40-2, ra-comments-19-10]].

The Boomerang does not arrive until the middle of the dungeon. Until then, jellyfish can be handled with Deku Sticks or by throwing Princess Ruto at them [[checklist-sidequests-13-1]]. The Great Fairy gives Farore's Wind, and you will want it before the boss [[gf1-103-3, ra-comments-18-1]].

### Chapter Items to Collect

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Farore's Wind | Great Fairy's Fountain, Zora's Fountain | Bomb the wall, then play Zelda's Lullaby on the Triforce emblem | [[gf1-103-2, gf1-103-3]] |
| Gold Skulltula #48 | Tree on the southeast island, Zora's Fountain | Roll into the tree | [[checklist-skulltulas-9-7]] |
| Boomerang | Stinger Room chest, Jabu-Jabu | Kill all the Stingers | [[gf1-109-2]] |
| Dungeon Map | Orange Tentacle Room chest | Kill the tentacle with the Boomerang | [[gf1-110-3]] |
| Compass | Shabom Room chest | Kill all Shaboms in 40 seconds | [[gf1-111-3]] |
| Gold Skulltula #56 | Ivy wall in the Water Room | Slingshot it, raise the water, climb | [[checklist-skulltulas-11-1]] |
| Gold Skulltulas #57 and #58 | Wall after the former Green Tentacle pit | Boomerang | [[checklist-skulltulas-11-2, checklist-skulltulas-11-3]] |
| Gold Skulltula #59 | Vine wall in the last room before the boss | Slingshot or Boomerang | [[checklist-skulltulas-11-4, gf1-489-2]] |
| Heart Container | Barinade's room | Defeat Barinade | [[gf1-115-6]] |
| Zora's Sapphire | Outside, from Ruto | Exit through the blue ring and agree | [[gf1-115-7]] |
| Gold Skulltula #49 | Wall across from the log, Zora's Fountain (night) | Slingshot, then Boomerang | [[checklist-skulltulas-9-8]] |

### Zora's Domain

🗺️ **Map:** [View Zora's River/Domain Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/56-zoras-river-domain-map)

Make your way to King Zora's Throne Room and show him the Bottled Letter. He moves aside and begs Link to save his daughter from Jabu-Jabu's Belly [[gf1-102-2, checklist-sidequests-12-5]]. Using the Letter also leaves you with the Empty Bottle that held it [[gf2-38-4]].

If you did not already empty the torches' Piece of Heart chest earlier, it is available here for Young Link. Light a Deku Stick at the torch on the throne room stairs and relight at the shop torch. Then light the burned-out brazier by the cave mouth, the one in the shallow water by the stones, and the two behind the waterfall. If every torch is lit before any burns out, a chest holding a Piece of Heart appears behind the waterfall [[gf1-441-1, checklist-heartpieces-6-2, gf2-36-3]]. Sources also say the lit-torch run can be done with Din's Fire for the two waterfall torches [[gf1-97-1]].

Now catch a fish. Go back to the bottom level and wade into the shallow water near the waterfall. Use the empty Bottle on one of the swimming fish [[gf1-102-3]].

Climb back to the throne room and take the narrow path on the right side to the throne. Go through the tunnel behind the throne to reach Zora's Fountain [[gf1-102-3, gf1-102-4]].

### Zora's Fountain

Your first stop is the tree. Swim to the island in the very southeast of the Fountain and roll into the lone tree. A Gold Skulltula falls out. Kill it and take the token (Skulltula #48, Bombs required for the area) [[gf1-103-1, checklist-sidequests-12-6, checklist-skulltulas-9-7]].

Next, the Great Fairy. Drop a bomb between the two boulders against the wall, near the silver rock that cannot be bombed. The wall blows open and reveals a large cave [[gf1-103-2, gf3-31-4, ra-guide-8-10]]. Go through the cave to the Great Fairy's Fountain [[gf1-103-2]]. Stand on the Triforce emblem and play Zelda's Lullaby. The Great Fairy appears and teaches you Farore's Wind, which sets a checkpoint in a dungeon that you can warp back to [[gf1-103-3, gf2-39-2, gf2-154-6]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Take Me Away (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158169.png)
> *Learn Farore's Wind*


Leave the fountain. Climb up onto the altar on the west side in front of Jabu-Jabu's mouth. Stand directly in front of the mouth and use the bottled fish. Jabu-Jabu inhales you along with the fish [[gf1-104-1, checklist-sidequests-12-8, gf3-31-2]].

### Inside Jabu-Jabu's Belly

#### Entrance room

Walk forward a few feet and a pair of Octoroks appear. Kill them with the Slingshot, or deflect their shots back with the Deku Shield [[gf1-105-2, gf2-40-1]]. Kill the Shaboms with Deku Nuts [[gf1-105-2]]. Run to the north end and shoot the large yellow bump on the ceiling with the Slingshot, which unseals the door at the end of the hall [[gf1-105-3, gf2-40-2]].

#### Elevator Room (first pass)

The floating jellyfish here are Biri, and you cannot hurt them yet; touching them hurts you. If one gets close, stun it with a Deku Nut [[gf1-106-1, gf2-40-3]]. Run around the left side along the platform and take the door on the other side [[gf1-106-1]].

#### Pit Room (first pass)

Step in and you meet Princess Ruto. She runs away and falls through a pit. Follow her down. Talk to her when you land, and talk to her again; she agrees to come with you if you help her find her stone. From now on you carry Ruto [[gf1-106-2, gf1-106-3]]. Pick her up and leave through the east door, which is behind you as you land [[gf1-106-2]].

#### Water Room

Drop Ruto and kill the Shaboms in the hallway with Deku Nuts. Pick her up again and run to the hall's end [[gf1-107-1]]. Jump into the pit, but do not step on the switch in the middle of the room yet. If you do, the water rises and Ruto returns to the Pit Room [[gf1-107-1]]. Throw Ruto up onto the ledge on the north side, then kill the enemies in the pit with you [[gf1-107-1]].

Use Deku Nuts to make the submerged Stingers jump, then shoot them with the Slingshot [[gf1-107-2]]. Kill the Gold Skulltula on the west ivy wall with the Slingshot (Skulltula #56). Then step on the switch to raise the water and climb the ivy into the alcove for the token [[gf1-107-3, checklist-skulltulas-11-1, gf2-40-4]].

Smash the pots in the alcove for a Fairy and Slingshot seeds. Capture the Fairy if you have an empty bottle [[gf1-107-4]]. Jump back into the pit, step on the switch again, and get onto the northern ledge where you left Ruto [[gf1-107-4]]. Pick her up and run into the hall. Kill more Shaboms with Deku Nuts, then hit the ceiling switch with the Slingshot. You can also throw Ruto at it. The door at the end unlocks [[gf1-107-5, gf2-40-5]].

#### Elevator Room (second pass)

Wait for the elevator to come down and kill the Octorok if you have to. Ride up carrying Ruto [[gf1-108-1]]. A submerged tunnel in this room leads to a Business Scrub selling Deku Nuts [[gf1-108-2]]. At the top, take the door on the right back into the Pit Room [[gf1-108-1]].

#### Pit Room (second pass)

Cross straight to the opposite door, avoiding Biri and the floor pits. If you fall you must go back through the Water Room and Elevator Room. Note the pit covered by the giant green tentacle; you will drop down it later [[gf1-108-3]]. Watch for long creatures that pop out of the ground next. Charge a Spin Attack until it glows orange to kill them in one hit [[gf2-40-6]].

#### Crawling Hallway and Stinger Room

Run past enemies in the hall. Go right and step onto the yellow switch while carrying Ruto. Her weight lets the switch activate, and the door in front of it unlocks [[gf1-109-1, gf2-40-6]]. Enter the Stinger Room.

The room is full of Stingers. Use Deku Nuts or run in front of their paths to reveal them, then shoot them with the Slingshot. If any are underground, bombs kill them in one hit. When all four are dead, a chest appears holding the **Boomerang** [[gf1-109-2, gf2-41-1, gf3-33-2]]. The Boomerang is the dungeon item of Jabu-Jabu's Belly and lets you grab Skulltula tokens from range [[gf1-386-3, gf1-109-3]]. Pick up Ruto and go back.

#### Orange Tentacle Room (Dungeon Map)

> [!WARNING]
> **Map Warning:** Get the Dungeon Map now while Ruto is still with you. Commenters report the Map cannot be reached without her unless you use a glitch, and one reports being locked out of it after skipping it [[ra-comments-121-6, ra-comments-3-3, ra-comments-122-12, ra-comments-124-7]].

At the far end of the Crawling Hallway is a blue switch in front of a door. Jump onto it while carrying Ruto, then put her down on it with A before stepping off. The switch stays down [[gf1-110-1, gf2-41-2]]. Go through the door without her.

Z-target the long Orange Tentacle and hit the narrow segment near its base with the Boomerang. Move forward until it stretches and makes a swishing noise, then throw the Boomerang while backing away. You need four hits in total. Killing it removes the other orange tentacles in the dungeon [[gf1-110-2, gf2-41-3, gf3-33-3]]. A chest appears holding the **Dungeon Map** [[gf1-110-3, gf4-13-6]].

#### Shabom Room (Compass)

Back in the hall, Ruto waits on the other side of the door and scolds you. Pick her up. Go left from the blue switch through the now-unblocked door [[gf1-111-1, gf1-111-2]]. You have forty seconds to pop every Shabom. Deku Nuts and the Slingshot both work, and a sword also works. A chest appears with the **Compass** [[gf1-111-3, gf2-41-4]].

#### Blue Tentacle Room

Return to the Crawling Hallway. Go down the hall, take a left, go straight, then left again, and enter the door [[gf1-111-4]]. Hit the Blue Tentacle with the Boomerang, step forward to bait its strike, and hit it again as you dodge. Four hits kill it and clear all Blue Tentacles in the dungeon [[gf1-112-1]]. Pick up Ruto and return [[gf1-112-1]].

#### Green Tentacle Room

From the hall, go right down the hall and take another right to the door at the end [[gf1-112-2]]. Kill the Biri first, then use the same hit-and-dodge pattern on the Green Tentacle. Four Boomerang hits remove every Green Tentacle [[gf1-112-3]]. Pick up Ruto and return to the hallway [[gf1-112-3]].

#### Pit Room: Skulltulas #57 and #58

Run straight across the hallway to the Pit Room [[gf1-113-1]]. Drop down the pit closest to the west wall, to your right as you enter from the hallway; the green tentacle used to cover it [[gf1-113-2, gf1-108-3]]. When you land, set Ruto down and face north. Two Gold Skulltulas hang on the wall. Kill each with the Boomerang, then throw the Boomerang again to collect both tokens (Skulltulas #57 and #58) [[gf1-113-3, checklist-skulltulas-11-2, checklist-skulltulas-11-3, gf2-41-5, gf2-41-6]]. When aiming manually, use Link's head as a targeting reticle [[gf1-113-3]]. Pick up Ruto and go through the west door [[gf1-113-3]].

#### Bigocto (mini-boss)

Throw Ruto up onto the ledge so she can claim her stone. The ledge rises, and when it comes back down she is gone and Bigocto has arrived [[gf1-113-3, gf2-42-1]]. Its weak point is the green eye on its backside. The Boomerang stuns it and locks it in place, so stun it and circle behind it, hugging the inner wall. Another stun, then a Jump Attack on the eye. Two Jump Attacks defeat it [[gf1-113-4, gf1-113-5, gf1-516-1, gf1-516-2]].

Two easier methods exist. You can let Bigocto run into you, which turns it around and lets you stun and hit it quickly [[gf1-516-3]]. Or Z-target it and throw the Boomerang as you head to its back [[gf3-34-1]]. One guide says a Deku Stick jump attack after a stun kills it in one hit [[checklist-sidequests-13-2]]. Another says about three sword hits on the green spot are needed [[gf2-42-1, gf2-121-1]]. Its contact does half a heart and the spiked middle does a quarter heart [[gf2-120-2]]. Stay locked on during the fight [[gf2-42-1]].

After it dies, one of the pots behind you holds a fairy [[gf3-34-2]]. Step onto the middle platform and the elevator rises. Run south down the hall and through the door, killing any Biri in the way [[gf1-113-6]].

#### Pink Thing Room and Elevator Room

Two Pink Things block the way. Stun the first with the Boomerang and jump onto it, then stun the second and jump to the door. Kill the Octorok if it gets in the way [[gf1-114-1, gf2-42-1]]. In the Elevator Room, jump across to the platform on the opposite wall. The ledge lowers and you end up next to the dungeon entrance. Run around to the door by the Pit Room, grab one of the boxes, and step on the blue button. Drop the box so it sits on the button, then go through the door [[gf1-114-2, gf2-42-2]].

#### Biri Room (Skulltula #59)

Kill all the Biri. Go to the southeast side and kill the Gold Skulltula on the ivy wall, halfway up the left side, with the Slingshot or the Boomerang. Climb up for the token (Skulltula #59) [[gf1-115-1, gf1-489-2, checklist-skulltulas-11-4, gf3-34-3]]. Continue to the top of the ivy wall. Look right to see a switch. Z-target it and hit it with the Boomerang to unlock the door at the north end of the room [[gf1-115-2]].

A glass panel blocks the switch from straight shots, so the Boomerang has to curve around it. Stand back and throw, or step to the left of the glass. It can take several tries [[gf2-42-4, gf3-34-4]]. Jump down afterwards [[gf1-115-2]].

Before opening the boss door, set Farore's Wind in this room. Commenters recommend it so you can warp straight back here after a failed attempt [[ra-comments-18-1, ra-comments-18-2]]. The Boomerang switch at the start of this run still has to be hit again after a reset [[ra-comments-18-3]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Rolling Thunder (Defeat Barinade without getting hit) can be missed. A hit fails it, so set Farore's Wind outside the boss room first. Then fight from a distance and reset on failure. In the Options menu, set Z-targeting to **Hold**; a guide strongly suggests this to make the fight much easier [[ra-guide-9-1, ra-comments-17-1, ra-comments-18-2, ra-comments-20-3]].

#### Barinade (boss)

Barinade is the first boss with a real chance of killing you [[gf1-115-3]]. All pots around the room contain Recovery Hearts [[gf2-43-1, gf2-121-2]]. Keep moving the whole time; you can walk and throw the Boomerang at once [[gf2-43-2, ra-comments-19-11]].

**Opening (all phases):** Sources agree that players are often hit by the jellyfish as soon as a phase starts, and the jellyfish eject in an unpredictable way [[ra-comments-20-8, ra-comments-17-13]]. A speedrun-style opening for the first phase: as soon as the boss starts, roll forward twice and take a couple of steps forward. Target the three tentacles up top with the Boomerang. Hit one, side-hop twice, hit the next, then side-hop back and hit the third [[ra-guide-9-2]]. Another player says to lock on, keep sidestepping and hit the three tentacles up top [[ra-comments-20-10]].

**Phase 1 (tentacles):** Z-target the tentacles attached to the ceiling and circle the boss. The direction does not matter, but stick with it, since changing course gets you electrocuted [[gf1-518-5]]. Sever all three with the Boomerang [[gf1-115-3, gf2-43-2, gf3-35-1]]. Wait until you see Navi floating over a target before pressing the lock-on button [[ra-comments-20-4]].

**Phase 2 (carousel of jellyfish):** The jellyfish detach and circle the boss. The rotating jellyfish cannot hit you if your back is against the wall. Wait there, Z-locked on Barinade, and look for an opening [[ra-comments-17-2, ra-comments-18-5]]. Stun the base with the Boomerang, which releases the jellyfish [[gf1-115-4, gf1-518-6]]. Stay to the side, since there is a counter attack at the end of the phase [[ra-comments-18-11]]. If you move in, hit once with the Boomerang, then slash five times and dodge back immediately [[ra-comments-17-3]].

Deku Nuts take out the loose jellyfish instantly if they are not tethered to the main body. Used well, they remove two or sometimes three at a time [[ra-comments-17-12, ra-comments-18-6, ra-comments-19-5]]. A stun and then Din's Fire wipes out most of them [[ra-comments-17-11]]. Throwing the Boomerang at the boss again resets the stun timer while you clear the loose ones [[ra-comments-21-11]]. Kill one or two jellyfish at a time and do not rush [[ra-comments-20-1, ra-comments-17-6]].

**Phase 3 (smaller, faster carousel):** This is the dangerous phase. The ring of jellyfish is smaller and faster, and Barinade moves around the room [[gf1-519-1]]. Keep throwing the Boomerang until it penetrates the ring and stuns him, then destroy the jellyfish. The satellite dishes resume attacking while you work [[gf1-519-1, gf1-519-2]]. Z-lock on early and keep moving sideways and back while throwing [[ra-comments-17-4, ra-comments-18-7]]. It can corner you against the wall, so back away in a curve rather than a straight line [[ra-comments-20-6, ra-comments-21-5]]. When he is stunned, lock on to a single jellyfish. With luck you may kill two or three in one stun [[ra-comments-17-5]]. A missed Boomerang throw can take away camera control, so some players use the Slingshot on the jellyfish instead. It is quicker and does not take the camera if you miss [[ra-comments-19-12, ra-comments-19-13]].

**Final phase (core exposed):** Stay at a distance. If you are too close, Barinade zaps you [[ra-comments-17-7]]. Stun him with the Boomerang, then run in and jump-attack the base. After two Jump Attacks he retreats into the ground briefly; keep dodging until he re-emerges [[gf1-519-3, gf1-519-4]]. Five Jump Attacks in total defeat him [[gf1-115-5, gf1-519-4]]. Deku Stick jump slashes after a Boomerang hit work for the damage, and a shield stab after a stun also kills him faster [[ra-guide-9-3, gf3-35-2, gf3-260-4]]. Another player says the stick method takes about three hits [[ra-comments-18-9]]. In the final phase, sidestep his beams and try not to get trapped against the wall [[ra-comments-18-10]]. Keep moving and avoid staying near him too long after you down him [[ra-comments-20-14]].

If you are retrying, use your first try to learn the pattern and see the whole fight through a few times rather than restarting at the first mistake [[ra-comments-21-1, ra-comments-18-12]]. Commenters say patience and safe play matter more than speed [[ra-comments-18-13, ra-comments-19-6]]. One player says binding the C buttons to the D-pad avoids misclicking Navi [[ra-comments-19-4]].

When Barinade falls with no hits taken:


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Rolling Thunder (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158124.png)
> *Defeat Barinade without getting hit*


Take the **Heart Container** he leaves, which brings you to nine hearts [[gf1-115-6, gf2-43-3, gf3-36-1]]. Then step into the ring of blue light. It teleports you and Ruto outside. Talk to her, say you want the Spiritual Stone, and agree to marry her; she gives you the Zora's Sapphire [[gf1-115-7, gf2-43-4, gf3-36-2, ra-guide-9-4]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Wedding Band (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158125.png)
> *Receive the Spiritual Stone of Water*


### Back in Zora's Fountain

Before leaving, grab the second Fountain Skulltula. It only appears at night, so play the Sun's Song if it is daytime. Climb onto the log sticking out of the water and look at the wall across from you. Shoot it with the Slingshot and fetch the token with the Boomerang (Skulltula #49) [[checklist-skulltulas-9-8, gf1-117-1, gf3-37-1, gf2-46-10, gf4-14-1]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave Zora's Fountain:** Make sure you have Farore's Wind, the Map, the Compass, the Boomerang, the Heart Container, the Zora's Sapphire, and Skulltulas #48, #49 and #56 to #59. Then head back through the cave entrance on the west side of the area to return to Zora's Domain [[gf1-117-2, gf1-115-6, gf1-110-3, gf1-111-3]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 9 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 36 / 100 (#48, #49 and #56 to #59 collected this chapter) [[checklist-skulltulas-9-7, checklist-skulltulas-9-8, checklist-skulltulas-11-1, checklist-skulltulas-11-2, checklist-skulltulas-11-3, checklist-skulltulas-11-4]]
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 17 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 3 / 8 [[gf1-115-6]]
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 6 / 26 [[gf1-110-3, gf1-111-3]]
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 35 / 97 (190 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Gave Ruto's Letter to King Zora and kept the empty Bottle
- [ ] Caught a fish in the Bottle
- [ ] Gold Skulltula #48 from the southeast tree
- [ ] Learned Farore's Wind
- [ ] Got the Boomerang from the Stinger Room chest
- [ ] Got the Dungeon Map while Ruto was with you
- [ ] Got the Compass (40-second Shabom room)
- [ ] Gold Skulltulas #56, #57, #58, #59
- [ ] Set Farore's Wind outside the boss door
- [ ] Defeated Barinade without being hit
- [ ] Took the Heart Container
- [ ] Received the Zora's Sapphire
- [ ] Gold Skulltula #49 from the log wall at night

This chapter closes out Young Link's story. You collect the Ocarina of Time after the drawbridge cutscene and learn the Song of Time. Then you finish the child-only tasks before opening the Door of Time: the dying guard, the Happy Mask Salesman quest, the Mask of Truth Deku Nut upgrade and the Gold Skulltula rewards. After you draw the Master Sword you cannot return to childhood until after the Forest Temple [[gf1-10-1, gf1-129-2, ra-guide-10-6]]. The Master Sword comes last in this chapter for that reason.

> [!TIP]
> **Before you start:** The Boomerang is needed for most of the Skulltula and Heart Piece tasks below. Bring bombs and a bottle of bugs, and have Din's Fire ready for Skulltula #13 [[gf1-457-1, gf1-457-2, checklist-skulltulas-5-2]].

### Hyrule Field

🗺️ **Map:** [View Hyrule Field Map by thegamemaster8](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/5394-hyrule-field-map)

#### Zelda's escape and the Ocarina of Time

1. With all three Spiritual Stones, approach the closed gates of Hyrule Castle. A cutscene plays: Zelda and Impa race out and Zelda throws the Ocarina of Time into the moat [[gf1-123-4, ra-guide-10-3]].
2. When the scene ends, dive into the moat water to pick up the Ocarina of Time. Zelda then contacts you telepathically and teaches the Song of Time [[gf1-123-5, gf2-45-2, gf3-37-13]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Damsel in Distress (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158126.png)
> *Witness Zelda's great escape and obtain a royal heirloom*


The Song of Time is Right C, A, Down C, Right C, A, Down C [[gf1-394-1, gf3-234-5]]. The Ocarina of Time replaces the Fairy Ocarina [[gf3-147-2]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** The next stop is the dying guard in the Market back alley. Do this BEFORE the Temple of Time cutscenes, because the guard never appears again afterward [[ra-guide-10-4, checklist-sidequests-15-1]].

#### Hyrule Field tasks (Boomerang)

- **Skulltula #12 (lone tree near Kakariko):** Between Hyrule Castle and Kakariko is a lone tree. Place a bomb at its base to open a hole. Inside, kill the Skulltula with the Slingshot and take the token with the Boomerang [[checklist-skulltulas-5-1, gf1-457-1, gf1-119-4, gf1-119-5]].
- **Skulltula #13 (needs Din's Fire):** Outside the Gerudo Valley entrance is a circle of rocks. Bomb the center and drop down. Burn the webs with Din's Fire, then collect the Skulltula in the nook with the cow using the Boomerang [[checklist-skulltulas-5-2, gf1-457-2, gf2-45-1]]. If you lack Din's Fire, see the Hyrule Castle section below.
- **Fenced grotto by Lake Hylia:** its Business Scrub Piece of Heart (#12) was bought in Chapter 8, so there is nothing left to do there [[gf1-426-1, gf1-100-2, checklist-heartpieces-3-12]].

### Hyrule Market

#### The dying guard (Last Breath)

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Talk to the dying guard NOW, after Zelda's escape and before the Temple of Time. He exists only after you have the three Spiritual Stones and before you go to the Temple of Time [[ra-comments-78-6, ra-guide-10-4]].

1. Go to the back alleys of the Castle Market [[ra-comments-78-3]].
2. Find the guard and talk to him to hear his final words. He appears after Zelda and Impa escape [[ra-comments-78-2]].
3. One commenter found that searching at night failed and the guard had to be found during the day. Another says the achievement now triggers by day or night [[ra-comments-78-5, ra-comments-78-4]]. If it does not pop, try the other time of day.


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Last Breath (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158171.png)
> *Receive some news from a dying guard in his final moments*


One commenter reported that speaking to him after getting the Ocarina did not activate the achievement. The cause is unconfirmed [[ra-comments-78-1]].

#### Other Market tasks

- **Rupees:** Breaking the pots in the room at the Market entrance earns about 100 rupees as Young Link [[gf4-3-2]].
- **Market guardhouse crate Skulltula (#18):** the crate beside the guard in the room with the pots was already taken in Chapter 2, so skip it [[gf1-35-1, gf2-23-2, checklist-sidequests-8-2]].
- **Happy Mask Shop:** It is the small building near the Temple of Time, right of the Hyrule Castle Garden entrance. It is used in the quest below [[gf1-59-1]].

### Hyrule Castle

🗺️ **Map:** [View Hyrule Palace Map by ThE_bLImP](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/52-hyrule-palace-map)

🗺️ **Map:** [View Path to Zelda Map by TLS64](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/55-path-to-zelda-map)

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave:** The courtyard events (the window shot and the guard bribe) cannot be redone once the Master Sword is drawn, and the courtyard cannot be re-entered after all three stones [[ra-guide-5-4, ra-comments-58-3]]. Steps for those two achievements are not part of this chapter. Unable to verify from collected sources.

These castle tasks remain available.

- **Din's Fire:** Go through the Hyrule Castle Garden, bomb the boulder at the dead end past the gate and crawl through to the Great Fairy's Fountain. Stand on the Triforce emblem and play Zelda's Lullaby to learn Din's Fire [[gf1-90-1, gf1-90-2, gf3-233-2]].
- **Skulltula (Song of Storms tree):** Near the moat is a lone tree. Play the Song of Storms beside it to open a hole, bomb the walls inside and take the token with the Boomerang [[checklist-skulltulas-5-9, gf1-459-2, gf1-173-3]].
- **Skulltula (castle grounds tree):** Roll into the lone tree outside the outer gate to knock a Skulltula loose [[checklist-skulltulas-5-8, gf3-179-3]].

### Lost Woods

🗺️ **Map:** [View Lost Woods Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/54-lost-woods-map)

#### Mask of Truth on the Forest Stage (A Horrible Face)

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** The Forest Stage Deku Nut upgrade (A Horrible Face) becomes unobtainable once you get the Poacher's Saw in the adult trading quest, and one source says it is also lost after you pull the Master Sword [[ra-guide-10-2, gf3-38-4, ra-comments-44-2]]. Whether it can still be earned as an adult before the Poacher's Saw is unconfirmed, because players disagree [[ra-comments-44-9, ra-comments-44-1]]. Get it as Young Link before drawing the Master Sword, along with A Pretty Face and Stocking Nuts [[gf1-418-1, ra-comments-73-3]].

1. Finish the Happy Mask quest (section below) until you hold the Mask of Truth [[gf1-124-2, gf4-14-14]].
2. Enter the Lost Woods and follow right, left, right, left, left to the grassy area with the Forest Stage [[gf3-142-1, gf3-199-3]].
3. Near the large tree, find the cluster of butterflies in the tall grass and drop into the hidden hole [[gf1-125-2, ra-comments-44-8]].
4. Put on the Mask of Truth and step forward. Speak to the Chief (the one with the mustache) on the right side of the room to receive the Deku Nut upgrade [[gf1-125-3, gf1-418-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **A Horrible Face (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/511028.png)
> *Obtain the Deku Nuts upgrade from the Deku Scrub Brothers in the Lost Woods*


One player reports that doing the Mask of Truth upgrade before the boulder scrubs stopped Stocking Nuts from unlocking for them, so get the boulder-scrub upgrade first [[ra-comments-44-6]].

#### Skulltulas and beans in the Lost Woods

- **Forest Stage bean spot:** In the grassy Forest Stage area is the soft-soil patch whose bug Skulltula (#5) you took in Chapter 4. Plant a Magic Bean there. Adult Link can ride the plant at night to another Skulltula [[gf1-125-1, checklist-skulltulas-3-6, gf1-455-1, gf3-37-6]].
- **Bean spot (two lefts):** Plant a bean in the soft soil reached by going left twice from the entrance [[gf3-37-5, checklist-sidequests-13-22]].

### Kakariko Village

#### The windmill Piece of Heart (#5)

With the Boomerang, enter the windmill and look at the Heart Piece high on the left ledge. When the moving platform carries you away from the piece on the left side, throw the Boomerang at the top of the heart to pull it down [[gf1-121-2, checklist-sidequests-13-7, checklist-heartpieces-3-5]].

#### Kakariko Skulltulas

Several Skulltulas appear here at night: the tree near the entrance, the Skulltula House window, the unfinished house, and the lookout tower ladder [[gf3-20-4, gf3-20-5, gf3-20-6, gf3-20-8]].

### House of Skulltula

Return here as you reach the token counts below.

| Tokens | Reward | Citation |
|---|---|---|
| 30 | Giant's Wallet (500 rupees) | [[gf1-120-1, gf1-419-3]] |
| 40 | Bombchus | [[gf1-168-1, gf1-451-2]] |

> [!TIP]
> Get 30 Gold Skulltulas before the Temple of Time, because you cannot become Young Link again until after the Forest Temple [[ra-guide-10-6]].

At 30 tokens, speak to the man in the rear of the room for the Giant's Wallet [[gf1-120-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **A Woman's Best Friend (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158196.png)
> *Collect 30 Gold Skulltulas and obtain your reward*


At 40 tokens, collect the Bombchus [[gf1-168-1, gf2-47-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Bombchu (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158197.png)
> *Collect 40 Gold Skulltulas and obtain your reward*


### Kakariko Graveyard

- **Skulltula #28:** At night, a Skulltula sits on the stone wall on the right as you enter. Retrieve it with the Boomerang [[checklist-skulltulas-6-7, gf1-120-2]].
- **Bean spot:** Plant a Magic Bean in the soft soil in the Graveyard [[gf1-120-3, gf3-37-10]].

### Lon Lon Ranch

Collect these at night with the Boomerang.

- **Skulltula #16:** On the ranch house window [[checklist-skulltulas-5-5, gf1-123-1]].
- **Skulltula #17:** High on the wall to the right of the silo behind the corral [[checklist-skulltulas-5-6, gf3-37-15]].

### Lake Hylia

- **Skulltula #53 (night):** On the back of the Lakeside Laboratory. Stand on the bridge and use the Boomerang [[checklist-skulltulas-10-3, checklist-sidequests-13-18]].
- **Bean spot:** The soft-earth patch beside the Lakeside Laboratory can take a Magic Bean [[checklist-sidequests-12-4, gf3-174-6]].

### Gerudo Valley

- **Skulltula #61 (night):** Walking up the plank to the big bridge, turn right and use the Boomerang [[checklist-skulltulas-12-2, gf3-37-18]].
- **Bean spot:** Plant a Magic Bean in the soft soil on the lower ledge [[gf3-37-21]].

### Inside the Deku Tree

**Skulltula #11 (bombable wall):** Drop through the hole in the middle of the first floor, climb the block, crawl through the crawl hole on the far wall, bomb the weak wall, kill the Skulltula and take the token with the Boomerang [[checklist-skulltulas-4-4, gf1-484-2, checklist-sidequests-13-20]].

### Happy Mask Salesman quest

For each mask: borrow it at the shop, sell it, then return and pay the fee.

| Mask | Sell to | Notes | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Keaton Mask | Death Mountain gate guard, Kakariko | Show Zelda's Letter first; sells for 15 | [[gf1-58-3, gf1-59-3, gf1-350-1]] |
| Skull Mask | Lone Skull Kid, Lost Woods | Play Saria's Song first for the Heart Piece; sells for 10 | [[gf1-65-3, gf1-527-3, ra-comments-79-3]] |
| Spooky Mask | Graveyard kid (daytime) | Sells for 30 | [[gf1-92-2, gf1-351-1]] |
| Bunny Hood | Running Man in Hyrule Field | Wait for him to sit; appears after Zelda flees | [[gf1-351-2, ra-comments-79-5, ra-comments-79-7]] |

1. Show Zelda's Letter to the guard, bring him the Keaton Mask from the shop and sell it for 15 rupees [[gf1-59-3, gf2-47-2]].
2. Return and pay the 10-rupee fee, then borrow the Skull Mask [[gf1-60-1]].
3. Sell the Skull Mask to the Lone Skull Kid in the Lost Woods [[gf1-65-3, gf2-160-13]].
4. Pay the shop, then borrow the Spooky Mask and sell it to the Graveyard kid in the daytime [[gf1-92-1, gf1-92-2]].
5. Pay for the Spooky Mask and borrow the Bunny Hood [[gf1-93-1]]. The Running Man runs counter-clockwise around Lon Lon Ranch and appears after Zelda flees [[ra-comments-79-5, ra-comments-79-6]].
6. Sell the Bunny Hood to the Running Man. Without the Giant's Wallet you receive only 99 or 200 of the 500 rupees, so claim the Giant's Wallet first [[gf1-351-3, checklist-sidequests-13-8]].
7. Return to the shop, pay the fee and borrow the Mask of Truth [[gf1-124-2, checklist-sidequests-13-9]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Happy Mask Salesman (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158172.png)
> *Complete the Happy Mask Salesman quest and obtain your reward*


### Temple of Time

> [!CAUTION]
> **Point of No Return:** After the Temple of Time cutscene the dying guard (Last Breath) is gone, and with three Spiritual Stones Zelda's courtyard is closed. Finish both, the Happy Mask quest and the Mask of Truth Deku upgrade before drawing the Master Sword [[ra-guide-10-4, ra-guide-5-4, ra-comments-58-3]].

1. Enter the Temple of Time from the Market. It stands to the right of the Happy Mask Shop [[gf1-128-4]].
2. Stand on the red carpet before the Door of Time and play the Song of Time to open it [[gf1-129-1, gf4-15-1, ra-guide-10-7]].
3. Walk through and draw the Master Sword from its pedestal [[gf1-129-2]].
4. Link is sealed away for seven years and wakes up as Adult Link, and Rauru gives you the Light Medallion [[gf3-38-5, gf1-421-4]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Hero of Time (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158127.png)
> *Transform into Adult Link*


> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 10 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 42 / 97 (225 / 690 points)
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 44 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 18 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 3 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 6 / 26

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Ocarina of Time retrieved and Song of Time learned (Damsel in Distress)
- [ ] Spoke to the dying guard (Last Breath)
- [ ] 30-token Giant's Wallet claimed (A Woman's Best Friend)
- [ ] 40-token Bombchus claimed (Bombchu)
- [ ] Happy Mask quest finished (Happy Mask Salesman)
- [ ] Mask of Truth Deku Nut upgrade received (A Horrible Face)
- [ ] Master Sword drawn (Hero of Time)

## Chapter 11: Seven Years Later: Hookshot, Epona and the Goron Tunic

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Kokiri_Forest["Kokiri Forest"]
  Lost_Woods["Lost Woods"]
  Hyrule_Castle["Hyrule Castle"]
  Lon_Lon_Ranch["Lon Lon Ranch"]
  Kakariko_Village["Kakariko Village"]
  Kakariko_Graveyard["Kakariko Graveyard"]
  Goron_City["Goron City"]
  Temple_of_Time["Temple of Time"]
  Queen_Gohma{{"⚔ Queen Gohma"}}
  King_Dodongo{{"⚔ King Dodongo"}}
  Barinade{{"⚔ Barinade"}}
  Phantom_Ganon{{"⚔ Phantom Ganon"}}
  Morpha{{"⚔ Morpha"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Twinrova{{"⚔ Twinrova"}}
  Ganondorf{{"⚔ Ganondorf"}}
  Ganon{{"⚔ Ganon"}}
  Dark_Link{{"⚔ Shadow Link (Dark Link)"}}
  Dead_Hand{{"⚔ Dead Hand"}}
  Dying_Guard_Lockout>"⛔ Dying guard disappears after the Temple of Time"]
  Final_Battle>"⛔ Ganon's Tower final battle"]
  Kakariko_Village --> Kakariko_Graveyard
  Temple_of_Time -->|"Dampe's race"| Kakariko_Graveyard
  Ganondorf --> Final_Battle
  Final_Battle --> Ganon
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Dying_Guard_Lockout,Final_Battle ponr;
  class Queen_Gohma,King_Dodongo,Barinade,Phantom_Ganon,Morpha,Bongo_Bongo,Twinrova,Ganondorf,Ganon,Dark_Link,Dead_Hand boss;
```

This chapter starts the moment Link wakes as an adult. Opening the Door of Time and drawing the Master Sword sends Young Link seven years ahead as an Adult [[gf1-10-1, gf1-129-2]]. Rauru gives you the Light Medallion and sends you back to the Temple of Time, where Sheik meets you and advises a trip to Kakariko Village [[gf1-131-1, gf1-131-2]]. The guide also states that on first reaching adulthood you must obtain the Hookshot before anything else [[gf1-17-1]].

Three achievements are earned here: **Hook Me Away** (Hookshot), **The Great Escape** (ride Epona on Hyrule Field) and **Heat Resistant Tunic** (Goron Tunic). Along the way you collect two Pieces of Heart in Kakariko Village and the Graveyard, plus a handful of Gold Skulltulas.

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave the Lost Woods and Kokiri Forest for good:** a commenter reports the Goron Tunic can become completely unobtainable if you get the Longshot and beat the Fire Temple before getting it [[ra-comments-91-2]]. Another commenter says the achievement is story-related and not missable [[ra-comments-36-2]]. These comments conflict, so treat the tunic as urgent and take it in this chapter.

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Do not skip the Goron Tunic. One commenter confirms that getting the Longshot and beating the Fire Temple before the tunic makes it completely unobtainable [[ra-comments-91-2]]. Another commenter says it is story related and not missable [[ra-comments-36-2]]. This conflict is unconfirmed, so get the tunic now.

### Temple of Time

Drawing the Master Sword turns you into Adult Link, and Rauru gives you the Light Medallion [[gf1-131-1, gf1-421-4]]. Sheik meets you back at the Temple of Time and tells you to visit Kakariko Village before the Forest Temple [[gf1-131-2]]. Head to Hyrule Field and then to Kakariko Village.

### Kakariko Village

Entering Kakariko Village as an adult triggers a scene in which you learn the Nocturne of Shadow [[gf1-244-1]]. Another source places that scene after the Water Temple, so the song's timing is unconfirmed here [[gf4-28-6]].

#### Hookshot onto the roof (Piece of Heart #19)

Do this after you have the Hookshot. Jump onto the fence in front of the Windmill door, look right to the tall building with the blue roof, and Hookshot onto it when the red reticule shows [[gf1-136-3]]. Speak to the man gazing up at Death Mountain for a Piece of Heart [[gf1-136-3, checklist-heartpieces-4-5]].

#### Gold Skulltula #27 (night, Hookshot)

At night, stand in front of Impa's house door and Hookshot to the roof of the House of Skulltula, then Hookshot again onto the roof of Impa's house. The Skulltula is on the wall there [[checklist-skulltulas-6-6, gf1-463-3]]. Play the Sun's Song to make it night if needed [[gf1-136-4]].

### Kakariko Graveyard

🗺️ **Map:** [View Path to Zelda Map by TLS64](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/55-path-to-zelda-map)

#### Bean platform crate (Piece of Heart #20)

A Magic Leaf now stands where you planted a bean as Young Link on the left side of the Graveyard. Step on it to ride up to the high cliff, then roll into the crate for a Piece of Heart [[gf1-134-3, checklist-heartpieces-4-7]].

#### Dampe's race (Hookshot)

🗺️ **Map:** [View Dampe's Race Map by DrgNyt](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/7461-dampes-race-map)

Right next to the Magic Leaf is a gravestone with flowers. Grab it from behind and pull it away to reveal a pit, then drop in [[gf1-134-4, checklist-sidequests-20-2]]. Dampe's ghost challenges you to a race and starts without notice [[gf1-135-1]]. You only need to follow him, and when you reach the end he gives you the Hookshot [[checklist-sidequests-20-2, gf1-135-3]]. The route is: dodge flames, turn right at the end of the hall, take the next left wide, and so on through the course [[gf1-135-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Hook Me Away (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158174.png)
> *Obtain the Hookshot*


After the race you exit into the top of the Windmill, after playing the Song of Time to move the blue block [[checklist-sidequests-20-2, gf1-135-4]].

### Lon Lon Ranch

Go to the pasture at the rear of the ranch and speak to Ingo. He offers a horse ride for ten rupees [[gf1-353-3]]. Ignore the horse he offers, play Epona's Song, and Epona runs to you [[gf1-133-1, gf1-353-4]]. Jumping both fences earns back the 10 rupees [[gf1-133-5]]. You need at least 50 rupees to race him [[checklist-sidequests-15-4]].

Speak with Ingo while riding Epona and accept the 50-rupee race [[gf1-133-2]]. Ingo takes corners wide. Cut the inside rail, burn a few carrots to pass, then block him [[gf1-353-5]]. After you win, he bets Epona on a rematch and cheats at the start. Burn carrots at the first turn to pass him again [[gf1-353-6]]. After the second win he locks the gate. Run counter-clockwise around the paddock and burn all your carrots at the last turn to jump the gate [[gf1-133-3, gf1-354-1]].

For clearing fences, commenters advise lining up dead center and boosting a beat or two before the fence, not right at it [[ra-comments-85-1, ra-comments-85-6]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** Some players report the achievement firing at the starting screen, or Epona not responding to Epona's Song [[ra-comments-25-1, ra-comments-25-3]]. If it does not unlock, the reporter asks for a save made just before the escape attempt [[ra-comments-25-4]]. Do not use save states in Hardcore.


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **The Great Escape (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158128.png)
> *Ride Epona on Hyrule Field*


### Goron City

Stop the small rolling Goron on the third floor with a well-timed bomb, then talk to him [[gf1-171-2, ra-comments-91-5]]. Ask about the Dragon and the Gorons, and he gives you the Goron Tunic and opens Darunia's Chamber [[gf1-171-3]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Heat Resistant Tunic (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158181.png)
> *Obtain a Goron Tunic*


Equipping it removes the time limit in the Crater and the Fire Temple [[gf1-171-4]]. Do not buy the Giant's Knife from the big Goron here [[gf1-400-1]].

#### Gold Skulltula #35

Walk out on a rope in view of the pedestal where the Spiritual Stone of Fire rested and Hookshot the Skulltula on its back [[checklist-skulltulas-7-6, gf1-469-1]].

### Kokiri Forest

At night, a Skulltula is on the roof of the house with two pillars [[checklist-sidequests-24-1, gf2-59-2]]. This is Gold Skulltula #3 in the guide's list, on the back of the House of Twins, reached with the Hookshot [[checklist-skulltulas-3-3]].

### Lost Woods

🗺️ **Map:** [View Lost Woods Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/54-lost-woods-map)

🗺️ **Map:** [View Kokiri Forest Map by kingofthisgame](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/1099-kokiri-forest-map)

At night, ride the bean plant on the Forest Stage screen up to the ledge and slash the Gold Skulltula [[checklist-sidequests-23-3, gf3-44-7]]. Mido may block the path; play Saria's Song for him [[checklist-sidequests-23-2]].

### Hyrule Castle

🗺️ **Map:** [View Hyrule Palace Map by ThE_bLImP](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/52-hyrule-palace-map)

On the far side of the ruined outer gate of the former castle grounds, Hookshot the Gold Skulltula [[checklist-skulltulas-5-10, checklist-sidequests-5-31]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave this chapter:** confirm you have the Hookshot, Epona and the Goron Tunic, and the Skulltulas above.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 11 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 45 / 97 (240 / 690 points)
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 49 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 20 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 3 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 6 / 26

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Hookshot from Dampe
- [ ] Epona freed
- [ ] Goron Tunic
- [ ] Kakariko roof Piece of Heart
- [ ] Graveyard crate Piece of Heart
- [ ] Gold Skulltulas #3, #6, #21, #27 and #35

## Chapter 12: Ice Cavern and the Zora Tunic

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Zoras_River["Zora's River"]
  Zoras_Domain["Zora's Domain"]
  Zoras_Fountain["Zora's Fountain"]
  Ice_Cavern["Ice Cavern"]
  Barinade{{"⚔ Barinade"}}
  Morpha{{"⚔ Morpha"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Dark_Link{{"⚔ Shadow Link (Dark Link)"}}
  Dying_Guard_Lockout>"⛔ Dying guard disappears after the Temple of Time"]
  Zoras_River -->|"Zelda's Lullaby on the waterfall"| Zoras_Domain
  Zoras_Domain -->|"King Zora moves aside"| Zoras_Fountain
  Zoras_Fountain -->|"Frozen fountain"| Ice_Cavern
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Dying_Guard_Lockout ponr;
  class Barinade,Morpha,Bongo_Bongo,Dark_Link boss;
```

This chapter takes adult Link up frozen Zora's River at night for two Hookshot Skulltulas, across the iced-over Zora's Fountain to the Ice Cavern, and back out again. The Iron Boots, Serenade of Water, Compass, Map and Pieces of Heart #26 to #28 come from this route, as do the Zora Tunic and King Zora's kiss [[gf1-207-1, gf1-207-2, gf1-209-1, gf1-209-2, gf1-213-3, gf1-443-1, gf1-442-2, ra-guide-11-5, ra-guide-11-6]]. Three achievements are in play: Bring Me to the Bottom of the Oceans (Iron Boots), A Breath of Fresh Air (Zora Tunic) and Kiss That Frog (the only missable one). The order of the Zora's Domain steps matters, so read the warnings before you touch King Zora.

### Preparation

- **Blue Fire:** Blue Fire melts the Red Ice that blocks the Compass chest, a Piece of Heart, the Map chest and parts of the block room, and it is also needed on the Zora Shop door and on King Zora. Carry as many empty Bottles as you can [[gf1-211-5, gf2-69-3, gf1-212-3, gf1-215-3, gf1-215-2]]. Fill every Bottle with Blue Fire whenever you reach the blue torch [[gf2-69-3]]. Make sure you leave the Ice Cavern with at least two bottles of Blue Fire in your inventory, one for the shop door and one for King Zora [[ra-guide-11-4, gf1-215-3]].
- **Giant's Wallet:** the Zora Tunic costs 300 Rupees at the shop, so you need the Giant's Wallet [[gf1-403-2, ra-comments-37-2]]. A commenter advises defeating 30 Gold Skulltulas and getting the Giant's Wallet before this chapter so that you do not miss Kiss That Frog [[ra-comments-37-1]].
- **Weapons:** Bring the Fairy Bow for the Ice Keese and the Hookshot for the Skulltulas and the Freezzards [[gf1-211-4, gf1-496-1]]. Ice Keese freeze you on contact, so kill them from range [[gf1-211-4]].
- **Hardcore mode:** save states are off, so the tunic-first order below matters, because you cannot undo a wrong thaw.

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Kiss That Frog is missable. Thawing King Zora with Blue Fire before you have bought and equipped a Zora Tunic makes him hand you the free tunic instead of blowing a kiss, which costs you the achievement [[ra-comments-37-3, checklist-sidequests-18-12, ra-guide-11-6]]. Do not use Blue Fire on King Zora until the steps in "Zora's Domain, second visit" are complete.

### Key pickups in this chapter

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Piece of Heart #26 | Zora's Fountain, end of the east ice-platform string | **TAKE** | [[gf1-442-1]] |
| Dungeon Map | Ice Cavern, chest in Red Ice | **MELT** with Blue Fire, **OPEN** | [[gf1-212-2]] |
| Compass | Ice Cavern, chest in Red Ice | **MELT** with Blue Fire, **OPEN** | [[gf1-212-3]] |
| Piece of Heart #27 | Ice Cavern, inside Red Ice | **MELT**, **TAKE** | [[gf1-212-4]] |
| Iron Boots | Ice Cavern, chest after the White Wolfos | **OPEN** | [[gf1-213-3]] |
| Zora Tunic | Zora Shop, 300 Rupees | **BUY**, **EQUIP** | [[gf1-403-2, ra-guide-11-5]] |
| Piece of Heart #28 | Bottom of Zora's Fountain | Iron Boots, Zora Tunic | [[gf1-442-2, gf3-136-3]] |

### Zora's River

🗺️ **Map:** [View Zora's River/Domain Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/56-zoras-river-domain-map)

Enter from Hyrule Field. Both Skulltulas here appear only at night, and time passes normally in this zone, so you can play the Sun's Song here if it is day [[gf1-207-1, gf1-96-2]].

#### Skulltula on the south wall (#45)

Run forward from the entrance and jump across the river where you crossed as a kid. Run upstream to the next place where the river must be crossed, but do not jump yet. At night, face the south wall and look up to see the Skulltula, which is out of reach from here [[gf1-207-1]].

Climb up to the high ledge, face south again, and look for the cliff with a pit in it. Jump out to this ledge, avoiding the pit, and Hookshot the Skulltula for its token [[gf1-207-1, checklist-skulltulas-9-4]].

An alternative route is to ride the grown Magic Bean platform up to the high area and jump off as it passes. Then look southwest and play the Sun's Song if nothing is crawling on the wall [[gf2-68-2]].

#### Skulltula near the wooden bridge (#46)

Work your way upstream toward the Zora's Domain waterfall. Near the end of the wooden drawbridge just before the waterfall, look at the northwestern wall for the second Skulltula. You may need to stand on the fence to reach it with the Hookshot [[gf1-207-2, checklist-skulltulas-9-5, gf2-68-3]].

#### Opening the waterfall

Stand on the plaque in front of the waterfall and play Zelda's Lullaby. The waterfall retracts, and you can jump over to the doorway and enter Zora's Domain [[gf1-96-3, gf2-35-5, gf1-207-3]].

### Zora's Domain

🗺️ **Map:** [View Zora's River/Domain Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/56-zoras-river-domain-map)

Zora's Domain is frozen over. It does not thaw by your own actions; it begins to unfreeze only after the Water Temple [[gf1-526-1, gf2-68-4]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Climb to King Zora's throne room only to pass through. Do not use Blue Fire on him yet. This is the Kiss That Frog deadline [[ra-comments-37-3, checklist-sidequests-18-12]].

#### The waterfall-top Skulltula (later)

The Gold Skulltula at the top of the frozen waterfall (#47) is counted in Chapter 21, so the checkpoint below leaves it out. If it is night and you take it now, it simply counts early: Hookshot it from the lip at the top of the tunnel on the left side of the throne room [[gf1-472-1, gf1-208-1, gf2-97-4]].

#### On to the Fountain

Climb to the throne room and take the passage behind King Zora's throne into Zora's Fountain [[gf1-208-2, gf1-305-2]].

### Zora's Fountain

Jabu-Jabu is gone and a slab of ice has replaced him [[gf2-68-4]].

#### Piece of Heart #26 (ice platforms)

Climb the small frozen hill and jump onto the first of the frozen platforms. Jump carefully along them. At the second platform you can go north or northeast, and you should go northeast first for the Piece of Heart [[gf1-209-1]]. Another route heads for the huge iceberg and then runs east to the end of the string of icebergs [[gf1-442-1]].

The second set of platforms steadily rotates. Stay on a spinning platform until it goes around, then jump [[gf1-209-1, gf2-68-5]].

Take the **[Piece of Heart]** (#26) [[gf1-209-1, gf2-68-5]].

#### Into the cave

Head back to the giant platform and go north along the ice platforms. Jump to the northern edge of the pond and into the massive cave mouth, which leads to the Ice Cavern [[gf1-209-2]].

### Ice Cavern

🗺️ **Map:** [View Ice Cavern Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/53-ice-cavern-map)

The Ice Cavern holds one Piece of Heart and three Gold Skulltulas [[checklist-sidequests-27-1]].

#### Entry corridor and Freezzard room

When the passage opens into a large room with a frozen floor, three Freezzards are waiting [[gf1-211-1]]. Defeat them with sword strikes or Din's Fire. On the ice, use the Hookshot on a Freezzard to pull yourself right to it. Their ice breath freezes you [[gf1-211-1]]. Dodge the Blade Trap in the room, then enter the door that opens [[gf1-360-2]].

#### Spinning ice-blade room (Silver Rupees)

The next room has a huge rotating ice blade and Silver Rupees that you must collect to unlock the door at the north end [[gf1-211-2, gf1-360-3]]. One rupee is behind three blue stalagmites in the corner to the left as you enter, and a Gold Skulltula is on the ceiling above it [[gf1-211-3, gf2-69-1]]. The door at the top of the cliff unlocks once all are collected, and you climb up and go through it [[gf1-211-2]].

On the wall here is a Gold Skulltula, on the left wall as you enter from the southern door, also described as behind a cluster of icicles. Hookshot it for its token [[gf1-496-1, checklist-skulltulas-15-2, gf2-69-2]].

#### Ice Keese room and the Blue Fire

In the wide room beyond, Ice Keese freeze you on contact. Kill them from afar with arrows [[gf1-211-4]].

This room has a platform with a pillar of Blue Fire. Fill your empty Bottles with Blue Fire here [[gf1-211-5, gf2-69-3, gf1-360-4]].

A Jump Attack with the Biggoron Sword or Megaton Hammer kills the Freezzard that hogs the path in one hit [[gf1-212-1]]. Use Blue Fire to melt the Red Ice around the large chest and open it for the **[Dungeon Map]** [[gf1-212-2, gf2-69-3]]. The pot frozen in Red Ice contains a fairy [[gf1-212-2]]. Another pillar of Red Ice hides a pot that holds a Purple Rupee [[gf2-69-4]].

#### Compass, Piece of Heart #27 and Skulltula

Two walls with a red tinge are visible in the blade room, one on each side, and both can be melted with Blue Fire. One leads to the Compass and Heart Piece room, the other to the block-pushing room [[gf1-443-1, gf3-136-1, gf1-496-2, gf1-497-1]]. Sources differ on which side is which, so if you open the wrong wall, go back and open the other one [[gf1-212-1, gf1-496-2, gf1-497-1]].

Use Blue Fire to melt the Red Ice over the chest on the right and open it for the **[Compass]** [[gf1-212-3, gf2-70-1]]. Melt the Red Ice on the left side for **[Piece of Heart]** #27 [[gf1-212-4, gf2-70-2]]. A Gold Skulltula is on the wall beside or above the Piece of Heart, so Hookshot it [[gf1-212-5, gf2-70-3]]. Refill your Bottles with Blue Fire and go back to the blade room [[gf1-212-3]].

#### Block-pushing room

Melt the other red-tinged wall and go to the sliding-block room [[gf1-497-1]]. The Skulltula is in the corner to your right as you come in, and you can Hookshot it. It is also described as above the pit to the left [[gf1-212-6, gf1-497-1, gf2-70-4]]. Take care near the pit, because a fall can drop you back at the start of the dungeon [[gf2-70-4]].

You need all five Silver Rupees to open the exit. Pushing the ice block off a cliff makes it respawn at the room entrance [[gf1-212-7]]. One solution is as follows:

1. Grab the block from its east side and push it forward. Climb on top and use it to reach the rupee on the tall stone block [[gf1-212-7]].
2. Push the block east from its west side. Climb into the small alcove, melt its Red Ice with Blue Fire, and take the second rupee [[gf1-212-7]].
3. Push the block off a cliff so it respawns, then push it south from its north side. Jump on and take the rupee on the rock [[gf1-212-7]].
4. Push the stone block west from its east side. Climb onto the ice block and take the rupee on top of the stone [[gf1-212-7]].
5. Jump down, go to the south side of the block, and push it north. Grab the rupee on the stone where it comes to rest, and the door opens [[gf1-212-7]].

A second solution exists and uses a different push order [[gf2-70-5]].

To reach the exit ledge, push the block west, south, west, climb to the Blue Fire torch, then push it south, east, south and jump to the opened hallway [[gf2-70-6]].

If you want the bundle of Red Rupees hanging high above the ground, play the Song of Time here to make a Block of Time appear and use it to climb [[gf1-213-1]].

#### White Wolfos and the Iron Boots

The White Wolfos waits in the shrine room [[gf3-62-1]]. Block with your shield and attack only after it strikes. A jump attack after defending works well [[gf2-70-7]]. You can also let it strike, backflip away at the last moment, and jump attack as you land. Even with the Master Sword it dies in one shot [[gf1-213-2]].

A large chest appears in the middle of the room. Open it for the **[Iron Boots]** [[gf1-213-3, gf2-70-8, gf3-62-1]]. The Iron Boots are needed in the Water Temple [[gf1-404-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Bring Me to the Bottom of the Oceans (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158133.png)
> *Obtain the Iron Boots*


This achievement is story-related and not missable [[ra-comments-36-1]].

Sheik then appears and teaches you the Serenade of Water: A Button, C-Down, C-Right, C-Right, C-Left. It takes you to the Water Temple in Lake Hylia [[gf1-213-4, gf1-396-1, gf3-235-6]]. Do not use it yet, because the Tunic and a Piece of Heart come first.

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave the Ice Cavern:** check that you have both the Map and Compass, the Piece of Heart (#27), all three Skulltulas, and at least two bottles of Blue Fire [[gf1-212-2, gf1-212-3, gf1-212-4, checklist-sidequests-27-1, ra-guide-11-4]].

### Zora's Domain, second visit

Return through the west entrance of the Fountain to Zora's Domain [[gf1-117-2]]. You will do the shop and King Zora in strict order.

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Do not thaw King Zora yet. The Tunic must be bought and worn first, or he hands you a tunic and the kiss is lost [[ra-comments-37-3, ra-guide-11-6]].

#### Step 1: Buy the tunic

Use Blue Fire on the frozen Zora Shop door [[gf1-215-3, ra-guide-11-5]]. The shop sells the Zora Tunic for 300 Rupees. You need the Giant's Wallet to hold that much [[gf1-403-2, gf2-140-38]]. Buy it.


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **A Breath of Fresh Air (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158134.png)
> *Obtain a Zora Tunic*


You can also get this achievement from King Zora's free tunic, but doing so costs you the kiss [[gf1-403-1, ra-comments-37-3]].

#### Step 2: Equip and save

Equip the Zora Tunic from the equipment subscreen and use the game's normal save before you continue. Whether the tunic must be worn at the exact moment of thawing is unconfirmed, as sources differ. Follow the safe order: buy, equip, save, then thaw, and talk to him while still wearing it [[ra-guide-11-6, ra-comments-92-3]].

#### Step 3: Thaw King Zora and take the kiss

Climb to the throne room and stand next to King Zora wearing the tunic. Use the Blue Fire on him [[checklist-sidequests-18-12, ra-guide-11-6]]. Then talk to him. He blows you a kiss instead of giving you a tunic [[ra-guide-11-6, ra-comments-37-3]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Kiss That Frog (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158182.png)
> *Get a kiss from King Zora*


Do not show him the Prescription yet unless you intend to start the Biggoron trade chain right now. He gives the Eyeball Frog, which has a three-minute limit [[gf1-360-5, gf3-62-6]]. Do not warp with Serenade of Water or Bolero of Fire during that quest [[gf1-215-4]]. That sequence is covered in the next chapter.

### Back at the Fountain: Piece of Heart #28

Go back through the passage behind the throne to Zora's Fountain. Equip the Iron Boots, and with the Zora Tunic on, sink into the deep water to the exact center and bottom of the pool. Take the **[Piece of Heart]** (#28) [[checklist-heartpieces-4-15, gf2-97-2, gf3-136-3, gf4-16-10]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave:** confirm Pieces of Heart #26, #27 and #28, the Map, Compass, Iron Boots, Serenade of Water and the Zora Tunic. Zora's River GS #45 and #46 and the Ice Cavern Skulltulas #74 to #76 are also done [[gf1-442-1, gf1-443-1, gf1-442-2, gf1-207-1, gf1-207-2, gf1-496-1, checklist-skulltulas-15-1, checklist-skulltulas-15-3]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 12 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 54 / 100 [[gf1-207-1, gf1-207-2, gf1-496-1, checklist-skulltulas-15-1, checklist-skulltulas-15-3]]
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 23 / 36 [[gf1-442-1, gf1-443-1, gf1-442-2]]
> - **Heart Containers:** 3 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 8 / 26 [[gf1-212-2, gf1-212-3]]
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 48 / 97 (255 / 690 points)

### Chapter checklist

- [ ] Gold Skulltula on the Zora's River south wall (#45)
- [ ] Gold Skulltula near the wooden drawbridge (#46)
- [ ] Piece of Heart #26 (ice platforms)
- [ ] Ice Cavern Dungeon Map
- [ ] Ice Cavern Compass
- [ ] Piece of Heart #27 (Red Ice)
- [ ] Ice Cavern Gold Skulltulas (#74 to #76)
- [ ] White Wolfos defeated, Iron Boots obtained (Bring Me to the Bottom of the Oceans)
- [ ] Serenade of Water learned
- [ ] Extra Blue Fire bottled
- [ ] Zora Tunic bought and equipped (A Breath of Fresh Air)
- [ ] King Zora thawed while wearing the tunic (Kiss That Frog)
- [ ] Piece of Heart #28 from the bottom of the Fountain
