## Chapter 13: The Biggoron's Sword Trading Quest

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Lost_Woods["Lost Woods"]
  Kakariko_Village["Kakariko Village"]
  House_of_Skulltula["House of Skulltula"]
  Death_Mountain_Trail["Death Mountain Trail"]
  Zoras_Domain["Zora's Domain"]
  Lake_Hylia["Lake Hylia"]
  Gerudo_Valley["Gerudo Valley"]
  King_Dodongo{{"⚔ King Dodongo"}}
  Barinade{{"⚔ Barinade"}}
  Phantom_Ganon{{"⚔ Phantom Ganon"}}
  Morpha{{"⚔ Morpha"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Twinrova{{"⚔ Twinrova"}}
  Ganondorf{{"⚔ Ganondorf"}}
  Ganon{{"⚔ Ganon"}}
  Dark_Link{{"⚔ Shadow Link (Dark Link)"}}
  Dead_Hand{{"⚔ Dead Hand"}}
  Dying_Guard_Lockout>"⛔ Dying guard disappears after the Temple of Time"]
  Final_Battle>"⛔ Ganon's Tower final battle"]
  Kakariko_Village -->|"Show Zelda's Letter to the gate guard"| Death_Mountain_Trail
  Zoras_Domain -->|"Underwater tunnel"| Lake_Hylia
  Ganondorf --> Final_Battle
  Final_Battle --> Ganon
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Dying_Guard_Lockout,Final_Battle ponr;
  class King_Dodongo,Barinade,Phantom_Ganon,Morpha,Bongo_Bongo,Twinrova,Ganondorf,Ganon,Dark_Link,Dead_Hand boss;
```

This chapter covers the whole adult trading sequence for the Biggoron's Sword, plus the two Gerudo Valley Gold Skulltulas and the 50-token House of Skulltula reward. Once Link has Epona, the Biggoron Sword sidequest can be done at any time [[gf1-17-2]]. The sidequest also requires King Zora to be freed from the red ice and Epona to be available for the broken bridge [[ra-guide-11-7]]. You will run through Kakariko Village, the Lost Woods, Gerudo Valley, Death Mountain Trail, Zora's Domain and Lake Hylia, and finish back at the House of Skulltula [[checklist-sidequests-18-1, gf2-81-1, gf2-81-5, gf2-81-6]].

> [!TIP]
> **Check first:** Before you start, confirm that both Lost Woods Deku upgrades are done (A Horrible Face and Stocking Nuts; the Mask of Truth and Deku Nut upgrade pair) [[ra-comments-44-2, ra-comments-73-3]]. The Poacher's Saw blocks them later in this chapter [[ra-comments-44-3]]. Whether the Lost Woods Deku Nut upgrades can still be earned as an adult is unconfirmed: some players report success after the Forest Temple [[ra-comments-44-9]], while others report that the saw's flag locks them out [[ra-comments-44-3]]. Do not rely on the doubtful option. Get the upgrades before taking the saw. If either is still missing, put the Master Sword back in the Temple of Time to become Young Link and follow Chapter 6 (Stocking Nuts) or Chapter 10 (A Horrible Face) before you hand over the Odd Potion. One commenter says both Deku upgrades must be obtained before the whole succession of exchanges [[ra-comments-44-7]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** Three items in this sequence are timed: the Odd Mushroom (3 minutes), the Eyeball Frog (3 minutes) and the Eye Drops (4 minutes) [[gf2-53-2, ra-guide-12-1, ra-guide-12-2]]. Do not use transportation songs such as the Serenade of Water or the Bolero of Fire while delivering the Eyeball Frog or the Eye Drops [[gf1-215-4]]. Decide your route before you accept each timed item.

### Chapter Items to Collect (12 Trading Items Total)

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Pocket Egg | Cucco Lady, Kakariko Village (daytime) | **TAKE**, then play the Sun's Song twice to hatch it | [[checklist-sidequests-18-1, gf2-51-2]] |
| Pocket Cucco | Hatched from the Pocket Egg | **USE** next to sleeping Talon | [[gf1-356-3]] |
| Cojiro (blue Cucco) | Cucco Lady, after Talon is awake | **SHOW** the Pocket Cucco to her | [[gf1-356-4]] |
| Odd Mushroom | Man in the Lost Woods | **GIVE** Cojiro; 3-minute timer | [[gf2-53-2]] |
| Odd Potion (Odd Medicine) | Witch behind the Kakariko Potion Shop | **GIVE** the Odd Mushroom | [[gf2-53-3, gf3-44-4]] |
| Poacher's Saw | Kokiri girl in the Lost Woods | **GIVE** the Odd Potion | [[gf1-358-1]] |
| Broken Goron's Sword | Carpenter outside the tent, Gerudo Valley | **GIVE** the Poacher's Saw | [[gf1-358-2]] |
| Prescription | Biggoron, Death Mountain summit | **SHOW** the Broken Goron's Sword | [[gf2-114-2]] |
| Eyeball Frog | King Zora | **SHOW** the Prescription; 3-minute timer | [[gf1-360-5, ra-guide-12-1]] |
| Eye Drops | Scientist, Lakeside Laboratory | **GIVE** the Eyeball Frog; 4-minute timer | [[gf1-361-1, ra-guide-12-2]] |
| Claim Check | Biggoron | **GIVE** the Eye Drops | [[gf2-81-7]] |
| Biggoron's Sword | Biggoron, after 3 days | **SHOW** the Claim Check | [[gf1-363-2]] |

### Kakariko Village (Part 1: Egg to Cojiro)

Make sure it is daytime and talk to the Cucco Lady, who stands near the pasture at the rear of town, close to the entrance to the Graveyard [[checklist-sidequests-18-1, gf1-356-1]]. As Adult Link she gives you the **[Pocket Egg]** [[gf2-160-2, gf4-16-11]]. Play the Sun's Song twice; the egg hatches into the Pocket Cucco [[gf2-51-2, gf4-16-12]]. Another guide only says to play the song until it hatches, so keep going if it has not hatched yet [[gf1-356-2]].

Check that it is daytime again. Enter the building at the bottom of the stairs near the middle of town, where Talon is asleep on the bed [[gf1-356-3]]. Stand beside him with the Pocket Cucco equipped on a C button and use it, and Talon wakes up [[gf2-51-3]].

Go back to the Cucco Lady and use the Pocket Cucco near her. She is pleased and gives you **[Cojiro]**, a blue Cucco [[gf1-356-4, gf2-51-3, checklist-sidequests-18-2]].

If you have not yet learned the Song of Storms, the windmill is close by, and one guide suggests learning it before you leave [[checklist-sidequests-18-3]]. You play A, Down, Up, A, Down, Up [[gf1-394-3]].

### Lost Woods (Part 1: Cojiro to Odd Mushroom)

🗺️ **Map:** [View Lost Woods Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/54-lost-woods-map)

Enter the Lost Woods and go left from the entrance to the area where the Skull Kid used to stand [[checklist-sidequests-18-4, gf4-16-13]]. A man sits beside the stump [[gf2-113-4]]. Equip Cojiro on a C button and use it while standing in front of him, then accept his offer. He gives you the **[Odd Mushroom]** [[gf2-53-2]].

The timer starts the moment you accept: you have three minutes to reach the Potion Shop in Kakariko Village [[gf2-53-2, gf1-357-2]]. Do not stop for anything else.

### Kakariko Village (Part 2: Odd Mushroom to Odd Potion)

Run to the Kakariko Potion Shop, enter it, and go through the back door, then enter the red house to find the hidden witch's shop [[gf3-44-4]]. Climb the stairs and enter the other door at the back to reach the witch [[gf2-53-3]].

Use the Odd Mushroom while standing in front of her, and she makes the **[Odd Potion]** (called the Odd Medicine in one guide) [[gf2-53-3, gf3-44-4]]. One player reports she accepted it only on the second try [[checklist-sidequests-18-5]]. From now on she also sells blue potions for 100 rupees, per the same report [[checklist-sidequests-18-5]].

### Lost Woods (Part 2: Odd Potion to Poacher's Saw)

> [!CAUTION]
> **Point of No Return:** Taking the Poacher's Saw makes the Lost Woods Deku upgrades unobtainable (A Horrible Face, Stocking Nuts) [[ra-comments-44-3, ra-comments-73-2]]. Finish both Deku upgrades before you hand over the Odd Potion.

Go back to where the man was sitting in the Lost Woods. A Kokiri girl now stands in his place [[checklist-sidequests-18-6, gf2-53-4]]. Give her the Odd Potion and she hands over the **[Poacher's Saw]** [[gf1-358-1, gf3-167-1]].

### Gerudo Valley

Ride Epona to Gerudo Valley and jump the broken bridge, then dismount and continue on foot [[checklist-sidequests-15-7]]. Alternatively the Longshot can pull you across [[gf1-278-1]]. The Master Carpenter waits on the far side [[gf1-278-2]].

Stand in front of the carpenter outside the tent, equip the Poacher's Saw on a C button and use it. He recognizes it as his own and gives you the **[Broken Goron's Sword]** [[gf1-358-2, gf2-113-7]].

While you are here, collect the two Gold Skulltulas on this side of the bridge. Make it night with the Sun's Song if needed [[gf2-87-2]].

- **Skulltula #62:** behind the tents, on the wall. Hookshot it [[checklist-skulltulas-12-3, gf1-278-3]].
- **Skulltula #63:** on the back of the stone pillar to the left. Hookshot it [[checklist-skulltulas-12-4, gf3-197-3]].

Both require Epona or the Longshot to cross the broken bridge [[checklist-skulltulas-12-3, checklist-skulltulas-12-4]].

If you want extra rupees, breaking all the rocks near the carpenter with the Megaton Hammer reveals a chest with a Purple Rupee [[gf2-87-3, gf3-75-1]].

### Death Mountain Trail (Part 1: Biggoron and the Prescription)

Make your way to Kakariko Village and through the northern gate onto Death Mountain Trail [[gf1-60-2]]. Dodge the boulders that crash down the path [[gf1-169-1]]. Near the top, the rocks fall and a climbable wall leads up to Biggoron [[gf2-97-6, gf2-114-2]].

Stand on the right side of the cliff to get his attention, then show him the Broken Goron's Sword [[gf1-359-1]]. He says he cannot repair it until he has eyedrops from King Zora, and gives you the **[Prescription]** [[gf2-81-4, gf3-61-5]].

The Piece of Heart above Dodongo's Cavern (#22) is already yours from Chapter 4, where you backflipped onto the cliff [[gf1-66-3]]. The two Death Mountain Trail Skulltulas behind red rocks need the Megaton Hammer and are collected in Chapter 15 [[gf1-206-1, gf1-206-2]].

### Zora's Domain

🗺️ **Map:** [View Zora's River/Domain Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/56-zoras-river-domain-map)

King Zora is already free from the Ice Cavern chapter [[ra-guide-11-7]]. Stand on the platform in front of him and use the Prescription. He gives you the **[Eyeball Frog]**, which has to be delivered to the scientist in Lakeside Laboratory within three minutes [[gf2-81-5, gf1-360-5, ra-guide-12-1]].

Route to Lake Hylia inside the three minutes, as the sidequest guide runs it [[checklist-sidequests-19-1]]:

1. Leave Zora's Domain for Zora's River and take the portal on the left into the Lost Woods.
2. In the Lost Woods go straight, left, right, straight and left. This brings you to the Lost Woods bean plant you planted two lefts from the entrance in Chapter 10.
3. Ride the plant and jump off onto the bridge, then keep rolling toward Lake Hylia. The guide arrived with about 40 seconds left.

Do not play the Serenade of Water to shortcut [[gf1-215-4]]. The frozen-waterfall Skulltula here is collected in Chapter 21 [[gf1-472-1]].

### Lake Hylia

Go to the Lakeside Laboratory and enter. Use the frog next to the old man [[gf2-81-6, gf2-114-4]]. He mistakes it for his dinner, but then gives you the **[Eye Drops]** (World's Finest Eye Drops) [[gf1-361-1, gf4-17-2]].

You now have four minutes to climb back to Biggoron [[gf1-362-1, ra-guide-12-2]]. Step outside, get on Epona, and ride across Hyrule Field to Kakariko Village. Dismount, enter the village and take Death Mountain Trail to the summit [[gf1-362-2]]. Do not play the Bolero of Fire [[gf1-215-4]].

The laboratory crate Skulltula needs the Iron Boots and comes in Chapter 16 [[checklist-skulltulas-10-5]].

### Death Mountain Trail (Part 2: Claim Check and the Sword)

Climb the wall and use the Eye Drops in front of Biggoron to get the **[Claim Check]** [[gf2-81-7, gf4-17-3]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** Save the game before turning in the Claim Check [[ra-comments-96-7]]. Hardcore turns off save states, so use the game's own save. Mash A, not B, through the dialogue. One player reports that mashing B leaves a small window in which a sword slash cancels the trade [[ra-comments-96-4]], and another confirms mashing A earned the achievement in hardcore [[ra-comments-96-6]].

The sword takes three days. Play the Sun's Song six times to force three days to pass [[gf1-363-1, ra-guide-12-3]]. Another guide says about five times [[gf2-82-1]], so keep going until three days have passed [[gf4-17-4]]. Then show Biggoron the Claim Check and he gives you the **[Biggoron's Sword]** [[gf1-363-2, gf3-62-7]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Biggoron's Sword (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158186.png)
> *Obtain the Biggoron's Sword*


The Biggoron's Sword deals twice the damage of the Master Sword, but it needs both hands, so you cannot use a shield with it equipped [[gf1-363-3, gf3-62-8]]. It does not break [[gf1-400-3]].

Some players report problems: the sword showing in the equipment screen but being unselectable [[ra-comments-96-2]], and the achievement failing after buying the knife from Midgoron [[ra-comments-96-3]]. A commenter says the trade flag can be repaired by purchasing the Giant's Knife [[ra-comments-96-5]]. The Giant's Knife is sold by a giant Goron in Goron City [[gf2-61-6]].

### House of Skulltula

With 56 tokens, claim the 50-token reward at the House of Skulltula. The cursed family's house is the first house on the right as you enter from the main gate [[checklist-skulltulas-2-1]]. The rewards come in order: 10 tokens for the Adult's Wallet, 20 for the Stone of Agony, 30 for the Giant's Wallet, 40 for Bombchus and 50 for a Piece of Heart [[gf1-451-3, checklist-skulltulas-2-2, checklist-skulltulas-2-3, checklist-skulltulas-2-4, checklist-skulltulas-2-5]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** Collect the 40-token Bombchus reward before the 50-token reward. One player did not get the achievement after talking to the 50 NPC first [[ra-comments-108-3]]. Another says that, because of battery protection, the achievement only triggers if the reward event is repeated [[ra-comments-108-1]]. Save before collecting the reward [[ra-comments-108-2, ra-comments-108-4]].

Go inside and speak to the man in the upper right corner. He gives you the **[Piece of Heart]** [[gf2-60-6, gf3-134-1, gf4-17-10]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **My Broken Heart (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158198.png)
> *Collect 50 Gold Skulltulas and obtain your reward*


The Piece of Heart is collected as Adult Link without trouble [[gf2-60-6]]. This is Piece of Heart #32 [[checklist-sidequests-2-28]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave:** Make sure the Biggoron's Sword is in your equipment, that you have collected the Gerudo Valley tokens (#62 and #63), and that you have claimed every House of Skulltula reward up to 50 [[gf1-363-2, checklist-skulltulas-12-3, gf1-451-3]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 13 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 56 / 100 [[gf1-451-3]]
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 24 / 36 [[gf2-60-6]]
> - **Heart Containers:** 3 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 8 / 26
> - **Biggoron's Sword:** Obtained [[gf1-363-2]]
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 50 / 97 (275 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Both Lost Woods Deku upgrades are done before taking the Poacher's Saw
- [ ] Pocket Egg obtained and hatched (Sun's Song twice)
- [ ] Talon woken and Cojiro received
- [ ] Odd Mushroom delivered within 3 minutes and Odd Potion made
- [ ] Poacher's Saw received from the Kokiri girl
- [ ] Broken Goron's Sword received in Gerudo Valley
- [ ] Gerudo Valley Skulltulas #62 and #63 collected at night
- [ ] Prescription received from Biggoron
- [ ] Eyeball Frog delivered to Lakeside Laboratory within 3 minutes
- [ ] Eye Drops delivered to Biggoron within 4 minutes
- [ ] Game saved, then Claim Check turned in with A mashed
- [ ] Three days passed and Biggoron's Sword obtained (achievement 48975)
- [ ] 40-token reward claimed, then 50-token Piece of Heart collected (achievement 48247)

## Chapter 14: Forest Temple

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Sacred_Forest_Meadow["Sacred Forest Meadow"]
  Forest_Temple["Forest Temple"]
  Phantom_Ganon{{"⚔ Phantom Ganon"}}
  Sacred_Forest_Meadow -->|"Hookshot up to the entrance"| Forest_Temple
  Forest_Temple --> Phantom_Ganon
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Phantom_Ganon boss;
```

This chapter takes Adult Link through the Sacred Forest Meadow, where Sheik teaches the Minuet of Forest, and then through the Forest Temple, the first Adult Link dungeon. You collect the Fairy Bow, the Map, the Compass and the Boss Key, beat the four Poe Sisters, ride the elevator down and fight Phantom Ganon. Saria then gives you the Forest Medallion, and you return to the Temple of Time for the Prelude of Light [[gf1-141-5, gf1-143-1, gf1-163-3, gf1-167-1]].

Two achievements are in this chapter: **Drunken Master** (missable, earned against Phantom Ganon) and **Awakening** (Forest Medallion) [[ra-guide-12-4, ra-guide-12-6]].

> [!TIP]
> **Preparation:** The Hookshot is required for most of the Gold Skulltulas here [[checklist-skulltulas-13-1]]. Before the boss, have 30 arrows and full health, and set the Bow and two empty Bottles on your C buttons so you cannot grab another item by mistake [[ra-comments-29-1, ra-comments-29-4, ra-comments-30-10]]. One commenter suggests leaving the temple after getting the bow, winning the quiver upgrade in Kakariko and entering the fight with 40 arrows [[ra-comments-26-9, ra-comments-30-9]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Drunken Master (10 pts) is lost for the attempt if you use the sword, the Hookshot or any other item before Phantom Ganon is at 1 HP. Do not press the sword button at all, and do not switch to the Hookshot on the way to the boss [[ra-guide-12-4, ra-guide-12-5, ra-comments-26-1, ra-comments-26-2, ra-comments-26-12, ra-comments-28-1, ra-comments-28-9]]. One player used the Hookshot on a Skulltula along the way and that cancelled the achievement [[ra-comments-28-2]]. Plan the order: collect the Hookshot Gold Skulltulas first, then follow the commenters' advice to leave the temple and re-enter it before the boss, opening only the Bow or a Bottle [[ra-comments-28-10, ra-comments-29-2]].

### Sacred Forest Meadow

🗺️ **Map:** [View Sacred Forest Meadow Map by R351D3NT3V1L4](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/5354-sacred-forest-meadow-map)

The Sacred Forest Meadow lies in the midst of the Lost Woods, and the Forest Temple is in the midst of the meadow [[gf1-8-2, gf1-8-3]].

**Optional side stops (Stocking Nuts).** The Deku Nut upgrade is sold in a secret grotto revealed by bombing the large boulder just outside the Sacred Forest Meadow entrance. Two Business Scrubs are there, and one sells the upgrade for 40 Rupees [[ra-comments-73-4, gf3-141-1]]. The Stocking Nuts achievement refers to this one, not the Mask of Truth one on the forest stage [[ra-comments-73-1]]. One guide warns that this upgrade is permanently lost if you become adult before showing the Mask of Truth on the forest stage [[checklist-sidequests-14-2]]. A bomb placed in the meadow's first area also opens a cave where defeating two Wolfos produces a chest with a Purple Rupee [[gf3-44-8]].

#### The hedge maze

1. Enter the maze. The Scrubs are gone, replaced by Moriblins that charge if you enter their sight. Wait for each to face away and shoot it in the back with the Hookshot. Move slowly so it does not hear you [[gf1-141-1]].
2. Use Z to look around the first corner, kill the first Moriblin, go right and kill the second. At the end of that passage go right, peek around the next corner and kill the third. Turn left at the flowery passage and kill the fourth, then go right, then left at the end and into the pool of water [[gf1-141-1]].
3. Climb out of the pool and up the ladder on the left. If a Moriblin is there, wait until it turns away before climbing out [[gf1-141-2]].
4. **Gold Skulltula (Sacred Forest Meadow).** This one appears at night only, as adult, and needs the Hookshot. At the top of the ladder go left a short way, face left and Hookshot it [[checklist-skulltulas-3-7, gf1-456-1, gf1-141-2]].
5. A pit in the center of the maze holds a Fairy Fountain, useful before the dungeon [[gf1-141-3]]. The Minuet of Forest also brings you back to it later [[gf1-395-2]].

#### Sheik and the Minuet of Forest

1. Climb back down the ladder and go up the stairs opposite it. At the top a club-wielding Moblin sends shockwaves down the passage [[gf1-141-4]].
2. Dodge the shockwaves by running left and right between each one, run around behind him and hit his back with two Jump Attacks. He drops a large number of Rupees [[gf1-141-4]]. Another source says his club pattern is left, right, left and that it takes three sword slashes [[gf2-53-7, gf3-251-3]].
3. Go up the stairs behind him and step onto the Triforce pedestal. Sheik appears and teaches the **Minuet of Forest** (A, C-Up, C-Left, C-Right, C-Left, C-Right) [[gf1-141-5, gf1-395-1]]. Playing it warps you here from anywhere, giving access to the Forest Temple and a Fairy Fountain [[gf1-395-2]].
4. Hookshot the large tree branch above the shattered staircase and go through the doorway into the temple [[gf1-141-6]].

### Forest Temple

The central goal is to kill the four Poe Sisters who steal flames from the torches, which retracts the central elevator. The dungeon loops around that central chamber [[gf1-143-1]]. Killing the four Poes opens the way to the boss [[gf1-9-9]]. Wallmasters drag you back to the dungeon entrance if they land on you [[gf1-151-1]].

#### Chapter Items to Collect

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Small Key | Chest on tree branch in entrance courtyard | **TAKE** | [[gf1-144-3]] |
| Small Key | Chest in Lower Stalfos Chamber | **TAKE** | [[gf1-146-2]] |
| Dungeon Map | Chest in Balcony Hallway | **TAKE** | [[gf1-148-1]] |
| Small Key | Chest at end of drained well | **TAKE** | [[gf1-149-1]] |
| Fairy Bow | Chest after Upper Stalfos Room | **TAKE** | [[gf1-152-4]] |
| Small Key | Chest after West Stairwell Poe (red) | **TAKE** | [[gf1-153-4]] |
| Boss Key | Blue chest in West Twisted Hallway | **TAKE** | [[gf1-154-2]] |
| Small Key | Chest after Floormaster | **TAKE** | [[gf1-156-3]] |
| Compass | Chest after East Stairwell Poe | **TAKE** | [[gf1-158-2]] |
| Heart Container | After Phantom Ganon | **TAKE** | [[gf1-163-2]] |

#### Entrance courtyard

1. Step forward and kill the two Gray Wolfos by backflipping out of range when they attack and immediately landing a Jump Attack while their backs are turned [[gf1-144-1]].
2. Turn right and climb the vines on the wall up to the trees, then go left a little [[gf3-45-2, gf2-54-2]].
3. **Gold Skulltula (Forest Temple, first room).** From the top of the tree, look to the top-right of the ivy wall and Hookshot the Skulltula [[gf1-144-2, checklist-skulltulas-13-1]].
4. Edge along the branch, jump to the adjacent branch and open the chest at its end for a **Small Key** [[gf1-144-3]]. Drop down and go through the door under the stone archway [[gf1-144-3]].

#### South Passage and Torch Room

1. A Big Skulltula is in the hall. A single Hookshot hit kills it instantly [[gf1-145-1]]. If you are attempting Drunken Master on this pass, kill it with the bow instead [[ra-comments-28-4]]. Go through the door at the far end [[gf1-145-1]].
2. In the Torch Room, the four Poe Sisters appear and steal the four flames, lowering the central elevator [[gf1-145-2]].
3. Run across to the opposite end, jump onto the ledge and go up the stairs. **Gold Skulltula:** look right at the top and Hookshot it [[gf1-145-3, checklist-skulltulas-13-2]].

#### North Passage and Lower Stalfos Chamber

1. The door at the top of the stairs leads to a Blue Bubble. Hold up your shield while Z-targeting it and let it touch you to put out its flame, then kill it [[gf1-146-3, gf2-55-4]].
2. In the Lower Stalfos Chamber, Z-target one Stalfos (they will not attack while you target one), circle, keep your shield up and attack from the side or behind right after they attack [[gf1-146-1]]. Deku Nuts can interrupt their attacks [[checklist-sidequests-7-3]].
3. When both die, a chest in the center holds a **Small Key**. Smash the pots at the rear for a Recovery Heart and a Fairy [[gf1-146-2]]. Return to the Torch Room [[gf1-146-2]].

#### Song of Time block and the West Courtyard

1. Run down the stairs and go right to the small hallway. Stand by the blue block and play the Song of Time (right, A, down, right, A, down) to reveal a door [[gf1-147-1, gf2-54-7]].
2. In the West Courtyard, kill the Big Deku Baba and the Octorok. Hookshot the first two Skullwalltulas on the ivy wall on the right (the third is out of reach), then climb as far right as possible to avoid it and enter the alcove door at the top [[gf1-147-1, gf1-147-2]].

#### Balcony Hallway and East Courtyard

1. Kill the Blue Bubble. The far door opens and a chest appears with the **Dungeon Map** [[gf1-148-1]].
2. In the East Courtyard, kill the Deku Baba and Hookshot the Skullwalltula on the ivy wall. Hookshot the white circle above the left balcony and step on the switch to drain the well [[gf1-148-1, gf1-148-2]].
3. Jump down. Kill the Octorok and Big Deku Baba. From the right side of the dead-end dirt path, Hookshot the chest on the cliff. Aim at the part of the chest closer to the wall so you stay up. The chest holds a Recovery Heart [[gf1-148-3, gf2-55-2, gf3-45-10]].
4. **Gold Skulltula:** it clings to the wall around the corner from the chest [[gf1-148-3, gf1-491-2]].
5. Jump back down, go to the right side and drop into the well [[gf1-148-3]].

#### The Well

Run to the end collecting the Recovery Hearts. Open the chest for a **Small Key**, then climb the ivy wall back to the West Courtyard [[gf1-149-1, gf1-149-2, gf2-55-1]]. Return through the archway door to the Torch Room, climb the ledge on the right, go up the stairs and use a Small Key on the locked door [[gf1-149-3]].

#### West Passage and Stone Block Room

1. Kill the Big Skulltula and go through the far door [[gf1-149-3]].
2. Defeat the Blue Bubble, climb the ladder, then the next on the right, go left and left again through the doorway. A stone block with floor arrows is to the left [[gf1-150-1, gf1-150-2]].
3. Go forward through the doorway, left down the hall, left, and left through another doorway to reach the block's far side. Push it forward until a ladder appears on the right (ignore it), keep pushing until a gap opens on your left, slip through behind the block and push it back until the gap closes [[gf1-150-3]].
4. Push it forward until it stops, return down the passage, go around the ledge taking three rights, and push it again until it falls into a small alcove. Climb onto it for the third level [[gf1-150-4]].
5. Climb the ladder, turn right, right again through the doorway, and push the block in front of you until it stops. Drop back down [[gf1-150-5]].
6. Climb on the placed block, run down the passage and push the third-level block into the pit, then climb to the top [[gf1-150-6]].
7. Go right after two ledges, up the ladder, right around the corner, and kill or dodge the two Blue Bubbles before the door [[gf1-150-7]].

#### West Twisted Hallway to the Bow

1. Run to the end of the hall. Navi warns of Wallmasters. Run in large circles to dodge and kill them when they land, or enter First Person Mode to make them vanish [[gf1-151-1, gf1-151-2]].
2. Jump to the pillar ahead, then to the right, and enter the door [[gf1-151-3]].
3. This is the West Stairwell (stairs on the left, red Poe Sister in the portraits). It is a series of stairs with three portraits. Run down both stairsets and through the door at the bottom of the second [[gf1-152-1, gf1-153-1]].
4. In the Upper Stalfos Room, beware the pit. A Stalfos walks over it. Beat it and an elevator drops with two more Stalfos. Beat both quickly or they regenerate. A chest then appears with the **Fairy Bow** [[gf1-152-2, gf1-152-3, gf1-152-4]]. The quiver holds 30 arrows [[gf1-406-2]]. A trick for the pair: hit each twice with Jump Attacks, then quickly finish both [[gf2-55-7]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** The Hookshot Gold Skulltulas still ahead (and the Hookshot-only route steps) conflict with Drunken Master. Finish collecting every token you want first. Then leave the temple, re-enter, and go straight to the boss using only the Bow or a Bottle [[ra-comments-28-9, ra-comments-28-10, ra-comments-29-2]].

#### West Stairwell Poe (red)

1. Return to the West Stairwell through the Upper Stalfos Room door. Shoot the Poe's portrait with the Bow from as far away as possible, at the bottom or top of the stairwell. If you get close, she moves. Burn all three portraits and she appears at the bottom [[gf1-153-1, gf1-153-2]].
2. Shoot her with arrows. Do not Z-target her, because she vanishes. If you get too close she spins her torch [[gf1-153-3]].
3. The chest that appears has a **Small Key**. Her flame returns to the torch here and in the Torch Room [[gf1-153-4]].

#### Back to the Stone Block Room

Climb back up and return through the West Twisted Hallway to the Stone Block Room. Turn around, look above the door and shoot the silver eye to straighten the hallway on the other side, then go back through [[gf1-153-4, gf1-154-1]]. On the third level, a golden eye shot gives a chest with arrows [[gf1-157-2, gf3-46-7]].

#### Boss Key

Run down the straightened hall and drop into the room. Watch for Wallmasters. Open the blue chest for the **Boss Key**, then drop through the pit [[gf1-154-2]]. Kill both Blue Bubbles to open the south door [[gf1-154-3]].

**Gold Skulltula (Second floor):** you arrive on the upper West Courtyard ledge. Kill the Big Deku Baba, jump to the broken column platform with two Recovery Hearts, walk slowly to the far edge, look up and Hookshot the Skulltula [[gf1-155-1, gf1-155-2, checklist-skulltulas-13-4]]. Take the alcove door at the far end of the ledge [[gf1-155-3]].

#### Floormaster Room

Attack the Floormaster when it is not glowing green. It splits into three small ones; kill them quickly with the Bow. The chest has a **Small Key** [[gf1-156-1, gf1-156-2, gf1-156-3]]. Return to the previous room, turn right on the ledge and go through the door [[gf1-157-1]]. Drop down in the Stone Block Room, then shoot the silver eye above the doorway to re-twist the hallway and return to the West Stairwell and Upper Stalfos Room [[gf1-157-3]].

#### East Stairwell (blue Poe) and Compass

Take the opposite door in the Upper Stalfos Room [[gf1-158-1]]. Shoot all three portraits as before and kill the Poe. The chest has the **Compass** [[gf1-158-2]]. Climb to the top and go through the locked door [[gf1-158-3]].

#### East Twisted Hallway and Flaming Arrow Room

1. Drop to the floor, wait for the Wallmaster, dodge and kill it [[gf1-158-4]].
2. Climb the tall ladder to the right of the entrance door and go through the hallway with two Green Bubbles. Shoot them when their flame turns off, or ignore them, then enter the end door [[gf1-158-5, gf1-158-6]].
3. In the Flaming Arrow Room, ride the rotating platforms and shoot an arrow through the center torch flame onto the frozen eyeball switch. The previous room twists [[gf1-159-1, gf2-57-1]].
4. Return, run to the end of the twisted hall, drop down, kill the Wallmaster and drop down the pit in the corner to the right [[gf1-159-2]].

#### Crushing Ceiling Room and Puzzle Block Room

1. Run south through the gaps as the ceiling falls. Kill Big Skulltulas with arrows. Halfway down on the right, step on the switch to open the exit [[gf1-160-1]]. A chest here has a bundle of arrows [[gf2-57-2]].
2. In the Puzzle Block Room, shoot the portrait, push the mismatched fifth block aside and assemble the other four into the yellow Poe Sister within a minute. Kill her [[gf1-160-2, gf2-57-3]].
3. Climb the ledge near the torch and exit [[gf1-160-3]].

#### East Hallway and Amy

Kill the Big Skulltula on the ceiling and go through the far door [[gf1-161-1]]. Jump into the Torch Room to meet Amy, who splits into four. The real Amy spins when she splits. Shoot her with an arrow and repeat. Z-targeting is fine [[gf1-161-2, gf1-516-4]]. When Amy dies, the last flame returns and the elevator rises. Go down it [[gf1-161-3]].

#### Basement 1: the rotating room

Push the wall handles to rotate the room. One guide's route: grab the left handle's north side and push it south, then step on the switch in the northwest room. Push the right handle's north side south and step on the switch in the eastern alcove. Push the eastern side of the handle west, enter the southwest room, kill the Big Skulltula and open the chest for arrows. Finally push the south side of the handle north and step on the south switch to open the grating over the north doorway [[gf1-162-1, gf1-162-2, gf1-162-3, gf1-162-5]]. Another guide describes the same room as needing three switches, pressed by rotating the room clockwise in turns [[gf2-57-5, gf2-57-7]].

**Gold Skulltula (Basement 1):** in the southwest alcove, on the wall to the left; Hookshot it [[gf1-162-4, checklist-skulltulas-13-5]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave the basement:** Take the Basement 1 token now if you want it. After this point, the Drunken Master rules mean no Hookshot until the achievement pops [[ra-comments-28-2, ra-comments-28-9]].

### Phantom Ganon

Go to the right and up the stairs into the large stage. When you turn to leave, spears trap you and Phantom Ganon appears [[gf1-162-6]]. Biggoron Sword owners are advised to stay with the Master Sword for the dungeon, though that sword is not used until the achievement pops [[gf1-144-1, ra-comments-26-6]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Save before this fight. If you break the rules, Drunken Master (10 pts) is lost for that attempt. Never press the sword button. If you are aiming with the bow, press Z to stop, not B, and do not switch to the Hookshot [[ra-comments-26-12, ra-comments-29-3, ra-comments-30-2, ra-comments-30-4]]. Pressing the sword button in phase 1 cancels the bow and can make the achievement fail [[ra-comments-26-13]]. If you fail, one player reset the emulator without saving and retried the fight [[ra-comments-26-14]].

**Phase 1 (bow only).**
1. He rides a flying horse into a painting and comes out of two paintings. The fake turns back at the last minute; the real one emerges and scatters electricity [[gf1-519-6]].
2. Shoot him with an arrow as he emerges from the brighter painting [[gf1-519-5]]. The loudest footsteps and the darker Ganon with whiter whites are the real one. Stand in the middle of the corner floor lines so you are not hit if you miss [[ra-guide-12-4]]. Wait for the portal, then shoot into it [[gf2-58-1]]. The corner spots let you take careful shots and save arrows for phase 2 [[ra-comments-26-10, ra-comments-27-7]].
3. Three hits start phase 2 [[gf1-519-7]]. Phase 1 needs 3 arrows and phase 2 about 11 [[ra-comments-27-4]].

**Phase 2 (bottle, then bow).**
1. Have an empty bottle equipped. It is easier to hit the ball than you expect, and the bottle lingers longer than you would expect, so swing a little early [[ra-guide-12-5, ra-comments-28-5]].
2. Reflect his lightning balls to stun him. Standing at the far end is easier [[gf1-519-8, gf1-519-9]]. Stand back to give yourself time to judge the swing [[ra-comments-30-8]].
3. When he falls, shoot arrows while he is down (about four per stun). Do not hit Navi, or he gets up before you can shoot [[ra-comments-29-14, ra-comments-30-13]].
4. Repeat until he reaches 1 HP. Do not waste arrows [[ra-guide-12-5, ra-comments-29-7]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Drunken Master (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158129.png)
> *Lower Phantom Ganon to 1 HP by using only the Bow and Bottles*


The achievement triggers at 1 HP. Only then may you use the sword for the final blow [[ra-comments-26-2, ra-comments-28-3, ra-comments-29-16]]. The challenge indicator may not appear, but the achievement still works [[ra-comments-27-12, ra-comments-28-12]]. If your arrows run out without it popping, restart and try again [[ra-comments-28-11]].

**Finishing.** Once he is stunned, use one Jump Attack and then regular strikes. Repeat the reflect-and-attack cycle until he dies [[gf1-519-10, gf1-163-1]]. Take the Heart Container that appears and step into the beam of light [[gf1-163-2, gf3-49-1]].

### Chamber of Sages

1. Step into the beam of light after taking the Heart Container [[gf1-163-2, gf3-49-1]].
2. You arrive in the Chamber of the Sages, where Saria appears and reveals that she is the Sage of the Forest Temple [[gf1-163-3, gf3-49-2]].
3. Saria gives you her power in the form of the **Forest Medallion**. It is the reward for breaking the curse of the temple [[gf1-163-3, gf3-161-2, gf1-422-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Awakening (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158130.png)
> *Awake Saria and obtain the Forest Medallion*


4. Afterwards you are returned outside to Kokiri Forest, where a new Deku Tree sprouts [[gf1-163-3]].

### Temple of Time

Return to the Temple of Time and run into the Master Sword chamber. Sheik teaches the **Prelude of Light** (C-Up, C-Right, C-Up, C-Right, C-Left, C-Up), which warps you to the Temple of Time [[gf1-167-1, gf1-397-3]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave:** Confirm you picked up the Map, Compass and Boss Key [[ra-comments-123-14]], and that you have the Gold Skulltulas listed above (Forest Temple tokens #64-#68 and the meadow token #7) [[checklist-skulltulas-13-1, checklist-skulltulas-13-2, checklist-skulltulas-13-3, checklist-skulltulas-13-4, checklist-skulltulas-13-5, checklist-skulltulas-3-7]]. The Skulltula in the northeast room can be reached by Hookshotting the island chest, or more easily from the nearby room you fall into from the second twisted room [[checklist-skulltulas-13-3]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 14 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 62 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 24 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 4 / 8 (Forest Temple Heart Container [[gf1-163-2]])
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 11 / 26
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 52 / 97 (290 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Minuet of Forest learned from Sheik
- [ ] Meadow Gold Skulltula collected
- [ ] Fairy Bow obtained
- [ ] Dungeon Map, Compass and Boss Key collected
- [ ] All four Poe Sisters defeated
- [ ] Drunken Master (48818) unlocked
- [ ] Heart Container taken
- [ ] Forest Medallion obtained (Awakening, 48819)
- [ ] Prelude of Light learned in the Temple of Time

## Chapter 15: Death Mountain Crater and the Fire Temple

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Kakariko_Village["Kakariko Village"]
  Death_Mountain_Trail["Death Mountain Trail"]
  Death_Mountain_Crater["Death Mountain Crater"]
  Fire_Temple["Fire Temple"]
  King_Dodongo{{"⚔ King Dodongo"}}
  Volvagia{{"⚔ Volvagia"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Dead_Hand{{"⚔ Dead Hand"}}
  Flare_Dancer{{"⚔ Flare Dancer"}}
  Kakariko_Village -->|"Show Zelda's Letter to the gate guard"| Death_Mountain_Trail
  Death_Mountain_Crater --> Fire_Temple
  Fire_Temple --> Volvagia
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class King_Dodongo,Volvagia,Bongo_Bongo,Dead_Hand,Flare_Dancer boss;
```

Sniper Elite is available as soon as you have the Fairy Bow and arrive in Kakariko Village as an adult. After that, head up Death Mountain to learn the Bolero of Fire, collect two crater Pieces of Heart, and clear the Fire Temple. Hot Hot Hot is the one achievement here that can be lost: you must beat Volvagia without wearing the Goron Tunic. The Fire Medallion (Eternal Brothers) follows the boss. [[gf1-134-2, gf2-60-7, gf1-172-2, ra-guide-13-3]]

### Preparation and Overview

The Fire Temple does not strictly need the Fairy Bow from the Forest Temple, but you have it, and the map chest is easiest with it. [[gf1-18-1, gf1-187-1]]

- **Goron Tunic:** Wear it in the Crater and the Fire Temple, because it removes the heat time limit. Without it, Young Link dies when the heat timer runs out in the Crater, and fairies do not save him. [[gf1-171-4, gf1-435-2]]
- **Tunic caution:** One commenter confirms that if you get the Longshot and beat the Fire Temple before getting the Goron Tunic, the tunic becomes completely unobtainable. Collect it from Link the Goron before finishing the temple. [[ra-comments-91-2]]
- **Magic:** Bring at least one or two Green Potions, since Din's Fire eats a small magic bar quickly. [[gf3-98-10]]
- **Fairies:** Bottle some for the boss. Two sit in the containers on the right side of the room before the boss room, and two more are in the pots near the platform by the boss door. [[ra-comments-32-21, ra-comments-32-22]]
- **Dungeon structure:** The boss chamber is next door to the entrance. The challenge is reaching the top floor to activate the route to its door. Keys come from rescuing the trapped Gorons, and even the Boss Key is held by one of them. [[gf1-178-1, gf1-178-2]]

### Chapter Items to Collect (Fire Temple dungeon items)

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Dungeon Map | Chest in the Lava Curtain Room, reached through the silver-eye door off the Narrow Span Room | **TAKE** | [[gf1-187-2]] |
| Compass | Chest at the end of the Compass Corridor off the Fire Trap Room | **TAKE** | [[gf1-194-1]] |
| Megaton Hammer | Chest in the Tippy-Top Room (5F) | **TAKE** | [[gf1-197-2]] |
| Boss Key | Ornate chest in the Goron's cell in the Boss Key Prison Room | **TAKE** | [[gf1-202-1]] |

### Kakariko Village

#### Sniper Elite: the adult Shooting Gallery

Head for the building across from the Windmill. It was the unfinished structure in your childhood and is now the Shooting Gallery. [[gf1-168-2, gf1-134-2, ra-guide-13-1]] Pay 20 Rupees to play with the Fairy Bow. [[gf3-55-1]]

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** Only play for the reward once you hold the Fairy Bow. One commenter reports that winning before getting arrows paid 50 rupees but did not unlock the achievement. Another had the bigger quiver yet no achievement after winning at the wrong stage. [[ra-comments-90-2, ra-comments-90-3]]

Your goal is to hit all ten Rupees in a single run. The prize is the Big Quiver, which raises arrow capacity from 30 to 40. [[gf2-60-7, gf2-102-3, gf1-407-1]]

- Hitting at least eight of ten earns a free replay. [[gf2-60-8]]
- The targets follow these patterns: [[gf1-168-3]]
  1. In the middle. [[gf1-168-3]]
  2. From the left, then from the right. [[gf1-168-3]]
  3. Jumps from the middle. [[gf1-168-3]]
  4. From the left and right at once. [[gf1-168-3]]
  5. Two rupees from the right, going left. [[gf1-168-3]]
  6. Two rupees from the left, going right. [[gf1-168-3]]
- If a single blue rupee comes from the left, the next one always comes from the right. [[gf1-168-4]]
- Memorize the patterns to predict what comes next. You can also tell what the last pattern will be from what has already appeared. [[gf1-511-2]]
- On RetroArch, a stick sensitivity of 0.9 or 0.8 is recommended. [[ra-comments-90-4]]
- Arrows are sold at the Kakariko Bazaar: 10 for 20 Rupees, 30 for 60, 50 for 90. [[gf2-140-43, gf2-140-40, gf2-140-41]]


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Sniper Elite (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158180.png)
> *Obtain a reward at the Shooting Gallery as Adult Link*


When you are finished, leave by the northern gate to Death Mountain Trail. You already showed Zelda's Letter to the gate guard as a child. [[gf1-58-2, gf1-60-2]]

### Death Mountain Trail

Climb the trail and take the right fork at the large wooden post with the red flag to reach Goron City. [[gf1-62-5]]

If you have not yet received the Goron Tunic, bomb the small rolling Goron on the floor beneath Goron City's top level to stop him. Talk to him and ask about both the Dragon and the Gorons. He then gives you the Goron Tunic and opens Darunia's Chamber. [[gf2-61-5, gf1-171-3]]

Put the tunic on to remove the time limit in the Crater and the Fire Temple. [[gf1-171-4]]

Drop to the bottom floor and enter Darunia's Chamber. Grab the statue at the top of the room and drag it backward to reveal the secret path into the Crater. [[gf2-62-1, checklist-sidequests-20-6]]

### Death Mountain Crater

#### The Bolero of Fire

Go left as you enter the Crater to find a broken bridge. Stand at the edge and Hookshot the wooden beam hanging above the far side to pull yourself across. [[gf1-172-1, gf1-204-1]]

Walk a short way down the bridge and Sheik appears to teach you the Bolero of Fire, which warps you to the Fire Temple at any time. [[gf1-172-2, gf3-55-3]]

The notes are Down C, A, Down C, A, Right C, Down C, Right C, Down C. [[gf1-395-3]]

#### Piece of Heart (#24): the cliff-face ledge

If you planted a bean in the soft soil beside the warp-in point as a child, it has grown into a platform. Ride it up until it passes close to the high cliff near the south entrance. At that point, jump out to the rock beside the cliff. [[gf1-176-2, gf1-436-1]]

From there, walk slowly off the edge to grab the cliff wall and climb down a short way to an alcove halfway down the cliff face. [[gf1-176-2, gf3-26-9]]

Do not drop into the alcove from above, because you will fall into the lava. Climb below it, then climb up into it. Take the Piece of Heart. [[gf1-176-2, gf1-435-1]]

Play the Bolero of Fire to warp back to the pedestal, which is faster than climbing back up. [[gf1-176-2]]

#### Piece of Heart (#25): riding the bean plant

Step onto the grown plant and ride it up. Near the start of its path it loops around a tall pile of rubble with a Piece of Heart on top. Jump off onto the rubble to collect it. [[gf1-176-1, checklist-sidequests-33-3, gf2-62-4]]

Drop back to the Triforce pedestal afterward. [[gf1-176-1]]

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave the Crater, check that you wear the Goron Tunic as you head to the temple, and that you carry fairies and Green Potions. [[gf1-171-4, gf3-98-10, ra-comments-32-4]]

From the pedestal, cross the bridge north into the large stone structure. Climb down the ladder into the pit and take the doorway beside it into the Fire Temple. [[gf1-176-3]]

### Fire Temple

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Hot Hot Hot requires defeating Volvagia without the Goron Tunic. Wear the tunic while crossing the temple, but take it off before the boss fight, not after. Fairies are allowed for this challenge. [[ra-comments-33-7, gf3-59-1, gf4-23-3, ra-comments-32-1]]
>
> Timing the swap: take it off in the boss room just before you jump to the arena platform, and equip the green tunic only when you are very close to the platform. [[ra-comments-32-5, ra-comments-33-16]]

#### Entrance Hall

Run up the stairs, watching for Fire Keese, and take the door on the left at the top. [[gf1-179-1]]

#### Pillar Room (first visit)

Darunia appears here, runs off to fight Volvagia, and begs you to rescue the imprisoned Gorons. [[gf1-179-2]]

Look left after the scene. Jump across the lava pillars to the platform in the left corner, then step on the switch to open the cell. Talk to the Goron and open the chest for a **Small Key**. [[gf1-179-3, gf3-55-6]]

If you need fairies, two sit on the right side of the room before the boss room. [[ra-comments-32-21]]

Return to the Entrance Hall.

#### Entrance Hall to Lava Cavern

Run straight across the hall and enter the door opposite. [[gf1-180-1]]

In the Lava Cavern, work left across the platforms in the middle of the room, along the border, or straight through the lava. [[gf1-180-1]]

#### Prison Corridor 1

Run to the end of the hall, step on the switch, talk to the Goron, and open his chest for a **Small Key**. Return to the Lava Cavern. [[gf1-180-2]]

#### Block of Time, Killer Tile Room (Skulltula #69)

Play the Song of Time near the Block of Time to move it. Climb onto it and take the door on top of the sandstone platform. [[gf1-181-1, gf2-63-4]]

The room beyond has flying floor tiles. Shield yourself against them (hold R). [[gf3-58-4]]

A Like-Like can swallow you and steal your Goron Tunic and Hylian Shield. Kill it from a distance, and if it swallows you, kill it to get both back. [[gf1-181-2, gf3-99-1, gf3-55-7]]

Kill the Gold Skulltula on the wall (#69) and take the token, then return to the previous room. [[gf1-181-3, checklist-skulltulas-14-1]]

#### Prison Corridor 2

Jump onto the sandstone platform across the cavern and bomb the oddly colored wall to reveal a door. Go through it. [[gf1-182-1]]

Step on the switch to open the prison cell directly in front of you, talk to the Goron, and open the chest for a **Small Key**. [[gf1-182-2, gf1-182-4]]

Leave by the north door of the cavern, which is the door to your right as you come out of the corridor. [[gf1-182-3]]

#### Lava Column Room

Run forward and slide down the steep log bridge into the cage. Grab the cage wall and climb to its top level. [[gf1-183-1]]

On the east sandstone pillar, push the massive block off the ledge onto the lava column that periodically bursts in the middle of the room. Jump down, stand on the block, and ride it up as an elevator the next time the column erupts to reach the upper level door. [[gf1-183-2]]

#### Three-Tiered Room

Fight the Fire Slug aggressively. Raise your shield if it expands into a column shape, because it is about to attack. [[gf1-184-1]]

Now solve the block puzzle:

1. Push the stone block off the ledge so it lands in the stone track below. It can only be pushed one way. [[gf1-184-2]]
2. Jump down, get in front of the block, and pull it backward onto the stone face carved into the floor. [[gf1-184-2]]
3. Drop a bomb off the cliff so it lands near the Diamond Switch, then quickly climb onto the ledge behind you. [[gf1-184-3]]
4. The explosion activates the switch and temporarily removes the pillar of flames around the wire wall. Climb the wire wall to the top level and go through the door at the top. [[gf1-184-3]]

#### Boulder Maze (3F, Skulltula #70)

Use a bomb on the left side of the wall in the hollow alcove. It isn't discolored, but it sounds hollow when struck with your sword. [[gf1-185-1, gf1-493-3]]

Kill the Gold Skulltula on the other side (#70) and take the token. [[gf1-185-1, checklist-skulltulas-14-2]]

Keeping your right side to the wall, follow the perimeter of the room to another small alcove with a door inside it. Go through. [[gf1-185-2]]

#### Prison Corridor 3

Run down the hall, step on the switch, talk to the Goron, and open his chest for a **Small Key**. [[gf1-186-1]]

Go around to the far left side of the maze, where a Goron's prison cell sits at a dead end. Step on the switch in front of it, speak to the Goron, and take his **Small Key**. [[gf1-186-2]]

Return to the locked door you passed earlier and go through it. [[gf1-186-3]]

#### Narrow Span Room and the Dungeon Map

Shoot the Silver Eye on the wall with an arrow to unlock the sealed door on the right from where you enter. [[gf1-187-1]]

In the Lava Curtain Room beyond it, look right as you enter to find a chest containing the **Dungeon Map**. [[gf1-187-2]]

If you prefer not to use the bow, you can climb over the side of the cage and drop directly onto the map. [[checklist-sidequests-21-1]]

When you come out of the Lava Curtain Room, enter the locked door on your right. [[gf1-187-3]]

#### Lava Curtain Room

Run all the way along the wire platform; the curtain moves slowly and is easy to dodge. Jump across the gap to the next wire platform, keep running, jump onto a third wire platform, and then jump onto the large sandstone steps. Climb to the top of the stairs and go through the door on the left. [[gf1-188-1]]

#### Top of the Boulder Maze

1. Look left to a small platform with a switch. Jump out and step on it. A cell door on the far side of the room opens, revealing another Goron. [[gf1-189-1]]
2. Speak with that Goron and open his chest for a **Small Key**. [[gf1-189-2]]
3. Drop a bomb onto the huge gash in the floor to reveal a massive pit, then drop to the bottom. [[gf1-189-3]]

At the bottom, step on the switch in the hall and the Goron's double-sided cell doors open. Speak with him and open his chest for a **Small Key**. [[gf1-190-1, gf1-190-2]]

Turn around, go back down the hall, and climb the wire wall back up to the Boulder Maze. [[gf1-190-3]]

> [!TIP]
> The Scarecrow's Song route here (play it to summon Pierre on the wall platform, then Hookshot to the circular target and ride the rising platform) leads to extra Skulltulas. Per the planned route, Skulltula #73 in this area is saved for chapter 21, when you have the Longshot. [[gf1-190-4, gf1-190-5, checklist-skulltulas-14-5]]

#### Two-Tiered Room and Time Trial Room

The room reached by the rising platform has a Gold Skulltula in the corner wire mesh of its upper level. Beyond it, the Time Trial Room holds another on the cliff wall above the pit, plus a chest with 200 rupees behind a flame pillar that a switch removes only temporarily. These are optional extras. [[gf1-191-1, gf1-191-2, gf1-191-3]]

Dropping through the pit in the center of the Time Trial Room returns you to the Narrow Span room. [[gf1-191-3]]

#### Back to the Lava Curtain Room, West Corridor

From the Narrow Span Room, take the south door, which is to the right of the sealed door and closest to the silver eye target, to return to the Lava Curtain Room. [[gf1-192-1]]

Jump from the jutting platform up to the west-center sandstone ledge. It looks like a jump you can't make, but you can. Go through the locked door. [[gf1-192-2]]

#### Fire Trap Room and the Compass

Keeping the wall on your right, move slowly forward until a wall of flames appears in your path. [[gf1-193-1]]

1. Go to the left, watching out for a roving boulder. [[gf1-193-1]]
2. Take a right immediately after passing the first post on your right. [[gf1-193-1]]
3. Go right again and head back toward the wall to find a door. [[gf1-193-1]]

Run to the end of the hall and open the chest for the **Compass**. [[gf1-194-1]]

Back in the Fire Trap Room, watch out for the giant totem pole whose faces spit fire. Hug the wall and go around, keeping your left side facing the wall. When another wall of flames appears, go right and pass between the pair of poles directly in front of you. Take a left immediately on the other side to reach the door. [[gf1-194-2]]

#### Fire maze and the dummy door

Go left and pass between the pair of posts just to the left of the fire wall to reach the switch. The giant curtain of flames on the north side of the room disappears. [[gf1-195-1]]

> [!TIP]
> The door here is a dummy that smashes down on you if you try to open it. Blow it up with a bomb, which reveals the real door behind it. [[gf1-195-2, gf3-57-2]]

Unable to verify from collected sources: the exact route between the Compass Corridor and this fire maze beyond what is described above.

#### Upper Flare Dancer Room (mini-boss)

To damage the Flare Dancer, first use the Hookshot to separate its heart from its flaming body. The heart then runs around the room very quickly. [[gf1-196-1, gf1-516-5]]

Circle the room in the opposite direction so you intercept the heart halfway around, and hit it with a jump attack. Use a regular strike if you have difficulty timing the jump. [[gf1-196-2, gf1-516-5]]

The Biggoron Sword gives a faster victory. [[gf1-516-6]]

Alternatively, charge an Advanced Spin Slash and release it as the exposed heart gets near you. Three or four hits are enough to defeat it. [[gf3-58-1, gf2-65-7, gf2-124-1]]

Once it dies, the pillar of flames in the middle of the room disappears. Climb onto the middle platform, which begins to rise, and take the door at the top of the shaft. [[gf1-196-3]]

#### Diamond Switch Room and Tippy-Top Room (5F, Megaton Hammer, Skulltula #71)

Once up on the second floor, drop a bomb down to the bottom floor, right next to the diamond switch. The explosion removes the pillar of flames surrounding the mesh ladder leading to the next level. [[gf1-197-1]]

In the Tippy-Top Room, a Gold Skulltula (#71) sits near the entrance. [[checklist-skulltulas-14-3]]

Step on the switch to make the pillar of flames around a chest near the top of the room disappear. Run up the narrow staircase on the inside of the giant ring to reach the chest before time runs out. [[gf1-197-2]]

Open the large chest for the **Megaton Hammer**. [[gf1-197-2, gf2-66-1]]

Stand on the large stone face coming out of the floor and smash it with the Hammer. The block falls to the room below. [[gf1-197-3]]

Smash both pieces of the corner totem pole from the left or right until they are gone to reveal a door. [[gf1-197-4]]

#### Hidden Staircase Room, Fire Trap Room (revisit)

Smash the stone block that rises out of the pit with the Hammer, and a stairwell appears inside the pit. [[gf1-198-1]]

Drop the wooden box onto the blue switch at the bottom of the stairs to unseal the door. Go through it. [[gf1-198-2]]

Stand on the stone block in the corner of the chamber above the Fire Trap Room and smash it with the Hammer to fall to the floor below. The block creates a booster that lets you reach doors in this room that were previously out of reach. [[gf1-198-3]]

Smash the rusted switch beside the sealed door on the higher ledge to unseal the door, then go through it. [[gf1-198-4]]

#### Two-Floored Prison (revisit)

Play the Song of Time to transport the Block of Time down to the floor. [[gf1-199-1]]

Jump across to the opposite ledge and smash the rusty switch with the Hammer to open the cell below. Drop down, speak with the Goron, and take the **Small Key** from the chest in his cell. [[gf1-199-2, gf1-199-3]]

Jump out to the large stone block held by scaffolding, stand on top of it, and smash it with the Hammer. Ride the block all the way down. [[gf1-199-4]]

#### Pillar Room, Entrance Hall, Room of Fiery Enemies

On the east side of the Entrance Hall, smash the totem pole pieces with the Hammer, standing on the left or right side of the totem, to reveal a door. Go through it. [[gf1-200-1]]

The next room is full of Fire Keese and Fire Slugs. Kill every enemy and the sealed door on the other end opens. [[gf1-200-2]]

#### Deja Vu Room (Skulltula #72)

Flying floor tiles, a Like-Like and a Gold Skulltula are here. Kill the Like-Like, then kill the Gold Skulltula on the rear wall (#72) and take its token. [[gf1-201-1, checklist-skulltulas-14-4, gf1-495-1]]

#### Lower Flare Dancer Room

Fight another Flare Dancer the same way. Use the Biggoron Sword if you have it, and don't bother with the Megaton Hammer, which attacks too slowly. [[gf1-201-2]]

When it dies, its pillar of flames disappears and reveals a chest with **Bombs**. [[gf1-201-3, gf2-66-5]]

#### Boss Key Prison Room

Smash the rusted switch with the Hammer to open the double-sided cell. Talk to the Goron and open the ornate chest for the **Boss Key**. [[gf1-202-1, gf2-66-6]]

Back in the Pillar Room, the knocked-down pillar now lets you jump across to the Boss Key door that you couldn't reach before. Jump across and go through it. [[gf1-202-2]]

### Boss: Volvagia

Before you jump onto the slab, double-check your tunic: you must not be wearing the Goron Tunic. Jump to the large slab in the middle of the cavern and run to the middle of the platform to begin. [[gf1-519-11, ra-comments-32-5]]

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** If you are wearing the Goron Tunic during the Volvagia fight, Hot Hot Hot is lost. Remember to put the Goron Tunic back on afterward. [[gf3-59-1, ra-comments-32-12]]

**Heat timer and preparation**

- Without the tunic, a countdown applies. One player saw 24 seconds after changing armor. Each fairy revival adds roughly 24 seconds. [[ra-comments-32-3, ra-comments-32-17]]
- The timer is tied to your current heart count, so more hearts means more time. 12 hearts gave 1:44, and 18 hearts gave 2:14. [[ra-comments-33-4, ra-comments-34-11, ra-comments-33-5]]
- Bring fairies. Players report that having three bottles with fairies is enough regardless of heart count. [[ra-comments-32-4, ra-comments-32-23]]
- Din's Fire stalls the clock while Volvagia is airborne. Magic Potions refill the bar. [[ra-comments-32-18, ra-comments-33-19]]
- The emulator's Full speed setting makes the timer run faster. [[ra-comments-32-8]]

**Phase 1: pits and Hammer**

Volvagia emerges at random from one of nine pits, which you can identify by smoke and cinders around them. Run toward that pit and keep clear of her fiery hair sweep. [[gf1-202-3, gf3-262-1, ra-comments-134-15]]

Smash her head with a Megaton Hammer jump attack to stun her. Then land one more hit with a jump attack from the Hammer, or from the Biggoron Sword. [[gf1-520-1, gf1-520-2]]

With the Hammer drawn, press A while Z-targeting her to jump attack. Landing all of these jump attacks kills her in four hits. [[ra-comments-34-3, ra-comments-33-17]]

As she takes damage, she may fake a pit with fire and surface in another. [[gf3-262-2]]

**Phase 2: flying**

When she flies and breathes fire, run continuously in a small circle near the center. [[gf1-202-4, gf1-520-3]]

The speedy alternative is to drop off the arena edge and hang from it. This makes her lose interest and go back underground immediately. [[ra-guide-13-3, gf3-59-2]]

**Phase 3: falling boulders**

When she flies to the ceiling and the roof starts raining boulders, run to the edge and hang off. Some rocks can still go over the edge, so there is a little randomness. [[gf1-202-5, ra-comments-134-13]]

Reported runs used about four to eight Hammer jump attacks in total. [[ra-comments-33-10, ra-comments-32-11]]

Commenters also hang from the edge while she flies, then Hammer her when she surfaces and follow up with a two-handed jump slash for double damage [[ra-comments-34-4, ra-comments-34-5]]. Use the Biggoron's Sword you got in Chapter 13 for that follow-up. Do not buy the Giant's Knife: it breaks easily [[gf2-61-6]], and one player's Biggoron's Sword achievement failed after buying it [[ra-comments-96-3]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Hot Hot Hot (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158131.png)
> *Defeat Volvagia without wearing the Goron Tunic*


When Volvagia dies, take the **Heart Container** that appears. [[gf1-202-6, gf3-60-1]]

Walk into the ring of light to meet Darunia, the Sage of Fire. In the Chamber of Sages he awakens and gives you the **Fire Medallion**. [[gf1-202-7, gf3-60-2, ra-guide-13-4]]


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Eternal Brothers (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158132.png)
> *Awake Darunia and obtain the Fire Medallion*


### Death Mountain Crater (after the temple)

Use the Hookshot on the planks hanging off the cliff to cross the shattered bridge again. [[gf1-204-1]]

Two rocks cover a partially visible doorway on the other side of the drawbridge. Smash them with the Hammer, possibly more than once, to open a Great Fairy Fountain. [[gf1-204-2, checklist-sidequests-22-1]]

Stand on the Triforce emblem and play Zelda's Lullaby. The Great Fairy doubles your magic meter. [[gf3-61-2, gf1-416-1]]

Outside, smash the gray rock you are thrown into to open a path to upper Crater. Jump to the ladder and climb to its top. [[gf1-205-2]]

### Death Mountain Trail (night Skulltulas #32 and #33)

Make it night with the Sun's Song if needed. These Skulltulas only appear at night. [[gf1-206-1, gf3-61-3]]

- **#33:** On the long path up to the cliff face, where rocks used to rain on you, smash the red rock that sits flush against the wall with the Hammer. [[gf1-206-1, gf3-61-4, checklist-skulltulas-7-4]]
- **#32:** At the cliff above Dodongo's Cavern, where the Bomb Flower used to be, smash the red rock to reveal a Gold Skulltula. [[gf1-206-2, gf3-61-3, checklist-skulltulas-7-3]]

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave Death Mountain, confirm that you have the Fire Medallion, the Megaton Hammer, the Map, Compass, and Boss Key, and that you swapped the Goron Tunic back on. Then play the Prelude of Light to return to the Temple of Time. [[gf1-206-3, ra-comments-32-12, ra-comments-123-15]]

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 15 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 68 / 100 (Fire Temple #69, #70, #71, #72 collected; #32 and #33 at night; #73 deferred to chapter 21) [[checklist-skulltulas-14-1, checklist-skulltulas-14-2, checklist-skulltulas-14-3, checklist-skulltulas-14-4, checklist-skulltulas-7-3, checklist-skulltulas-7-4]]
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 26 / 36 (Crater ledge #24 and bean-plant #25) [[gf3-126-3, gf1-176-1]]
> - **Heart Containers:** 5 / 8 (Volvagia) [[gf1-202-6]]
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 14 / 26 (Fire Temple Map, Compass, Boss Key) [[gf1-187-2, gf1-194-1, gf1-202-1]]
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 55 / 97 (310 / 690 points)

- [ ] Learned the Bolero of Fire from Sheik across the broken bridge

- [ ] Crater bean-plant Piece of Heart (#25)
