## Chapter 16: Water Temple

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Lake_Hylia["Lake Hylia"]
  Water_Temple["Water Temple"]
  Morpha{{"⚔ Morpha"}}
  Dark_Link{{"⚔ Shadow Link (Dark Link)"}}
  Lake_Hylia --> Water_Temple
  Water_Temple --> Morpha
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Morpha,Dark_Link boss;
```

### Chapter overview

This chapter covers two linked stops: Lake Hylia as an adult, where you unlock the Scarecrow's Song, and the Water Temple beneath the lake [[gf1-8-5, gf1-9-3]]. The temple is built around raising and lowering the water so you can reach doors on the different levels of a central tall room, the "Vertical Hallway" [[gf1-9-11, gf1-220-1]]. You will earn the Longshot, the Map, the Compass and the Boss Key here, and you will finish by defeating Morpha and receiving the Water Medallion from Ruto [[gf1-389-1, gf1-241-2]].

After the temple you return to Lake Hylia to shoot the rising sun for the Fire Arrows [[gf1-243-1]]. You also collect the Longshot-only Skulltula in the lake's tree and the underwater crate Skulltula in the laboratory [[gf1-243-2, gf1-218-2]].

Four achievements are in this chapter:
- **Pierre the Wanderer**
- **Day of the Tentacles** (missable)
- **Bride to Be**
- **The Flaming Arrow**

**What you need before you start:**
- **Iron Boots** from the Ice Cavern. Sheik also teaches the Serenade of Water there, which warps you to the Water Temple in Lake Hylia [[gf1-213-3, gf1-213-4, gf1-396-2]].
- **Zora Tunic** from King Zora. It cancels the underwater time limit while you wear the Iron Boots [[gf1-403-3, gf3-62-3]].
- **Hookshot, Fairy Bow, Bombs, Ocarina, Zelda's Lullaby and the Song of Time.** The route below uses them all [[gf1-221-4, gf1-223-1, gf1-232-2]].
- **The eight-note melody you taught the scarecrow as a child.** If you forgot it or never taught one, Sheik at the Temple of Time explains how to return to childhood and teach Pierre a new song [[gf1-165-2]].

The Water Temple only needs the Ice Cavern and Forest Temple finished first, so it can be done before the Fire Temple [[gf1-18-2]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** **Day of the Tentacles** (Defeat Morpha without falling into the water or getting captured by its tentacle) can be missed during the Morpha fight [[gf1-240-3, gf1-240-4, ra-comments-38-1, ra-comments-38-2]]. Do not enter the water, and do not stand on the four inner platforms [[gf1-240-3]]. The full plan is in the Morpha section below.

### Chapter Items to Collect

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Scarecrow's Song | Lake Hylia shore scarecrow | **PLAY** your childhood eight-note song as Adult Link | [[gf1-165-1, gf1-398-2]] |
| Compass | Chest in the water-pillar room off the Stinger tunnel | **OPEN** (bomb the diamond switch, or arrow the crystal) | [[gf1-221-4, gf2-72-1]] |
| Dungeon Map | Chest in the Spike Room beside Water Switch One | **OPEN** after defeating all Spikes | [[gf1-222-2]] |
| Small Keys | Chests across the temple (see route) | **OPEN** each | [[gf1-223-3, gf1-225-2, gf1-226-4, gf1-227-1, gf1-232-4, gf1-235-2]] |
| Longshot | Chest after Shadow Link | **OPEN** | [[gf1-232-1]] |
| Boss Key | Ornate chest in the Boss Key Room | **OPEN** | [[gf1-239-2]] |
| Heart Container | Dried-up pool after Morpha | **TAKE** | [[gf1-241-1]] |
| Water Medallion | From Ruto in the Chamber of the Sages | **RECEIVE** | [[gf1-241-2]] |
| Fire Arrows | Smaller island east of Lake Hylia's main island | **SHOOT** the rising sun, then swim over | [[gf1-243-1]] |
| Skulltulas #77-#81 | Water Temple | **KILL** and collect each token | [[checklist-skulltulas-15-4, checklist-skulltulas-15-5, checklist-skulltulas-15-6, checklist-skulltulas-15-7, checklist-skulltulas-15-8]] |
| Skulltula #54 | Dead tree, Lake Hylia (night) | **LONGSHOT** up the tree | [[checklist-skulltulas-10-4]] |
| Skulltula #55 | Laboratory crate | **ROLL** into it with the Iron Boots | [[checklist-skulltulas-10-5]] |
| Piece of Heart | Laboratory roof tower | **CLIMB** via bean plant or Pierre | [[gf1-445-1, checklist-heartpieces-4-8]] |

### Lake Hylia

#### The shore scarecrow: Pierre the Wanderer

Go to the scarecrows on the northeastern shore of Lake Hylia. This is where you taught your song as a child [[gf1-165-1]]. As Adult Link, play the same eight-note song you taught as Young Link. The scarecrow remembers it and it becomes the Scarecrow's Song [[gf1-398-2, gf3-235-1]].

Sources disagree about which of the two scarecrows asks for the song. One says the one closest to the shore, another the one farthest from the water [[gf3-30-9, checklist-sidequests-11-8]]. Another source names the scarecrow Bonooru [[ra-guide-14-1]]. Play it to the same scarecrow you taught as a child.

The song must be exactly 8 notes [[ra-comments-86-2]]. It must also have at least two different notes and cannot be any other song in the game [[gf1-398-1]].

Once learned, playing the song at certain spots makes Pierre appear as a makeshift Hookshot target [[gf1-398-2, gf2-153-16]]. Navi flutters at those spots [[checklist-sidequests-17-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Pierre the Wanderer (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158177.png)
> *Obtain the services of Pierre the Scarecrow*


#### Entering the Water Temple

Equip the Iron Boots and the Hookshot and jump down to the gate underwater on the north side of the central island [[gf2-71-7, gf1-218-3]]. You will find a door sealed by an iron grating [[gf1-218-3]]. Shoot the diamond switch above the door with the Hookshot and the gate unseals [[gf1-218-3, gf4-26-1]]. Enter to begin the dungeon.

> [!TIP]
> Wear the Zora Tunic before you step into the water, since it cancels the Iron Boots' underwater time limit [[gf1-403-3]]. Expect to swap between the Kokiri Boots and the Iron Boots many times in this dungeon [[gf1-221-1]].

### Water Temple

Orientation: the temple has a central tall room with doors on three levels. Three Triforce plaques ("Water Switches") change the water level when you play Zelda's Lullaby in front of them [[gf1-220-1, gf1-222-1]]:
- **Switch One** lowers the water to the lowest level.
- **Switch Two** sets it to the second-highest level.
- **Switch Three** sets it to the maximum.

The Hookshot is the only weapon usable underwater [[gf1-221-2]].

#### The first hallway

Equip the Iron Boots and sink to the bottom of the right-hand hallway pool. Run down the hallway and grab the stone block set in the wall directly in front of you. Pull it back as far as it will go [[gf1-221-1]].

#### The Stinger tunnel and the Compass

Follow the underwater passage onward. Stingers appear here, and one Hookshot shot kills each [[gf1-221-2]].

At the end of the hall, look up above the spikes to the ledge and Hookshot to the target there. Turn around, look at the ceiling, and Hookshot to the furthest of the three ceiling targets. That pulls you out of the water [[gf1-221-3]].

This room holds a pillar of water and a diamond switch in the corner. Drop a bomb near the switch, then run to the pillar. When the bomb explodes the switch fires and the pillar lowers temporarily. Open the chest for the **[Compass]** [[gf1-221-4]]. An alternative is to stand beside the water spray and chest and shoot the crystal with an arrow, then open the large chest while it is available [[gf2-72-1, gf3-64-1]].

Unable to verify from collected sources how the route returns from the Compass room to the main chamber.

#### Ruto, Water Switch One and the Map

In the main room, use the Iron Boots to sink to the bottom floor. Spike enemies are here, and you kill each with two Hookshot shots, one to stun and one to kill [[gf1-221-5]]. On the east side of the room, marked by two torches, is a tunnel. Run down it and you meet Ruto again [[gf1-221-6]].

Rise to the air pocket. On the wall is a Triforce plaque. Play Zelda's Lullaby in front of it and the water drops to its lowest level. This is Water Switch One [[gf1-222-1, gf2-72-3]]. Go through the door to the left of the plaque into the Spike Room [[gf1-222-1]].

The room is full of Spikes. Hit each twice with the Hookshot and a chest appears with the **[Dungeon Map]** [[gf1-222-2, gf3-64-2]]. Another method is to run away until they turn into small rocks, then use a spin attack [[gf2-72-2]].

#### Ruto's room: torches and the first Small Key

Go to the room where you met Ruto. Stand at the entrance so the middle flame lines up with a corner torch. Shoot arrows through the flame so the arrowheads catch fire, and light the corner lanterns to unseal the door [[gf1-223-1, gf2-72-4, gf3-64-3]]. Go through the door.

In the Shell Blade room, the Shell Blades are invulnerable until they briefly open their mouths just before attacking. Fire the Hookshot into the open mouth to kill them [[gf1-223-2]]. Kill them all and a chest appears with a **[Small Key]** [[gf1-223-3]]. Return through the only door to the previous room [[gf1-223-3]].

#### South hallway: Skulltula #77

Go down the south hallway and use a Bomb on the oddly colored floor tile at its end to reveal a water-filled pit. Equip the Iron Boots and sink [[gf1-224-1]]. When you land, head west and defeat a Shell Blade with the Hookshot [[gf1-224-2]].

On the next floor, climb onto the ledge and step on the switch. The water in this room rises slightly and a Hookshot target appears on the ledge above [[gf1-224-3]]. The switch to open the grating is on the other side of it, so use a Super Spin Attack to reach it [[gf1-224-4]]. Kill the Gold Skulltula on the other side and take its token. This is **Skulltula #77** [[gf1-224-5, checklist-skulltulas-15-4]]. It requires the Iron Boots [[gf1-498-1]].

#### The west block, the pillar and the Whirlpool Room

On the west side of the chamber, jump onto the wooden platform, grab the stone block and push it forward until it falls into a water-filled pit [[gf1-224-6]]. Climb out of the water at the top of the passage and strike the diamond switch on the right. A pillar of water rises from the pit in the middle of the room. Jump onto the pillar and then across to the other side before the time limit runs out [[gf1-224-7]].

In the Whirlpool Room, look in the mouth of the dragon statue on the way down for a diamond switch. To the right of the statue is a small alcove with a Hookshot target [[gf1-225-1]]. Pull yourself to the alcove and stand on its edge as far from the statue as possible. From there you can just barely hit the corner of the switch with the Hookshot [[gf1-225-1]].

Climb out of the pool behind the grating and open the chest for another **[Small Key]** [[gf1-225-2]]. A diamond switch in this small room reopens the grating below. Strike it, then sink back down before time runs out [[gf1-225-3]]. Exit through the door in the alcove back to the Vertical Hallway [[gf1-233-1]].

#### Central Tower: Water Switch Two and another Small Key

On the south side of the tower in the middle of the Vertical Hallway is a locked door. Go through it [[gf1-226-1]].

You find Water Switch Two here. Play Zelda's Lullaby to raise the water to the second-highest level [[gf1-226-2]]. A passage appears below, so equip the Iron Boots, sink to the bottom level and go down it [[gf1-226-2]].

Hit the corner switch with the Hookshot. A grating opens in the ceiling, releasing six Spikes and three Shell Blades. Defeat them all and a grating opens across from the switch [[gf1-226-3]]. Switch to the Kokiri Boots and float up the new passage. At the top, climb out and open the chest for a **[Small Key]** [[gf1-226-4, gf3-64-8]].

#### The gash in the wall

Use Bombs to blow open the huge gash in the wall. Enter the opening and open the chest on the other side for another **[Small Key]** [[gf1-227-1, gf3-64-7]].

#### Water Pillar Room and Water Switch Three

Stand on the low water pillar and activate the diamond switch with the Hookshot or an arrow. The pillar lifts you through the shaft in the ceiling [[gf1-228-1]]. A Tektite waits at the top. Charge a Super Spin Attack as you ride up and unleash it when you see the Tektite [[gf1-228-2]].

You reach Water Switch Three, the final plaque. Play Zelda's Lullaby in front of it to raise the water to the maximum height [[gf1-228-3]].

#### Waterfall-Elevator Room

At the edge of the cliff, look to your right to see a Gold Skulltula very high on the wall. You cannot reach it yet [[gf1-229-1]].

The elevators that steadily descend in the waterfall all have Hookshot targets on their fronts. Latch onto one and pull yourself progressively higher until you reach the top of the waterfall [[gf1-229-2]]. Platforms in this room move down with the waterfall and the water knocks you off once they pass behind it, so hook one while it is moving down, then quickly hook the one above [[gf2-74-2]].

#### Dragon's Head Room

Shoot the diamond switch in the middle of the room to raise and lower the dragon heads, which reveals Hookshot targets. Climb onto a lowered head, then shoot the switch while standing on it. The head rises and lets you reach the alcove on top of the room [[gf1-230-1]].

Defeat the Like Like behind the large spikes by throwing bombs at it [[gf1-230-2]]. Use the Hookshot target on the ceiling near the door to pull yourself over the spikes blocking the path [[gf1-230-3]].

#### The Serene Pool: Shadow Link

Run to the island in the middle and keep running around it until Dark Link appears near the tree [[gf1-231-1]].

**Shadow Link (Dark Link) strategy:**
- **Do not Z-target him** with regular sword attacks. Targeting makes you clash swords endlessly, and without it he lines up his deflections less easily [[gf1-231-3, gf2-75-1, gf3-66-7]].
- **Do not use the stab attack** (forward plus attack while Z-targeted). He jumps on your sword and hits you every time [[gf1-517-2, gf2-75-2]].
- **The Megaton Hammer is the easy option.** He cannot deflect it and will not try [[gf1-231-2, gf1-517-1]].
- **Other options from the sources:** the Iron Boots slow his movements, Din's Fire is effective, and a Z-targeted thrust with the Biggoron's Sword hits him consistently [[gf3-66-3, gf3-66-1, gf3-66-4]]. A Deku Nut stuns him only once [[gf3-66-6]].
- His health equals yours, and each Master Sword hit takes one heart [[gf3-66-2]].

When he falls, the pool disappears and the north door unlocks [[gf1-231-4]].

#### Longshot Room and the river

Open the chest in front of you to get the **[Longshot]**, which has double the Hookshot's range [[gf1-232-1]]. Go around behind the chest to a Block of Time in the floor. Play the Song of Time to move it and drop down the pit beyond [[gf1-232-2, gf2-75-4]].

As you pass the second corner in the river you will see a Gold Skulltula hanging on the ceiling directly above the third whirlpool. Use the Iron Boots to come to a stop and grab the Gold Skulltula and its token from the bottom of the river. This is **Skulltula #78** [[gf1-232-3, checklist-skulltulas-15-5, gf1-498-4]].

Near the fifth whirlpool, at the final corner of the river, climb onto the small platform and shoot the golden eye switch with an arrow. The grating across from the platform opens. Use the Longshot on the chest inside to pull yourself over before the time limit runs out and the grating closes, then open it for a **[Small Key]** [[gf1-232-4, gf3-67-4]].

#### Skulltula #80 and the water reset

Float to the top floor of the main chamber and go through the west door to the Waterfall-Elevator Room [[gf1-233-2]]. Look on the wall to the right and use the Longshot to kill the Gold Skulltula and take its token. This is **Skulltula #80** [[gf1-233-3, gf1-499-5, checklist-skulltulas-15-7]]. Return to the Vertical Hallway [[gf1-233-3]].

Equip the Iron Boots, drop to the bottom level and go through the east hall back to the room where you met Ruto. Switch to the Kokiri Boots, float all the way up and play Zelda's Lullaby in front of the plaque to lower the water to its bottom level [[gf1-234-1]].

#### Central Tower again: Skulltula #79

Pull yourself up to the second level of the tower with the Longshot and play Zelda's Lullaby in front of the plaque to set the water to its middle height [[gf1-234-2]]. Climb onto the floating platform and look high on the wall near the ceiling. Longshot the Gold Skulltula and take its token. This is **Skulltula #79** [[gf1-234-3, checklist-skulltulas-15-6]]. Then go out the door to the second level of the Vertical Hallway [[gf1-234-3]].

One source says the tower door is sealed by an unlit torch. Light it with a Fire Arrow, or by shooting an arrow through the nearby lit torch into the unlit one, and the door unseals [[gf1-499-2]]. It also says that after going through you spot the Skulltula on the ceiling above and take it with the Longshot [[gf1-499-3]].

#### South side of the tower: another Small Key

Work around to the south side of the tower's exterior, where a doorway is covered by a grating. Shoot the eye in the water with an arrow to make the grating open [[gf1-235-1]]. Push the stone block forward until it stops, then take the passage that opens to the right and open the chest for a **[Small Key]** [[gf1-235-2, gf3-67-6]].

Ride the water pillar to the third floor and go through the door at the top [[gf1-236-1]]. Play Zelda's Lullaby at the plaque to raise the water back to its maximum height [[gf1-236-2]].

#### Toward the Boss Key

Equip the Iron Boots, sink all the way to the bottom level and head for the north end of the room to find a doorway with a deep pit. Sink down it, go down a short hallway, then change back to the Kokiri Boots to float to the surface [[gf1-236-3]].

In the next pond, equip the Iron Boots and walk along the bottom, watching for boulders. Run to the northeast corner, switch back to the Kokiri Boots, and as you float up grab the ledge on the north corner of the room and pull yourself up [[gf1-237-1]].

In the Stone Block Room, bomb the walls in the northwest and northeast corners to reveal a hidden tunnel with a stone block. Push it forward until it falls into the pool of water, pressing down on the switch and permanently activating it. The water level rises [[gf1-237-2, gf2-76-4]].

In the Water Pillar Triplets Room, step on the switch in the corner and three water pillars rise and the door across the room unlocks [[gf1-238-1]]. To cross fastest, jump only to the second pillar and then straight for the door [[gf1-238-2]].

#### Skulltula #81 and the Boss Key

Run forward into the passage of water, watching for rolling boulders. Look to the right and kill the Gold Skulltula on the wall above the water pit, then take its token [[gf1-239-1]]. Another source places it behind the waterfall in the tunnel right before the Boss Key and says to use the Longshot for both kill and token. This is **Skulltula #81** [[gf1-500-1, gf1-500-2, checklist-skulltulas-15-8]].

Use the Iron Boots to drop down the water pit beneath the Skulltula [[gf1-239-1]]. Kill the Shell Blade, then float to the top and open the ornate chest for the **[Boss Key]** [[gf3-67-10, gf1-239-2]].

#### Blade Trap Room and the boss door

Head to the top level of the Vertical Hallway and go to the north side of the tower. Use the Longshot on the dragon's head to pull yourself up and go through the door behind the statue [[gf1-240-1]].

In the Blade Trap Room, climb the steep hill past three slow Blade Traps using the Kokiri Boots, and keep pressing forward. Once at the top of the ramp, enter the Boss Door [[gf1-240-2]].

### Boss: Morpha

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Day of the Tentacles requires beating Morpha without falling into the water or being grabbed [[gf1-240-3, gf1-240-4, ra-guide-14-2]]. Follow the corner plan below.

**What it does:**
- Morpha spins in the water to generate tentacle arms, which grab you and hurl you into the wall [[gf1-520-7]].
- Damage figures differ by source: four to six hearts, about one heart for a hit and up to three for a grab, or three hearts for a grab [[gf1-520-5, gf2-77-1, gf3-68-1]].
- Its nucleus (the large white brain/eye) usually enters one of the arms [[gf1-520-7]].
- It spins rapidly in the water when it is preparing a new arm [[gf1-520-7]].

**The plan:**
1. **Stay out of the water and off the four inner platforms.** The water gives Morpha an easy shot at you, and standing on the platforms lets it attack from several sides [[gf1-240-3, gf1-520-6]]. A commenter notes that jumping in the water and swimming to a platform before Morpha spawns did not disqualify them, as long as they got back to a corner without falling in [[ra-comments-38-5]]. Do not rely on that, and stay on the perimeter.
2. **Go to a corner as soon as the fight starts.** The guide says Morpha cannot reach you in a corner, and a commenter had the same result [[ra-guide-14-2, ra-comments-38-2]].
3. **Camp with the Longshot.** The Longshot outranges Morpha's arms by a considerable distance [[gf1-520-7, ra-comments-38-1]].
4. **When the nucleus enters an arm, Longshot it.** This stuns it long enough for one or two hits [[gf1-240-4, gf1-521-1]]. Aim near the end of the tentacle [[gf2-77-2]].
5. **Hit it hard.** Use a Jump Attack with the Biggoron's Sword, or the Megaton Hammer if you lack it [[gf1-240-4]]. A spin attack is another option [[gf2-126-3]]. One commenter pulled with the Longshot and finished with the Megaton Hammer, repeating until it died [[ra-comments-38-9]].
6. **Longshot between slashes** so the brain does not escape [[gf3-68-2]].
7. **Repeat.** After a hit, Morpha bounces around the room, then returns to the water and spawns more arms [[gf1-521-1]].

**Extra tips from commenters:**
- A faster but less safe method is to Longshot Morpha, go around it, and trap it against a wall. A second tentacle may spawn and put you in grab range [[ra-comments-38-3, ra-comments-38-4]].
- Another commenter pulled Morpha into a wall corner and trapped it there [[ra-comments-38-6]].
- When fighting normally, keep your distance and watch for two tentacles spawning close together, which is the one dangerous moment [[ra-comments-38-7]].
- Only two tentacles exist, so circle the edges and do not jump onto the platforms [[ra-comments-38-10]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Day of the Tentacles (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158135.png)
> *Defeat Morpha without falling into the water or getting captured by its tentacle*


After the fight, take the Heart Container left in the dried-up pool [[gf1-241-1, gf3-68-3]].

Enter the beam of light to reach the Chamber of the Sages. Ruto reveals she is the Sage of Water and gives you the Water Medallion [[gf1-241-2, gf3-68-4, gf1-422-3]]. Lake Hylia fills back up with water [[gf1-241-3]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Bride to Be (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158136.png)
> *Obtain the Water Medallion from Princess Ruto*


### Lake Hylia after the Water Temple

#### The Fire Arrows

After the cutscene you are in Lake Hylia. Stand on the stone at the plaque set into the ground on the east side of the island [[gf1-243-1, ra-guide-14-3]]. Aim at the sun and fire an arrow into it as it rises [[gf3-70-2, ra-guide-14-3]]. The Fire Arrows fall onto the smaller island east of the larger one. Swim over and take them [[gf1-243-1, gf1-389-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **The Flaming Arrow (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158183.png)
> *Obtain the Fire Arrow*


If you miss, play the Sun's Song twice to retry [[ra-guide-14-3]]. Another source says the Sun's Song will not work and you must wait for time to pass normally [[gf1-389-2]]. These sources conflict, so start with the first and wait for sunrise if it fails. Playing the Sun's Song on the Fire Arrow platform spawns a fairy that restores health and magic, but it cannot be bottled [[gf3-70-3]].

#### Skulltula #54: the dead tree (night)

Return to the larger island and Longshot to the top of the dead tree. Aim for the bit of the trunk that sticks up over the large branch [[gf1-243-2, gf1-476-2]]. Make it night and a Gold Skulltula appears on top of the trunk. Do not jump up to kill it. Stand on the branch and use the Longshot [[gf1-243-2, checklist-sidequests-29-2]]. This is **Skulltula #54** [[checklist-skulltulas-10-4]].

#### Skulltula #55: the laboratory crate

Enter the laboratory and equip the Iron Boots. Sink to the bottom of the pool and roll into the crate. This reveals the game's only underwater Gold Skulltula. You cannot use a sword underwater, so kill it with the Hookshot and collect the token [[gf1-218-2, gf3-62-10, gf4-17-15]]. This is **Skulltula #55** [[checklist-skulltulas-10-5]].

#### Laboratory roof Piece of Heart

Ride the grown Magic Bean plant beside the laboratory to its roof. This works only if you planted a bean in the soft soil as a child [[gf1-445-1, gf2-71-3, gf3-174-6]]. Jump onto the roof, then climb the ladder to the high tower. The Guays can harass you on the way up [[gf1-216-2, gf3-41-12]]. The Piece of Heart is on top [[gf1-216-2]].

Alternatively, reach the roof using the Scarecrow's Song and Hookshot [[gf1-445-1, checklist-heartpieces-4-8]]. Unable to verify from collected sources exactly where on the shore to play the song for this.

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave Lake Hylia, check that you have the Fire Arrows, Skulltulas #54 and #55, and the roof Piece of Heart. The sources show a Prelude of Light warp to the Temple of Time as the next step after the tree Skulltula [[gf1-243-3]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 16 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 75 / 100 (Water Temple #77-#81, Lake Hylia #54 and #55) [[checklist-skulltulas-15-4, checklist-skulltulas-15-5, checklist-skulltulas-15-6, checklist-skulltulas-15-7, checklist-skulltulas-15-8, checklist-skulltulas-10-4, checklist-skulltulas-10-5]]
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 27 / 36 (laboratory roof) [[gf1-216-2]]
> - **Heart Containers:** 6 / 8 (Morpha) [[gf1-241-1]]
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 17 / 26 (Water Temple Map, Compass, Boss Key) [[ra-comments-123-17]]
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 59 / 97 (335 / 690 points)

### Chapter checklist

- [ ] Played the childhood eight-note song to the scarecrow as Adult Link
- [ ] Entered the Water Temple with Iron Boots, Zora Tunic and Hookshot
- [ ] Opened the Compass chest
- [ ] Opened the Dungeon Map chest
- [ ] Collected every Small Key chest on the route
- [ ] Beat Shadow Link without Z-targeting him and got the Longshot
- [ ] Skulltulas #77-#81 collected
- [ ] Opened the Boss Key chest
- [ ] Beat Morpha without falling in or being grabbed
- [ ] Took the Heart Container and received the Water Medallion from Ruto
- [ ] Shot the rising sun for the Fire Arrows
- [ ] Skulltula #54 (dead tree, night) and #55 (laboratory crate) collected
- [ ] Laboratory roof Piece of Heart collected

### Chapter Overview

This chapter covers the stretch between the Water Temple and the Shadow Temple. Walking into Kakariko Village after the Water Temple starts a cutscene, and Sheik then teaches you the Nocturne of Shadow [[gf1-244-1, gf4-28-6]]. You then learn the Song of Storms from the windmill as an adult [[gf1-136-2]]. Next you return to childhood and play that song in the windmill to drain the well, which opens the Bottom of the Well [[gf1-246-3, gf1-246-4]]. The Lens of Truth is waiting there [[gf1-390-1]].

The main targets are:

- **Seeing Is Believing** (the Lens of Truth, in the Bottom of the Well).
- **The Fabulous Froggish Tenors** (the Zora's River frog minigame).
- Three Gold Skulltulas in the Well.
- Gold Skulltula #20, from the tree by the castle moat.
- Gold Skulltula #37, in Death Mountain Crater.
- Three Pieces of Heart: #23 and #18 from the frogs, and #33 from the Market Treasure Chest Game.

Once you have done the Forest Temple you can return to childhood at any time, and that is what makes the Well available [[gf1-18-4]]. The Well can only be entered as Young Link, and it needs both the Ocarina of Time and the Song of Storms [[gf3-204-4, gf1-394-4]].

### Chapter Items to Collect

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Song of Storms | Kakariko windmill (as Adult Link) | Stand in front of the Organ Grinder and take out the Ocarina | [[gf1-136-2]] |
| Lens of Truth | Bottom of the Well, chest after Dead Hand | **TAKE** | [[gf1-250-3]] |
| Compass | Bottom of the Well, Big Chest in the room off the trench | **TAKE** | [[gf1-251-2]] |
| Dungeon Map | Bottom of the Well, chest beyond the rocks in the Cess Pool | **TAKE** | [[gf1-255-5]] |
| Gold Skulltulas (3) | Bottom of the Well | Like Like room plus the two locked rooms of the center area | [[checklist-skulltulas-16-1, checklist-skulltulas-16-2, checklist-skulltulas-16-3]] |
| Piece of Heart #23 | Zora's River frog log | Play the Song of Storms | [[checklist-heartpieces-4-10]] |
| Piece of Heart #18 | Zora's River frog log | Win the frog minigame | [[checklist-heartpieces-4-4]] |
| Piece of Heart #33 | Hyrule Market Treasure Chest Game | Night, Lens of Truth | [[checklist-heartpieces-5-6]] |
| Gold Skulltula #20 | Hyrule Castle grounds, tree by the moat | Song of Storms, Bombs, Boomerang | [[checklist-skulltulas-5-9]] |
| Gold Skulltula #37 | Death Mountain Crater, soft soil by the warp | Bolero of Fire, Bottle of Bugs | [[checklist-skulltulas-7-8]] |

### Kakariko Village (Adult Link)

#### Nocturne of Shadow

When you enter Kakariko Village after clearing the Water Temple, a cutscene plays and the village is on fire. Watch it, and Sheik will teach you the Nocturne of Shadow [[gf3-70-4, checklist-sidequests-29-3]]. This song is the only way to reach the Shadow Temple [[gf1-244-1]].

The notes are C-Left, C-Right, C-Right, A, C-Left, C-Right, C-Down [[gf1-397-1]].

#### The windmill: Song of Storms

1. From Kakariko Graveyard, take a right up the long staircase.
2. Enter the door on the right at the top of the stairs, behind the crate [[gf1-121-1]].
3. If you skipped the Piece of Heart in the windmill as Young Link, you can still take it now from the top of the staircase [[gf1-136-1]].
4. Drop down to the main floor and stand in front of the Organ Grinder.
5. Take out the Ocarina of Time, and he teaches you the Song of Storms [[gf1-136-2]].

The song is A, C-Down, C-Up, A, C-Down, C-Up [[gf1-394-3, gf2-51-1]]. Besides opening the Well later, it is used for the frog heart piece and the castle Skulltula in this chapter.

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave Kakariko as an adult:** Impa's roof Skulltula (#27) should already be yours from Chapter 11. If you skipped it, Hookshot from the House of Skulltula roof onto Impa's house roof at night and kill the Gold Skulltula on the wall [[gf1-136-4, checklist-skulltulas-6-6]].

### Hyrule Castle Ruins (Adult Link)

Head back to the Market and the former castle grounds. The Market is full of Redeads, and they drop Magic Pots if you need to refill [[gf1-132-1]].

The Gold Skulltula on the back of the fallen outer gate (#21) was already collected in Chapter 11, so there is nothing to pick up here [[checklist-skulltulas-5-10, checklist-sidequests-5-31]].

### Temple of Time

The Temple of Time is to the right of the Happy Mask Shop [[gf1-128-4]]. Run into the Master Sword chamber and replace the Master Sword on its pedestal to return to childhood [[gf1-172-3, gf3-50-2]].

The Prelude of Light also warps you to the Temple of Time from anywhere, so you can switch ages quickly [[gf1-167-2, gf1-397-4]]. You will pull the Master Sword again later to become an adult [[gf1-175-4]].

### Hyrule Castle (Young Link)

🗺️ **Map:** [View Hyrule Palace Map by ThE_bLImP](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/52-hyrule-palace-map)

🗺️ **Map:** [View Path to Zelda Map by TLS64](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/55-path-to-zelda-map)

Do the castle errands now, while you are a child and have Bombs and the Boomerang. If you want Gold Skulltula #20 (the tree by the moat), you have to sneak through the Garden again.

#### Sneaking to the moat tree

1. From the Market, enter the Garden and climb the ivy wall at the gate.
2. At the top, run forward past the gossip stone to the top of the large marble arch.
3. Drop down on the other side of the arch. This puts you past the locked gate and the first two guards [[gf1-173-1]].
4. Run up the hill at the third graphical break, near its center, toward the cluster of flowers at the top.
5. Run straight at the flowers, then forward and slightly left to a wall with brick tiling, and climb it.
6. At the top, run forward past another gossip stone and jump off the cliff into the moat.
7. Swim to the end of the moat where it breaks left, climb onto the small patch of dirt, then climb up to the stone platform [[gf1-173-2]].

If a guard spots you at any point here, you are thrown back outside the gate. Redo the route from the start.

#### Gold Skulltula #20 (Song of Storms)

1. As you climb out of the moat, find the tree to your right at the corner of the fence.
2. Stand beneath it and play the Song of Storms. A pit opens.
3. Drop in. There are three bombable walls: one in front, one to the left, one to the right.
4. Bomb the wall on the left, slightly behind you from where you land, to reveal a Gold Skulltula [[gf1-173-3]].
5. Kill it and take the token with the Boomerang, since Young Link has no Hookshot [[gf1-459-2, gf3-50-3]].

To leave, run back the way you came and let a guard spot you. He throws you out of the gate [[gf1-173-4]].

#### Din's Fire (optional)

If you have not yet taken Din's Fire, climb the ivy on the right to the cliff above the gate and drop down past the marble gate. Run to the "Dead End" sign and bomb the boulder to reveal a tunnel, then crawl through to the Great Fairy's Fountain. Stand on the Triforce emblem and play Zelda's Lullaby [[gf1-90-1, gf1-90-2]].

### Kakariko Village (Young Link): Drain the Well

Get to Kakariko as a child. Go to the windmill and play the Song of Storms to the Organ Grinder, and the well outside drains [[gf1-246-3, gf3-50-4]]. If it does nothing, you are still Adult Link [[gf1-246-3]].

Once the well is drained, go outside and drop to its bottom. Go through the passage you find there [[gf1-246-4]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave the Well entrance:** you need the Boomerang for the Skulltulas, Bombs for several rocks and walls, and Deku Sticks for the torches [[gf1-252-1, gf1-255-4, gf1-253-2]]. Fewer Magic Meter problems will come up if you also carry a Hylian Shield, since Fire Keese burn Deku Shields [[gf1-254-3]].

### Bottom of the Well

This dungeon is easy, and gets easier once you have the Lens of Truth. The Lens sees through the large number of fake walls and floors, and the only other reason to stay is the three Gold Skulltulas [[gf1-248-1, checklist-sidequests-30-1]].

#### Entrance and Reservoir

1. Run forward from the entrance and crawl through the small tunnel [[gf1-248-2]].
2. Drop down the cliff on the other side and kill the Big Skulltula hanging from the ceiling [[gf1-249-1]].
3. At the apparent dead end with a skeleton, walk forward. You pass through the fake wall [[gf1-249-2, gf3-50-5]].
4. Stay in the shallow water that circles the room. No pits appear there [[gf1-249-3]].
5. If you fall down a pit, go to the southwest corner of the room beneath the Reservoir and climb the ladder. Collect the Silver Rupees around the cesspool at the bottom of the ladder, which opens the door back up [[gf1-249-4]].

#### Trench: drain the water

1. Jump across the pool in front of you into the narrow water-filled trench. Remember this pool; you return to it in a moment.
2. Follow the trench around two corners, watching for the giant green bubble that patrols it.
3. Halfway down the hall after the second corner, stand on the Triforce symbol painted on the trench floor and play Zelda's Lullaby. The water drains [[gf1-249-5, gf3-51-1]].
4. Return to the pool near the entrance, which is now an empty pit. Drop in and open the chest for Bombs, then go through the crawlspace [[gf1-249-6, gf3-51-2]].

#### Dead Hand room

1. Kill the Big Skulltula that comes down, climb the ivy wall, and go through the door at the far end [[gf1-250-1]].
2. Four giant hands stick out of the floor. Run near one and it grabs you, which summons Dead Hand [[gf1-250-2]].

**Dead Hand strategy**

Unable to verify from collected sources: Dead Hand's HP and elemental weaknesses.

- Struggle free by rotating the control stick and pressing buttons. Then Z-target Dead Hand's head [[gf1-250-2, gf1-517-3]].
- Stand still, out of the hands' reach, and wait for Dead Hand to approach. Its head lowers to attack [[gf1-250-2]].
- Press A for a Jump Attack as the head lowers. It then runs away and sinks into the ground [[gf1-517-3, gf2-127-1]].
- Let another hand grab you to lure it out again, and repeat until it dies [[gf1-250-2, gf3-244-2]].

When Dead Hand dies, a chest appears. Open it for the Lens of Truth [[gf1-250-3, gf3-51-3]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Seeing Is Believing (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158185.png)
> *Obtain the Lens of Truth*


Seeing Is Believing unlocks when you obtain the Lens of Truth. One commenter says it can be earned the moment you become an adult [[ra-comments-95-1]]. The collected sources place the Lens in this dungeon for Young Link [[gf1-390-1, ra-guide-15-1, gf3-149-2]], so get it here.

Two more details about the Lens:

- It uses magic while active, but rapidly tapping the assigned C-button to toggle it on and off avoids using Magic Power [[gf1-250-4]].
- Use the Lens to look behind the Lens chest. An invisible chest there holds a Huge Rupee [[gf1-250-5, gf3-51-4]].

The Lens is required to beat the Shadow Temple boss and to reach the Spirit Temple entrance [[gf1-390-2]].

#### Trench: keys and Compass

1. Climb out of the pit with the ivy on the opposite wall and run into the drained trench. Run left toward the corner and use the Lens to see an opening on the right. Go through and open the chest for a Small Key [[gf1-251-1]].
2. Go back out and around the corner. Use the Lens to spot another opening on the right, leading to a room with a large pit. Watch the pit with the Lens, then open the Big Chest for the **Compass** [[gf1-251-2]].
3. Go back down the trench in the opposite direction, around the corner to the end of the hall, and take a left. Use the Lens to find an opening on the left wall and open the chest for another Small Key [[gf1-251-3]].
4. Further down the hall, use the Lens to see an opening on the left. Immediately inside there is a locked door on the right [[gf1-251-4]].

Other guides list additional chests and small keys in this area. Examples are a Small Key in a hidden chest behind a fake wall to the east, and a Small Key under a cracked dirt mound that you bomb [[gf3-51-10, gf3-104-6]]. Unable to verify from collected sources: the exact total number of Small Keys in the dungeon.

#### Locked rooms (Gold Skulltulas)

- **Right (east) locked room:** Kill all four Keese, then activate the Lens. A narrow ledge on the right goes around the center pit. Walk it to the Gold Skulltula, kill it, and take the token with the Boomerang [[gf1-252-1, checklist-skulltulas-16-3]].
- **Left (west) locked room:** Kill the Deku Baba and avoid the flying pots, which hurl themselves at you. Kill the Gold Skulltula on the wall and take the token with the Boomerang [[gf1-252-3, checklist-skulltulas-16-2]].

After the east room, reactivate the Lens in the small prison and go right to the other locked door, which leads to the west room [[gf1-252-2]]. Return to the Reservoir afterward.

#### Hall chest, Coffin Room and the Like Like room

1. Run down the hall directly across from the opening in the wall and drop down the cliff at the end. Open the chest at the bottom for a Recovery Heart, climb the ivy wall, and go through the door at the top [[gf1-253-1]].
2. **Coffin Room:** Kill the Gibdo on the right. Light a Deku Stick at the nearby torch, then run to the southwest corner and light the torch next to the coffin there. The coffin opens and reveals a Small Key [[gf1-253-2]]. Other coffins hold enemies; put the stick away after each torch [[gf3-51-7]].
3. Run to the northeast corner of the Reservoir area. Use the Lens to dodge the pit near the wall, then crawl through the crawlspace in the alcove [[gf1-254-1]].
4. Go right out of the crawlspace into the Tiny Room and through the locked door [[gf1-254-2]].
5. **Pit Room:** Use the Lens to see the pits. Kill the enemies without falling, using the Boomerang on the Fire Keese and a Bomb on the Beamos [[gf1-254-4]]. The chest in the corner holds a replacement Deku Shield if yours burns [[gf1-254-5]].
6. Go through the next door to the Like Like room. Kill the Like Like at close quarters, then kill the Gold Skulltula on the wall and take its token [[gf1-255-1, gf1-255-2, checklist-skulltulas-16-1]].

A Like Like can swallow your shield. Kill it quickly to get it back [[gf3-52-2]].

#### Cess Pool and Dungeon Map

1. Fall through one of the pits in the Pit Room to reach the Cess Pool [[gf1-255-3]].
2. Run south from where you land. Use the Bomb Flowers to blow up the rocks blocking your path [[gf1-255-4]].
3. Bomb the rocks in the torch-lit hall, run to the end, and kill the Redead. Open the chest for the **Dungeon Map** [[gf1-255-5, gf3-50-7]].

If a Redead freezes you, the Sun's Song can stun the Redeads [[gf3-105-1]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave the Well:** you should have the Lens of Truth, the Compass, the Dungeon Map and all three Gold Skulltulas (the Like Like room and both locked rooms). Some guides also list smaller chests with Deku Nuts, Bombchus and rupees, and these are optional [[gf2-80-3, gf3-51-5, gf3-52-3]]. Unable to verify from collected sources: the full list of small chests in the dungeon.

### Zora's River (Young Link): the Frogs

🗺️ **Map:** [View Zora's River/Domain Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/56-zoras-river-domain-map)

Go upstream to the Musical Frogs [[gf1-174-1]]. Walk out onto the log and stand on the white circle at its end before pulling out the Ocarina [[gf1-95-6]]. This has to be done as Young Link [[gf2-146-29]].

#### Piece of Heart #23

Play the Song of Storms. The frogs give you a Piece of Heart [[gf1-174-1, checklist-heartpieces-4-10, gf3-52-5]]. Also play any other songs they have not yet heard.

The songs to play for the frogs are Zelda's Lullaby, Epona's Song, the Sun's Song, Saria's Song, the Song of Storms and the Song of Time [[gf1-174-2]].

- Each new song earns rupees: 50 rupees each in one guide, Purple Rupees in others [[gf1-95-6, gf2-35-3]].
- Playing the Song of Time earns a reward as well [[gf1-124-3, gf2-78-5]].
- Another source says that playing five of the upper-row songs, excluding the Song of Storms, is what starts the minigame [[checklist-heartpieces-4-4]]. Playing all six covers either version.

#### The Frog minigame

After all the songs, the frogs challenge you to another minigame [[gf1-174-2]]. Stand on the log and take out the Ocarina [[gf1-174-2]].

Flies appear above the frogs' heads and you must press the button for that frog. The lowest frog is the A Button. The other four frogs, in a cross shape, match the C-buttons [[gf1-174-3]]. One guide lists the frog buttons from left to right as A, Left, Down, Up, Right [[gf2-112-3]]. Either way, simply follow the button sequence below.

The sequence is [[gf1-174-4, gf1-439-1, ra-comments-94-1, ra-comments-94-3]]:

**A, C-Left, C-Right, C-Down, C-Left, C-Right, C-Down, A, C-Down, A, C-Down, C-Right, C-Left, A**

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** You must play the sequence very fast or it fails [[gf3-52-6, ra-comments-94-6]]. Setting the C-buttons to the D-pad is reported to be easier than the analog stick, and even slight stick drift can stop notes from registering [[ra-comments-94-5, ra-comments-94-4]]. Unable to verify from collected sources: whether you can retry freely after a failure.

Finish it and you receive Piece of Heart #18 [[checklist-sidequests-2-34, checklist-sidequests-30-3, gf2-146-30, gf3-134-3]]. This is Piece of Heart #30 in another guide's numbering [[gf2-78-6, gf2-113-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **The Fabulous Froggish Tenors (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158184.png)
> *Complete the Frog minigame on Zora's River*


Together, the song heart and the minigame heart give you the two Pieces of Heart from the frogs [[gf1-246-1, gf3-120-1]].

### Hyrule Market (Young Link): Treasure Chest Game

This minigame is only open at night. Play the Sun's Song to make it night if you have to, then head for the door in the southeast corner of the Market, to the left of the Bombchu Bowling Alley and near the entrance to the back alley [[gf1-257-1]]. One other guide places it in the southwest corner instead [[gf4-21-11]]. Look for the one lit building [[checklist-sidequests-8-6]].

1. Pay 10 rupees to play [[gf3-52-8, gf2-80-9]].
2. In each room there are two chests, one with a Small Key and one with a rupee booby prize. The key lets you progress [[gf1-428-2]].
3. Use the Lens of Truth to see inside each chest, and take the Small Key in each room [[gf1-257-2, checklist-heartpieces-5-6]].
4. Repeat for every room. In the last room a lone chest holds a Piece of Heart (#33) [[gf1-257-3, gf1-428-1]].

If you run short of rupees, break pots in the room at the entrance to the Market [[gf4-3-2]].

### Death Mountain Crater (Young Link): Gold Skulltula #37

The Bolero of Fire warps you to Death Mountain Crater even as Young Link [[gf1-395-4]]. Without the Goron Tunic your time is limited, and if time runs out you die. Fairies do not help [[gf1-175-1, gf1-435-2]]. Move fast.

1. Warp in. Look left of the warp point for a patch of soft earth.
2. Drop a Bottled Bug into it. Three bugs come out, so recapture one.
3. Kill the Gold Skulltula and take its token [[gf1-175-2, checklist-skulltulas-7-8]].
4. If you still have a Magic Bean, plant one in the patch [[gf1-175-2]]. When you return as an adult, the bean has grown into a platform [[gf1-176-1]].
5. Play the Prelude of Light to return to the Temple of Time [[gf1-175-3]].

Bugs are sold at the Kakariko Potion Shop for 50 rupees [[gf2-140-49]].

### Back to Adulthood

In the Temple of Time, take the Master Sword to become an adult again [[gf1-175-4, gf1-257-4]]. From here, the Nocturne of Shadow warps you to the outside of the Shadow Temple [[gf1-258-1]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave:** confirm you have the Song of Storms and Nocturne of Shadow, the Lens of Truth, the Compass and Map, the three Well Skulltulas, castle Skulltula #20, Crater Skulltula #37, and Pieces of Heart #23, #18 and #33.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 17 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 80 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 30 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 6 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 19 / 26 (Bottom of the Well Map and Compass)
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 61 / 97 (345 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Learned the Nocturne of Shadow in Kakariko.
- [ ] Learned the Song of Storms as an adult.
- [ ] Drained the well as a child.
- [ ] Beat Dead Hand and took the Lens of Truth. [ACHIEVEMENT:48951 is placed above.]
- [ ] Took the Compass and Dungeon Map.
- [ ] Killed all three Bottom of the Well Skulltulas.
- [ ] Piece of Heart #23: Song of Storms for the frogs.
- [ ] Piece of Heart #18: frog minigame. [ACHIEVEMENT:48950 is placed above.]
- [ ] Piece of Heart #33: Treasure Chest Game with the Lens.
- [ ] Gold Skulltula #20 at the castle moat tree.
- [ ] Gold Skulltula #37 in Death Mountain Crater.
- [ ] Returned to adulthood via the Temple of Time.

---

## Chapter 18: Shadow Temple

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Kakariko_Graveyard["Kakariko Graveyard"]
  Shadow_Temple["Shadow Temple"]
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Kakariko_Graveyard -->|"Nocturne of Shadow ledge"| Shadow_Temple
  Shadow_Temple --> Bongo_Bongo
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Bongo_Bongo boss;
```

This chapter takes you from the Kakariko Graveyard ledge into the Shadow Temple, through its Lens of Truth puzzles, the Dead Hand miniboss, the invisible-spike rooms, the cursed boat and the fire walls, and finally to Bongo Bongo. You collect the Map, Compass and Boss Key, five Gold Skulltulas, the Hover Boots and a Heart Container, and Impa gives you the Shadow Medallion [[gf1-260-1, gf1-261-3, gf1-262-4, gf1-263-4, gf1-272-3, gf1-275-8, gf1-275-9]].

The Shadow Temple is reached only by the Nocturne of Shadow, which takes Young or Adult Link to the high ledge in Kakariko Graveyard [[gf1-397-2]]. Access depends on having the Forest, Fire and Water Medallions [[gf1-422-4]]. The Bottom of the Well must be finished before the Shadow Temple can be completed [[gf1-19-1]]. That is also where the Lens of Truth comes from [[gf1-390-1]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Invisible One (**Invisible One** below) can be lost during the Bongo Bongo fight. It requires beating Bongo Bongo without using the Lens of Truth and without being trapped by his hands [[gf4-29-2, ra-guide-15-2, ra-comments-40-1, ra-comments-40-2]]. Read the boss section before dropping into the pit.

### What to bring

- **Din's Fire** is needed at the entrance and again in the Shadow Temple. It comes from the Great Fairy in the Hyrule Castle garden [[gf1-90-2, gf1-258-2]].
- **Lens of Truth, Longshot, Hover Boots (found here), Iron Boots and Kokiri Boots, bombs and the Fairy Bow with Fire Arrows** are all used during the temple [[gf1-260-1, gf1-270-3, gf1-275-1]].
- **Biggoron's Sword** is recommended for the miniboss and the boss [[gf2-83-2, gf1-275-6]].
- **Arrows and magic.** Bongo Bongo's hands drop more when stunned if you run low [[gf1-275-7]].
- **Farore's Wind.** Set it before the boss room. If you fail, reset the game and use it to teleport back near the boss's room [[ra-comments-40-8]].
- The Lens of Truth is only said to fail the achievement if you activate it. Carrying it is fine [[ra-comments-40-1]].

### Chapter Items to Collect

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Dungeon Map | Redead Room off the Catacombs | Kill the Redead and two Keese | [[gf1-261-3]] |
| Hover Boots | Dead Hand room | Defeat Dead Hand, open chest | [[gf1-262-4]] |
| Compass | Gibdos Room | Kill both Gibdos | [[gf1-263-4]] |
| Boss Key | Crushing Walls Room | Burn walls with Din's Fire, kill Redead | [[gf1-272-3]] |
| Heart Container | Bongo Bongo's arena | Defeat Bongo Bongo | [[gf1-275-8]] |
| Shadow Medallion | Beam of light after boss | Meet Impa | [[gf1-275-9]] |

### Kakariko Graveyard

Play the Nocturne of Shadow (C-Left, C-Right, C-Right, A, C-Left, C-Right, C-Down) to warp to the Graveyard ledge [[gf1-397-2, gf2-82-2]]. Run east down the stairs near the warp point to reach a large room of torches [[gf1-258-2]]. Stand on the pedestal in the middle of the torches and cast Din's Fire. All the torches light and the Shadow Temple door opens [[gf1-258-2, gf2-82-3]]. Go through it.

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave the Graveyard for good, note that its Pieces of Heart and Skulltulas are covered by their own routes. Dampe's race (Hookshot, then under one minute with the Longshot) rewards a Piece of Heart [[gf1-245-1]]. The Heart Piece from the grave-digging tour is lost forever if you dig it up and leave without collecting it [[ra-guide-20-1]].

### Shadow Temple

#### Entrance and the Raven Room

Run forward and look right around the corner. A long pit has a Longshot target on the far end; pull yourself across [[gf1-260-1]]. Run straight through the face carved into the opposite wall to reach the Raven Room [[gf1-260-2]].

Use the Lens of Truth on the skull posts around the Raven statue. Find the skull that does not disappear. Push the handle on the statue's front until the Raven faces that real skull, and do not stop pushing until it does, or the floor disappears. The grating across the room then opens [[gf1-260-3, gf3-70-8]]. A large pit blocks the grating for now [[gf1-260-3]].

Turn to the wall opposite the pit. With the Lens, find the fake face on the right side, run through it, down the hall and through the door [[gf1-260-4]].

#### Catacombs and the Map

The skull on the next wall has glowing blue eyes; every fake wall in this area has glowing blue eyes, while flashing blue eyes mark secret messages [[gf1-261-1]]. Go through the wall, right around the corner, use the Lens to see a fake wall on the right, and go through it and the door beyond [[gf1-261-2]].

In the Redead Room, kill the Redead and the two Keese high above. The Dungeon Map appears. Take the **Dungeon Map** (✅ Map), then return to the Catacombs [[gf1-261-3, gf2-83-1]].

#### Dead Hand and the Hover Boots

Back through the fake wall, go right, then left around the next corner. Use the Lens to pass through the wall flanked by two pots, go down the passage and through another fake wall, go right then left, and use the Lens to see through the wall at the hall's end. Go through to the door [[gf1-262-1]].

Dead Hand is the miniboss. Let one hand grab you so his body and head appear, struggle free by rotating the stick and mashing buttons, Z-target the head, stand still out of the hands' reach, and execute a Jump Attack as the head lowers [[gf1-262-2]]. Repeat until he dies. The Biggoron Sword kills him in a few hits, though Jump Attacks tend to fly past him [[gf1-262-3]]; two jump attacks are enough with it [[gf2-83-2]].

Open the chest that appears for the **Hover Boots** [[gf1-262-4, gf2-83-3]]. Return to the Raven Room.

#### Compass and the Beamos

In the Raven Room, run through the fake wall to the cliff, equip the Hover Boots and run off the cliff onto the tongue-shaped ledge. Switch back to Kokiri Boots and go through the doorway and down the tunnel [[gf1-263-1]].

Throw a bomb or two into the Beamos's head, then go to the wall on the right, which is fake, and through the door [[gf1-263-2, gf1-263-3]].

In the Gibdos Room, kill both Gibdos. A chest appears with the **Compass** [[gf1-263-4, gf2-83-5]].

#### Scythe Room (Small Key)

Return through the fake wall, kill or dodge the Beamos, and go north through the fake wall north of it [[gf1-264-1]]. In the Scythe Room, grab the two Silver Rupees in the middle in the scythe's path; you can roll under the blades if timed well [[gf1-264-2, gf1-264-3]]. Then Longshot to the northwest target and jump off the wood stack for the third, take the fourth in the west alcove and the fifth in the northeast alcove [[gf1-264-3]]. Do not enter the small enclosed area with the creepy face panel; there is a hole in the ground [[gf2-83-6]].

The southeast grating opens on a chest with a **Small Key**. Leave through the south door [[gf1-264-4]].

#### Guillotine Chamber

Return to the Raven Room and bomb the rough wall to the east. Go down the passage and through the door [[gf1-265-1]]. Move slowly down the ramps and kill the hanging Big Skulltulas with the Longshot, then jump off the cliff at the bottom [[gf1-265-2]].

Run around each rising guillotine or roll under them, watching for the Big Skulltula between the first and second blades [[gf1-265-3]]. Around the corner, Navi warns of Wall Masters; wait for it to drop and kill it, because knocking it off the cliff does not count [[gf1-265-4]]. Jump the three guillotines, and on the small platform fight the Stalfos with your shield up, attacking only right after it attacks [[gf1-265-5]].

With the Lens, jump across the invisible platforms east, then to a corner platform, then north to a door [[gf1-265-6]].

#### Invisible Scythe Room (Skulltula)

Keep the Lens on here; rapidly toggling it uses no magic [[gf1-266-1]]. Kill the Like-Like and the Keese; the rear grating opens [[gf1-266-2, gf2-84-1]]. Go through, kill the Gold Skulltula and take the token (**Skulltula #85**) with the Longshot, and open the two chests, a Blue Rupee and Arrows, the second visible only with the Lens [[gf1-266-2, gf1-266-3, gf1-503-2, checklist-skulltulas-17-1]]. Return to the Guillotine Chamber.

#### Guillotine Chamber, second half

Jump back across the invisible platforms to the Stalfos platform; it appears again. Fight it, then jump west to the rising and falling platforms [[gf1-267-1]]. When the platform is halfway to its apex, jump to the narrow cliff on the right and run southwest to the wide platform with blade traps and five Silver Rupees. Take four by running around, standing safely in the large gap between blades, and bomb the Beamos to reach the fifth beneath it [[gf1-267-1, gf2-84-3]]. The west grating opens.

Follow the twisting hall to the crushing-ceilings room. With the Lens, find the stone block in the right wall, pull it out until it lines up with the smiling face on the floor, then push it forward so both spiked ceilings are blocked [[gf1-267-2, gf2-84-4]]. Enter the cell to the south, kill the Gold Skulltula and Longshot the token (**Skulltula #86**) [[gf1-267-3, gf1-503-4, checklist-skulltulas-17-2]]. The cell across holds a chest with Arrows [[gf2-84-5]].

Pull the block back, climb it to the north platform, then use a crushing ceiling as a bridge to the south ledge. Hop the spike to the opposite side for a chest with a Blue Rupee [[gf1-267-4, gf2-84-7]]. Step on the south switch to make a chest appear in the northeast, cross to it and open it for a **Small Key** [[gf1-267-4, gf2-84-8]].

Go back east past the blade traps to the narrow ledge, head north, use the Lens to see the platform past the guillotine, jump as it rises, then jump across the horizontally moving platform to the door [[gf1-267-5]].

#### Silver Rupee Room (invisible spikes)

Kill both Redeads; a chest appears with a Blue Rupee. Keep the Lens on to avoid the invisible spikes [[gf1-268-1]]. Collect all five Silver Rupees using the Longshot targets: the first in the middle behind the nearest spikes, the second by Longshotting the east target, the third at the west target, the fourth via the invisible ceiling target above the north ledge, and the fifth on the invisible northeast ledge via its wall target [[gf1-268-2]]. The west door opens.

#### Skull Room

Climb either staircase to above the large skull. Kill the four Keese, then toss a bomb into the hollow top; the skull explodes and reveals a **Small Key** [[gf1-269-1]]. Kill the Gold Skulltula behind the skull for its token (**Skulltula #87**) [[gf1-269-2, gf1-504-2, checklist-skulltulas-17-3]]. All Keese must die for the door to unseal [[gf1-269-1]].

Return to the Silver Rupee Room, Longshot the invisible ceiling target to the north platform, and go through the locked door [[gf1-269-3]].

#### The Fan Room

Wait for the first fan to stop before running past the blade traps in Kokiri Boots [[gf1-270-1]]. Around the corner, kill the Big Skulltula, then equip Iron Boots against the stronger fan and walk slowly [[gf1-270-2, gf1-270-3]]. At the pit, Longshot the opposite ledge while wearing Iron Boots, and drop off the far side [[gf1-270-4]]. Walk across the hall in Iron Boots, holding Z with your shield up to block the Silver Eye's flames [[gf1-270-5]]. At the last fan on the right wall, switch to Hover Boots; the fan blows you through a fake wall onto a ledge. Take the door [[gf1-270-6]].

#### The Other Gibdos Room and the Cursed Boat

Return to Kokiri Boots and kill the Gibdos while dodging blade traps. A chest gives a Blue Rupee [[gf1-271-1]]. Bomb the rubble in the southeast corner, use the Lens, and open the hidden chest for a **Small Key**. Use it on the north door [[gf1-271-2]].

In the boat room, push or pull the large block along its track into the trench near the ladder [[gf1-271-3]]. Climb the block and ladder to the Triforce symbol. From the boat's front, look southwest to the alcove with a Gold Skulltula. Longshot it directly, or play the Scarecrow's Song to bring Pierre to the alcove (**Skulltula #88**) [[gf1-271-4, gf1-504-4, checklist-skulltulas-17-4]]. The skulltula can also be reached by calling the scarecrow to the platform above where the block started and Longshotting it [[checklist-sidequests-34-3]].

Stand on the boat's Triforce symbol and play Zelda's Lullaby; the boat moves west [[gf1-271-5]]. Several waves of Stalfos attack; block their attacks or fight [[gf1-271-6]]. When the boat reaches the west side, jump off the south side immediately before it sinks [[gf1-271-7]]. Go through the west door.

#### Floor Master Room (invisible maze)

The room is a maze with invisible walls; use the Lens, and kill or avoid the Floor Masters [[gf1-272-1]]. Reach each door in turn.

- **North door, Crushing Walls Room:** Burn both spiked wooden walls with Din's Fire. Kill the Redead on the east and open the ornate chest for the **Boss Key**. The west chest holds a Blue Rupee [[gf1-272-2, gf1-272-3, gf1-272-4]].
- **South door, Invisible Floor Master Room:** Use the Lens to see the Floor Master. When it splits into three invisible small ones, Din's Fire kills all three. A chest appears with a **Small Key** [[gf1-273-1, gf1-273-2, gf1-273-3]].
- **West door, Triple Skull Room:** You need not destroy the spinning skulls (they give rupees). Shoot the Gold Skulltula on the wall behind them (**Skulltula #89**) [[gf1-274-1, gf1-274-2, gf1-505-2, checklist-skulltulas-17-5]].
- **East door:** Leads back toward the boat room [[gf1-274-3]].

#### The bridge and Geometric Shape Room

In the boat room's south section, a pit is too wide even for the Hover Boots. Shoot a Fire Arrow at a bomb flower around the tall pillar on the left of the opposite ledge; the pillar falls and forms a bridge. Cross and enter the south door [[gf1-275-1, gf3-72-5]].

In the next room, equip Hover Boots and use the Lens. Run to the T-shaped platform in front of the door, go left to the straight platform, then right to the next, then to the ledge with the Boss Door [[gf1-275-2]].

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave this room, set Farore's Wind and make sure you have plenty of arrows and magic; the Lens of Truth should be unequipped and out of use for the boss [[ra-comments-40-8, ra-comments-40-13]].

### Boss: Bongo Bongo

Drop down the pit in the middle of the room. The entire fight takes place on a giant drum [[gf1-275-3]]. Bongo Bongo attacks with his hands or tries to knock you off by pounding the drum [[gf1-521-3]]. Each attack deals at least two hearts [[gf2-86-1]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Do not use the Lens of Truth in this fight, and do not let his hands grab and trap you, or Invisible One will be missed [[ra-comments-40-1, ra-comments-40-2, gf4-29-2]]. If it fails, reset and use Farore's Wind [[ra-comments-40-8]].

**Standard strategy:** Z-target and shoot both hands with arrows to stun them. They recover quickly, so shoot fast. A struck hand turns blue and drops an arrow or magic [[gf1-275-5, gf2-86-2]]. Normally you would use the Lens between the hands to see the eye, but for the achievement you cannot [[gf1-275-6, ra-comments-40-1]].

**Achievement approach (no Lens):**
- After both hands are stunned, Bongo Bongo charges at you. Aim down the middle between the stunned hands and shoot, which hits his eye [[ra-comments-40-3, ra-comments-40-10, ra-guide-15-2]].
- Equip Hover Boots at the start to Z-target a hand, then unequip them to dodge the second hand if it becomes a fist [[ra-guide-15-2]]. One player failed every attempt with Hover Boots and succeeded first try with normal boots [[ra-comments-40-5]]. Choose what suits you, and Kokiri Boots are also recommended by the walkthrough [[gf1-275-4]].
- Taking the left hand first (on Link's right side) means you are bounced around less [[ra-comments-41-4]].
- Hold the bow after stunning both hands until they form fists, then fire straight ahead at the eye [[ra-comments-41-7]].
- Once he drops, Jump Attack with the Biggoron's Sword then crouch-stab to finish him in one cycle [[ra-guide-15-2, gf3-73-2]]. Two sets of swings with Biggoron's Sword kill him [[gf2-86-3]].
- Alternatives reported by players: charged spin attack when he rushes you [[ra-comments-41-5]]; uncharged spin attacks until he runs into you [[ra-comments-40-16]]; Din's Fire makes you ungrabbable but does not pause time for Bongo Bongo, and one player found it did not help [[ra-comments-40-6, ra-comments-40-7]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Invisible One (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158137.png)
> *Defeat Bongo Bongo without using the Lens of Truth or getting trapped by his hands*


After he dies, take the **Heart Container** [[gf1-275-8, gf3-73-3]]. Enter the beam of light to meet Impa, the Sage of Shadow, and receive the Shadow Medallion [[gf1-275-9, gf3-73-4]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **The Shadow of the Hylians (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158138.png)
> *Awake Impa and obtain the Shadow Medallion*


If the medallion achievement fails to trigger, a manual unlock can be requested on the RetroAchievements Discord or by DM to RAdmin [[ra-comments-42-1]]. You return to the Graveyard afterward [[gf3-73-4]].

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave, confirm you have the Map, Compass, Boss Key, Hover Boots and Gold Skulltulas #85 to #89. The route onward leads from the Graveyard via Hyrule Field to Gerudo Valley [[gf1-277-2]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 18 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 85 / 100 (five in the Shadow Temple) [[checklist-sidequests-34-2]]
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 30 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 7 / 8 (Bongo Bongo) [[gf1-275-8]]
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 22 / 26 [[gf1-261-3, gf1-263-4, gf1-272-3]]
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 63 / 97 (360 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Warped with the Nocturne of Shadow and lit the torches with Din's Fire
- [ ] Dungeon Map from the Redead Room
- [ ] Hover Boots from Dead Hand
- [ ] Compass from the Gibdos Room
- [ ] Boss Key from the Crushing Walls Room
- [ ] Gold Skulltulas #85 to #89
- [ ] Set Farore's Wind before Bongo Bongo
- [ ] Invisible One: Bongo Bongo defeated without the Lens and without being trapped
- [ ] Heart Container taken
- [ ] The Shadow of the Hylians: Shadow Medallion from Impa

---

## Chapter 19: Gerudo Valley and Gerudo's Fortress

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Gerudo_Valley["Gerudo Valley"]
  Gerudo_Fortress["Gerudo's Fortress"]
  Twinrova{{"⚔ Twinrova"}}
  Ganondorf{{"⚔ Ganondorf"}}
  Ganon{{"⚔ Ganon"}}
  Final_Battle>"⛔ Ganon's Tower final battle"]
  Gerudo_Valley -->|"Jump the broken bridge on Epona or Longshot"| Gerudo_Fortress
  Ganondorf --> Final_Battle
  Final_Battle --> Ganon
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Final_Battle ponr;
  class Twinrova,Ganondorf,Ganon boss;
```

This chapter has two jobs. Cross the broken bridge in Gerudo Valley, then free the four captured carpenters in Gerudo's Fortress to earn the Gerudo's Membership Card [[gf1-278-1, gf1-420-1]]. With the card you can play the Horseback Archery range, which gives a Piece of Heart at 1,000 points and the Biggest Quiver at 1,500 points [[gf1-285-3, gf1-286-7, gf1-286-8]]. Two achievements are placed here: Gerudo's Fan Club (the card) and Ultimate Horse Master (the top archery reward). The new collectibles are the roof-chest Piece of Heart, the archery Piece of Heart, and two night-only Gold Skulltulas at the fortress [[checklist-heartpieces-5-7, gf1-286-7, checklist-skulltulas-18-1, checklist-skulltulas-18-2]].

> [!TIP]
> **Preparation:** Bring Epona or the Longshot for the bridge [[gf1-278-1]]. Bring the Bow, because arrows stun patrolling guards [[gf1-279-3]]. Bring Deku Nuts for the sword-wielding Gerudo fights [[checklist-sidequests-16-2]]. The Hover Boots help in the kitchen room [[gf1-282-1]]. The Longshot reaches the roof chest [[gf1-447-1]]. Have 20 Rupees ready for each archery round [[gf2-88-7]].

### Chapter Items to Collect (5 Items Total)

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Piece of Heart (roof chest) | Chest on the roof above the jail | **LONGSHOT** to it and open | [[checklist-heartpieces-5-7, gf1-283-3]] |
| Piece of Heart (archery) | Horseback Archery Range | **SCORE** 1,000 points | [[gf1-286-7]] |
| Gold Skulltula #90 | Far target at the archery range, at night | **LONGSHOT** to kill and collect | [[gf1-286-4, checklist-skulltulas-18-1]] |
| Gold Skulltula #91 | Stone wall at the back of the fortress, at night | **LONGSHOT** to kill and collect | [[checklist-skulltulas-18-2, gf1-283-2]] |
| Gerudo's Membership Card | Shiro's cell | **FREE** all four carpenters | [[gf1-285-2, gf1-420-1]] |

The Biggest Quiver from the 1,500-point round is also earned here [[gf1-286-8]].

### Gerudo Valley

Go up the log bridge near the valley entrance. At the fallen bridge spanning the canyon, either jump the gap on Epona or pull yourself across with the Longshot [[gf1-278-1]]. If you ride, charge straight at the gap at full speed [[gf1-358-2]]. The valley's Skulltulas and Pieces of Heart were already collected in Chapters 8, 10 and 13, so this section is only a crossing [[gf1-278-1]].

Once across, speak with the Master Carpenter. He explains that he came to repair the bridge, but his assistants ran off to join the Gerudo Thieves and were captured [[gf1-278-2]].

> [!TIP]
> If you still have the Poacher's Saw from the Biggoron sword trading sequence, show it to this carpenter. He gives you the Broken Goron's Sword in exchange [[gf1-358-2, gf2-81-1]].

Rocks near the bridge hide a chest with a Purple Rupee, which you can break with the Megaton Hammer [[gf3-75-1, gf2-87-3]]. If you play the Song of Storms behind the carpenter's tent, a secret cave opens. Inside, two Business Scrubs sell Green and Red Potions for 40 Rupees each [[gf3-239-1, gf3-52-11]].

Then continue to the fortress [[gf1-278-2]].

### Gerudo's Fortress

#### Getting caught

The Gerudo Thieves throw you in prison if they see you [[gf1-279-1]]. Run north from the entrance until you reach a stairset on the right, climb it, and let a purple-suited patroller see you [[gf1-279-1]]. After that, any time you are spotted in the fortress you are sent back to the cell [[gf1-279-1]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** If you are caught without the Hookshot or Longshot, you are thrown out in front of the carpenter's tent instead of into the cell [[checklist-sidequests-16-1]].

In the cell, look south and high on the wall for a window. Shoot the wooden awning with the Longshot to pull yourself up [[gf1-279-2]]. From the sill you can see the patrolling Gerudos below. Stun the one closest to the door on the left with an arrow, then jump down and enter before she recovers [[gf1-279-3]]. The Longshot also stuns, but not for nearly as long [[gf1-279-3]].

#### Carpenter 1: Ichiro

Run around the corner to find the first carpenter, Ichiro. Speak with him through the bars and a Gerudo Thief attacks [[gf1-280-1]]. She fights somewhat like a Stalfos but faster. Attack right after she attacks, and watch for her stun attack [[gf1-280-1]]. She telegraphs it by crouching slightly and holding her swords far apart [[gf1-280-1]]. If it hits, you lose the fight and go back to the cell [[gf1-280-1, gf3-247-2]].

Deku Nuts can stun her in the middle of any attack, including that special one [[checklist-sidequests-16-2]]. When she is stunned, a jump attack knocks her down and you can follow with another [[checklist-sidequests-16-2]]. If she blocks the nut, follow with a jump attack as she lowers her guard [[checklist-sidequests-16-2]]. Arrows also work well, though it takes many of them [[gf1-280-1]]. Without nuts, dodge her special attack by jumping aside once or twice [[checklist-sidequests-16-2]].

Defeat her, take the Small Key she leaves, and open Ichiro's cell [[gf1-280-2]]. Speak with him to free him. The carpenters will not leave until you talk to them [[gf1-280-3, checklist-sidequests-16-3]]. Then leave by the door on the left [[gf1-280-3]].

A fairy fountain is hidden near the crates across from the exit. You need the Song of Storms to open it [[checklist-sidequests-16-4]]. Stand in the small enclosed area on the left and play it [[gf2-88-1]].

#### Carpenter 2: Sabooro

The hallway route from Ichiro's exit to the roof corner is not covered in detail here. Unable to verify from collected sources.

When you come out on the roof, run toward the corner and stop before you go around it. Peek around using Z-Focus and you will see a patrolling Gerudo Guard. Wait until she faces away, then shoot her in the back with an arrow [[gf1-281-1]]. Run around the corner, jump up the ledge, and take the door on the right [[gf1-281-1]].

Run to the top of the ramp and speak with Sabooro in his cell at the far end of the room. The closer cell holds only pots with junk [[gf1-281-2]]. Defeat the Gerudo Thief who appears, take her Small Key, and open Sabooro's cell. Speak with him again to free him [[gf1-281-2, gf1-281-3]]. Head right and up around the corner, then through the doorway [[gf1-281-3]].

#### The kitchen and the roof chest

The next door on the left leads back into the kitchen. From the doorway, you can see two guards down the ramp to the right [[gf1-282-1]]. Shoot both with arrows to stun them. Then equip the Hover Boots, run out to the ledge directly ahead, and run across to the ramp on the other side. Swap back to the Kokiri Boots and go through the doorway [[gf1-282-1]].

> [!TIP]
> Reaching the platform in this room requires the Hover Boots or the Hookshot/Longshot. You cannot simply jump to it [[checklist-sidequests-17-1]].

Outside, it needs to be night for the Skulltula. Play the Sun's Song if it is not [[gf1-283-1]]. Jump up the ledge to the south and run along the roof to its end. Face west and jump the gap to the adjacent roof. Go around the corner to a wall covered in ivy and climb it to reach the top level of the fortress [[gf1-283-1]].

Follow the wall so you are running north. At the north end, an adjacent roof holds a chest. At night a Gold Skulltula clings to its east wall. Kill it and take the token with the Longshot; this is Gold Skulltula #91 [[gf1-283-2, checklist-skulltulas-18-2]]. The gap is too far to jump. Longshot the chest to pull yourself across; the Longshot range only just reaches it [[gf1-283-3]]. If you cannot reach it, play the Scarecrow's Song to make Pierre appear as a closer target [[gf1-283-3, checklist-sidequests-24-5]]. Open the chest for a **Piece of Heart** [[gf1-283-3, checklist-heartpieces-5-7, gf3-75-2]]. You can also smash the crate near here for a Purple Rupee [[gf2-87-5]].

Then play the Sun's Song to warp back to the doorway from the kitchen [[gf1-283-3]].

#### Carpenter 3: Jiro

From the doorway, look north. A small jump down leads to a ledge with a single door. Drop down and enter [[gf1-283-3]]. As soon as you are in, pull out the Bow and stand still. When the guard comes around the corner, shoot her before she sees you [[gf1-284-1]]. Run around the corner to the right, left at the next corner, down the hall and around another corner to a cell at a dead end [[gf1-284-1]].

Speak with Jiro and defeat the next Gerudo Thief. Take her Small Key and open Jiro's cell [[gf1-284-1]]. Speak with him and he escapes [[gf1-284-2]]. Return the way you came, watching for the thief near the entrance, who may no longer be stunned [[gf1-284-2]].

#### Carpenter 4: Shiro, and the card

Outside the door from Jiro's cell, look west and drop off the cliff to the roof below. The door on the right as you land leads on [[gf1-284-2]]. Run down the hall and around the corner to the last carpenter, Shiro, locked in his cell [[gf1-285-1]].

Speak with him and defeat the Gerudo Thief who appears. Use the Small Key she drops to open the cell, then run inside and speak with Shiro [[gf1-285-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Gerudo's Fan Club (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158139.png)
> *Obtain the Gerudo's Membership Card*


After Shiro is freed, a Gerudo Lieutenant appears. She is impressed by your sneaking and makes you an honorary Gerudo Thief with the **Gerudo's Membership Card** [[gf1-285-2, gf2-88-3, gf3-75-6]]. That is the condition for Gerudo's Fan Club [[ra-guide-16-1]].

The card does the following:
- It lets you roam the fortress without being harassed or thrown in jail [[gf1-285-3, gf1-420-2]].
- It opens the Gerudo Training Ground and the Horseback Archery Range [[gf1-285-3, gf1-420-2]].
- It lets you open the northwest gate to the Haunted Wasteland [[gf1-285-3]].
- The bridge back in Gerudo Valley is now repaired [[gf1-285-3]].

If you want the Ice Arrows, the Training Ground entrance is in the southeast corner. Speak to the Gerudo by the grating and pay the fee [[gf1-286-1, gf2-116-1]]. The guide quotes a 10 Rupee fee [[gf2-89-2]]. Finishing every room later needs the Silver Gauntlets from the Spirit Temple, but the Ice Arrows can be taken now [[gf1-286-2]]. This is optional for this chapter.

### Horseback Archery Range

Make sure it is daytime for the minigame; the Gerudo who runs it appears only then, so use the Sun's Song if needed [[gf1-286-5]]. Epona's Song summons Epona if she is not nearby, and she appears near the south entrance [[gf1-286-5]]. If you lack Epona, the Gerudos lend you a horse [[gf1-286-6]]. Ride east up the passage on Epona to reach the range [[gf1-286-3]].

For Gold Skulltula #90, go to the range at night first. It is high on the lone target on the north side. Kill it with the Longshot and take the token [[checklist-skulltulas-18-1, gf1-286-4, gf1-479-2]]. Then return to the awning on the south side [[gf1-286-4]].

Speak to the Gerudo dressed in white beneath the awning. You must be riding Epona when you talk to her [[gf1-286-5]]. One round costs 20 Rupees and you get 20 arrows [[gf2-88-7]]. You do not steer Epona; she runs automatically and you only fire [[gf2-115-2]].

Scoring:
- Pots are worth 100 points each [[gf1-512-1]].
- Targets are worth 100 at the exact center, 60 on the outer circle and 30 on the edge [[gf2-88-4]].

Epona runs up and down the course once in each direction before the game ends [[gf1-512-2]].

Strategy:
- On the first pass, shoot every pot as you pass. There are eight pots on the way down [[gf1-512-2, checklist-sidequests-24-4]].
- After the last pot, shoot the big target at the end as Epona loops around it [[checklist-sidequests-24-4]].
- On the way back, put one in each of the three bull's-eyes, then spend remaining arrows on the last target [[checklist-sidequests-24-4]].
- Another version of the plan is to shoot the east targets until you are near enough to hit the south target accurately [[gf1-512-2]].
- Move your aim right to collect all the pots on the first pass, then spend most of your remaining arrows on the target at the end of the track [[ra-comments-97-6]].
- Aim for the pots before the bull's-eyes [[ra-comments-97-3]].
- Another guide suggests shooting all the pots at the bottom, then hitting the first target on the middle left as much as you can while turning around [[ra-guide-16-3]].

One player lowered analog stick sensitivity to about 0.8 and cleared it on the next try after 15 failed attempts [[ra-comments-97-7, ra-comments-97-8]]. Another needed around 100 to 150 tries and finished at 1,510 [[ra-comments-97-5]]. Expect a grind.

Score at least 1,000 points to win the **Piece of Heart** [[gf1-286-7, checklist-heartpieces-5-8]]. Then play again and score at least 1,500 to win the **Biggest Quiver**, which raises your arrow capacity to 50 [[gf1-286-8, gf2-88-6, gf2-155-7]].
