If you reach 1,500 on your very first attempt, you must play again and repeat it to earn the Quiver [[gf1-512-3, gf1-286-8]]. Scoring a perfect 2,000 gives nothing extra [[gf1-286-8]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** A commenter says you must already own the big quiver from the shooting gallery, or the achievement will not unlock [[ra-comments-97-1]]. The achievement is tied to the Biggest Quiver reward for 1,500 points [[ra-comments-97-2, ra-guide-16-2]]. Treat the Quiver as the goal and do not leave the range until you have it.


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Ultimate Horse Master (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158187.png)
> *Complete the Horseback Archery minigame and obtain the top reward*


### Before you leave

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave Gerudo's Fortress:** Confirm you have the Gerudo's Membership Card and the Biggest Quiver [[gf1-285-3, gf1-286-8]]. Collect the roof-chest Piece of Heart and the archery Piece of Heart [[checklist-heartpieces-5-7, gf1-286-7]]. Pick up Gold Skulltulas #90 and #91 now, since the Longshot is already in your hands [[checklist-skulltulas-18-1, checklist-skulltulas-18-2]]. When ready, head for the northwest corner of the fortress, climb the tower, and speak to the Gerudo at the top. She opens the gate to the Haunted Wasteland [[gf1-287-1, gf1-277-1]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 19 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 87 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 32 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 7 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 22 / 26
> - **Chapter collectibles:** Gerudo's Membership Card, roof-chest Piece of Heart, archery Piece of Heart, Biggest Quiver [[gf1-285-2, checklist-heartpieces-5-7, gf1-286-7, gf1-286-8]]
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 65 / 97 (375 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Crossed the broken bridge on Epona or with the Longshot
- [ ] Freed Ichiro, Sabooro, Jiro and Shiro
- [ ] Gerudo's Membership Card obtained (Gerudo's Fan Club, 48896)
- [ ] Roof-chest Piece of Heart opened
- [ ] Gold Skulltula #91 collected (night, Longshot)
- [ ] Gold Skulltula #90 collected (night, Longshot)
- [ ] Horseback Archery 1,000 points: Piece of Heart
- [ ] Horseback Archery 1,500 points: Biggest Quiver (Ultimate Horse Master, 49040)
- [ ] Ready to open the northwest gate

---

## Chapter 20: Desert Colossus and the Spirit Temple

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Temple_of_Time["Temple of Time"]
  Haunted_Wasteland["Haunted Wasteland"]
  Desert_Colossus["Desert Colossus"]
  Spirit_Temple["Spirit Temple"]
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Twinrova{{"⚔ Twinrova"}}
  Ganondorf{{"⚔ Ganondorf"}}
  Ganon{{"⚔ Ganon"}}
  Dying_Guard_Lockout>"⛔ Dying guard disappears after the Temple of Time"]
  Final_Battle>"⛔ Ganon's Tower final battle"]
  Haunted_Wasteland --> Desert_Colossus
  Desert_Colossus --> Spirit_Temple
  Spirit_Temple --> Twinrova
  Ganondorf --> Final_Battle
  Final_Battle --> Ganon
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Dying_Guard_Lockout,Final_Battle ponr;
  class Bongo_Bongo,Twinrova,Ganondorf,Ganon boss;
```

This chapter takes you across the Haunted Wasteland to the Desert Colossus and then through both halves of the Spirit Temple. Gerudo's Fortress and the Bottom of the Well must be finished first [[gf1-19-2]]. The temple is the only dungeon that needs both Young and Adult Link, with a small West Wing for the child and a much larger East Wing for the adult [[gf1-294-1]]. Along the way you learn Nayru's Love, the Requiem of Spirit (the last song), the Silver Gauntlets, the Mirror Shield, the Spirit Medallion and the final Gold Skulltulas and Heart Piece in the desert [[gf1-291-3, gf1-303-1, gf1-323-3, checklist-sidequests-2-36]].

> [!TIP]
> **Bring before you go:**
> - Longshot, to cross the sand gap and reach crates, trees and ledges [[gf1-289-1]].
> - Lens of Truth, to see the Poe guide [[gf1-289-7]].
> - Bombs, for the cracked wall [[gf1-290-2]].
> - Din's Fire, which the child half uses constantly [[gf1-298-1]].
> - An empty bottle of bugs for the soft soil patch [[gf1-292-2]].
> - A Magic Bean for that patch [[gf1-292-4]].
> - Hover Boots, Fire Arrows and the Mirror Shield's eventual partner, the Biggoron Sword, if you have it [[gf1-310-3, gf1-314-1, gf1-321-2]].

### Chapter Dungeon Items (3 Items)

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Dungeon Map | Colossus Chamber, chest from lighting the two torches in front of the statue with Din's Fire (Young Link) | **TAKE** | [[gf1-300-2]] |
| Compass | Triforce/Compass Room, chest on the far ledge after Zelda's Lullaby (Adult Link, Longshot) | **TAKE** | [[gf1-311-3]] |
| Boss Key | Boss Key Room, ornate chest behind the flame pillar (Adult Link) | **TAKE** | [[gf1-319-2]] |

### Haunted Wasteland

You begin between a pair of flags. Run forward past the crate to a wide gap of sinking sand and Longshot the crate on the far side [[gf1-289-1]]. Then head for the next flag to the northwest and keep going from flag to flag. If you stray too far from a flag you are sent back to the start [[gf1-289-2]]. If you cannot see the next flag, play the Song of Storms and the lightning lights up the horizon briefly [[gf1-289-3]].

After the second flag a sign points to the Carpet Merchant, who only sells Bombchus [[gf1-289-4]]. The price is 200 rupees [[gf2-91-1]]. Skip him unless you need Bombchus.

The flags end at a large stone structure ringed with red flags. Enter the opening near its south side and drop down to the bottom [[gf1-289-5]]. Kill the Gold Skulltula inside and take its token with the Longshot [[gf1-289-5, checklist-skulltulas-18-3]]. This is Skulltula #92 and it sits in plain sight [[checklist-skulltulas-18-3]]. Light both torches with Din's Fire or Fire Arrows to make a chest with a Purple Rupee appear [[gf1-289-6]]. Climb out by the ladder on the south side [[gf1-289-5]].

Climb the ramp on the north side to the top of the structure. Face north, turn on the Lens of Truth and a ghost appears [[gf1-289-7]]. Keep the Lens on and follow the ghost. Leevers start surfacing when you are close, so roll out of their way. When you reach a pair of flags, run toward the single flag to the west to enter the Desert Colossus [[gf1-289-7]].

### Desert Colossus (first visit, as Adult Link)

The Colossus is crawling with Leevers. They can only appear in sand, so stand on solid ground to be safe [[gf1-290-1]].

#### Great Fairy's Fountain: Nayru's Love

Go to the pair of trees on your right as you enter from the Wasteland. Bomb the cracked wall between them and walk through the doorway [[gf1-290-2, ra-guide-16-4]]. Stand on the Triforce symbol and play Zelda's Lullaby. The Great Fairy gives you Nayru's Love [[gf1-290-3]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Nayru's Love (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158189.png)
> *Learn Nayru's Love*


Nayru's Love makes you invulnerable, costs a quarter of a full magic bar and is very useful against the temple's Iron Knuckles [[gf1-290-4, gf1-391-1]]. Leave the fountain.

#### The oasis and Skulltula #94

Head to the dried-up oasis in the south. Stand on the pile of flat rocks and play the Song of Storms to fill it and make a Fairy Fountain [[gf1-291-1]]. This is optional. At night, a Gold Skulltula (#94) sits near the top of one of the palm trees, and you take its token with the Longshot [[gf1-291-2, checklist-skulltulas-18-5]]. Standing in the shallows lets you fire the Longshot undisturbed [[gf1-291-2]]. If it is not night yet, wait or come back after the bean ride below. You can also pick it up on your later adult visit.

#### Entering the temple and the Requiem of Spirit

Go to the huge statue at the west end and enter the small door at its base [[gf1-291-3]]. There is nothing to do inside yet, so turn around and walk back outside [[gf1-292-1]]. Sheik appears and teaches the Requiem of Spirit, which warps you to the Colossus [[gf1-292-1, gf3-235-8]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Musician (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158190.png)
> *Learn all 12 songs*


The Requiem of Spirit is the sixth warp song, and it is the 12th and last song. Musician needs every other song first, so if it does not pop, one of the earlier songs is still missing [[gf1-396-3, gf2-153-11]]. Players also report odd timing on this achievement. One got it on learning the Requiem, and another had it fail until the Sun's Song was learned last, after which a developer update fixed it [[ra-comments-100-2, ra-comments-100-5]]. If it fails to award, a developer advised restarting the emulator to load the updated achievement [[ra-comments-100-6]].

### Temple of Time (switch to Young Link)

Play the Prelude of Light to warp to the Temple of Time [[gf1-292-1]]. Return the Master Sword to its pedestal to become Young Link [[gf1-172-3]]. Do nothing else here for now.

Play the Requiem of Spirit to warp back to the Colossus as a child [[gf1-292-1]]. The Gerudo will not let Young Link cross the fortress bridge, so this song is the way in [[gf1-396-4]].

### Desert Colossus (as Young Link)

Go to the entrance of the Spirit Temple. To the left of the door is the game's last patch of soft soil [[gf1-292-2]]. Drop a Bottled Bug into it and a Gold Skulltula (#93) appears. Kill it and take the token. You do not need to recapture the bugs afterwards [[gf1-292-2, checklist-skulltulas-18-4]]. If you need more bugs, look under a rock north of the oasis [[gf1-292-3]].

Plant your final Magic Bean in the soil, then go into the temple [[gf1-292-4]]. This bean is the key to the last Skulltula and Heart Piece later [[gf1-307-1]].

### Spirit Temple, West Wing (Young Link)

Fire enemies are common here, so switch back to the Hylian Shield if you have been using the Deku Shield [[gf1-295-1]].

#### Entrance and Nabooru

Run up the stairs, watching out for the pair of pots that fly at you. At the top, go left. A Gerudo Thief is trying to crawl into a hole that is too small for her. This is Nabooru [[gf1-295-1, gf1-295-2]]. Talk to her. When she asks what you are up to, say "Nothing, really." The rest of her questions do not matter. She asks for your help against Ganondorf and moves aside, and you crawl through the crawlspace [[gf1-295-3, gf1-295-4]].

#### Armos Knight Room

Run forward, dodge the Blade Trap and kill the Fire Keese. Climb the stairs and go right to find an Armos Knight circled by a Blade Trap. Drop a bomb beside it and hit it with your sword just before the bomb goes off. Bombchus also work if you mess up [[gf1-296-1]]. Then use the Boomerang on the Keese on the wall above the second crawlspace [[gf1-296-2]]. When every enemy is dead the doors unseal, so take the door to the left of the crawlspace [[gf1-296-3]].

#### Stalfos Room

A Stalfos attacks. Dodge its swings and hit it right after it attacks. Nayru's Love before the fight makes it safer [[gf1-297-1]]. Another option is to push it off the spinning platform by slashing it when it is near the edge [[gf3-109-10]].

Next, stun the Green Bubble in the pit with the Boomerang and kill it with the Slingshot. Then aim the Boomerang just left of the bridge so it curves into the Crystal Switch behind the grating. The bridge falls [[gf1-297-2, gf1-297-3]]. Cross the bridge and open the chest for a Blue Rupee [[gf2-92-1]]. Then take the door on the far side.

#### Anubis Pit Room

An Anubis flies at you here. Only fire can kill it, and since you have no Fire Arrows as a child, use Din's Fire. The fire trap from the crystal switch also works but is slow [[gf1-298-1]]. Stay away from it so it cannot shove you into the pit. When it dies, the door on the right unseals [[gf1-298-2]].

#### Lower Silver Rupee Chamber

A Wallmaster is in this room. Run in small circles until it drops and kill it, then clear the Fire Keese. You cannot collect rupees with them around [[gf1-298-2]]. Collect the five Silver Rupees from the grating. Two are near the bottom, two near the top and one is in the opposite corner [[gf1-298-3]]. Do not climb near the Gold Skulltula on the left of the grating, because it damages you even through the fence [[gf1-298-3]]. Collecting all five makes a bridge appear [[gf1-298-3]].

Kill the Keese before crossing so they cannot knock you off. On the far side, turn around. The Gold Skulltula (#96) on the right of the grating is killed and collected with the Boomerang [[gf1-298-4, checklist-skulltulas-19-1]]. Then light both torches near the wall with Din's Fire. A chest appears on the other side of the room containing a Small Key. Cross back, open it and exit through the door [[gf1-298-5]].

#### Armos Knight Room (second pass) and Small Hall

You come out of the right-hand door. To its right is a crawlspace, so go through it [[gf1-299-1]]. Open the locked door on the other side of the Small Hall [[gf1-299-1, gf1-298-5]].

#### Sun Cavern

A tiled wall is on your right. Boomerang the two Skullwalltulas, then climb to the top. Turn around and Boomerang the Gold Skulltula (#97) behind you [[gf1-299-2, checklist-skulltulas-19-2]]. Run into the large cavern, where two Lizalfos appear. Kill the Skulltulas on the wall too. Find the rock higher on the wall that has a beam of light around it and blow it open with a Bombchu. If you have none, hit the crystal switch on the wall for a chest of Bombchus [[gf1-299-3, gf3-80-10]]. The sunlight now hits the sun panel on the floor and unseals the door on the opposite wall [[gf1-299-3]].

#### Colossus Chamber and the Dungeon Map

Push the statue near the door off the cliff. It lands on a button and unseals a door on an upper level [[gf1-300-1]]. Jump down to the floor. Light the pair of torches in front of the statue with Din's Fire and open the chest for the **[Dungeon Map]** [[gf1-300-2]]. Then return to the east side, climb the tiles, go up the stairs, up another flight, and through the door on the left [[gf1-300-2]].

#### West Stairwell and Upper Silver Rupee Chamber

Run up the stairs and through the door [[gf1-301-1]]. This room has Blade Traps, low walls and a sun panel puzzle. Run through the gap on the right, rolling past the Blade Trap. Pull out the stone block with the sun panel and push it into the beam of light on the northwest side. The south door opens [[gf1-301-2]]. Kill the Beamos near the blocks if it bothers you [[gf1-301-3]].

You still need the Small Key. Collect the Silver Rupees by walking along the low walls. The platforms under the three Beamos Statues let you jump up once you destroy the statues [[gf1-301-3]]. When you have all five, the gold torch on the east side lights [[gf1-301-4]]. Light a Deku Stick on it and light the three unlit torches (near the east door, in the northeast, and near the south door) before any goes out. A chest with a Small Key appears on the low wall in the south [[gf1-301-5]]. Go through the south door.

#### Upper West Stairwell and the Iron Knuckle

Look above the door you came through for the Gold Skulltula (#98). Boomerang it, then take the door at the top of the stairs [[gf1-302-1, checklist-skulltulas-19-3]].

Run left to find a suit of armor on a throne. Strike it to start the fight [[gf1-302-2]].

**Iron Knuckle (mini-boss).** Its attacks can deal up to four hearts of damage [[gf2-93-4]]. You can dodge and strike afterwards, or cast Nayru's Love and attack while invulnerable (you can still be knocked around) [[gf1-302-3]]. A third method as Young Link is approach, backflip as it begins to slash, jump slash when it is open and backflip away again, repeating until it dies [[gf3-110-8]]. Bombs thrown at it also work [[gf3-82-1]]. Without Nayru's Love expect at least seven or eight jump attacks [[gf2-93-4]]. At half health part of its armor breaks and it moves and attacks faster [[gf1-302-4]]. Blocking does little [[gf1-302-4]]. Pillars in the room hold health and items, so get it to knock them over if you are low [[gf1-302-3]].

When it dies, the door behind its throne opens. Walk through the tunnel and you are back outside, high on one of the Colossus's arms [[gf1-302-5]].

### Silver Gauntlets

Kaepora Gaebora gives a speech. After that, open the chest in front of you for the **[Silver Gauntlets]** [[gf1-303-1, gf3-82-2]]. A cutscene shows Koume and Kotake capturing Nabooru [[gf1-303-1]]. Careful, the ledge is narrow and a fall costs you the trip back [[gf2-93-5]]. Sources disagree on which arm the chest is on, so take whichever chest the tunnel leads you to [[gf1-303-1, gf3-158-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Infamous Silver Gauntlets (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158140.png)
> *Obtain the Silver Gauntlets from the Spirit Temple*


Young Link is finished here. Play the Prelude of Light to return to the Temple of Time [[gf1-303-2]].

### Temple of Time (back to Adult Link)

1. After the Prelude of Light drops you in the Temple of Time, walk through the Door of Time to the Master Sword pedestal [[gf1-303-2, gf1-397-4]].
2. Retake the Master Sword to become Adult Link [[gf1-305-1]].
3. Play the Requiem of Spirit to warp back to the Desert Colossus. It works for either age, so you skip the Haunted Wasteland [[gf1-396-4]].

### Desert Colossus (second visit, as Adult Link)

Make it night with the Sun's Song if necessary [[gf1-307-1, gf1-393-3]]. Because you planted the bean as a child, a Magic Leaf platform now floats to the left of the temple door [[gf1-307-1]]. Ride it. It flies around the Colossus, passing two rocks and finally the arch near the entrance. At night, jump off after the second rock to find a Gold Skulltula (#95) on top of it [[gf1-307-2, checklist-skulltulas-18-6, gf1-482-1]].

Run back to the leaf and ride it again until it passes the arch east of the temple entrance. Jump onto the arch for the final **Piece of Heart** (#36) [[gf1-307-3, gf1-448-1, checklist-sidequests-33-7]]. A player reported that this heart stayed put after saving and resetting, so you cannot lose it by leaving [[ra-comments-101-7]].

A white rock to the left of the temple hides a Secret Cave with Business Scrubs selling Green and Red Potions [[gf3-86-1]]. They cost 40 rupees a bottle each, and the Silver Gauntlets are required to lift the boulder [[gf3-239-6]]. This is optional.

### Spirit Temple, East Wing (Adult Link)

#### Entrance and Rolling Boulder Room

Run up the stairs, avoiding the flying pots. At the top, go right to a doorway blocked by a giant blue block. Grab it and push it forward until it falls into a pit [[gf1-310-1]]. The chest up the stairs contains Bombchus [[gf3-109-2]]. Continue up the stairs. Bomb the Beamos and hit the Crystal Switch on the ceiling to open the left and right doors. Take the right door [[gf1-310-2]].

In the Rolling Boulder Room, collect five Silver Rupees around the pit. One hangs above the floor, so use the Hover Boots to run out to it and switch back to the Kokiri Boots afterwards. The far door unseals when all five are collected [[gf1-310-3, gf1-310-4]].

Stand near the Block of Time on the left and play the Song of Time. It moves into an adjacent alcove and the Gold Skulltula (#99) is behind its original spot [[gf1-310-5, gf1-507-2, checklist-skulltulas-19-4]]. Go through the door on the north side.

#### Small Chest Room

Step in and a Like-Like drops from the ceiling. Kill it and open the chest for a Small Key. Return through the boulder room to the entrance [[gf1-311-1]].

#### Triforce/Compass Room

Take the unlocked door on the west side of the entrance [[gf1-311-2]]. Step toward the Triforce symbol and a Wolfos appears. Backflip when it attacks and Jump Attack its back. Then stand on the symbol and play Zelda's Lullaby. A chest appears on the opposite ledge, so Longshot to it and open it for the **[Compass]** [[gf1-311-3]]. Drop down, climb the tiled wall on the opposite ledge, and return to the entrance through the door at the top [[gf1-311-3]].

#### Lower Mirror Chamber

Take the locked door on the north side [[gf1-312-1]]. Move slowly because a Like-Like falls from the ceiling and pots on the right throw themselves at you. Climb the tiled wall on the east side [[gf1-312-2]]. At the top, an invisible Floor Master hides behind the mirror. Z-Target it, Jump Attack it once, then kill its offspring with Din's Fire [[gf1-312-2]].

Stand behind the mirror, grab the left handle and push it forward so it shines sunlight onto each sun panel. The first two panels make chests appear (a recovery heart and a frost trap), the third unseals the west door, and the last summons a Wallmaster [[gf1-312-3]]. Do not open the frost trap chest [[gf3-82-8]]. Go through the west door.

#### Colossus Chamber (adult)

You are back in the Colossus Chamber. Climb the stairs in front of you, up two flights, and look northwest at the statue's left hand, which has a Triforce symbol on it. Equip the Hover Boots and run out to it. If you miss, return to the east side and Longshot the target on the ceiling [[gf1-313-1]]. Play Zelda's Lullaby on the symbol and a chest appears on the right hand. Re-equip the Kokiri Boots, Longshot to the chest and open it for a Small Key. Drop to the bottom floor [[gf1-313-1]]. Back near the hands, a chest behind some crates holds a Blue Rupee [[gf3-111-4]].

Climb the tiled wall on the west side and both flights of stairs. At the top, look north and Navi flutters to the other side of the pit. Play the Scarecrow's Song and Pierre appears. Longshot over to him and kill the Gold Skulltula (#100) behind him. This is your hundredth token [[gf1-313-2, gf1-313-3, checklist-skulltulas-19-5]].

Return to the west side and use the Longshot target on the ceiling to climb up to the next level. Go up both flights again and through the locked door in the southeast corner [[gf1-313-4]].

#### Lower East Stairwell and Treble Anubis Room

Bomb the Beamos and go up the stairs and through the door [[gf1-314-1]]. Three Anubis are here. Fire Arrows kill them easily. Bomb the Beamos statue on the north side to open the southeast door [[gf1-314-1, gf1-314-2]].

#### Quad Armos Room

A blue switch is in front of you. To open the sealed south door, coax an Armos into standing on it. Stand near the door and shoot the Armos in the northeast corner with an arrow. It hops toward you and steps on the switch, then you go through the door at once [[gf1-314-3]].

#### Upper East Stairwell and East Iron Knuckle Room

Two chests here hold recovery hearts and are visible with the Lens of Truth [[gf1-315-1]]. Take the south door. Run in and right, then slash the armor on the throne to start the fight [[gf1-315-1, gf1-315-2]].

**Iron Knuckle (adult).** The shield is useless against it, so use the Biggoron Sword. With it, four jump attacks are enough [[gf2-95-2]]. Use Nayru's Love if things get hot [[gf1-315-2]]. A reliable tactic is to wait for it to attack, backflip out of range, Jump Attack as you land, then backflip again, repeating until it dies [[gf1-517-5]]. Defeating it opens the door behind the throne and a tunnel [[gf1-315-3]].

#### Mirror Shield

On the Colossus statue's outstretched hand, a chest appears. Open it for the **[Mirror Shield]**, which reflects light, absorbs certain magic in one boss fight and cannot be stolen by Like-Likes [[gf1-316-1, gf1-316-2]]. Be careful not to fall, or you must run back through the dungeon [[gf1-316-2]]. Equip the shield and return through the door [[gf1-316-2]].

#### Small Key Room

Run through the door in front, then through the northeast door [[gf1-317-1]]. Go down the stairs and through the north door to the Quad Armos Room [[gf1-317-2]]. Run to the north end past the Blade Trap, stand in the beam of light and hold R to aim the Mirror Shield at the sun panel on the wall. The north door unseals [[gf1-317-3]]. Open the chest in the Small Key Room for a Small Key and go back out [[gf1-317-4]].

#### Sliding Panels Room

Take the west door of the Quad Armos Room into the Treble Anubis Room, then go through the locked door in the southwest corner [[gf1-318-1, gf1-318-2]]. Throw bombs at the two Beamos on the pillars. The wall of sliding panels can be climbed as they align, or you can Longshot the top panel. At the top, destroy both Beamos and take the north door [[gf1-318-3, gf1-318-4]].

#### Triforce Door Room and Boss Key Room

Go up the stairs, stand on the Triforce symbol and play Zelda's Lullaby. The door before it unseals [[gf1-319-1]]. In the Boss Key Room, kill the Fire Slugs, then use Bombs and the Megaton Hammer to destroy all the false doors. The false door left of the flame pillar hides a golden eye. Shoot it with an arrow, and a second platform appears high above. Longshot the target above that platform, jump to the platform above the flame pillar and step on the switch. The pillar vanishes and you can open the ornate chest for the **[Boss Key]** [[gf1-319-2]]. Another route is to Longshot to the ceiling panel, step on the button and drop down to the chest [[gf2-95-5]]. Return to the previous room.

#### Upper Sun Chamber

Go right and through the west door [[gf1-320-1]]. Block the two pots with your shield, then hit the crystal switch behind the bars on the left with a vertical slash or spin attack, which opens the sealed door [[gf1-320-2]]. A Lizalfos drops in. Kill it and take the left doorway. In the mirror room, kill the White Bubbles, bomb the odd rock formation on the east side, push the mirror until it faces east through the opening, then run through [[gf1-320-3]]. Kill another Lizalfos, push that mirror until it faces north, and return the way you came [[gf1-320-3]].

Back in the first area, the two mirrors now shine sunlight on the ceiling mirror. Jump into the pit, stand in the beam and use the Mirror Shield to light the sun panel on the west wall. The floor is an elevator and lowers you [[gf1-320-3]].

#### Colossus Room and the Boss Door

Face north to see the statue's face. Use the Mirror Shield to reflect the beam onto it. It begins to disintegrate into a grating. Longshot the grating and the Boss Door is revealed. Go through [[gf1-321-1]].

#### Nabooru's Throne Room

After the cutscene, the Iron Knuckle comes to life. Use the same strategies, with the Biggoron Sword, Nayru's Love and the backflip/Jump Attack trick [[gf1-321-2]]. When it falls, the armor breaks to reveal Nabooru. Koume and Kotake brainwashed her, and they capture her again and vanish [[gf1-321-3]]. Go through the door behind her throne and down the North Hall to the boss door [[gf1-321-3, gf1-322-1]].

### Boss: Twinrova

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Twins of Evil requires beating Twinrova without falling from the platform or reviving [[gf1-322-1, ra-comments-46-1]]. Players report that if you die and save during this fight the achievement can fail [[ra-comments-46-1]]. The title says you have 12 seconds to hook to the top for attempts after the first [[ra-comments-46-3]]. Fairies may be used, but a Game Over followed by a fairy revive does not work [[ra-comments-46-5]]. Make sure you have full health, magic, the Mirror Shield, the Hover Boots and your best sword equipped before the door.

You enter on a bottom floor below tall ledges. Climb the tiled wall to the center ledge and walk to the middle of the platform. A cutscene starts the fight [[gf1-322-1]].

**Phase one.** Kotake is Ice and Koume is Fire [[gf1-521-6]]. Do not Z-Target. Run around so both sisters stay in view [[gf1-322-1, ra-guide-16-6]]. When one charges a spell, a beam appears. Use the Mirror Shield to reflect it into the other sister [[gf1-322-1]]. The beam is constant, so you can sweep it around for a couple of seconds, and each sister must take two or three hits [[gf2-96-1]]. Aiming with Z or L ruins the shot [[gf1-521-7]]. Some say to lock on and then release the lock to face the witch directly [[gf3-265-2]]. A missed beam leaves a damaging ring on the floor, and Kotake's ice also freezes you, so reflect every beam anyway [[gf1-521-7]]. Fire and Ice Arrows do nothing [[gf1-322-1]].

**Phase two.** The sisters merge into Twinrova, so you can Z-Target now. Use the Biggoron Sword (kept put away until needed) or the Master Sword [[gf1-522-8]]. You must absorb three spells of the same element in a row with the shield. Two fires then an ice cancels the fires [[gf1-522-1]]. Twinrova waves her right wand for Ice and her left for Fire. Your shield flashes red for fire and white for ice, faster as more are absorbed [[gf1-522-2]]. Release R to dodge unwanted beams, but do not dodge off the ledge [[gf1-522-1]]. After three the shield fires a massive beam, so keep it drawn to connect. Twinrova is stunned and falls to the lower platform [[gf1-322-2, gf1-323-1]].

Quickly equip the Hover Boots, draw your sword and run out. Halfway across, Jump Attack the rest of the way to hit her, then use basic sword strikes [[gf1-323-1, gf1-522-3]]. When she recovers, return to the main platform and switch back to the Kokiri Boots. It helps to release the Z-lock momentarily [[gf1-522-3]]. Repeat. With the Biggoron Sword she goes down after two knockdowns [[gf2-96-2]].

Players warn that the Hookshot must be aimed at the highest point of the platform where the fight begins. Climbing the wall counts as falling, and so does catching the ledge to pull up [[ra-comments-47-1, ra-comments-47-2, ra-comments-47-9]]. After a death, hookshot onto the platform right after entering the door [[ra-comments-46-3]]. Another player says running up counts as falling because you touch the ground around the platform [[ra-comments-46-4]]. Hover Boots help with the jumps to and from the lower platform in the final phase [[ra-comments-47-7]]. This achievement applies to the normal fight only [[ra-comments-46-6]]. Some players use the Longshot to reach her and then crouch-stab [[ra-comments-47-8]]. A speed trick is to run toward her as you absorb the third beam [[gf3-265-4]]. Do not hold R when you do not want a beam, since absorbing the wrong element resets your count [[gf1-522-1]].

When she dies, a cutscene plays and a Heart Container is left on one of the outer platforms [[gf1-323-2]]. Take it, then step into the beam of light opposite it.


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Twins of Evil (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158141.png)
> *Defeat the Twinrova Sisters without falling from the platform or reviving [you have 12 sec to hook to the top for any attempt after the 1st]*


Nabooru appears as the Sage of Spirit and gives you the Spirit Medallion [[gf1-323-3]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Brainwashed (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158142.png)
> *Awake Nabooru and obtain the Spirit Medallion*


A player reported Brainwashed not unlocking right after the boss and a moderator suggested connection issues [[ra-comments-48-1]]. Now play the Prelude of Light to warp back to the Temple of Time [[gf1-325-1]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave the Colossus:** you should have Skulltulas #92 to #100 and the Colossus Piece of Heart. Do not enter the Temple of Time for the Light Arrows scene until the next chapter [[checklist-skulltulas-18-3, checklist-skulltulas-19-5, checklist-sidequests-2-36]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 20 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 96 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 33 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 8 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 25 / 26
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 70 / 97 (405 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Followed the Poe guide to the Desert Colossus
- [ ] Nayru's Love from the Great Fairy
- [ ] Requiem of Spirit learned (all 12 songs)
- [ ] Skulltula #92 in the Wasteland, bugs for #93, bean planted
- [ ] Child half: Map, Skulltulas #96 to #98, Iron Knuckle
- [ ] Silver Gauntlets
- [ ] Night bean ride: Skulltula #95, Piece of Heart #36, palm tree Skulltula #94
- [ ] Adult half: Compass, Skulltulas #99 and #100, Mirror Shield, Boss Key
- [ ] Twinrova beaten without falling
- [ ] Spirit Medallion from Nabooru

## Chapter 21: Light Arrows, Gerudo Training Ground and the Big Poes

This chapter closes out the optional work before the final dungeon. You collect the Light Arrows in the Temple of Time [[gf1-325-3, gf1-391-3]], clear the Gerudo Training Ground for the Ice Arrows [[gf1-364-1]], hunt the ten Big Poes for the fourth Bottle [[gf1-374-3]], and beat the Running Man's time trial [[gf1-529-2]]. You also pick up one more Piece of Heart in Hyrule Field and the last four Gold Skulltulas, which brings the count to 100 / 100 [[gf3-86-2]].

### Before you start

Bring everything below. Each item is needed somewhere in this chapter.

- **Epona and the Fairy Bow** for Big Poes. They can only be captured as Adult Link on Epona with the Bow [[gf1-374-1]].
- **Empty Bottles.** Empty your Bottles before the Poe hunt [[gf3-152-3]].
- **Spare rupees for arrows.** Expect to spend a good amount [[ra-comments-88-10]].
- **Longshot and Silver Gauntlets** for the Zora's Domain and Zora's Fountain Skulltulas [[checklist-skulltulas-9-6, checklist-skulltulas-9-9]]. You also need the **Lens of Truth** in the Fountain passage [[gf1-306-3]].
- **Scarecrow's Song** for the Dodongo's Cavern and Fire Temple Skulltulas [[checklist-skulltulas-8-5, checklist-skulltulas-14-5]].
- **Iron Boots** for the Hyrule Field Heart Piece [[gf1-216-1]].
- **Hover Boots**, plus the items from earlier dungeons, for the Gerudo Training Ground [[ra-comments-137-12]].
- **Megaton Hammer, Song of Time and Bombs** for the Training Ground rooms [[gf1-369-2, gf1-370-4, gf1-372-3]].
- **Silver Gauntlets** are also needed to finish the Training Ground fully [[gf1-364-3]].

> [!TIP]
> Dodongo's Cavern and the Fire Temple each hold map and compass items, and the Fire Temple also holds a Boss Key. A single achievement tracks these, and revisiting Dodongo's Cavern as an adult is a common miss [[ra-comments-121-4, ra-comments-123-12, ra-comments-123-15]]. Confirm you hold the Fire Temple compass as well [[ra-comments-122-4]]. Where each one sits:
> - Dodongo's Cavern map: chest behind the wall blown open on the west side of the main room [[gf3-23-1]].
> - Dodongo's Cavern compass: chest in the Armos trap room [[gf1-73-3]].
> - Fire Temple map: chest reached by dropping over the side of the cage, or by shooting the silver eye [[checklist-sidequests-21-1]].
> - Fire Temple compass: chest at the end of the hall in the fire maze [[gf1-194-1]].
> - Fire Temple Boss Key: chest in the Goron's cell opened by the rusted switch [[gf1-202-1]].

The route diagram marks a lockout involving the dying guard after the Temple of Time. Unable to verify from collected sources.

### Chapter Items to Collect (8 Items Total)

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Light Arrows | Temple of Time | **WATCH** the cutscene as Adult Link with all medallions | [[gf4-33-14, gf1-325-3]] |
| Ice Arrows | Gerudo Training Ground, left side of the treasure room | **OPEN** the big chest at the end of the locked doors | [[gf1-373-5, gf2-90-10]] |
| Fourth Bottle | Poe shop, old castle gatehouse | **DELIVER** 10 Big Poes | [[gf1-132-2, gf1-374-3]] |
| Piece of Heart (#29) | Hyrule Field grotto at a lone tree | **BOMB** the tree, then sink with Iron Boots | [[gf1-216-1, checklist-heartpieces-5-1]] |
| Gold Skulltula #47 | Zora's Domain, night | **LONGSHOT** it | [[checklist-skulltulas-9-6]] |
| Gold Skulltula #50 | Zora's Fountain, night | **LIFT** the gray rock with Silver Gauntlets, **BOMB** the rock beneath it | [[checklist-skulltulas-9-9]] |
| Gold Skulltula #42 | Dodongo's Cavern, first floor | **PLAY** Scarecrow's Song, **HOOKSHOT** | [[checklist-skulltulas-8-5]] |
| Gold Skulltula #73 | Fire Temple, fifth-floor area | **PLAY** Scarecrow's Song, **LONGSHOT** | [[checklist-skulltulas-14-5]] |

### Temple of Time

Play the Prelude of Light to warp to the Temple of Time [[gf2-97-5, gf1-167-2]]. Enter as Adult Link after you have collected all the medallions [[gf4-33-14]]. A cutscene starts as soon as you walk in [[gf1-325-2]]. Zelda reveals herself and gives you the Light Arrows [[gf3-86-3]]. Ganondorf captures her seconds afterward, and she is revealed as the Seventh Sage, the Sage of Time [[gf1-325-2]]. The Light Arrows are the only weapon besides the Master Sword that can hurt Ganondorf, and they are also needed to break the seals in Ganondorf's Keep [[gf1-325-3, gf1-391-4]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** To the Rescue is based on a text ID and fires when a specific line is read. One player reports it popping just as Zelda was captured [[ra-comments-49-1, ra-comments-49-2]]. Do not skip or mash through the dialogue.


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **To the Rescue (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158143.png)
> *Meet Princess Zelda one last time before the final showdown*


Zelda's capture finishes the scene [[ra-guide-18-1]]. Leave the Temple when control returns.

### Gerudo Training Ground

**Overview.** The Training Ground is a ring of obstacle courses and timed challenges around a central Treasure Room that holds the Ice Arrows [[gf1-364-1]]. The reward cannot be missed. At worst you come back later with the Silver Gauntlets [[checklist-sidequests-31-1]]. The route needs a minimum of seven Small Keys [[gf1-364-2]]. The dungeon holds nine keys in total, but only seven are needed on the correct path [[ra-comments-98-1]]. A chest cannot be permanently lost here [[ra-comments-122-8]]. Use the Small Keys at the end in the correct order, or you will need the Silver Gauntlets [[ra-comments-137-13]].

Warp songs do not work inside, so you must walk out when you finish [[gf1-373-6]]. The entrance to the Training Ground from the Fortress: Unable to verify from collected sources.

#### Entrance room

From the top of the stairs, turn around and shoot the golden eye switch above the entrance door with an arrow. A chest appears between two pillars [[gf1-365-1]]. This guide lists a bundle of arrows, while other guides list a Blue Rupee [[gf2-116-2, gf3-76-1]]. Open the chest between the opposite pair of pillars for a Blue Rupee [[gf1-365-2]].

There are three doors. The one directly opposite the entrance leads to the Treasure Chamber, which should be your last stop. The left and right doors lead into the Training Ground [[gf1-365-3]]. Take the right door first [[checklist-sidequests-31-2]].

#### Right wing: timed enemy room

Two Lizalfos and a Beamos wait here, and you are timed [[checklist-sidequests-31-2]]. One guide calls the Lizalfos "Dinolfos" and gives the limit as 1:30 [[gf1-372-2]]. Blow up the Beamos with a Bomb or Bombchus, then kill both enemies [[gf1-372-3]]. A chest with a Small Key appears [[gf1-372-4]]. If you attack a Lizalfos mid-swing it stops what it was doing, so mauling them works [[checklist-sidequests-31-2]].

#### Right wing: lava room (Sea of Fire)

This room has a huge lava pit with pillars. Falling in forces you to recollect all five Silver Rupees [[gf1-370-1]]. Two ways to handle it:

1. Jump to the front pillar, slightly to your left, and play the Song of Time. Two blue blocks appear [[checklist-sidequests-31-3]]. The same song works anywhere Navi flutters in the middle of the room, and one block is enough [[gf1-370-4]]. If you get stuck in this room, just play the Song of Time there [[ra-comments-122-10]].
2. Go left and step on the switch, which clears the circle of fire. Return to the block pillar and jump to the lowest pillar to take a key [[checklist-sidequests-31-3]]. Another account puts the key on a high ledge against the right wall, reached from the Blocks of Time [[gf1-370-5]].

Silver Rupees in this room:
- **Rupee 1:** Longshot to the target above the entrance doorway [[gf1-370-2]].
- **Rupee 2:** a platform to the left of the cliff edge. Jump down to it [[gf1-370-3]].
- **Rupees 3 and 4:** from the lower Block of Time, jump left, then jump out to the next platform [[gf1-370-6]].
- **Rupee 5:** hit the switch on the platform to the right to shut off the pillar of flames [[gf1-370-7]].

When all five are collected the left door unseals [[gf1-370-8]].

#### Right wing: underwater rupee room

Enter the side door with the torch beside it [[checklist-sidequests-31-4]]. Play the Song of Time in the middle of the room to make the floor blocks vanish. Equip the Iron Boots and Zora Tunic and sink into the pool [[gf1-371-1]]. Kill the Shell Blades with the Hookshot and watch for the Blade Trap [[gf1-371-2]]. With the Longshot you avoid swapping boots repeatedly [[checklist-sidequests-31-4]]. When all five Silver Rupees are collected, a chest with a Small Key appears above the pool [[gf1-371-3]].

#### Right wing: Hammer and fire room

Kill the Fire Slugs and Fire Keese. A door unseals and a chest with arrows appears [[gf1-369-1]]. Smash every perimeter statue with the Megaton Hammer. The two statues beside the arrow chest hide a switch and a golden eye [[gf1-369-2]]. Step on the switch to extinguish the flames around the central chest and take the Small Key [[gf1-369-2]]. Shoot the eye to make another chest drop [[checklist-sidequests-31-5]].

#### Right wing: rotating statue room

Stand on the round platform as it circles the room. Shoot every statue eye in one pass. A chest drops and the catwalk door unlocks [[checklist-sidequests-31-6]]. Use the Longshot to reach the chest, or just jump if you lack it, and take the Small Key [[gf1-368-2]].

The Totem Room has a door on its high right ledge. Its chest holds a Small Key [[gf1-368-1]].

#### Left wing: sand and rock room

Back in the entrance room, take the left door. You have one minute to kill two Stalfos. Their chest holds a key [[checklist-sidequests-31-7]].

#### Left wing: timed silver rupee room

A timer starts as you enter. A Wallmaster drops on you if you stand still too long in the middle section, and the ceiling has Hookshot targets [[checklist-sidequests-31-8]]. The rolling-boulder version of this challenge has a 1:30 limit and opens the exit door at the back when finished [[gf1-366-2]].

If your room has icicles and a Freezard, one guide gives these steps for the 1:30 timer [[gf3-108-3]]:
1. Move forward and Longshot up, fall to the rupee, then Longshot back up.
2. Take a right, then a left, and slash the icicles for rupee two.
3. Double back to the end and walk off the edge toward the rupee, grabbing the edge to get it.
4. Stand in front of the fire wall on your left and Longshot up to the rupee.
5. Take a right, then two lefts, and kill the Freezard for the last rupee.

#### Left wing: wolf room, catwalk and Like Likes

Four Wolfos attack as you enter. Kill them and a chest with arrows appears [[gf1-367-1]]. Use the Lens of Truth to see a hole in the wall above the fake door [[checklist-sidequests-31-9]]. Destroy the fake door with Bombs or the Hammer, look above it with the Lens, and Longshot to the target [[gf1-367-3]]. Step on the switch to unlock the door, then walk through the door ahead onto the catwalk above the statue room. The door there is already unlocked, so take the key from the chest [[checklist-sidequests-31-9]].

Return to the wolf room and push the silver gauntlet block with the Silver Gauntlets. Three Like Likes sit in three holes, so shoot them with arrows or sword spin [[checklist-sidequests-31-10]]. Afterward, use the Lens. The back-right hole's chest holds a key [[checklist-sidequests-31-10]]. Ignore the chest on the ledge, because it freezes you [[gf3-77-7]]. Another account adds that two chests (a Huge Rupee and a Blue Rupee) appear when the Like Likes die [[gf3-77-6]].

#### Treasure room and the Ice Arrows

Return to the entrance room and take the center door. The Treasure Room has nine locked doors, and the final key is hidden in the room itself [[gf1-373-2]]. Loot as you go [[checklist-sidequests-32-1]]:
- Chests to the right of the entrance hold Bombchus and a Bundle of Arrows [[gf1-373-4]].
- Two locked doors in on the left lead to a Purple Rupee chest [[gf2-90-6]].
- The next two doors lead to a Red Rupee chest [[gf2-90-7]].
- The next door leads to a chest of arrows [[gf2-90-8]].

For the hidden key, unlock the left door and climb the fence wall immediately to your left into the hidden room [[checklist-sidequests-32-1]]. In the first room on the left, use the Lens of Truth to spot the ceiling passage, climb the wire wall beside the door, and open the chest at the top [[gf1-373-3]]. Continue through the remaining locked doors to the center [[checklist-sidequests-32-1]]. Open the big chest at the end for the Ice Arrows [[gf4-33-11, gf1-390-4]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Graduate of Gerudo's Training Ground (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158188.png)
> *Complete the Gerudo's Training Ground and obtain your reward*


The only real use of Ice Arrows noted in the sources is killing Like Likes in one hit [[checklist-sidequests-32-8]]. Walk out of the dungeon [[gf1-373-6]].

### Hyrule Field

🗺️ **Map:** [View Hyrule Field Map by thegamemaster8](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/5394-hyrule-field-map)

#### The Big Poe hunt

Big Poes only appear to Adult Link on Epona, and the hunt can be done any time once you have Epona and the Bow [[gf1-374-1, gf1-18-5]]. Regular Poes appear in the same places on foot but do not count [[gf1-374-4]]. Shoot a Big Poe with two arrows before it disappears [[gf4-22-3]]. It turns into a Wisp. Dismount, walk to it and choose to bottle it [[gf3-52-13, gf1-374-2]]. Bring empty Bottles [[gf3-152-3]].

**Tips for the shooting:**
- Steer a Poe away from walls by moving toward the direction you want it to turn away from [[checklist-sidequests-25-1]].
- Poes keep respawning along the same path while you stay put, and they respawn at the same spot [[ra-comments-88-6, ra-comments-87-2]]. A respawn can take 15 to 30 seconds [[ra-comments-87-5]].
- Make Epona walk instead of run, and approach at a good angle [[ra-comments-87-3]].
- Let her walk slowly and keep your aim pointed where the Poe will spawn [[ra-comments-88-4]].
- Time your shots to get two off before the Poe vanishes. A Poe is briefly invincible while flashing red, so you cannot fire rapidly [[ra-comments-88-7, ra-comments-88-8]].
- Shoot again the moment the first arrow lands, and try to spawn the Poes along a straight line [[ra-comments-88-9]].
- You may need to buy arrows again in Kakariko Village after every one or two Poes [[ra-comments-87-7]]. Another player finished with 40 arrows [[ra-comments-87-8]].
- Commenters call the last spawns very stubborn [[ra-comments-87-9, ra-comments-88-1, ra-comments-87-13]]. The game gives no hints about spawn spots [[ra-comments-87-12]].

The guides number the Poes differently. The list below is the Sidequest Walkthrough's order, from hardest to easiest, with other guides' notes alongside.

| # | Spawn | Citation |
|---|---|---|
| 01 | Tree near the steep hill by Gerudo Valley. Ride past it bumping its right side and go up the hill. At the top use remaining carrots to shoot. | [[checklist-sidequests-25-2]] |
| 02 | Ride straight across the path fork in front of Gerudo Valley, straight at the Poe so it will not sway into the wall. | [[checklist-sidequests-25-3]] |
| 03 | Back Epona into the land bridge near Kakariko Village. The Poe appears in front of you, so shoot it before the fence. | [[checklist-sidequests-25-4]] |
| 04 | Stream north of Hyrule Castle. Back into the far corner, or ride forward through the bushes. | [[checklist-sidequests-25-5]] |
| 05 | Ride between the tree at the Lon Lon Ranch entrance and the ranch itself. Steer it right to avoid the stone wall. | [[checklist-sidequests-25-6]] |
| 06 | Pass the corner of the stone wall, usually toward the castle. | [[checklist-sidequests-25-7]] |
| 07 | Pass the lone silver rock near the stone wall. | [[checklist-sidequests-25-8]] |
| 08 | Ride toward Lake Hylia through the woods next to Kokiri Forest and pass a center tree. | [[checklist-sidequests-25-9]] |
| 09 | Ride through the middle of the bush patch outside those woods. | [[checklist-sidequests-25-10]] |
| 10 | Ride past the sign directly in front of Hyrule Castle. | [[checklist-sidequests-25-11]] |

**Cross-checks from other guides:**
- The cliff overlooking the Kakariko Village entrance is called the hardest Poe in one guide. You ride right up to the edge and the Poe appears and flies off [[gf1-375-2]].
- Another guide says to hug the wall at the Kakariko entrance, head south to the water, turn around, and pass to the right of the stone pillar [[gf3-53-8]].
- One guide has the first Poe at the signpost just south of Hyrule Castle [[gf1-375-1]].
- Other guides place Poes at the wall east of Lon Lon Ranch [[gf1-376-1]] and at the Y-shaped road southeast of the ranch [[gf1-376-2]].
- Another puts a Poe south of the tall tree at the bottom tip of the Y [[gf1-377-1]] and one in the center of the golden-leafed grove [[gf1-377-2]].
- Poes also appear at the three-way road near Gerudo Valley [[gf1-378-1]] and at the lone tree west of Lon Lon Ranch. For that last one, follow it to where the hill levels out before shooting [[gf1-379-1, gf1-379-2]].
- Last, a Poe appears south of the river in the northwest field [[gf1-379-3]] and at the lone tree outside the Lon Lon Ranch entrance [[gf1-380-1]].
- The Ranger-style summary: the ten are south of the Market, by Lon Lon Ranch, east of the ranch at the Y-shaped wall, south of Kakariko Village, east of the Gerudo Valley path, west of Kokiri Forest, in the southeast trees, and in the northwest trees [[gf4-22-11]].

#### The Poe shop and the fourth Bottle

Deliver the bottled Poes to the Poe Shop in the former gatehouse at the Hyrule Castle drawbridge [[gf1-132-2, gf1-374-3]]. Each Big Poe is worth 100 points. A prize arrives at 1,000 points, which takes ten Poes [[gf2-59-6]]. The payoff is the fourth Bottle plus 500 rupees in total [[checklist-sidequests-25-12, gf2-60-5]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Poe Collector (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158178.png)
> *Catch all 10 Big Poes and obtain your reward*


For Drunkard you must hold all four Bottles:
1. Talon's Super Cucco game at Lon Lon Ranch [[gf1-42-2]].
2. The Cucco Lady in Kakariko Village [[gf1-50-2]].
3. Ruto's Letter Bottle at the bottom of Lake Hylia [[gf1-383-6, gf3-152-2]].
4. The Big Poe Bottle you just earned [[gf1-383-7]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Drunkard (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158179.png)
> *Obtain all 4 Bottles*


#### Iron Boots grotto Piece of Heart (#29)

Sources describe the tree in different places. Heart Piece FAQ says it is to the right of the castle (to the left of the wall) and is the tree away from the others [[checklist-heartpieces-5-1]]. The Walkthroughs put the lone golden tree northwest of Lon Lon Ranch [[gf1-216-1, gf2-71-2, gf4-17-17]]. Follow whichever matches, as follows:

1. Drop a bomb at the base (the south side) of the tree to open a pit [[gf1-216-1]].
2. Drop in. A Tektite floats on the water, so kill it [[checklist-sidequests-17-5]].
3. Equip the Iron Boots and sink to the bottom. The Zora Tunic is not needed [[checklist-heartpieces-5-1]]. The Piece of Heart sits there [[gf1-216-1]].

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave the Poe hunt behind, confirm the fourth Bottle is in your inventory and the Piece of Heart is collected. Only then move on to the Running Man.

### Gerudo Valley

#### Wind Runner: the Running Man time trial

The race only unlocks once the bridge has been repaired [[ra-comments-102-1]]. As Adult Link, challenge the Running Man (who bought your Bunny Hood as a child) to a footrace from the Gerudo Valley Carpenter's Tent to the entrance to Kokiri Forest [[gf1-529-2]]. The exact challenge dialogue: Unable to verify from collected sources.

The Running Man always beats you by one second, and the race is effectively a time trial, since he keeps your best time [[gf1-529-2]]. Reach the Kokiri Forest entrance in 1:30 or less [[ra-comments-102-2]].

**Strategy:**
- Summon Epona before the challenge [[ra-guide-17-17]].
- Spend all your carrots before entering Hyrule Field, because carrots reset on a map change [[ra-comments-102-9]].
- One player finished in 1:19 on the first try using Epona and went around the fences in Hyrule Field instead of risking a failed jump [[ra-comments-102-5]].
- Another had Epona near the small fences outside the Gerudo Bridge area, close and positioned to get up quickly, and took the lower way [[ra-comments-102-7]].
- Galloping non-stop and taking the left path without fences does not work [[ra-comments-102-6]].
- Make sure Epona does not rear up [[ra-comments-102-8]].
- Backwalking or hessing also works on a controller that can do it [[ra-comments-102-3]]. Using Epona also works [[ra-comments-102-4]].
- Do not get stuck on the bridge or at the blank area at Gerudo. One player stuck on a tree root may have softlocked [[ra-comments-102-10, ra-comments-102-11]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** The time limit is 1:30 from the tent to the Kokiri Forest entrance. A slow run just means you retry [[ra-comments-102-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Wind Runner (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158192.png)
> *Finish the Running Man challenge in less than 1:30*


### Zora's Domain

🗺️ **Map:** [View Zora's River/Domain Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/56-zoras-river-domain-map)

Enter from Zora's River, which opens off Hyrule Field at the 2:00 position [[gf1-95-1]].

**Gold Skulltula #47.** This one appears at night. Play the Sun's Song to change the time [[gf1-119-1]]. Climb the tunnel on the left of King Zora's throne room to the top of the frozen waterfall [[gf1-472-1]]. Walk to the edge of the waterfall, at the location where the Diving Game proprietor used to stand, look out and to the left, and Longshot the Skulltula on the wall [[checklist-skulltulas-9-6, gf3-62-5]].

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave Zora's Domain, confirm the night-time Skulltula is collected. The only entrance to the Fountain from here is the passage behind King Zora's throne, which opens after King Zora moves [[gf1-305-2, checklist-sidequests-12-5]].

### Zora's Fountain

Go through the opening behind King Zora's throne [[gf1-305-2]]. Wait for night. This is the final Skulltula [[gf3-86-2]].

**Gold Skulltula #50:**
1. Go to the south end of the Fountain where the Great Fairy cavern entrance is [[gf4-33-12]].
2. Lift the large gray rock in the middle of the cave mouth with the Silver Gauntlets [[gf1-306-1]].
3. Another rock sits underneath. Bomb it to open a pit [[gf1-306-2]].
4. Drop in and land at the bottom of a ramp winding up through the outer walls [[gf1-306-2]].
5. Use the Lens of Truth on the way up. Invisible Big Skulltulas line the ramp, and you can kill them with the Longshot [[gf1-306-3]].
6. At the top, kill the one visible Big Skulltula, then check the wall left of the ladder's top. The final Gold Skulltula is there [[gf1-306-4, gf4-33-12]].

That is 100 / 100 [[gf3-86-2]]. If a different Skulltula keeps you at 99, check Zora's Domain first [[ra-comments-110-1]].

### Dodongo's Cavern

**Gold Skulltula #42** (first floor). Enter as Adult Link [[checklist-skulltulas-8-5]]. From the Antechamber, ride out to the middle platform in front of the giant Dodongo Skull. Go right onto the next elevator, jump to the lower ledge without riding up, and take the southern darkened tunnel [[gf1-169-2]]. Baby Dodongos emerge from the ground in the hall [[gf1-170-1]].

When Navi flies up toward the ceiling and turns green, play the Scarecrow's Song to summon Pierre [[checklist-skulltulas-8-5, gf1-487-1]]. Hookshot to Pierre and kill the Gold Skulltula in the alcove [[gf1-170-2]]. Walk out or use Farore's Wind to return to the start [[gf1-170-2]].

### Fire Temple

**Gold Skulltula #73** (fifth-floor area). The Fire Temple is in the middle of Death Mountain Crater [[gf1-8-8]]. The Bolero of Fire warps you back [[gf2-153-2]]. Retrace the route to the boulder maze room by the same sequence you used the first time [[gf1-183-2, gf1-184-3, gf1-185-2]]. Then go to the upper part of the boulder maze room, to the northwestern exit [[checklist-skulltulas-14-5]]:
1. Play the Scarecrow's Song to make Pierre appear [[checklist-skulltulas-14-5]].
2. Longshot to Pierre, then to the nearby target [[checklist-skulltulas-14-5]].
3. Ride up and make your way to the fifth-floor area. The Skulltula is close by [[checklist-skulltulas-14-5]].

Other guides number this same climb differently and describe the setup. The scarecrow appears on a platform, and a rising platform follows [[gf1-190-4, gf1-190-5]]. A grate leads up to a Skulltula on the wall [[gf3-56-8]], and in the next room a Skulltula waits on the wall [[gf2-65-2]].

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave the Fire Temple, check the count. This is the last Skulltula, and the cumulative total should read 100 / 100 [[gf3-86-2]].

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 21 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Chapter collectibles:** Light Arrows, Ice Arrows, fourth Bottle, Piece of Heart #29 and Gold Skulltulas #42, #47, #50, #73 [[gf1-325-3, gf1-373-5, gf1-374-3, gf1-216-1, gf3-86-2]]
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 75 / 97 (435 / 690 points)
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 100 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 34 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 8 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 25 / 26

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Light Arrows received; To the Rescue unlocked
- [ ] Ice Arrows from the Gerudo Training Ground; Graduate of Gerudo's Training Ground unlocked
- [ ] All ten Big Poes delivered; Poe Collector unlocked
- [ ] All four Bottles held; Drunkard unlocked
- [ ] Running Man beaten in under 1:30; Wind Runner unlocked
- [ ] Hyrule Field Iron Boots grotto Piece of Heart (#29)
- [ ] Skulltula #47 (Zora's Domain, night)
- [ ] Skulltula #50 (Zora's Fountain, night)
- [ ] Skulltula #42 (Dodongo's Cavern)
- [ ] Skulltula #73 (Fire Temple)
- [ ] 100 / 100 Gold Skulltulas

## Chapter 22: Lon Lon Ranch, Dampe's Race and the Fishing Pond

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Lon_Lon_Ranch["Lon Lon Ranch"]
  Kakariko_Graveyard["Kakariko Graveyard"]
  House_of_Skulltula["House of Skulltula"]
  Lake_Hylia["Lake Hylia"]
  Fishing_Pond["Fishing Pond"]
  Morpha{{"⚔ Morpha"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Dark_Link{{"⚔ Shadow Link (Dark Link)"}}
  Lake_Hylia --> Fishing_Pond
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Morpha,Bongo_Bongo,Dark_Link boss;
```

This chapter cleans up a run of adult-Link side content: Malon's obstacle course on Epona, Dampe's second race, the three fishing-pond achievements, the Lakeside Laboratory dive, and the House of Skulltula's final reward. Hearts are tallied here too, since the Laboratory dive gives the last Piece of Heart you need for 20 hearts [[gf3-123-1, ra-guide-20-2]]. The route runs Lon Lon Ranch, then Kakariko Graveyard, then the Fishing Pond and Lake Hylia, then back to the House of Skulltula.

> [!NOTE]
> **Plenty of Hearts check:** Dampe's gravedigging Piece of Heart was a Young Link task in Chapter 3. If it was dug up and left behind, it is lost forever and turns into 50 rupees [[ra-guide-20-1, ra-comments-101-1]]. Count your Pieces of Heart against the checkpoints before you rely on this chapter's total.

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning (Mischievous Hero):** Do not save and quit, or reset, between hooking the owner's hat and sinking it in the pond. After a reload the owner stays hatless but the achievement does not count [[ra-comments-113-7, ra-comments-113-8, ra-comments-3-9]].

### Chapter Items to Collect

| Item | Where Found | Action / Requirement | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Cow (for Link's House) | Lon Lon Ranch | Beat Malon's course on Epona | [[gf1-166-2]] |
| Piece of Heart #31 | Dampe's race, Kakariko Graveyard | Finish under 1:00 | [[gf1-245-1]] |
| Golden Scale | Fishing Pond | Catch a large fish | [[gf2-107-2]] |
| Piece of Heart #30 | Lakeside Laboratory | Dive to the bottom with the Golden Scale | [[checklist-heartpieces-5-2, gf1-218-1]] |
| Huge Rupee | House of Skulltula | 100 Gold Skulltulas | [[checklist-skulltulas-2-7]] |

### Lon Lon Ranch

Malon's course needs the Biggoron Sword trading sequence's wake-up of Talon: once he is back at the Ranch, Malon lets you run a timed course on Epona [[gf1-166-1]]. Commenters note it is done after Malon takes over the Ranch [[ra-comments-85-7]].

1. Ride Epona into the Ranch (call her with Epona's Song if needed) [[gf1-354-2]].
2. Talk to Malon while mounted. Talking to her twice gets the offer of the obstacle course; the time to beat is 50 seconds [[gf2-52-5, checklist-sidequests-24-8]].

#### Course technique

- Hug the inside wall and use a carrot just before each hurdle. The pair of very low hurdles a third of the way round needs only one carrot for both [[gf1-166-3]].
- Epona clears the smallest fences without a boost, so save carrots for the big gates and straightaways [[ra-comments-85-3, checklist-sidequests-24-9]].
- Recover carrots on the long empty stretch before the last hurdle, then burn them all on the last leg of the second lap [[gf1-166-3]]. Carrots come back about every six seconds [[checklist-sidequests-24-9]].
- To jump a gate Epona must be dead center and moving fast. Boost a beat or two before the fence, not right at it. If she does not jump, you boosted too late [[ra-comments-85-1, ra-comments-85-2, ra-comments-85-4, ra-comments-85-5, ra-comments-85-6]].
- Finish in 0:49 or better; one commenter got 48.20 [[gf2-109-2, ra-comments-85-10]]. Aim for 50 seconds or less [[checklist-sidequests-24-9]].

Malon says the present is too big to carry and sends it to your house. It is a cow living in your Kokiri Forest home [[gf2-52-5, ra-comments-85-8]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Jump Over (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158176.png)
> *Complete the obstacle course at Lon Lon Ranch and obtain a reward*


The site does not mark this as missable [[ra-comments-85-9]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave Lon Lon Ranch:** With 100 tokens collected you will also need the Ranch Skulltulas (lone tree, corral wooden section, window shutters, wall beside the silo) if any are outstanding [[checklist-sidequests-3-10, checklist-sidequests-3-11, checklist-sidequests-4-6, checklist-sidequests-4-7]]. The Ranch's Piece of Heart is in the crawlspace behind the milk crates in the back building [[gf3-18-3]].

### Kakariko Graveyard

Dampe's second race pays Piece of Heart #31 [[gf1-245-1]]. The Longshot is required for the last gap [[checklist-heartpieces-5-4]].

1. Enter the Graveyard and find Dampe's grave: the only gravestone with flowers on the left aisle. Pull it backward and drop into the pit [[gf1-244-2]].
2. Dampe challenges you and starts without notice [[gf1-135-1]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** You must finish in 1:00 or less. A commenter's 59-second run is reported as winning the prize but not the achievement, and 1:00:20 was over the limit, so aim for a comfortable margin [[ra-comments-84-1, ra-comments-84-2]].

#### Race route

🗺️ **Map:** [View Dampe's Race Map by DrgNyt](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/7461-dampes-race-map)

Roll the whole way; it is slightly faster [[gf1-245-1]]. Wear the Goron Tunic, hug inside corners, and avoid fireballs [[checklist-heartpieces-6-5]]. Listen for his fire throw before rolling into a corner [[checklist-sidequests-20-3]]. Being hit once does not ruin a run [[ra-comments-84-8]]. The route [[gf1-245-2]]:

1. Run forward, dodging flames. Turn right at the hall's end, tight.
2. Turn left at the next corner, wide.
3. Run between the pair of flames along the left side of the gap.
4. Turn left, then right immediately, wide.
5. Turn right, left, then left tight. Take another tight turn and go through the door.
6. Right, right, wide left. Run along the left or right side of the tunnel, enter the wide room, and take the door on the right.
7. Left, wide right, through another door. Run to the wall, turn right, then tight left.
8. Jump onto the ledge on your left and hug the left wall. Wide left, then tight left into the tall room with the pit.
9. Longshot to a torch near the top and pull yourself up. One commenter found lining this up the hardest part; another missed the first shot and still made 58:75 [[ra-comments-84-5, ra-comments-84-10]].

Go through the door on the far side and climb the stairs to the Windmill [[gf1-245-3]]. Dampe gives the Piece of Heart if you beat the minute [[gf1-245-1]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Ghost Chaser (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158175.png)
> *Complete Dampe's race in 1 minute or less*


#### Plenty of Hearts: other Graveyard hearts

If any earlier Graveyard hearts are outstanding: the bean-platform crate [[gf1-433-1]], the Redead grave with the Sun's Song chest [[gf1-432-1]], and Dampe's digging. Dampe's piece is in one of twelve patches and moves each time you leave; stand on a patch so he stops and pay 10 rupees [[gf1-56-1, gf1-56-2]]. Bring about 100 rupees and dig only on soil patches [[checklist-heartpieces-6-1]]. Collect the piece before leaving [[ra-guide-20-1]].

### Fishing Pond

🗺️ **Map:** [View Fishing Pond Map by TLS64](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/51-fishing-pond-map)

Do these three in order: big fish first without the sinking lure, then the loach, then the hat. The pond costs 20 rupees per round [[gf2-37-2]]. The door to the pond is on the small island north of the lake shore [[gf1-98-5]].

#### 1. Expert Angler: the Golden Scale

Do not use the sinking lure for this; one source says it disqualifies you [[checklist-heartpieces-6-4]]. The sinking lure only becomes available after a 15 lb. catch [[gf3-236-1]].

- The large fish sits near the submerged log on the left and the reeds on the right [[gf1-217-1]]. The big one usually stays underwater near the big log, and you may need to catch the small fish swimming with it first [[checklist-heartpieces-6-4]].
- Walk onto the log to its halfway point, cast, and when it bites pull down to the bottom right and reel [[ra-comments-112-5]]. Z-target a fish, cast with B, and hold A while pulling back to reel [[gf1-515-1]]. Alternating back-left and back-right helps [[gf1-217-3]].
- Fish that break free vanish. If the pond runs low, leave and re-enter to respawn them [[gf1-413-2]].

> [!NOTE]
> The needed weight is unconfirmed. Sources say 15 lb. [[gf1-217-3]], but commenters report 13 lb worked and 12 lb did not, and one says it must beat your child record by 10 lb [[ra-comments-112-11, ra-comments-112-12, ra-comments-112-8]]. Keep fishing until the owner hands over the Golden Scale. A 19 lb catch from under the log worked [[ra-comments-112-3, ra-comments-112-4]].

Take the catch to the owner for the Golden Scale [[gf2-107-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Expert Angler (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158562.png)
> *Catch a large fish and obtain a golden reward as Adult Link*


#### 2. Accomplished Angler: the Hylian Loach

The Hylian Loach is a rare fish that appears only on some visits [[gf3-236-3]]. One commenter says fish as an adult first [[ra-comments-3-7]].

Reported spawn method (counts are commenter reports and not fully consistent, so verify by looking):
- The spawn is tied to a counter of paid fishing rounds, not entering the area. It carries over across resets and ages [[ra-comments-117-1, ra-comments-117-4, ra-comments-117-5, ra-comments-117-6]].
- Bring at least 80 rupees. Talk to the owner, say yes, then quit. Repeat three times, leave, re-enter at once, and pay a fourth time [[ra-comments-115-1, ra-comments-115-2, ra-comments-115-3, ra-comments-115-4]]. Another says it spawns every 4th time you pay [[ra-comments-116-5, ra-comments-116-13]]. Some commenters disagree on the cycle [[ra-comments-114-3, ra-comments-114-4]], and one found the method failed for them [[ra-comments-116-11]].
- Check by looking: it is on the left, under the lily pads, shadowier than normal fish [[ra-comments-115-3, ra-comments-118-5, ra-comments-118-7]]. Use C-Up to check [[ra-comments-116-6]]. If you cannot see it, pay and leave, repeating until it appears. Once found, do not pay again [[ra-comments-117-9, ra-comments-117-10]].
- Playing the Sun's Song outside and re-entering is another reported approach [[ra-guide-17-15]].

Catching it:
- It is only catchable at or swimming to the lily pads [[ra-comments-116-8, ra-comments-118-2]]. It ignores bait until it heads there [[ra-comments-120-1]].
- Let the two nearby fish bite, then do nothing so they release and leave [[ra-comments-114-12, ra-comments-115-5, ra-comments-115-6]]. Do not hook another fish or you startle it [[ra-comments-119-13]].
- Stand on the log, cast into the lily pads, and lure with soft tugs [[ra-comments-118-3]]. A regular lure works; the sinking lure can help [[ra-comments-116-16]]. To get the sinking lure, take it and talk to the owner so he allows it [[gf3-236-2]].
- When it bites, press A at once to snag it, then hold A and the stick left or right [[ra-comments-115-12, ra-comments-115-13]]. Tilt toward the fish's head and ease off when it jumps or kicks up dust [[ra-comments-119-9]]. Keep the rod pulled left while holding the button [[ra-comments-119-14]].
- If it escapes, save inside the Water Temple beforehand so you can reset and retry nearby [[ra-comments-117-7, ra-comments-117-11, ra-comments-115-8]].

After landing it, talk to the owner; the achievement pops [[ra-comments-115-9, ra-comments-114-8]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Accomplished Angler (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158203.png)
> *Catch the rare Hyrule Loach*


#### 3. Mischievous Hero: the owner's hat

As an adult the owner wears a hat you can snare with a perfect cast [[gf3-236-4]].

1. Stand close in front of the owner, face to face. Cast so the lure bypasses him and hits the wall [[ra-comments-113-1]].
2. Hold A to reel in so the line attaches to the hat [[ra-comments-113-2]]. The hitbox and invisible walls around him are odd [[ra-comments-113-10]].
3. Walk far into the pond, cast, and let the hat sink. The line does not cast far with the hat attached [[ra-comments-113-3]].
4. Throwing it back unhooks and sinks it, unlocking the dialogue and achievement [[ra-comments-113-4]]. You are fined 50 rupees [[gf3-236-4, ra-comments-113-9]].

Comments conflict on whether the sinking lure works [[ra-comments-113-5, ra-comments-113-6]]; do not use it here. Do not save or reset between hooking and sinking [[ra-comments-113-7]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Mischievous Hero (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158202.png)
> *Have the pond owner lose his hat permanently*


### Lake Hylia

With the Golden Scale, enter the Lakeside Laboratory, jump in the pool, and dive to the very bottom (9 meters), then talk to the scientist for Piece of Heart #30 [[gf1-218-1, gf3-42-1, checklist-sidequests-17-4]]. Iron Boots will not work for this dive [[checklist-heartpieces-5-2]]. Heart pieces now total 36 with 8 Heart Containers, making 20 hearts [[gf3-123-1, ra-guide-20-2]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Plenty of Hearts (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158191.png)
> *Obtain all 20 hearts*


Remaining Lake Hylia tokens: crate at the pool's bottom (Iron Boots, then Hookshot) [[gf1-218-2]], the dead-tree top (Longshot at night) [[gf1-243-2]], and the soil mound by the Laboratory [[gf3-30-11]].

### House of Skulltula

Reaching 100 tokens requires every dungeon except Ganon's Castle [[ra-comments-109-8]]. The last one reported is in Zora's Fountain: lift the white boulder with the Silver Gauntlets at night and climb the cave [[gf3-232-1, gf4-33-12]]. Save after each token, since a crash loses unsaved ones [[ra-comments-109-2, ra-comments-109-3]]. The world map marks a finished zone with a Skulltula icon [[ra-comments-109-1, ra-comments-110-9]].

Talk to the man in the House of Skulltula for the Huge Rupee (200 rupees) [[checklist-skulltulas-2-7, gf2-97-7]]. It repeats by leaving and re-entering [[gf1-451-4]]. If the reward does not trigger, restart the emulator, make sure achievements are loaded, and repeat the event [[ra-comments-110-5]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Family Reunion (25 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158199.png)
> *Collect 100 Gold Skulltulas and obtain your final reward*


> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave:** Confirm the cow, Piece of Hearts #30 and #31, and the Huge Rupee are all done.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 22 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 100 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 36 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 8 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 25 / 26
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 82 / 97 (500 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Malon's course finished in under 50 seconds; cow delivered (Jump Over)
- [ ] Dampe's race under 1:00; Piece of Heart #31 (Ghost Chaser)
- [ ] Golden Scale won without the sinking lure (Expert Angler)
- [ ] Hylian Loach caught and shown to the owner (Accomplished Angler)
- [ ] Owner's hat hooked and sunk without saving in between (Mischievous Hero)
- [ ] Laboratory dive; Piece of Heart #30; 20 hearts (Plenty of Hearts)
- [ ] 100 Gold Skulltulas and Huge Rupee claimed (Family Reunion)

This chapter is one long errand: as Young Link wearing the Mask of Truth, listen to all 32 Gossip Stones in a single play session for **Lifestyle and Stones**. [[gf1-352-1, ra-comments-80-9, ra-comments-81-26]] The stones are spread across Hyrule, and the warp songs are the quickest way between clusters. [[ra-guide-19-2]] Nothing in this chapter is permanently lost, but the achievement does not tolerate interruptions. [[ra-comments-82-3]]

### Preparation and Rules of the Tour

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** Do not reset, exit the emulator or save-quit until the achievement pops. The achievement description says it has to be done in one session, and one player reports the stone progress resets every time the game is started. [[ra-comments-80-9, ra-comments-82-3, ra-comments-80-1, ra-comments-82-4]]

- **Keep the mask on its C button.** A commenter lists clicking off the game screen, hitting a stone with a sword, saving, or unequipping the item from the C button as things that may have stopped the achievement for them. [[ra-comments-3-10]]
- **Stay Young Link.** Only Young Link can wear the Mask of Truth. [[gf1-352-1]] The Master Sword in the Temple of Time returns you to adulthood, so leave it alone while you are there. [[gf1-175-4, gf1-257-4]]
- **Get the mask first.** The Mask of Truth comes from finishing the Happy Mask trading sequence, which ends by paying 50 rupees for the Bunny Hood and then borrowing the mask. [[gf1-124-2, gf1-349-2]] One commenter was unsure whether the mask must be worn, and their achievement did not unlock. [[ra-comments-80-2]] Wear it at every stone to be safe.
- **Warp songs.** You need the warp songs, and they work for Young Link: Minuet to the Sacred Forest Meadow, Prelude to the Temple of Time, Nocturne to the Graveyard ledge, Serenade to Lake Hylia, Requiem to the Desert Colossus. [[ra-guide-19-2, gf1-395-2, gf1-397-4, gf1-397-2, gf1-396-2, gf1-396-4]]
- **Items to carry.** You will want Bombs (the castle grotto, Goron City's rock maze, the stone circle near Gerudo Valley), Din's Fire, the Song of Storms, the Silver Scale and a Cucco source. [[gf1-173-3, gf1-468-2, gf1-457-2, gf1-90-2, gf1-412-2]]
- **Which stones to talk to.** Don't skip one. One player failed twice because a single stone was missed. [[ra-comments-80-6]] The underground grotto stones are identical, so only one of them needs to be heard. [[ra-comments-80-8]] Bring a guide for the hidden ones, since some are hard to find. [[ra-comments-80-10]]
