### Kokiri Forest

🗺️ **Map:** [View Kokiri Forest Map by kingofthisgame](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/1099-kokiri-forest-map)

Begin in Kokiri Forest. The first two stones are by the Great Deku Tree. Mido's archway to the Deku Tree's clearing is on the east side of the forest. [[gf1-21-3, gf1-24-1, ra-comments-81-5]]

1. **Stone #1, Deku Tree's Left Stone.** Walk to the clearing and listen to the stone there. [[ra-comments-81-5]]
2. **Stone #2, Deku Tree's Right Stone.** This is the hidden one. In the original version it sits behind one of the Deku Tree's roots. Climb the root just before it and sidehop across. [[ra-comments-81-2, ra-comments-80-4]] If you struggle, head to the northwest stone and circle around. [[ra-comments-81-3]] A commenter says the full route is to climb the tree and go all the way around, and that it is easy to miss. [[ra-comments-82-1]]

Unable to verify from collected sources: the exact coordinates of either stone beyond the descriptions above.

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave: confirm the mask is still on its C button, then climb the vines behind Mido's House to the hollow log that leads to the Lost Woods. [[gf1-23-2, ra-comments-3-10]]

### Lost Woods

🗺️ **Map:** [View Lost Woods Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/54-lost-woods-map)

3. **Stone #3, Kokiri Clifftop Stone.** This stone is just outside the Lost Woods. [[ra-comments-81-6]] The Song of Storms near the stone outside the entrance also opens a cave with a chest holding a Red Rupee, which is optional. [[gf3-44-2]]
4. **Stone #4, Stick Scrub's Stone.** Enter the Lost Woods and go left. Take the first two lefts, drop down the cliff, and the stone sits next to the Deku Scrub who sells the Deku Stick upgrade. [[ra-comments-81-7, checklist-sidequests-7-7, ra-guide-4-1]] Defeat the Scrub by reflecting its Deku Nut or with a jump attack if you need to get close. [[gf1-23-3]]

When you are done, return to the Lost Woods entrance for the Minuet.

### Sacred Forest Meadow

🗺️ **Map:** [View Sacred Forest Meadow Map by R351D3NT3V1L4](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/5354-sacred-forest-meadow-map)

Play the Minuet of Forest. It carries Young or Adult Link to the Sacred Forest Meadow. [[gf1-395-2]] Three stones are here: Saria's Stone, Above Maze Right Stone and Above Maze Left Stone. [[ra-comments-81-8]]

5. **Stone #5, Saria's Stone.** Saria's courtyard is at the top of the stairs, past the hedge maze. [[gf1-47-3, ra-comments-81-8]]
6. **Stones #6 and #7, Above Maze Right and Left.** The hedge-maze ladder at the end puts you on top of the hedges, where the two stones are. [[gf3-44-10, ra-comments-81-8]]

Unable to verify from collected sources: which stone is on which side, and the exact ground route as Young Link from the Minuet arrival point.

### Temple of Time

Play the Prelude of Light to warp to the Temple of Time. [[gf1-397-4]] Four stones sit around here: Blinky, Pinky, Inky and Clyde. [[ra-comments-81-9]]

8. **Stones #8 to #11.** Listen to all four. Unable to verify from collected sources: which stone stands where around the plaza.

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave: do not touch the Master Sword. Drawing it returns Link to adulthood, and the mask only works for Young Link. [[gf1-175-4, gf1-352-1]]

### Hyrule Castle

🗺️ **Map:** [View Hyrule Palace Map by ThE_bLImP](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/52-hyrule-palace-map)

🗺️ **Map:** [View Path to Zelda Map by TLS64](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/55-path-to-zelda-map)

Walk to the Castle Garden. Two stones are here, and a third is in a grotto by the moat. [[ra-comments-81-10, ra-comments-81-11]]

12. **Stone #12, Stone Above The Vines.** Climb the ivy wall by Malon and run forward past the gossip stone to the top of the large marble arch. Listen to that stone. [[gf1-38-3, gf2-15-1, ra-comments-81-10]] Drop down on the far side of the arch. [[gf1-38-3]]
13. **Stone #13, Stone Above The Stone Wall.** Run up the hill at the third graphical break near its center toward the flowers, then forward and slightly left to the brick wall. Climb it. At the top, run forward past another gossip stone. [[gf1-38-4, gf1-38-3]]
14. **Stone #14, Stone Beneath The Royal Tree.** Jump off the cliff past that stone into the moat, swim to the end where it breaks left, and climb up to the stone platform. [[gf1-38-5]] Stand beneath the tree at the corner of the fence and play the Song of Storms to open a hole. [[gf1-173-3, ra-comments-81-11]] Drop in. There are three bombable walls, one in front of you, one left (slightly behind where you land) and one right. [[gf1-173-3, gf3-238-2]] The room with the bugs, pots and rocks holds a Gossip Stone. [[gf3-238-2]] Unable to verify from collected sources: which wall hides that room.

Do not let a guard spot you in the Garden. A spotted Link is thrown back outside the gate. [[gf1-90-3, gf1-173-4]]

### Kakariko Graveyard

Play the Nocturne of Shadow. It takes Young or Adult Link to the high ledge of the Graveyard. [[gf1-397-2]]

15. **Stone #15, Non-Grave Stone.** Listen to the stone on that ledge. [[ra-comments-81-12]] Unable to verify from collected sources: its position on the ledge.

Young Link can do little else here. Run west and jump the fence to the lower Graveyard and Kakariko Village. [[gf1-246-2]]

### Dodongo's Cavern

From Kakariko, take the guard's gate to Death Mountain Trail and walk to the cavern. [[gf1-58-2, gf1-62-5]] If you ever need to reopen the entrance, throw a Bomb Flower so it lands on or near the boulder, and throw one near the cracked wall inside. [[gf1-66-2, gf1-68-1]]

16. **Stone #16, Delicious Stone.** In the main chamber, go to the back right and listen to the stone. [[ra-comments-81-13]]

### Goron City

Climb the trail, keep going right at the large post with the red flag, and enter the archway into Goron City. [[gf1-62-5]]

17. **Stones #17 and #18, Unbreakable Stone and Medigoron's Stone.** Both are in Goron City. [[ra-comments-81-14]] The boulder maze on the top floor has a Gossip Stone in it. Bomb through the rocks to the end of the maze (or follow right, left, left, right). [[checklist-sidequests-14-3, gf3-186-2, gf1-468-2]] Unable to verify from collected sources: which stone is Medigoron's.

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** An earlier Goron City achievement is voided if you destroy the three entrance boulders to the left of the tightrope, so leave them alone. [[ra-comments-68-3, ra-guide-8-2]]

### Death Mountain Trail

18. **Stone #19, Biggoron's Stone.** Exit by the top of Goron City and climb the trail. [[gf1-66-1, ra-comments-81-15]] Boulders crash down the path. Dodge them and crouch under the Hylian Shield with R when lava shadows appear. [[gf1-169-1, gf1-86-2]] Unable to verify from collected sources: the stone's position on the climb.

### Death Mountain Crater

19. **Stone #20, Antisocial Stone.** Enter through the cave to the right of Kaepora Gaebora at the summit. [[gf1-87-2]] Go right inside the Crater. [[ra-comments-81-16]]

> [!WARNING]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** Without the Goron Tunic, Young Link's time in the Crater is limited. He dies if time runs out, and fairies do not help. Walk straight to the stone and leave. [[gf1-175-1, gf1-435-2]]

### Lake Hylia

Play the Serenade of Water. It sends Young or Adult Link to Lake Hylia. [[gf1-396-2]] Three stones are here, easy to forget. [[ra-comments-80-11, ra-comments-81-1]]

20. **Stone #21, Southeast Drowned Stone.** Head southeast. [[ra-comments-81-17]]
21. **Stones #22 and #23, Southwest Drowned and Little Bridge.** Go clockwise from there. [[ra-comments-81-18]] The two stones are at the very far edge of the water, next to the wall. [[ra-comments-80-3]] Tip: pressing B while swimming makes you faster. [[ra-comments-80-15]]

Take the underwater tunnel in the submerged ruins near the shore, east of the laboratory, back to Zora's Domain. The Silver Scale is required. [[gf1-102-1, gf1-412-2]]

### Zora's Domain

🗺️ **Map:** [View Zora's River/Domain Map by EverythingZelda](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/56-zoras-river-domain-map)

22. **Stone #24, Mweep Stone.** Take the Zora's Domain warp. [[ra-comments-81-19]] Unable to verify from collected sources: its exact location in the Domain.

From King Zora's throne room, take the passage behind the throne to Zora's Fountain. [[gf1-102-4, gf1-305-2]]

### Zora's Fountain

23. **Stones #25 and #26, Jabu's Stone and Farore's Stone.** Both are in the Fountain. [[ra-comments-81-20]] Farore's Stone may be near the Great Fairy island behind Jabu-Jabu, where the weak wall by the white rock is bombed. [[gf3-233-3]] Unable to verify from collected sources: exact positions of either stone.

When done, return to the Domain, go up to the front entrance and out to Zora's River. [[gf1-119-3]]

### Zora's River

24. **Stone #27, Sleepless Waterfall Stone.** Go down to the river for this one. [[ra-comments-81-21]] Unable to verify from collected sources: its exact position.
25. **Stones #28 and #29, Grotto Stone and River Grottos Stone.** Grab the bottom Cucco at the start of the river and glide to the upper grottos area, following the hidden left river path near the fork. [[ra-comments-81-22, checklist-heartpieces-4-2]] Enter the generic grotto on the left for the Grotto Stone, then exit for the River Grottos Stone. [[ra-comments-81-23]] The open-air cave on the high platform holds a Gossip Stone and a Red Rupee chest, and may be the generic grotto. [[gf3-238-12, gf3-29-6]]

Follow the river downstream back to Hyrule Field. [[gf1-119-3]]

### Hyrule Field

🗺️ **Map:** [View Hyrule Field Map by thegamemaster8](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/n64/197771-the-legend-of-zelda-ocarina-of-time/map/5394-hyrule-field-map)

26. **Stone #30, Webbing Stone.** Go to the circle of rocks outside the Gerudo Valley entrance and bomb the center. [[ra-comments-81-24, checklist-skulltulas-5-2]] Drop down, burn the webs with Din's Fire, and find the stone. [[gf1-457-2, gf3-237-9]] Unable to verify from collected sources: whether the stone can be heard without burning the webs.

### Gerudo Valley

Head west through the canyon into the valley. [[gf1-100-3]]

27. **Stone #31, Valley Waterfall Stone.** Jump down to the ledge at the bottom of the valley. [[ra-comments-81-25]] Grab the Cucco near the bridge and glide down. [[gf1-101-2, gf4-9-8]]

### Desert Colossus

28. **Stone #32, Sandstone.** Play the Requiem of Spirit, then walk south from where you arrive. [[ra-comments-81-26, gf1-396-4]] The achievement should pop when you hear it. [[ra-comments-81-26]]


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Lifestyle and Stones (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158173.png)
> *Listen to all 32 rumors from the Gossip Stones around the world in one session*


If it does not pop, you missed a stone. Cross-check the table above. [[ra-comments-80-6]] Unable to verify from collected sources: which stones are the commonly missed ones beyond the three at Lake Hylia and the hidden Deku Tree stone.

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave: only after the achievement pops is it safe to save or quit. [[ra-comments-80-9]]

## Chapter 24: Ganon's Castle and the Final Battle

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Ganons_Castle["Ganon's Castle"]
  Ganons_Tower["Ganon's Tower"]
  Ganondorf{{"⚔ Ganondorf"}}
  Ganon{{"⚔ Ganon"}}
  Final_Battle>"⛔ Ganon's Tower final battle"]
  Ganons_Castle -->|"Six trials dispel the barrier"| Ganons_Tower
  Ganons_Tower --> Ganondorf
  Ganondorf --> Final_Battle
  Final_Battle --> Ganon
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Final_Battle ponr;
  class Ganondorf,Ganon boss;
```

This is the end of the first file. You clear the six trials in Ganon's Castle, take the Golden Gauntlets, visit the Great Fairy outside for the defense upgrade, climb the tower for the last Boss Key, beat Ganondorf, escape, and beat Ganon. Four achievements land here: The Rock (49289), King of Thieves (49290), My Last Breath (49291) and Home Sweet Home (48275). Cartographer (49294) is checked when the game is completed. [[gf1-327-1, gf1-332-5, gf1-334-1, gf1-342-2, ra-comments-122-1]]

The Light Arrows come from the Temple of Time, and the road to the castle appears once you hold both the Shadow and Spirit Medallions. [[checklist-sidequests-35-2, gf1-423-3, gf1-391-4]]

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Cartographer (49294) needs every Map, Compass and Boss Key. The Boss Key inside Ganon's Tower is the 26th item, and the achievement is checked when you complete the game, not when you pick up the last item. Check the counter in your achievements menu before you fight Ganon. [[ra-comments-3-4, ra-comments-121-2, ra-comments-121-3, ra-comments-122-1, ra-comments-123-10]]
>
> If you finish the game while an item is missing, you must go back for it and beat Ganondorf and Ganon again. [[ra-comments-124-5, ra-comments-122-3]]
>
> Only boss keys count, not small keys. [[ra-comments-122-11]]

> [!CAUTION]
> **Point of No Return:** Beating Ganon ends the first file. Cartographer is checked on completion, so every Map, Compass and Boss Key must already be in hand. Finish every other first-file achievement before climbing the tower. [[ra-comments-121-3, gf1-345-1]]

### Before you enter: preparation

- Check that every earlier dungeon Map, Compass and Boss Key is collected. The count is 25 of 26 before the tower. [[ra-comments-123-22, ra-comments-121-3]]
- Bring at least two empty Bottles for Blue Fire, and also Din's Fire, Fire Arrows, Light Arrows, Ice Arrows, the Lens of Truth, the Longshot, the Hover Boots, Iron Boots, Goron Tunic, Megaton Hammer, Mirror Shield, the Ocarina (Song of Time, Zelda's Lullaby) and bombs. These are used in the trials below. [[gf1-330-5, gf1-331-3, gf1-328-5, gf1-329-1, gf1-335-1, gf1-339-5, checklist-sidequests-35-4]]
- Bombchus are not needed in your pack at the start; one trial supplies them. [[gf1-339-2]]
- **Gold Skulltulas outside the castle:** Hookshot the Gold Skulltula behind the fallen gate and shattered arch. It is only there at night as Adult Link. [[gf1-137-1, gf1-459-1, checklist-sidequests-29-4]]

### Ganon's Castle

#### Entrance and the Keep

Run down the stairs, go right between the two Beamos statues, and go through the door into the Keep. The Keep is a hub with six hallways, one per Sage. Each hallway holds a seal, and destroying all six removes the barrier around the tower. [[gf1-328-1, gf1-327-1, gf1-330-1]]

You can do the trials in any order, but the Shadow trial must be done before the Fire and Light ones can be completed. Do Shadow first. [[checklist-sidequests-35-3]]

#### Shadow trial (do this first)

Go right up the stairs and through the door with the Shadow Medallion. [[gf1-332-2]]

1. Reach the chest on the left with the Longshot (or use the Song of Time to make three Blocks of Time and cross with the Hover Boots). It holds a **Blue Rupee**. [[gf1-332-3, gf2-98-3, gf3-86-4]]
2. Turn around and light the torch with a Fire Arrow. Temporary platforms appear toward the back. [[gf1-332-4]]
3. Run along them to the larger platform. A Like Like waits on it, and you can kill it with an Ice Arrow. Relight the same torch to renew the platforms. [[gf1-332-7, checklist-sidequests-35-4]]
4. On the next platform, run down the opaque platforms to the right and step on the switch before time runs out. A chest drops: open it for the **Golden Gauntlets**. [[gf1-332-5, gf3-86-5, gf4-34-3]]
5. Use the Lens of Truth on the left to find an invisible path to a rusty switch and smash it with the Megaton Hammer. Then follow another invisible path back toward the unsealed door. [[gf1-332-7, gf2-98-4]]
6. The Shadow trial also has an eye switch on the invisible path that makes a chest with a Small Key appear, and a bomb flower on the far right that, when shot, makes a chest with arrows appear (Longshot to it). [[gf3-114-3, gf3-114-4]]
7. In the final room, shoot the seal with a Light Arrow. Impa returns you to the Keep. [[gf1-333-1]]

#### The Rock: Great Fairy outside the castle

> [!TIP]
> Do this now. The Gold Gauntlets let you toss the black obelisks inside and outside the castle, and they appear nowhere else in the game. [[gf1-332-6]]

Leave the castle, cross the bridge of light, and go left under the arch. Climb over the rubble to the end of the small canyon and pick up the black obelisk with the Golden Gauntlets, then throw it aside. A Great Fairy Fountain is revealed. [[gf1-333-2, gf1-411-4, gf4-34-4, ra-comments-103-2]]

Stand on the Triforce symbol and play Zelda's Lullaby. The Great Fairy of Courage gives you the defense upgrade, which doubles your health in effect and puts a white border on your hearts. [[gf1-334-1, gf1-416-2, gf2-154-2, gf3-87-1, gf3-233-6]]


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **The Rock (5 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158193.png)
> *Enhance your defensive power, reducing any damage received by half*


Head back into the castle and return to the Keep. [[gf1-334-1]]

#### Forest trial

Go right from the Keep to the door with the Forest Medallion. [[gf1-328-2]]

- **Room one:** Step into the middle and a Gray Wolfos appears. Backflip away from its attack and jump-attack its back. A chest then appears with a **Blue Rupee**. Stand on the carpet and use Din's Fire to light the four torches, then light the torch above the sealed door with a Fire Arrow. The door unseals. [[gf1-328-3, gf1-328-4, gf1-328-5, gf2-98-1, gf2-98-2, gf4-34-5]]
- **Room two (Silver Rupees, fans and a pit):** If you fall in the pit you must collect the Silver Rupees again. [[gf1-329-1]]
  1. Play the Song of Time when Navi flutters under the first Silver Rupee above the pit. A Block of Time appears. [[gf1-329-1]]
  2. Equip the Iron Boots so the fan doesn't blow you, and walk onto the block for the rupee. [[gf1-329-2]]
  3. When the fan stops, switch to the Hover Boots, wait for it to start again, and run toward the left ledge. The wind carries you across. [[gf1-329-3]]
  4. Switch back to the Kokiri Boots and bomb the Beamos statue. Jump to its platform and go right for another rupee. [[gf1-329-4, gf1-329-5]]
  5. Climb to the front of the sealed door, and when the right fan stops, jump out to grab the next rupee. [[gf1-329-6]]
  6. Wait for the fan on the left of the door to stop, then drop to the switch on the platform beneath it. A Longshot statue appears under the last high rupee. Longshot to it and grab the rupee. The door on the other side unseals; cross via the Beamos platform. [[gf1-329-7, gf1-329-8]]
  7. Room two also has a chest with a Small Key above the door, reached by Longshot to the torch, and chests behind eye switches in the fans: arrows (shoot the eye in the right fan) and bombs (Fire Arrow the frozen eye). [[gf3-113-3, gf3-113-4, gf3-113-5]]
  8. Playing the Song of Time twice with the Iron Boots on drops an Armos onto a blue switch and opens the door ahead. [[gf3-113-6]]
- **Room three:** Shoot the green seal with a Light Arrow. Saria sends you back. [[gf1-330-2]]

#### Water trial

Head right out of the Forest hallway and down the stairs to the door with the Water Medallion. [[gf1-330-3]]

- **Room one:** Stalactites fall and two Freezzards appear. Fire Arrows work well. The door behind the ice unseals once both are dead. Catch Blue Fire from the center in an empty bottle and melt the ice over the door. Fill a second bottle too. Do not bother with the other chests, because one freezes you. The chest on the right has a **Recovery Heart**. [[gf1-330-4, gf1-330-5, gf2-99-1]]
- **Room two (two-minute limit):** Push the rear ice block to the right so it hits a rock, then forward into the pit. Push the other block forward across the filled pit, forward again from the door side, then forward to the far wall. Climb up, melt the red ice with Blue Fire, and hit the rusted switch with the Megaton Hammer. [[gf1-331-1, gf1-331-2, gf1-331-3, gf2-99-2]]
- **Room three:** Shoot the orb with a Light Arrow. Ruto returns you to the Keep. [[gf1-332-1]]
- A red-ice-covered chest in the Water trial holds a **Red Rupee** if you want it. [[gf3-114-1]]

#### Fire trial

Past the Shadow seal, enter the door with the Fire Medallion, wearing the Goron Tunic. You need the Golden Gauntlets here. [[gf1-334-2, gf1-335-1, gf3-113-7]]

1. Jump onto the sinking platform, go right, and grab the first Silver Rupee. [[gf1-335-2]]
2. Return, go right, left at the corner, and straight at the branch to the ledge with rotating flames. Hang on the edge until it is safe. Take the second rupee. [[gf1-335-2]]
3. Follow the path to the black obelisk, throw it aside with the Gauntlets, and take the third rupee under it. [[gf1-335-2]]
4. Go straight at the split to the Fire Slug ledge, kill it, and take the fourth rupee. [[gf1-335-2]]
5. Cross the thrown pillar like a bridge to the last rupee. The rear door unseals. [[gf1-335-2]]
6. Return to the sinking platform, Longshot the target to the right of the door and go through. [[gf1-335-3]]
7. Shoot the seal with a Light Arrow. Darunia sends you back. [[gf1-336-1]]

#### Light trial

At the bottom of the stairs to the right of the Fire door, throw the black obelisk aside with the Gauntlets. It shatters against the shield. The door behind it bears the Light Medallion. [[gf1-336-2, gf3-114-5]]

- **Room one:** Use the Lens of Truth to kill an invisible Big Skulltula and four invisible Keese. A chest in the middle appears with a **Small Key**. Go through the locked door. Other chests are junk and traps. [[gf1-336-3, gf3-87-3]]
- **Room two:** Stand on the Triforce symbol and play Zelda's Lullaby. A chest appears with another **Small Key**. [[gf1-337-1, gf3-87-4]]
- **Room three (one-minute limit):** Longshot to the ceiling target above the central pillar for the first Silver Rupee, then collect the other four from the alcoves and pillar area as the boulders pass. [[gf1-337-2]]
- **Final room:** The Lens of Truth shows the far wall is fake. Walk through it, shoot the orb with a Light Arrow, and Rauru returns you. [[gf1-338-1, gf1-338-2]]

#### Spirit trial

Go right from the Light door and up the stairs to the Spirit Medallion door. [[gf1-338-3]]

- **Room one:** Bomb the Beamos, Longshot to the ceiling target for the rupee above it, then take the second behind it. Gather the other three near the Blade Traps in the corners. The door unseals. [[gf1-338-4, gf1-338-5, gf1-338-6]]
- **Room two:** Kill the two Fire Slugs, then hit the crystal switch beyond the bars with a Spin Attack. A chest appears with **Bombchus**. Send a Bombchu through the gap near the top of the bars to the second crystal between the torches. [[gf1-339-1, gf1-339-2, gf1-339-3, gf3-87-2]]
- **Room three:** Burn the ceiling web with a Fire Arrow, then reflect the sunlight with the Mirror Shield onto the sun panel to the right of the tunnel you came through. Other panels summon Wall Masters. [[gf1-339-4, gf1-339-5]]
- **Final room:** Shoot the orb with a Light Arrow. Nabooru returns you to the Keep and the barrier around the tower disappears. [[gf1-340-1]]

> [!TIP]
> Before you leave the Keep, confirm the counter reads 25 / 26 for Cartographer. The Boss Key in the tower will make it 26 / 26. Then finish every other first-file achievement and save, because the next fights end the file. [[ra-comments-121-3, ra-comments-123-10]]

### Ganon's Tower

Head right in the Keep until you see a bridge on the left. Cross it and enter the tower. [[gf1-340-2]]

#### Tower floors

- **Floor one:** Kill the four Fire Keese, climb the spiral staircase on the left (watch for more Keese), and take the door on the right. [[gf1-340-3, gf1-340-4]]
- **Floor two:** Both doors seal. Defeat both Dinolfos to unseal them and go through the far door. Climb the stairs and take the door on the left. [[gf1-341-1, gf1-341-2]]
- **Floor three:** Two Stalfos appear around a pillar of flames. Kill both and the flames die, revealing an ornate chest with the **Boss Key**. This makes 26 / 26. Go through the door behind it and up the stairs, then take the door on the left. [[gf1-342-1, gf1-342-2, gf2-100-1, gf3-88-1, gf4-35-3, ra-comments-121-3]]
- **Floor four:** Two Iron Knuckles. Shooting one with an arrow wakes it alone, so you can use the backflip-then-jump-attack pattern. If both wake, Nayru's Love is a good idea. Defeating both unseals the doors. Take the door the camera focuses on, climb the stairs, and take the boss door on the left. [[gf1-343-1, gf1-343-2, gf1-343-3]]
- **Floor five:** Pots hold Magic Power and Recovery Hearts. Take the door to the right of where you came in, climb the stairs, and go through the boss door on the left. [[gf1-344-1, gf1-344-2]]

> [!TIP]
> Smash the pots on Floor five to refill magic and hearts before the boss door. You need magic for Light Arrows. [[gf1-344-1, gf1-344-6]]

#### Boss: Ganondorf (King of Thieves)

> [!NOTE]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** King of Thieves needs Ganondorf beaten without falling off or climbing down the platform and without using Fairies. Equip the Hover Boots as soon as the fight starts. A commenter reports it also counted after going down to pick up magic on a 3-heart run, but that is not confirmed by the achievement text. [[gf4-34-1, ra-guide-18-3, ra-comments-50-5]]

Navi cannot Z-Target or advise until a Light Arrow stuns him. [[gf1-344-3, gf1-522-4, gf3-88-2]]

1. When Ganondorf pounds the ground at the start, roll to the outer rim so you do not fall. [[gf3-89-1]]
2. Stand on the perimeter and reflect his lightning balls back with sword swings (or empty Bottles) until one hits him. [[gf1-344-4, gf3-89-2, gf4-34-1]]
3. Once he is struck, shoot him with a Light Arrow before he recovers. He drops to the ground. [[gf3-89-5, gf1-523-1]]
4. Hover across the pit with the Hover Boots and jump-attack when halfway across, then slash. When he recovers, run back to the perimeter and switch back to the Kokiri Boots. [[gf1-344-5, gf1-523-2]]
5. Do not go near him before he is stunned: he uses a Shockwave that collapses floor pieces into pits. A missed ball destroys the panel you stand on and drops you a floor, and you have to climb back up the pillar tiles. Avoid this for the achievement. [[gf1-522-5, gf1-522-6, gf1-522-7]]
6. If he raises both arms to charge a big spell, shoot him in the face with a Light Arrow to cancel it and stun him. If you cannot, deflect the volley with an Advanced Spin Slash. [[gf1-344-6, gf3-89-3, gf3-89-4, gf4-34-2]]
7. Three knockdowns with the Biggoron's Sword are enough. His cape tears as he weakens. [[gf2-100-2, gf2-131-2, gf1-523-3]]


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **King of Thieves (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158145.png)
> *Defeat Ganondorf without falling or climbing down the Platform and without using Fairies*


Commenters suggest memorising the long staircase to the boss if you need to retry. [[ra-comments-50-4]]

#### Escape

Follow Zelda down the tower. You have three minutes and only she can open the sealed doors, so stay near her. [[gf1-345-1, gf1-345-2]]

- On Floor four, go around the burning rubble and through the door she opens. [[gf1-346-1]]
- On Floor three she is trapped in flame and two Stalfos appear. Use Nayru's Love and the Biggoron Sword to kill them quickly. [[gf1-346-3, gf1-346-4, gf3-90-2]]
- On Floor two, follow her through the burning rubble and down the stairs. Take the left door at the bottom. [[gf1-347-1]]
- In the Keep, run along the bridge, past or through the Redead. At the far end she opens the door. Go up the stairs and wait for her to open the last door. [[gf1-347-2, gf1-347-3]]

> [!TIP]
> My Last Breath tips: a commenter says falling debris or fire stones during the escape can drop you to one heart before the fight starts. Another says to avoid walking into the burning rocks if you don't want to risk it. Pick your method before this point. [[ra-comments-52-8, ra-comments-52-9, ra-comments-51-8]]

#### Boss: Ganon (My Last Breath and Home Sweet Home)

> [!NOTE]
> **Achievement Trigger Warning:** My Last Breath needs Ganon beaten with 1 heart or less, without Fairies or Bombs. Whether Bombchus count as bombs is unconfirmed by any source. Use neither bombs nor Bombchus against Ganon. Commenters say a fairy picked up from debris still counted, but that is not confirmed by the achievement text, so do not rely on it. [[ra-comments-51-2, ra-guide-18-4]]

Navi can Z-Target Ganon this time, but you start without the Master Sword. Shields are useless. [[gf1-347-4, gf1-523-5]]

**Reaching one heart:** Commenters say to get an odd number of hearts, then let Ganon take you down to one heart. Milk can change an even total to odd. A health total of one heart is the goal. The defense upgrade is allowed, and one report says it is probably needed. [[ra-comments-51-1, ra-comments-51-3, ra-comments-51-4, ra-comments-52-13, ra-comments-52-14]]

Other ways commenters used: after the first knockdown and getting the Master Sword back, let him hit you, or walk into the ring of fire around the arena until you reach one heart. Then cast Nayru's Love for the rest of the fight. [[ra-comments-51-10, ra-comments-51-11, ra-comments-52-10]]

**Phase one (no Master Sword):**
1. Z-Target Ganon and side dodge (hold left or right and press A) behind him. [[gf1-347-5, gf1-523-4]]
2. Jump-attack his tail with the Biggoron Sword (or Megaton Hammer). He raises the tail afterward, so strafe back to the front, then around to the tail again. [[gf1-347-5, gf1-523-5]]
3. Alternatively, shoot his face with a Light Arrow to stun him, or roll between his legs, then hit the tail. [[gf2-100-3, gf2-132-1, gf3-91-1, gf4-35-4]]
4. Repeat until a cutscene lowers the flames. Run to Zelda and retake the Master Sword. [[gf1-347-6, gf3-91-2]]

**Phase two:** Light Arrow to the face or roll between his legs, then Z-Target and jump-attack the tail. Commenters note only the Master Sword does real damage here, and Nayru's Love can expire if you swap swords, leaving you open. Light Arrows cannot be used while Nayru's Love is active. [[gf1-347-7, ra-comments-52-1, ra-comments-52-2, ra-comments-52-16]]

If Nayru's Love starts flashing, spam its C-button to recast it. After the Master Sword is back, a commenter says Ganon can take at least five more hits. [[ra-comments-51-15, ra-comments-51-16]]

When Ganon falls, Zelda holds him down. Strike the final blow with the Master Sword while the Sages power it. [[gf1-523-7, gf2-100-4, gf2-132-2, gf3-91-3, gf3-267-4]]


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **My Last Breath (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158146.png)
> *Defeat Ganon with 1 heart or less remaining without using Fairies or Bombs*


My Last Breath unlocks in the ending cutscene, when Link and Zelda talk in the sky. [[ra-comments-51-5, ra-comments-51-18, ra-comments-52-17]]


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Home Sweet Home (25 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158144.png)
> *Banish Ganon to the void of the Evil Realm*


Defeating Ganon banishes him to the void of the Evil Realm. Wait through the cutscenes, because one player reported Home Sweet Home not unlocking at the end screen. [[ra-guide-18-5, ra-comments-53-1]]

Cartographer pops when the game is completed, after the cutscenes that follow the Ganon fight. [[ra-comments-124-6, ra-comments-121-1]]


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Cartographer (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158204.png)
> *Complete the game with every Map, Compass, and Boss Key from all dungeons*


> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 24 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 100 / 100
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 36 / 36
> - **Heart Containers:** 8 / 8
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 26 / 26 (Boss Key in Ganon's Tower) [[gf1-342-2, ra-comments-121-3]]
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 88 / 97 (570 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] Shadow trial cleared and Golden Gauntlets taken
- [ ] Great Fairy defense upgrade received (The Rock, 49289)
- [ ] All six seals destroyed with Light Arrows
- [ ] Boss Key taken on Tower Floor three (26 / 26)
- [ ] Ganondorf beaten without falling and without Fairies (King of Thieves, 49290)
- [ ] Ganon beaten at one heart or less, no Fairies, no bombs or Bombchus (My Last Breath, 49291)
- [ ] Ending watched (Home Sweet Home, 48275)
- [ ] Cartographer (49294) popped on completion

## Chapter 25: 3-Heart Run: Child Dungeons

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  Inside_Deku_Tree["Inside the Deku Tree"]
  Dodongos_Cavern["Dodongo's Cavern"]
  Inside_Jabu["Inside Jabu-Jabu's Belly"]
  Queen_Gohma{{"⚔ Queen Gohma"}}
  King_Dodongo{{"⚔ King Dodongo"}}
  Barinade{{"⚔ Barinade"}}
  Morpha{{"⚔ Morpha"}}
  Bongo_Bongo{{"⚔ Bongo Bongo"}}
  Twinrova{{"⚔ Twinrova"}}
  Ganondorf{{"⚔ Ganondorf"}}
  Ganon{{"⚔ Ganon"}}
  Dark_Link{{"⚔ Shadow Link (Dark Link)"}}
  Dead_Hand{{"⚔ Dead Hand"}}
  Dying_Guard_Lockout>"⛔ Dying guard disappears after the Temple of Time"]
  Final_Battle>"⛔ Ganon's Tower final battle"]
  Inside_Deku_Tree -->|"Basement 2"| Queen_Gohma
  Dodongos_Cavern --> King_Dodongo
  Inside_Jabu --> Barinade
  Ganondorf --> Final_Battle
  Final_Battle --> Ganon
  classDef ponr fill:#7f1d1d,stroke:#f87171,color:#fff;
  classDef boss fill:#4c1d95,stroke:#a78bfa,color:#fff;
  class Dying_Guard_Lockout,Final_Battle ponr;
  class Queen_Gohma,King_Dodongo,Barinade,Morpha,Bongo_Bongo,Twinrova,Ganondorf,Ganon,Dark_Link,Dead_Hand boss;
```

This chapter covers the first three dungeons on a **second save file**, the 3-heart run. In Inside the Deku Tree, Dodongo's Cavern and Inside Jabu-Jabu's Belly you take every dungeon item the route needs. You never pick up a Heart Container, a Piece of Heart, a Map or a Compass. Two timed or no-damage boss achievements are earned here: **Dangerously Delicious** (King Dodongo) and **Race Against the Clock** (Barinade). The 3-heart run limits you to a maximum of 3 hearts, so heart upgrades are off the table [[ra-comments-1-1]]. The cumulative counters in the checkpoint belong to your first save file and do not change on this one.

> [!CAUTION]
> **Point of No Return:** Starting the 3-heart file is a commitment. If you get a heart, map or compass by accident, you have to start from the beginning [[ra-comments-141-7]]. At the start of the game you must choose between this run and the Cartographer achievement, which is also missable [[ra-comments-141-6]]. Create the new file only when you are ready to leave every Map, Compass and heart upgrade untouched.

### Preparation and Ground Rules

Replay the child route from the earlier chapters (Kokiri Forest through Hyrule Field and Death Mountain) on this new file. Pick up Bottles early so you have somewhere to keep fairies. Mido will not let you into the Deku Tree until you carry a Sword and a Shield [[gf1-21-3]]. The Kokiri Sword is in a chest behind the Kokiri Forest crawlspace [[gf2-133-1, gf2-159-2]].

Two sources disagree about whether a few Pieces of Heart break the 3-heart achievements. The RA guide says a Piece of Heart is okay as long as you never take the fourth [[ra-guide-21-1]]. One commenter says you may keep a few small parts as long as they do not complete a whole heart [[ra-comments-126-10]], while another says you may not upgrade hearts at all [[ra-comments-126-9]]. This question is **unconfirmed**. Pick up no Pieces of Heart and no Heart Containers on this file.

The same unconfirmed state applies to Deku Sticks and Dangerously Delicious. One commenter says the achievement triggered with two Deku Stick jump attacks [[ra-comments-125-5]], and another says it did not pop with Deku Sticks [[ra-comments-125-6]]. A third says a first hit with a stick failed to trigger it [[ra-comments-125-7]]. Use only the Bomb Flower and the Kokiri Sword against King Dodongo.

#### Take / Skip List

| Item | Where | Action | Citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Dungeon Map (Deku Tree) | Chest on the upper ledge of the Trunk | **SKIP** | [[gf1-26-3]] |
| Compass (Deku Tree) | Chest in the Compass Room | **SKIP** | [[gf1-28-2]] |
| Fairy Slingshot | Chest past the falling platform | **TAKE** | [[gf1-27-4]] |
| Heart Container (Queen Gohma) | Dropped after the boss | **LEAVE ON THE FLOOR** | [[gf1-32-5]] |
| Dungeon Map (Dodongo's Cavern) | Chest behind a sealed wall in the Antechamber | **SKIP** | [[gf1-72-2]] |
| Compass (Dodongo's Cavern) | Chest in the Armos Trap Room | **SKIP** | [[gf1-73-3]] |
| Bomb Bag | Chest on an upper ledge of the Blade Trap Room | **TAKE** | [[gf1-78-2]] |
| Heart Container (King Dodongo) | Dropped after the boss | **LEAVE ON THE FLOOR** | [[gf1-82-7]] |
| Boomerang | Chest in the Stinger Room, Jabu-Jabu | **TAKE** | [[gf1-109-2]] |
| Dungeon Map (Jabu-Jabu) | Chest in the Orange Tentacle Room | **SKIP** | [[gf1-110-3]] |
| Compass (Jabu-Jabu) | Chest in the Shabom Room | **SKIP** | [[gf1-111-3]] |
| Heart Container (Barinade) | Dropped after the boss | **LEAVE ON THE FLOOR** | [[gf1-115-6]] |

### Inside the Deku Tree

#### The Trunk (entry floor)

Kill the Deku Babas in the corners of the room. Hold the shield up with R while Z-targeting to absorb their attacks, and collect the Deku Sticks and Deku Nuts they drop [[gf1-26-2]]. Slash the bushes along the northeastern wall and climb the ivy wall in the north. At the top, run right along the ledge [[gf1-26-1]].

Partway along the ledge is a chest holding the Dungeon Map. **Do not open it** [[gf1-26-3]]. Keep going right and enter the door [[gf1-26-4]].

#### Scrub Room #1

Z-target the Deku Scrub and reflect his Deku Nut back into his face. He then runs around the room. Do not chase him; stand on his nest, and when he tries to return he walks into you [[gf1-27-1]]. He opens both doors and tells you to press forward on the joystick as you land to avoid fall damage [[gf1-27-2]]. Go through the northeast door [[gf1-27-3]].

#### Slingshot Room

Jump to the gray platform, but quickly hop to the next ledge because the platform starts to fall. Open the chest to get the **Fairy Slingshot** (with a Bullet Bag holding 30) [[gf1-27-4]]. Climb the ivy wall to the right and open the chest for a **Recovery Heart** [[gf1-27-5]]. Back at the floor, shoot down the ladder hanging above the entrance door with the Slingshot, climb it and go back through the door to Scrub Room #1 and the Trunk [[gf1-27-6]].

#### The Trunk (upper level)

Return along the ledge to the Map chest, which you leave closed. Shoot the three Skullwalltullas off the ivy wall with the Slingshot [[gf1-28-1]]. Climb the wall to the top, then go left to the highest level [[gf1-28-1]]. Ignore the Big Skulltulas that drop and keep going left to a door [[gf1-28-1]].

#### Compass Room

Step on the switch next to the lantern and the three platforms in the middle rise. The far chest holds the Compass; **do not open it** [[gf1-28-2]]. For a safe pickup, step on the switch again and use the platforms to reach the small northern alcove. Jump past the Big Skulltula and open that chest for a **Recovery Heart** [[gf1-28-3]]. The Gold Skulltula in the back of the alcove drops the game's first token. It is optional here, because the Skulltula counter belongs to your first file [[gf1-28-4]].

To leave, light a Deku Stick on the lit lantern and use it on the burned-out lantern. The door unlocks [[gf1-28-5]].

#### The Trunk (webbing drop)

Run right past the first Big Skulltula. Kill the second by Z-targeting it, waiting for it to spin around, and using a Jump Attack [[gf1-29-1]]. Jump off the edge and land on the webbing on the first floor to break through to the sub-basement [[gf1-29-2]].

#### Sub-Basement (the pool)

You land in a pool. Climb onto the southeast ledge and step on the switch to activate the lantern [[gf1-29-5]]. The chest behind the lantern holds a **Recovery Heart** [[gf1-29-5]]. Two Gold Skulltulas here, on the eastern grating and on the long ivy wall, are optional for this file [[gf1-29-3, gf1-29-4]].

Light a Deku Stick on the lantern and jump onto the shallow part of the pond, where Link can stand without swimming. Jump to the southwest ledge, run past the Deku Baba and burn the webs on the wall to reveal a door [[gf1-29-6]].

#### Scrub Room #2

Deflect the Scrub's Deku Nuts back with your Deku Shield; the Slingshot does not work on him. Stand on his nest when he runs. He says "23 is number 1", a hint for the last puzzle before the boss [[gf1-30-1]]. Shoot the Silver Eye above the west door with the Slingshot to unlock it [[gf1-30-2]].

#### Trap Room

Jump in the pool and swim to the button on the left wall, directly beneath the rotating spikes. Hold A to dive and touch it, and the water drops considerably [[gf1-30-3]]. Swim back to the entrance and jump onto the floating platform. Hold R to crouch as you pass under the spikes, then hop to the opposite ledge [[gf1-30-4]]. Kill the Big Skulltula, push the large stone block all the way to the right, climb onto it and jump to the door [[gf1-30-5]].

#### Torch Room and Small Nest

Kill the Deku Baba in the center. Light a Deku Stick on the lit torch and use it on the two unlit sconces to open the north door [[gf1-31-1]]. In the Small Nest, run circles in the center to drop the Gohma Larva and kill each with one Jump Attack. Their eye turns red just before they charge [[gf1-31-2]]. Light a Deku Stick on the northern torches, burn the southern webbing and crawl through the tunnel back to the Trunk [[gf1-31-3]].

#### The Trunk (final descent)

Push the stone block along its carved path off the cliff. Jump down, light a Deku Stick on the southeast torch, then climb back to the upper level [[gf1-32-1]]. Roll over the floor webbing with the lit stick to burn it, then drop to the lowest level [[gf1-32-2]].

Swim north to the shore where three Deku Scrubs attack. Hit them in the order middle, right, then left, which matches the "23 is number 1" hint [[gf1-32-7]]. A second source gives the order as 2, 3, 1 from left to right [[gf2-11-1]]. The door to the boss then unlocks [[gf1-32-7]].

#### Boss: Queen Gohma

Run to the center, press C-Up and look at the ceiling to start the fight [[gf1-32-3]]. Z-target Gohma immediately and shoot her in the eye with the Slingshot while it glows red. This stuns her, so follow with Jump Attacks [[gf1-32-3]]. After one or two attacks she climbs the ceiling and spawns Gohma Larva. If you shoot her eye before she spawns them she falls and stays stunned a long time, which gives you three or four more Jump Attacks [[gf1-32-4]]. Strike her eggs once each before they hatch [[gf2-11-3]]. The pattern repeats if you miss [[gf1-518-2]].

The Heart Container appears after the kill. **Leave it on the floor** [[gf1-32-5]]. Walk around it into the blue light, then listen to the Great Deku Tree, who gives you the Kokiri Emerald [[gf1-32-6]].

### Dodongo's Cavern

Dodongo's Cavern needs the Goron's Bracelet to pick up Bomb Flowers. Darunia gives it after you play Zelda's Lullaby to open his door and then Saria's Song for him [[checklist-sidequests-9-25, gf1-63-3]]. Outside the cave, grab a Bomb Flower and throw it at the cracked wall [[gf1-68-1]].

#### Antechamber

Run across the wooden flooring onto the island in the middle of the embers and dodge the Beamos. Grab the Bomb Flower in front of the window, Z-target the Beamos to its right and throw the flower at its head to destroy it [[gf1-68-2]]. Use another Bomb Flower on the sealed wall to the right of the Beamos and go through the dark tunnel [[gf1-68-3]].

#### Lower Southeast Hall

Kill the Baby Dodongos that burst from the ground and move away from their explosions [[gf1-170-1]]. At the sealed wall, stand beside it and kill a Baby Dodongo as it passes so the explosion opens the barrier [[gf1-69-1]].

The Torch Room beyond has three Keese. If they fly through the torch flame they catch fire and deal extra damage, so block with your shield [[gf1-69-2]]. A Gold Skulltula on the wall is optional here [[gf1-69-3]].

Back in the hall, push an Armos Statue onto the gray switch to unlock the door [[gf1-70-1]]. Shoot the Keese in the Southeast Passage with the Slingshot [[gf1-70-2]].

#### Lizalfos Nest (lower)

Block each Lizalfos attack with the shield and counter with the sword [[gf1-70-3]]. After two Kokiri Sword jump attacks, the one you are fighting runs away [[checklist-sidequests-7-2]]. A Deku Nut stun followed by a Jump Attack also sends one running [[checklist-sidequests-7-1]]. Kill both and the west door unlocks [[gf1-70-4]].

#### Dodongo Breeding Ground

Run behind the pair of Dodongos and hit their tails; they explode when defeated [[gf1-71-1]]. Light a Deku Stick on the torch and light the three empty sconces. The door at the west end then opens [[gf1-71-2]].

A sealed wall in the passage leads to a Scrub who sells Deku Sticks for fifteen rupees [[gf1-71-3]].

#### Antechamber (the window)

Step on the switch inside the window to open the far door [[gf1-72-1]]. Drop out of the window. A sealed wall near the unlocked door hides a Dungeon Map chest, which you **do not open** [[gf1-72-2]]. Take the door in the northwest corner [[gf1-72-3]].

#### Bomb Flower Room

An oddly colored wall at the south end is sealed. Break it with a Bomb Flower [[gf1-73-1]]. The room behind it holds only the Compass chest, guarded by an Armos Knight, and you skip it [[gf1-73-3]].

Throw a Bomb Flower exactly between the two long lines of Bomb Flowers around the Iron Pillar. They ignite together and lower the pillar into stairs [[gf1-74-1]]. Climb the stairs and follow the path to the ivy. A Gold Skulltula token there is optional [[gf1-74-2]].

#### Pillar Room

Shoot the pair of Fire Keese with the Slingshot, or use the shield [[gf1-75-1]]. Pull the Armos Statue off the ladder on the south side without touching the other statues, because they are Armos Knights in disguise [[gf1-75-4]]. Climb the ladder and step on the switch to unlock the east door [[gf1-75-2]]. From the upper catwalk leap the gap and run through the tunnel [[gf1-75-3]].

#### Blade Trap Room

Climb the stone block and ladder, then jump to the small platform for a chest with a red rupee. Rupees are safe to take [[gf1-76-1]]. Hold a Bomb Flower until it is nearly exploding and throw it at the wall above the ladder to open the tunnel [[gf1-76-2]].

#### Solo Eye, Lizalfos Nest (upper), Double Eye

Shoot the golden eye with the Slingshot to extinguish the ring of fire [[gf1-77-1]]. Defeat the two Lizalfos in the upper nest the same way as before to open the west door [[gf1-77-2]]. In the Double Eye Room, shoot the high eye, jump to the platform, then shoot the second eye in the alcove on your left [[gf1-78-1]].

In the upper Blade Trap Room, jump the gap, turn right, jump up and open the chest. It holds the **Bomb Bag** with 20 bombs, which you want [[gf1-78-2]].

#### Antechamber (upper level)

Step on the switch to raise a pillar [[gf1-79-1]]. Cross the catwalk to between the two gaps. Drop bombs through each gap into the skull's eye sockets, and the mouth opens [[gf1-79-2]]. A sealed wall at the catwalk's end hides a chest with a Deku Shield or blue rupee, which is safe to take [[gf1-79-3]]. Drop to the central platform and enter the mouth [[gf1-79-4]].

#### Back Room and Goron Graveyard

Kill the Fire Keese and climb east through the tunnel [[gf1-80-1, gf1-80-2]]. In the Graveyard pit, watch for two swooping Fire Keese. Bomb the sealed wall there to open the door [[gf1-80-3, gf1-80-4]]. The Armos Knight room beyond holds one optional token behind a statue [[gf1-81-1]]. Pots in the pit after it contain recovery items and a Fairy to bottle [[gf1-81-3]].

#### Backroom and The Pit

Push the brown stone block into the opening in the floor's center to unlock the western door [[gf1-82-1]]. In the pit room, open the chest for Bombs [[gf1-82-2]]. Drop a bomb on the iron plate to dissolve it and drop to the boss [[gf1-82-3]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Dangerously Delicious is missable on this file. The block puzzle before the boss must be redone every time you retry the fight [[ra-comments-126-1]]. The checks are: no Bombs from your bag, no damage and 3 hearts only [[gf1-82-4, ra-comments-125-1]]. Do not open the bag; enter the boss room with the Kokiri Sword equipped and make no hit with Deku Sticks [[ra-comments-125-7]].

#### Boss: King Dodongo (Dangerously Delicious)

King Dodongo lumbers in a circle and inhales before his fire breath. A bomb in his mouth stuns him for one sword swing, and the second phase has him roll around the room [[gf1-82-4, gf1-82-5]]. Standing on the edge of the lava or in a corner avoids the roll [[gf1-82-5]]. The 45 degree slope between the path and the central lava makes you immune to his attacks [[gf2-28-3]]. Three Jump Attacks or six normal sword hits kill him [[gf1-82-6]].

For this achievement the bomb must be a Bomb Flower, not one from your bag. Community routing:

1. Do not run to the Bomb Flower at the start. The key is making the boss roll [[ra-comments-125-1]].
2. Run behind him at the start so he rolls around the arena [[ra-comments-125-2]]. Running into him while he is not rolling does not hurt, so run closer to him than the lava [[ra-comments-126-5]]. You can also roll under his left side without damage [[ra-comments-126-6]].
3. After you pass him he starts rolling. Then run toward him carrying a Bomb Flower in front of him [[ra-comments-126-7]].
4. Step near the lava to dodge his roll, then run at him again [[ra-comments-126-8]]. Standing nearly beside him by the lava lets him roll right past you [[ra-comments-125-3]].
5. Wait for him to come around, put the Bomb Flower in his mouth, then run in and attack [[ra-comments-125-4]]. The hardest part is getting that first bomb in quickly enough [[ra-comments-126-12]].

Common mistakes are grabbing the bomb too early or not throwing it into his mouth [[ra-comments-125-13]]. A commenter reports the Slingshot can make him cancel his fire breath and move closer [[ra-comments-126-14]]. Whether a cancelled breath satisfies the achievement is **unconfirmed**. If you fail you must reset and redo the puzzle, and dying does not unlock it [[ra-comments-125-8]]. The achievement now checks for the victory music [[ra-comments-125-10]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Dangerously Delicious (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/158205.png)
> *Defeat King Dodongo before he breathes fire without using Bombs from your bag or taking damage, with 3 hearts only*


Do not touch the Heart Container he leaves behind [[gf1-82-7, gf2-28-5]]. Enter the ring of light and Darunia gives you the Goron's Ruby [[gf1-82-8]].

### Inside Jabu-Jabu's Belly

Empty a fish from a bottle in front of Lord Jabu-Jabu and he sucks you in [[checklist-sidequests-12-8, gf3-171-2]].

#### Entrance and Elevator Room

Two Octoroks appear on the left and right. Kill them with the Slingshot, then use Deku Nuts on the Shaboms [[gf1-105-2]]. Shoot the large yellow bump on the ceiling at the north end to unseal the door [[gf1-105-3]]. A Biri in the Elevator Room can be stunned with a Deku Nut [[gf1-106-1]].

#### Pit Room and Water Room

In the Pit Room, Ruto runs off and falls down a pit. Follow her, talk to her twice and carry her out the east door [[gf1-106-3, gf1-106-2]]. Do not step on the middle switch while Ruto is in the pit; the water rises and she returns to the Pit Room [[gf1-107-1]].

Use Deku Nuts to make Stingers jump and kill them with the Slingshot [[gf1-107-2]]. A Gold Skulltula on the west ivy wall is optional [[gf1-107-3]]. Pots in the alcove hold a Fairy and Seeds [[gf1-107-4]]. Shoot the ceiling switch in the north hall to unlock its door [[gf1-107-5]].

#### Elevator Room and Crawling Hallway

Ride the elevator up while carrying Ruto [[gf1-108-1]]. Cross the Pit Room avoiding Biri and floor pits [[gf1-108-3]]. In the Crawling Hallway, step on the yellow switch while carrying Ruto and go through the door [[gf1-109-1]].

#### Stinger Room

Kill all the Stingers with Deku Nuts and the Slingshot. A chest appears with the **Boomerang**, which you take [[gf1-109-2]].

#### Orange Tentacle Room (skip the Map)

Place Ruto on the blue switch at the hallway's end by stepping down and pressing A. Go through the door [[gf1-110-1]]. Strike the narrow segment of the Orange Tentacle four times with the Boomerang. This removes the other orange tentacles [[gf1-110-2]]. A chest with the Dungeon Map appears; **leave it closed** [[gf1-110-3]].

#### Hallway rooms

Ruto waits beyond the door and you pick her up [[gf1-111-1]]. The left door leads to the Shabom Room, whose chest holds the Compass and which you skip [[gf1-111-2, gf1-111-3]]. Take the route to the Blue Tentacle Room and hit it four times with the Boomerang while dodging [[gf1-111-4, gf1-112-1]]. For the Green Tentacle Room, go right down the hall, take another right and kill the Biri first, then the tentacle [[gf1-112-2, gf1-112-3]].

#### Pit Room (lower drop) and Bigocto

Run straight across the hallway to the Pit Room [[gf1-113-1]]. Drop down the pit closest to the west wall [[gf1-113-2]]. Two optional Gold Skulltulas hang on the north wall [[gf1-113-3]].

Bigocto's weak point is the green eye on his backside. Stun him with the Boomerang and use Jump Attacks; two kill him [[gf1-113-4, gf1-113-5]]. Letting him run into you turns him around, which is quicker [[gf1-516-3]]. One of the pots behind you holds a fairy [[gf3-34-2]]. Step onto the middle platform to raise the elevator [[gf1-113-6]].

#### Pink Things, Elevator Room, Biri Room

Stun each Pink Thing with the Boomerang and hop across [[gf1-114-1]]. In the Elevator Room, set a box on the blue switch to keep it pressed [[gf1-114-2]]. In the Biri Room, kill the Biri, then climb the ivy wall on the southeast side [[gf1-115-1]]. At the top, hit the switch to the right with the Boomerang to unlock the north door [[gf1-115-2]].

> [!WARNING]
> **Missable Achievement Warning:** Race Against the Clock is missable on this file. You need a sub-1:51 kill at 3 hearts [[gf1-115-3, ra-comments-4-2]]. The timer starts when you enter the room and runs through both parts of the cutscene (about 1 minute 10 seconds) [[ra-comments-128-1, ra-comments-127-2]]. It stops a few seconds after the boss is beaten, not on the last hit [[ra-comments-128-1, ra-comments-130-7]].

#### Boss: Barinade (Race Against the Clock)

Prepare before the door. Set Z-targeting to Hold to make the fight easier [[ra-guide-9-1]]. Put the Boomerang, Deku Nuts and Deku Sticks on your C buttons [[ra-comments-131-7]]. Another commenter also brought Din's Fire [[ra-comments-128-5]], but another says Deku Nuts are faster than Din's [[ra-comments-130-2]]. Stand in the room with a Heart Container in mind: it spawns two steps ahead of the death point, and one player ran straight into it [[ra-comments-129-10]]. Remember that no pot tricks are required; pots around the room each hold a Recovery Heart [[gf2-43-1]].

To shorten the intro, face the boss, save the game and reset [[ra-comments-127-9, ra-comments-131-3]]. After that the intro is shorter and wastes less time [[ra-comments-127-9]]. A commenter also found that playing from a continue skips the opening [[ra-comments-131-16]]. Another reported that letting the boss kill you first makes the cutscene shorter [[ra-comments-130-11]].

1. **Phase 1:** roll forward twice and take a couple steps [[ra-guide-9-2]]. Target the three top tentacles with the Boomerang, circling Barinade and hitting each one [[ra-comments-130-1, ra-guide-9-2]]. Pick a direction and stick to it, because changing course gets you electrocuted [[gf1-518-5]]. Use Link's yelp to know when he has caught the Boomerang so you can target and throw again quickly [[ra-comments-131-8]].
2. **Phase 2:** let go of the target and immediately throw the Boomerang at the center [[ra-guide-9-2]]. Throwing without Z-target at center mass can detach the jellyfish quickly. Then kill them all in one hit with Deku Nuts [[ra-comments-130-2]]. Attack Barinade directly rather than the jellyfish [[ra-comments-128-2]]. Try to stand between the cracks in the jellyfish armor so they miss you [[ra-comments-131-9]].
3. **Phase 3:** throw the Boomerang again before the jellyfish hitboxes become active so they disconnect, then use one or two Deku Nuts [[ra-comments-130-3]]. Throw it as the boss rises out of the floor; the jellyfish bunch under him and one nut kills them [[ra-comments-131-12]].
4. **Final phase:** chase Barinade, hit him with the Boomerang, then jump-slash with a Deku Stick [[ra-comments-130-4]]. When he buries into the ground, roll into him and take the minimal damage so he surfaces sooner [[ra-comments-130-5, ra-comments-129-8]]. The stick breaks after the jump attack, so you can roll right away [[ra-comments-131-13]]. Repeat twice more to end the fight [[ra-comments-130-6]].

The guide says five Jump Attacks defeat Barinade [[gf1-115-5]]. One commenter says three Deku Stick jump attacks were enough [[ra-comments-130-14]]. Be ready for either count. Keep dodging the satellite dishes, since they fire the whole time [[gf1-519-3]].


> [!ACHIEVEMENT]
> **Race Against the Clock (10 Points)**
> ![Badge](https://retroachievements.org/Badge/240846.png)
> *Defeat Barinade in less than 1:51 with 3 hearts only*


Do **not** pick up the Heart Container [[gf1-115-6]]. Step into the ring of blue light. Ruto gives you the Zora Sapphire if you agree to marry her [[gf1-115-7]].

> [!TIP]
> **Before you leave:** Confirm that you opened no chest holding a Map or a Compass [[gf1-26-3, gf1-72-2, gf1-110-3]]. Make sure every Heart Container stayed on the floor [[gf1-32-5, gf1-82-7, gf1-115-6]]. Your next stop is the adult temples.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> ### Chapter 25 Cumulative 100% Checkpoint
> - **Heart Containers:** 8 / 8 (all three child bosses' containers left on the floor on this file) [[gf1-115-6]]
> - **Pieces of Heart:** 36 / 36
> - **Maps, Compasses & Boss Keys:** 26 / 26
> - **Gold Skulltulas:** 100 / 100
> - **RetroAchievements Unlocked:** 90 / 97 (590 / 690 points)

### Chapter Checklist

- [ ] New 3-heart save file created; no Piece of Heart or Heart Container taken
- [ ] Deku Tree: Slingshot taken, Map and Compass chests left shut
- [ ] Queen Gohma defeated; Heart Container left on the floor; Kokiri Emerald received
- [ ] Dodongo's Cavern: Bomb Bag taken, Map and Compass chests left shut
- [ ] King Dodongo killed with a Bomb Flower and the Kokiri Sword only, no damage [ACHIEVEMENT:48918 already placed]
- [ ] Jabu-Jabu: Boomerang taken, Map and Compass chests left shut
- [ ] Barinade killed in under 1:51 [ACHIEVEMENT:48916 already placed]
- [ ] Barinade's Heart Container left on the floor; Zora Sapphire received
